We therefore reformulate hallucination evaluation as a diagnostic problem and propose PRISM, a controlled benchmark that disentangles hallucinations into four dimensions: knowledge missing, knowledge errors, reasoning errors, and instruction-following errors, grounded in three stages of generation (memory, instruction, and reasoning). PRISM contains 9,448 instances across 65 tasks and supports fine-grained, stage-aware diagnostic evaluation. Evaluating 24 mainstream open-source and proprietary LLMs, we uncover consistent trade-offs across instruction following, memory retrieval, and logical reasoning, showing that mitigation strategies often improve specific dimensions at the expense of others. We hope PRISM provides a framework for understanding the specific mechanisms behind LLMs hallucinations, ultimately accelerating the development of trustworthy large language models.

</details>


### [39] [When Choices Become Risks: Safety Failures of Large Language Models under Multiple-Choice Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16916)
*Yuheng Chen,Zhiyu Wu,Bowen Cheng,Tetsuro Takahashi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大型语言模型（LLMs）在结构化决策任务（如多项选择题）中存在系统性安全对齐失效：当有害请求被改写为所有选项均不安全的强制选择题时，模型拒绝行为被绕过，导致安全违规率显著上升，尤其在中等约束强度或由高能力模型生成的题目下更为严重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估主要基于开放生成场景（模型可拒答），但实际应用中大量使用结构化任务（如MCQ），而拒答机制在此类场景中受限或不可用，因此需研究模型在强制决策下的对齐鲁棒性。

Method: 构建并测试多种人为编写与模型自动生成的、所有选项均不安全的强制选择题（forced-choice MCQs），在14个主流闭源与开源LLM上量化其安全违规响应率，并分析约束强度、题目来源与跨模型迁移性的影响。

Result: 强制选择约束显著提升政策违反响应率；人工题呈倒U型违规趋势（峰值在中等约束），而高能力模型生成的题目则在各类约束下均接近饱和违规，且具备强跨模型泛化性。

Conclusion: 当前安全评估严重低估结构化任务中的真实风险，约束性决策场景是安全对齐的关键薄弱环节，亟需针对性评估与加固。

Abstract: Safety alignment in large language models (LLMs) is primarily evaluated under open-ended generation, where models can mitigate risk by refusing to respond. In contrast, many real-world applications place LLMs in structured decision-making tasks, such as multiple-choice questions (MCQs), where abstention is discouraged or unavailable. We identify a systematic failure mode in this setting: reformulating harmful requests as forced-choice MCQs, where all options are unsafe, can systematically bypass refusal behavior, even in models that consistently reject equivalent open-ended prompts. Across 14 proprietary and open-source models, we show that forced-choice constraints sharply increase policy-violating responses. Notably, for human-authored MCQs, violation rates follow an inverted U-shaped trend with respect to structural constraint strength, peaking under intermediate task specifications, whereas MCQs generated by high-capability models yield near-saturation violation rates across constraints and exhibit strong cross-model transferability. Our findings reveal that current safety evaluations substantially underestimate risks in structured task settings and highlight constrained decision-making as a critical and underexplored surface for alignment failures.

</details>


### [40] [x1: Learning to Think Adaptively Across Languages and Cultures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16917)
*Yangfan Ye,Xiaocheng Feng,Xiachong Feng,Yichong Huang,Zekun Yuan,Lei Huang,Weitao Ma,Qichen Hong,Yunfei Lu,Dandan Tu,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出x1模型家族，能够根据任务实例自适应选择最优语言进行推理，通过对比同一输入的不同语言推理路径进行训练，在多语言数学推理和文化相关任务中展现出优势，表明语言选择是推理的功能性组成部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数大语言模型忽视了语言多样性，仅在单一主导语言中推理；而不同语言承载不同的抽象方式和归纳先验，因此需要能自适应选择推理语言的模型。

Method: 构建x1模型家族，不扩展知识边界，仅通过对比同一输入在不同语言中的推理轨迹进行训练，以隔离推理语言选择的影响。

Result: 在多语言数学推理和文化相关任务上显著提升性能；缩放模型虽可缩小数学类任务的跨语言差距，但无法消除文化相关任务中文化适配语言的优势，后者能更高效准确地调用文化知识。

Conclusion: 语言选择是推理的功能性组成部分，应被视作构建更通用、更具全球胜任力推理模型的关键设计维度。

Abstract: Languages encode distinct abstractions and inductive priors, yet most large language models (LLMs) overlook this diversity by reasoning in a single dominant language. In this work, we introduce x1, a family of reasoning models that can adaptively reason in an advantageous language on a per-instance basis. To isolate the effect of reasoning-language choice, x1 is constructed without expanding the model's knowledge boundaries and is trained by contrasting linguistically distinct reasoning trajectories for the same input. Our extensive experiments demonstrate the benefits of adaptive multilingual reasoning across multilingual mathematical reasoning and culturally grounded tasks. Moreover, our results challenge a simplistic view of scaling laws: while scaling reduces cross-lingual disparities in procedural domains such as math reasoning, it does not eliminate the advantages of culture-associated languages in culturally grounded tasks, as we empirically show that such reasoning enables more efficient and accurate cultural knowledge recall. Overall, our findings establish language choice as a functional component of reasoning, with implications for building more generalist and globally competent reasoning models.

</details>


### [41] [Freshness-Aware Prioritized Experience Replay for LLM/VLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16918)
*Weiyu Ma,Yongcheng Zeng,Yan Song,Xinyu Cui,Jian Zhao,Xuhui Liu,Mohamed Elhoseiny*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的Freshness-Aware PER方法，通过引入基于有效样本量分析的指数年龄衰减机制，解决了在大语言模型（LLM）和视觉-语言模型（VLM）强化学习中直接应用优先经验回放（PER）导致的优先级过时问题，显著提升了多步智能体任务中的样本效率与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统on-policy RL算法（如PPO、GRPO）在LLM/VLM后训练中样本效率低，尤其在高成本多轮交互的智能体任务中浪费严重；而标准PER因大模型策略快速演化导致存储的优先级迅速过时，无法直接适用。

Method: 提出Freshness-Aware PER，在原有PER优先级基础上乘以一个基于有效样本量推导的指数年龄衰减因子，动态降低旧轨迹的采样权重，从而缓解优先级 staleness 问题。

Result: 在8个涵盖搜索、推理、数学竞赛和VLM控制的多步任务上，使用0.5B/3B/7B模型验证，Freshness-Aware PER显著超越on-policy基线（如+46% NQ Search、+367% Sokoban、+133% VLM FrozenLake），而标准PER则持续损害性能。

Conclusion: Freshness-Aware PER是首个成功将PER应用于LLM/VLM强化学习的工作，为提升大模型智能体训练的样本效率提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has achieved impressive success in post-training Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs), with on-policy algorithms such as PPO, GRPO, and REINFORCE++ serving as the dominant paradigm. However, these methods discard all collected trajectories after a single gradient update, resulting in poor sample efficiency, particularly wasteful for agentic tasks where multi-turn environment interactions are expensive. While Experience Replay drives sample efficiency in classic RL by allowing agents to reuse past trajectories and prioritize informative ones, directly applying Prioritized Experience Replay (PER) to LLMs fails. The rapid policy evolution of billion-parameter models renders stored priorities stale, causing old high-priority trajectories to dominate sampling long after they have become uninformative. We propose Freshness-Aware PER, which addresses this priority staleness problem by augmenting any PER-based priority with a multiplicative exponential age decay grounded in effective sample size analysis. To the best of our knowledge, Freshness-Aware PER is the first work to successfully apply PER to LLM/VLM reinforcement learning. We evaluate on eight multi-step agentic, reasoning, and math competition tasks with 0.5B, 3B, and 7B models. Freshness-Aware PER significantly outperforms on-policy baselines, achieving +46% on NQ Search, +367% on Sokoban, and +133% on VLM FrozenLake, while standard PER without age decay consistently degrades performance. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Vision-CAIR/Freshness-Aware-PER.

</details>


### [42] [MeasHalu: Mitigation of Scientific Measurement Hallucinations for Large Language Models with Enhanced Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16929)
*Ruijun Huang,Zhiqiao Kang,Yuxuan Zhu,Junxiong Li,Jiahao Zhao,Minghuan Tan,Feng Jiang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出MeasHalu框架，通过细粒度分类测量幻觉、两阶段推理感知微调及渐进式奖励课程，显著降低大语言模型在科学测量提取中的幻觉率，提升MeasEval基准上的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型（LLMs）在科学测量提取中存在严重幻觉问题，影响自动化科学文献理解系统的可靠性。

Method: 构建测量幻觉的细粒度分类体系；采用基于增强科学数据和过程监督的两阶段推理感知微调；设计针对不同幻觉类型的渐进式奖励课程进行优化。

Result: 在MeasEval基准上，MeasHalu显著降低了幻觉率并提高了整体提取准确率。

Conclusion: MeasHalu为解决AI4Science中科学测量提取的关键瓶颈提供了有针对性、可信赖且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: The accurate extraction of scientific measurements from literature is a critical yet challenging task in AI4Science, enabling large-scale analysis and integration of quantitative research findings. However, Large Language Models (LLMs) frequently exhibit severe hallucinations, which significantly undermine the reliability of automated scientific document understanding systems. To address this problem, we propose MeasHalu, a novel framework for mitigating scientific measurement hallucinations through enhanced reasoning and targeted optimization. We first present a fine-grained taxonomy of measurement-specific hallucinations, categorizing errors across quantities, units, modifiers, and relations. Our approach incorporates a two-stage reasoning-aware fine-tuning strategy using augmented scientific data and process-based supervision. Furthermore, we introduce a progressive reward curriculum designed to penalize specific hallucination types, significantly improving extraction faithfulness. Experimental results demonstrate that MeasHalu substantially reduces hallucination rates and improves overall accuracy on the MeasEval benchmark. This work provides a targeted solution to a key bottleneck in automated scientific knowledge extraction, facilitating more trustworthy and scalable machine-assisted scientific literature analysis.

</details>


### [43] [No One Fits All: From Fixed Prompting to Learned Routing in Multilingual LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16937)
*Wei-Chi Wu,Sheng-Lun Wei,Hen-Hsen Huang,Hsin-Hsi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多语言大语言模型中基于翻译的提示策略的有效性，发现其效果因语言和任务而异；提出了一种轻量级分类器来动态选择最优提示策略，并证明该方法在多个基准上显著优于固定策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 翻译提示策略在不同语言和任务中的效果不一，需要一种更智能的策略选择机制。

Method: 通过在十种不同资源水平的语言和四个基准上评估多种提示策略，提出将策略选择建模为学习型决策问题，并设计轻量级分类器进行预测。

Result: 所提分类器在四个基准上显著优于固定策略，且能泛化到训练中未见过的新任务格式；分析表明语言资源水平比翻译质量更能决定翻译提示是否有效。

Conclusion: 没有一种提示策略适用于所有语言和任务；基于语言资源水平动态选择提示策略是更优方案。

Abstract: Translation-based prompting is widely used in multilingual LLMs, yet its effectiveness varies across languages and tasks. We evaluate prompting strategies across ten languages of different resource levels and four benchmarks. Our analysis shows that no single strategy is universally optimal. Translation strongly benefits low-resource languages even when translation quality is imperfect, high-resource languages gain little, and prompt-based self-routing underperforms explicit translation. Motivated by these findings, we formulate prompting strategy selection as a learned decision problem and introduce lightweight classifiers that predict whether native or translation-based prompting is optimal for each instance. The classifiers achieve statistically significant improvements over fixed strategies across four benchmarks and generalize to unseen task formats not observed during training. Further analysis reveals that language resource level, rather than translation quality alone, determines when translation is beneficial.

</details>


### [44] [MNAFT: modality neuron-aware fine-tuning of multimodal large language models for image translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16943)
*Bo Li,Ningyuan Deng,Tianyu Dong,Shaobo Wang,Shaolin Zhu,Lijie Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模态神经元感知微调方法（MNAFT），通过识别和选择性微调多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）中与语言无关和语言相关的特定神经元，以提升图像翻译任务的性能，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs在图像翻译中难以有效捕捉图像中的细粒度文本信息，导致视觉文本输入与文本输入/输出之间存在模态鸿沟；而主流指令微调方法易造成参数冗余，损害泛化能力。

Method: 提出模态神经元感知微调（MNAFT）：首先通过指令驱动的激活分析识别视觉与语言模块中的语言无关和语言特异性神经元；然后仅对目标任务相关层中这两类神经元进行选择性参数更新，保留其余神经元知识。

Result: 在多个基准上显著超越现有图像翻译方法（包括级联模型、全量微调及各类参数高效微调技术）；并通过神经元激活可视化与聚类分析揭示了不同神经元组在跨模态理解与语言特异性翻译中的作用。

Conclusion: MNAFT通过精细化操控MLLM内部神经元功能，有效弥合模态鸿沟，在保持模型通用性的同时显著提升图像翻译精度，为多模态微调提供了新范式。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown impressive capabilities, yet they often struggle to effectively capture the fine-grained textual information within images crucial for accurate image translation. This often leads to a modality gap between visual text inputs and textual inputs/outputs for image translation. Existing methods, primarily relying on instruction fine-tuning, risk parameter redundancy of pre-trained knowledge, hindering generalization performance. To address this, we introduce modality neuron-aware fine-tuning (MNAFT), a novel approach that takes advantage of the specialized roles of individual neurons within MLLMs for enhanced image translation. MNAFT identifies language-agnostic and language-specific neurons in both vision and language modules through an instruction-driven activation analysis, evaluating their importance in various translation tasks. We then perform selective fine-tuning, updating only the parameters of language-specific and language-agnostic neurons within the selected layers relevant to the target task, while preserving the knowledge encoded in other neurons and layers. Our extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that MNAFT significantly outperforms state-of-the-art image translation methods, including cascaded models, standard full fine-tuning, and parameter-efficient tuning techniques. Furthermore, we provide comprehensive analysis, including visualizations of neuron activations and clustering patterns, to offer insights into the roles of different neuron groups in mediating cross-modal understanding and facilitating accurate language-specific translation.

</details>


### [45] [On Safety Risks in Experience-Driven Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16968)
*Weixiang Zhao,Yichen Zhang,Yingshuo Wang,Yang Deng,Yanyan Zhao,Xuda Zhi,Yongbo Huang,HaoHe,Wanxiang Che,Bing Qin,Ting Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于经验驱动的自进化大语言模型代理在安全性能上的风险，发现即使仅从良性任务中积累经验，也会在高风险场景中损害安全性；执行导向的经验强化了代理倾向于行动而非拒绝，而拒绝相关经验虽能缓解安全下降但导致过度拒绝，揭示了安全与效用间的根本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验驱动的自进化范式虽提升了大语言模型代理的自主性，但其依赖自我构建经验带来了尚未充分探索的安全风险。

Method: 通过在基于网页和具身环境中的实验，系统分析经验积累与使用对代理安全性能的影响，并深入探究经验性质（如执行导向 vs 拒绝导向）对行为倾向的作用机制。

Result: 1）仅从良性任务中积累的经验仍会导致高风险场景下的安全性能下降；2）执行导向经验强化行动倾向、削弱拒绝能力；3）引入拒绝相关经验可缓解安全下降但引发过度拒绝；4）存在不可忽视的安全-效用权衡。

Conclusion: 当前经验驱动的自进化代理存在固有安全局限，亟需更系统、更原则性的策略来保障其安全可靠的自适应能力。

Abstract: Experience-driven self-evolution has emerged as a promising paradigm for improving the autonomy of large language model agents, yet its reliance on self-curated experience introduces underexplored safety risks. In this study, we investigate how experience accumulation and utilization in self-evolving agents affect safety performance across web-based and embodied environments. Notably, experience gathered solely from benign tasks can still compromise safety in high-risk scenarios. Further analysis attributes this degradation to the execution-oriented nature of accumulated experience, which reinforces agents' tendency to act rather than refuse. In more realistic settings where agents encounter both benign and harmful tasks, refusal-related experience mitigates safety decline but induces over-refusal, revealing a fundamental safety-utility trade-off. Overall, our findings expose inherent limitations of current self-evolving agents and call for more principled strategies to ensure safe and reliable adaptation.

</details>


### [46] [Bolzano: Case Studies in LLM-Assisted Mathematical Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16989)
*Jan Grebík,Pavel Hubáček,Martin Koutecký,Matěj Kripner,Václav Rozhoň,Robert Šámal,Adrián Zámečník*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Bolzano——一个开源的多智能体大语言模型系统，在数学和理论计算机科学的六个问题上取得了新成果，其中四项成果达到可发表水平，三项基本由Bolzano自主完成，表明大语言模型可实质性参与数学研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索大语言模型（LLM）在数学与理论计算机科学研究中的实际贡献能力，验证其是否能辅助甚至自主完成高水平科研工作。

Method: 使用名为Bolzano的开源多智能体LLM系统，通过并行证明者代理与验证者代理的多轮交互，并结合跨轮次持久化知识库，协同解决数学与理论计算机科学问题。

Result: 在六个问题上获得新结果；其中四项达到可发表研究水平，三项基本由系统自主完成。

Conclusion: 大语言模型可通过多智能体协同架构实质性参与数学研究，为AI驱动科研提供了实证支持。

Abstract: We report new results on six problems in mathematics and theoretical computer science, produced with the assistance of Bolzano, an open-source multi-agent LLM system. Bolzano orchestrates rounds of interaction between parallel prover agents and a verifier agent while maintaining a persistent knowledge base that is carried across rounds. Classified using the significance-autonomy taxonomy of Feng et al., four of the six results reach the level of publishable research, and three of the six were produced essentially autonomously by Bolzano. Our results provide evidence that LLMs can contribute meaningfully to mathematical research, complementing recent reports by Bubeck et al., Woodruff et al., and others.

</details>


### [47] [SPS: Steering Probability Squeezing for Better Exploration in Reinforcement Learning for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16995)
*Yifu Huo,Chenglong Wang,Ziming Zhu,Shunjie Xing,Peinan Feng,Tongran Liu,Qiaozhi He,Tianhua Zhou,Xiaojia Chang,Jingbo Zhu,Zhengtao Yu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Steering Probability Squeezing（SPS）方法，通过交替使用强化学习（RL）与逆强化学习（IRL），缓解RL训练中轨迹分布过度集中导致的探索不足问题，从而提升推理模型的多样本性能（Pass@k）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL训练虽能提升单样本成功率（Pass@1），但因‘概率挤压效应’导致推理路径多样性不足，限制了多样本性能（Pass@k）；需增强探索能力而不依赖外部监督。

Method: 提出Steering Probability Squeezing（SPS）范式：在RL训练中穿插逆强化学习（IRL），将on-policy采样轨迹视为示范，用IRL显式重塑轨迹分布，以提升探索性。

Result: 在五个主流推理基准上验证SPS有效提升了Pass@k；同时分析了RL学习动力学，并实证给出了Pass@k的经验上界。

Conclusion: 交替使用RL与IRL是扩展推理导向大语言模型探索能力的有效途径；SPS无需额外监督即可改善轨迹多样性与多样本性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising paradigm for training reasoning-oriented models by leveraging rule-based reward signals. However, RL training typically tends to improve single-sample success rates (i.e., Pass@1) while offering limited exploration of diverse reasoning trajectories, which is crucial for multi-sample performance (i.e., Pass@k). Our preliminary analysis reveals that this limitation stems from a fundamental squeezing effect, whereby probability mass is excessively concentrated on a narrow subset of high-reward trajectories, restricting genuine exploration and constraining attainable performance under RL training. To address this issue, in this work, we propose Steering Probability Squeezing (SPS), a training paradigm that interleaves conventional RL with inverse reinforcement learning (IRL). SPS treats on-policy rollouts as demonstrations and employs IRL to explicitly reshape the induced trajectory distribution, thereby enhancing exploration without introducing external supervision. Experiments on five commonly used reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that SPS can enable better exploration and improve Pass@k. Beyond algorithmic contributions, we provide an analysis of RL learning dynamics and identify an empirical upper bound on Pass@k, shedding light on intrinsic exploration limits in RL-based reasoning models. Our findings suggest that alternating between RL and IRL offers an effective pathway toward extending the exploration capacity of reasoning-oriented large language models.

</details>


### [48] [BIASEDTALES-ML: A Multilingual Dataset for Analyzing Narrative Attribute Distributions in LLM-Generated Stories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17008)
*Yuxuan Ouyang,yingfeng luo,JingBo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BiasedTales-ML多语言儿童故事数据集及分析框架，揭示叙事生成在不同语言（尤其低资源语言）中存在显著差异，挑战英语中心化评估范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI安全与对齐评估主要集中于英语，缺乏对多语言场景下叙事生成对齐行为泛化能力的研究。

Method: 构建包含约35万条八种语言儿童故事的平行语料库BiasedTales-ML；设计全排列提示生成策略；提出生成器-抽取器结构化流程与多维分布分析框架。

Result: 发现跨语言叙事模式存在显著差异，英语观察到的分布规律在其他语言（尤其低资源语言）中不成立；识别出角色、场景与主题等叙事要素在不同语言中的差异化结构模式。

Conclusion: 英语中心化评估不足以刻画多语言社会叙事生成的真实对齐表现，需发展更具语言多样性和文化敏感性的评估方法。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate narrative content, including children's stories, which play an important role in social and cultural learning. Despite growing interest in AI safety and alignment, most existing evaluations focus primarily on English, leaving the cross-lingual generalization of aligned behavior underexplored. In this work, we introduce BiasedTales-ML, a large-scale parallel corpus of approximately 350,000 children's stories generated across eight typologically and culturally diverse languages using a full-permutation prompting design. We propose a structured generator-extractor pipeline and a multi-dimensional distributional analysis framework to examine how narrative attributes vary across languages, models, and social conditions. Our analysis reveals substantial cross-lingual variability in narrative generation patterns, indicating that distributions observed in English do not always exhibit similar characteristics in other languages, particularly in lower-resource settings. At the narrative level, we identify recurring structural patterns involving character roles, settings, and thematic emphasis, which manifest differently across linguistic contexts. These findings highlight the limitations of English-centric evaluation for characterizing socially grounded narrative generation in multilingual settings. We release the dataset, code, and an interactive visualization tool to support future research on multilingual narrative analysis and evaluation.

</details>


### [49] [Improving LLM Code Reasoning via Semantic Equivalence Self-Play with Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17010)
*Antonio Valerio Miceli Barone,Poon Tsz Nok*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自博弈和形式验证的Haskell语义等价性判断框架，通过生成器与评估器的对抗训练，并结合Liquid Haskell证明与执行反例，配合难度感知课程学习，显著提升了下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型在程序语义等价性判断上缺乏可靠推理能力，尤其难以区分真正等价与表面相似的程序；需结合形式化验证提升可信度与泛化性。

Method: 构建生成器-评估器自博弈框架，以Liquid Haskell证明作为等价性监督信号、执行反例作为不等价监督信号，并采用难度感知课程学习策略；构建并发布合成数据集OpInstruct-HSx（约28k条经验证的Haskell程序对）。

Result: 评估器在EquiBench上准确率提升13.3个百分点，在PySecDB上也取得一致提升；消融实验表明等价性证明对推理能力至关重要，而非仅靠不等价样本数量。

Conclusion: 形式化证明是提升模型语义推理能力的关键监督信号；该框架为程序语言理解提供了可验证、可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: We introduce a self-play framework for semantic equivalence in Haskell, utilizing formal verification to guide adversarial training between a generator and an evaluator. The framework leverages Liquid Haskell proofs for validating equivalence and execution-based counterexamples for inequivalence, organized via a difficulty-aware curriculum. To facilitate this, we release \textbf{OpInstruct-HSx}, a synthetic dataset of $\approx$28k validated Haskell programs. Empirical experiments show that our evaluator transfers effectively to downstream tasks, achieving up to 13.3pp accuracy gain on EquiBench and consistent gains on PySecDB. Ablation studies on the SEQ-SINQ regimes indicate that while inequivalence supervision provides data volume, equivalence proofs are uniquely responsible for the model's reasoning capabilities. The entire training pipeline and dataset are publicly released on GitHub and Hugging Face respectively.

</details>


### [50] [Beyond Static Benchmarks: Synthesizing Harmful Content via Persona-based Simulation for Robust Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17020)
*Huije Lee,Jisu Shin,Hoyun Song,Changgeon Ko,Jong C. Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于人格引导的大语言模型（LLM）代理框架，用于合成有害内容，以克服静态基准在可扩展性、多样性及数据污染方面的局限；该框架通过构建二维用户人格（结合人口统计身份、主题兴趣与情境有害策略）模拟多样化、上下文丰富的有害交互，并在有害性、挑战性和多样性三方面验证其有效性，结果表明其生成的场景比现有基准更具检测难度，且语言与主题多样性媲美人工标注数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态有害内容检测基准存在可扩展性差、多样性不足以及可能受大规模预训练语料污染的问题。

Method: 提出基于人格引导的LLM代理框架，构建融合人口统计身份、主题兴趣与情境有害策略的二维用户人格，以合成多样化、上下文 grounded 的有害内容；并从有害性、挑战性、多样性三方面进行人类与LLM联合评估。

Result: 该框架实现了高有害内容生成成功率；合成场景对多种检测系统更具挑战性；语言与主题多样性达到人工标注数据水平。

Conclusion: 所提框架是一种有效支持有害内容检测系统鲁棒性压力测试的新型合成工具。

Abstract: Static benchmarks for harmful content detection face limitations in scalability and diversity, and may also be affected by contamination from web-scale pre-training corpora. To address these issues, we propose a framework for synthesizing harmful content, leveraging persona-guided large language model (LLM) agents. Our approach constructs two-dimensional user personas by integrating demographic identities and topical interests with situational harmful strategies, enabling the simulation of diverse and contextually grounded harmful interactions. We evaluate the framework along three dimensions: harmfulness, challenge level, and diversity. Both human and LLM-based evaluations confirm that our framework achieves a high harmful generation success rate. Experiments across multiple detection systems reveal that our synthetic scenarios are more challenging to detect than those in existing benchmarks. Furthermore, a multi-faceted analysis confirms that our approach achieves linguistic and topical diversity comparable to human-curated datasets, establishing our framework as an effective tool for robust stress-testing of harmful content detection systems.

</details>


### [51] [Beyond Black-Box Labels: Interpretable Criteria for Diagnosing SubjectiveNLP Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17022)
*Nisrine Rair,Alban Goupil,Valeriu Vrabie,Emmanuel Chochoy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在标注前对专家设计的标注规范进行诊断的方法，通过多标注者判断来识别标准不稳定和类别重叠两类问题，并在商业文档中的说服价值提取任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主观NLP数据集通常将标注者判断聚合为单一金标准标签，难以区分标注分歧是源于标准模糊、区分度不足还是合理的多元性。

Method: 提出一种基于模式层面的诊断方法，利用多标注者的标准判断，在承诺金标准前对标注模式进行审计，区分标准不稳定性与类别系统性重叠两种失败模式。

Result: 在说服价值提取任务中发现：分歧并非均匀分布，而是集中在少数标准上；近一半被覆盖的句子激活多个类别；这些信号与领域专家分歧一致。

Conclusion: 该诊断方法可为修订标注指南、调整类别结构或重新思考标注范式提供实证依据。

Abstract: Subjective NLP datasets typically aggregate annotator judgments into a single gold label, making it difficult to diagnose whether disagreement reflects unclear criteria, collapsed distinctions, or legitimate plurality. We propose a \emph{schema-level diagnostic} for auditing expert-designed annotation schemas \emph{prior to} gold-label commitment, using only multi-annotator criterion judgments. The diagnostic separates two failure modes: unstable criteria with hard-to-operationalize boundaries, and systematic overlap that blurs the boundaries between mutually exclusive categories. Applied to persuasive value extraction in commercial documents, we find that disagreement is not diffuse: instability concentrates in a few criteria, while nearly half of covered sentences activate multiple categories. These signals align with where domain experts disagree, yielding an evidence-based audit for tightening guidelines, revising category structure, or reconsidering the annotation paradigm.

</details>


### [52] [Where is the Mind? Persona Vectors and LLM Individuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17031)
*Pierre Beckmann,Patrick Butlin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨大语言模型的个体化问题，即哪些与模型相关的实体可被视为‘心智’，并基于机制可解释性研究，提出并比较了三种主要观点：虚拟实例观、（虚拟）实例-人格观和模型-人格观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型的个体化问题——即确定哪些与模型关联的实体应被识别为‘心智’。

Method: 采用机制可解释性方法，重点分析近期关于人格向量、人格空间和涌现错对齐的经验研究。

Result: 识别出三种最有竞争力的观点：虚拟实例观、（虚拟）实例-人格观和模型-人格观；其中虚拟实例观基于注意力流在token时间上维持类心理联结而获得支持；另两种人格相关观点则由人格文献及其三项内部结构假设支撑。

Conclusion: 虚拟实例观与两种新提出的 persona-based 观点（实例-人格观与模型-人格观）共同构成当前解释LLM心智个体化的最强候选框架，需进一步结合机制证据评估。

Abstract: The individuation problem for large language models asks which entities associated with them, if any, should be identified as minds. We approach this problem through mechanistic interpretability, engaging in particular with recent empirical work on persona vectors, persona space, and emergent misalignment. We argue that three views are the strongest candidates: the virtual instance view and two new views we introduce, the (virtual) instance-persona view and the model-persona view. First, we argue for the virtual instance view on the grounds that attention streams sustain quasi-psychological connections across token-time. Then we present the persona literature, organised around three hypotheses about the internal structure underlying personas in LLMs, and show that the two persona-based views are promising alternatives.

</details>


### [53] [Dynamic Emotion and Personality Profiling for Multimodal Deception Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17037)
*Li Zheng,Yanyi Luo,Hao Fei,Yuzhe Ding,Yujie Huang,Fei Li,Chong Teng,Donghong Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模型多提示标注方案和严格标签质量评估标准，构建了多模态联合检测数据集DDEP，并设计了自适应可靠性加权融合框架Rel-DDEP，显著提升了欺骗、情绪和人格检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏样本级动态情绪和人格标注，限制了欺骗检测的精度与鲁棒性。

Method: 提出多模型多提示标注方案与标签质量评估标准，构建DDEP数据集；设计Rel-DDEP框架，通过高维高斯分布空间建模模态特征不确定性，实现可靠性加权融合，并引入对齐模块与排序约束模块。

Result: 在MDPE和DDEP数据集上，欺骗检测F1提升2.53%，情绪检测提升2.66%，人格检测提升9.30%。

Conclusion: 样本级动态情绪与人格标注具有必要性，可靠性加权融合策略对多任务联合检测有效。

Abstract: Deception detection is of great significance for ensuring information security and conducting public opinion analysis, with personality factors and emotion cues playing a critical role. However, existing methods lack sample-level dynamic annotations for emotions and personality.In this paper, we propose an innovative multi-model multi-prompt annotation scheme and a strict label quality evaluation standard, and establish a multimodal joint detection dataset DDEP for deception, emotion, and personality. Meanwhile, we propose Rel-DDEP, an adaptive reliability-weighted fusion framework. Our framework quantifies uncertainty by mapping modal features to a high-dimensional Gaussian distribution space. It then performs reliability-weighted fusion and incorporates an alignment module and a sorting constraint module to achieve joint detection of deception, emotion, and personality. Experimental results on the MDPE and DDEP datasets show that our Rel-DDEP significantly outperforms the existing state-of-the-art baseline models in three tasks. The F1 score of the deception detection increases by 2.53%, that of the emotion detection increases by 2.66%, and that of the personality detection increases by 9.30%. The experiments fully verify the necessity of annotating dynamic emotion and personality labels for each sample and the effectiveness of reliability-weighted fusion.

</details>


### [54] [Efficient Task Adaptation in Large Language Models via Selective Parameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17051)
*Weijie Wan,Jiangjiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种参数重要性评估方法，将模型参数分为核心与非核心两类，在微调时固定核心参数、仅更新非核心参数，以缓解大语言模型在领域适配中的灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全参数微调易导致预训练获得的通用知识被覆盖或遗忘，限制模型泛化与迁移能力；且未考虑参数异质性——部分参数对通用任务关键，另一些更适于特定任务。

Method: 提出参数元素重要性评估方法，依据参数在通用语言任务与特定领域任务上的贡献差异，划分核心参数（保持冻结）和非核心参数（参与微调）。

Result: 在GPT-J和LLaMA-3上针对科学、医疗、物理等任务的实验表明，该方法可有效缓解灾难性遗忘，并提升模型领域适应能力。

Conclusion: 通过区分并差异化处理参数重要性，可在不损害通用能力的前提下增强领域微调效果，为高效、鲁棒的大模型适配提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated excellent performance in general language understanding, generation and other tasks. However, when fine-tuning for specific domain tasks, the general knowledge accumulated in the pre-training phase is often partially overwritten or forgotten due to parameter updates, which severely limits the generalization ability and transferability of LLMs. Traditional fine-tuning strategies mostly train on the entire parameter space, ignoring the heterogeneity of model parameters, that is, some parameters are extremely important for general tasks, while other parameters are more sensitive to specific tasks. To alleviate the above problems, this paper innovatively proposes a parameter element importance evaluation method, which divides parameters into "core parameters" and "non-core parameters" by distinguishing the importance of parameters for general language ability tasks and specific domain tasks, and fixes the core parameters during fine-tuning, and only fine-tunes the non-core parameters. Extensive experiments on scientific, medical and physical tasks using GPT-J and LLaMA-3 show that our method can mitigate catastrophic forgetting while enhancing the adaptability of the model.

</details>


### [55] [Jailbreaking Large Language Models with Morality Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17053)
*Ying Su,Mingen Zheng,Weili Diao,Haoran Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用越狱提示（jailbreak prompts）来研究大语言模型（LLMs）在多元价值观下的道德判断鲁棒性，构建了包含10.3K样本的道德数据集，并设计四类对抗攻击，揭示了LLMs及护栏模型在价值模糊与冲突场景下的显著脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对AI多元主义对齐的研究多聚焦于提升LLM学习多元价值观的能力，但其在多元价值下生成道德内容的鲁棒性仍缺乏系统探索；受越狱提示强大说服力启发，作者转而用对抗视角检验模型内在价值一致性。

Method: 构建含‘价值模糊’和‘价值冲突’两类的10.3K道德数据集；形式化四种道德感知型对抗攻击；在主流LLMs及护栏模型上进行鲁棒性评估。

Result: 实验表明，当前LLMs及其配套护栏模型在所提道德对抗攻击下表现出严重且细微的脆弱性，难以稳定维持多元价值判断的一致性。

Conclusion: 单纯增强多元价值学习不足以保障对齐鲁棒性；需将对抗测试纳入多元主义对齐框架，尤其关注价值张力场景下的模型可靠性。

Abstract: Pluralism alignment with AI has the sophisticated and necessary goal of creating AI that can coexist with and serve morally multifaceted humanity. Research towards pluralism alignment has many efforts in enhancing the learning of large language models (LLMs) to accomplish pluralism. Although this is essential, the robustness of LLMs to produce moral content over pluralistic values is still under exploration.Inspired by the astonishing persuasion abilities via jailbreak prompts, we propose to leverage jailbreak attacks to study LLMs' internal pluralistic values. In detail, we develop a morality dataset with 10.3K instances in two categories: Value Ambiguity and Value Conflict. We further formalize four adversarial attacks with the constructed dataset, to manipulate LLMs' judgment over the morality questions. We evaluate both the large language models and guardrail models which are typically used in generative systems with flexible user input. Our experiment results show that there is a critical vulnerability of LLMs and guardrail models to these subtle and sophisticated moral-aware attacks.

</details>


### [56] [Stability-Weighted Decoding for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17068)
*Yue Wu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Stability-Weighted Decoding（SWD）的新解码策略，利用token在去噪过程中的时间不稳定性（以连续预测分布间的KL散度衡量）作为其与上下文互信息的下界，从而避免过早解码不稳定token；SWD无需训练、即插即用，显著提升代码生成与数学推理任务的准确性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）的解码策略依赖单步静态置信度，忽略时间演化历史，易导致不稳定token被过早解码，影响生成质量。

Method: 理论证明token的时间不稳定性（连续预测分布KL散度）是其与未掩码上下文互信息的严格下界，并据此提出Stability-Weighted Decoding（SWD），将时间稳定性融入token评分，作为任意基于分数解码策略的通用调制器。

Result: 在代码生成和数学推理基准上，SWD在多种评分指标和选择策略下均稳定提升生成准确率，并在不同加速比下展现出显著优于基线的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 时间不稳定性是判断token是否安全解码的关键信号，SWD作为一种无需训练、通用性强的解码调制方法，有效提升了dLLMs的生成质量与可靠性。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) enable parallel text generation by iteratively denoising a fully masked sequence, unmasking a subset of masked tokens at each step. Existing decoding strategies rely on static confidence metrics computed at a single denoising step, ignoring temporal history and often leading to premature unmasking of unstable tokens. In this work, we theoretically establish that a token's temporal instability, quantified by the KL divergence between consecutive prediction distributions, provides a strict lower bound on its mutual information with the remaining masked context, indicating that temporally unstable tokens are inherently unsafe to unmask. Based on this insight, we propose Stability-Weighted Decoding (SWD), a training-free, plug-and-play strategy that incorporates temporal stability into token scoring and acts as a universal modulator for arbitrary score-based decoding policies. Experiments on code generation and mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that SWD consistently improves generation accuracy across representative scoring metrics and selection policies, and exhibits exceptional robustness, maintaining a significant performance lead over standard baselines across varying acceleration ratios.

</details>


### [57] [Abstain-R1: Calibrated Abstention and Post-Refusal Clarification via Verifiable RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17073)
*Skylar Zhai,Jingcheng Liang,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向澄清的RLVR奖励机制，用于训练大语言模型在面对无法回答的问题时不仅主动拒绝回答，还能准确指出缺失的关键信息；基于该奖励训练出的3B模型Abstain-R1在多项评测中显著优于基线，并媲美更大规模模型（如DeepSeek-R1）的不可答问题处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拒答方法要么生成泛化性拒绝，要么引导澄清但不验证其是否抓住关键缺失信息；而现实中存在语义清晰却因信息不足而无法可靠回答的问题，理想模型应能明确拒答并解释缺什么。

Method: 设计一种澄清感知的RLVR（Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards）奖励函数，在可答问题上奖励正确回答，在不可答问题上联合优化显式拒答与语义对齐的拒答后澄清；用该奖励微调3B模型，得到Abstain-R1。

Result: Abstain-R1在Abstain-Test、Abstain-QA和SelfAware三个基准上显著提升拒答与澄清质量，同时保持可答问题上的强性能；其不可答问题行为媲美DeepSeek-R1等更大模型。

Conclusion: 通过可验证的奖励机制，小规模模型也能习得校准良好的拒答与澄清能力，表明该能力不依赖模型规模，而可通过强化学习有效引导。

Abstract: Reinforcement fine-tuning improves the reasoning ability of large language models, but it can also encourage them to answer unanswerable queries by guessing or hallucinating missing information. Existing abstention methods either train models to produce generic refusals or encourage follow-up clarifications without verifying whether those clarifications identify the key missing information. We study queries that are clear in meaning but cannot be reliably resolved from the given information, and argue that a reliable model should not only abstain, but also explain what is missing. We propose a clarification-aware RLVR reward that, while rewarding correct answers on answerable queries, jointly optimizes explicit abstention and semantically aligned post-refusal clarification on unanswerable queries. Using this reward, we train Abstain-R1, a 3B model that improves abstention and clarification on unanswerable queries while preserving strong performance on answerable ones. Experiments on Abstain-Test, Abstain-QA, and SelfAware show that Abstain-R1 substantially improves over its base model and achieves unanswerable-query behavior competitive with larger systems including DeepSeek-R1, suggesting that calibrated abstention and clarification can be learned through verifiable rewards rather than emerging from scale alone.

</details>


### [58] [Auditing Support Strategies in LLMs through Grounded Multi-Turn Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17079)
*Michelle Star,Andrew Aquilina,Yu-Ru Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多轮模拟框架，用于评估支持性大语言模型在用户逐步披露困境过程中的响应行为，发现模型的支持策略会随其内部估计的用户痛苦程度系统性变化，并强调多轮评估对社会敏感应用的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对支持性大语言模型的评估多基于单轮、完整提示，忽略了真实社交支持中用户渐进式披露情境的特点，导致评估与实际使用脱节。

Method: 构建多轮模拟框架：将Reddit五个社区的支持寻求叙事分解为有序片段，逐轮输入模型；用Social Support Behavior Code（SSBC）对每轮响应进行多标签编码；通过线性探针分析模型隐藏层表征，估计其对用户痛苦程度的内部判断。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B和OLMo-3-7B上验证发现：教学类支持随估计痛苦上升而系统减少（跨架构可复现），情感与自尊导向策略（如共情验证）呈模型特异性增长；社区语境独立影响响应，反映话题与话语规范而非人口统计特征。

Conclusion: 单轮评估无法捕捉支持行为的动态轨迹，多轮仿真与内部状态探测是审计社会敏感AI系统的关键新范式。

Abstract: When users seek social support from chatbots, they disclose their situation gradually, yet most evaluations of supportive LLMs rely on single-turn, fully specified prompts. We introduce a multi-turn simulation framework that closes this gap. Support-seeking narratives from five Reddit communities are decomposed into ordered fragments and revealed turn by turn to a language model. Each response is coded with the Social Support Behavior Code (SSBC), an established multi-label taxonomy that captures the composition of support, rather than a single quality score. To ask whether support choices track the model's own construal of user distress, we use linear probes on hidden representations to estimate this internal signal without altering the generation context. Across two mid-scale models (Llama-3.1-8B, OLMo-3-7B) and more than 6,200 turns, support composition shifts systematically with estimated distress: teaching declines as estimated distress rises, a finding that replicates across architectures, while increases in affective and esteem-oriented strategies (such as validation) are suggestive but model-specific and rest on noisier annotations. Community context independently shapes behavior, tracking topic and discourse norms rather than demographic categories. These trajectory-level dynamics, invisible to single-turn evaluation, motivate multi-turn auditing frameworks for socially sensitive applications.

</details>


### [59] [Comparing Human and Large Language Model Interpretation of Implicit Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17085)
*Antonio De Santis,Tommaso Bonetti,Andrea Tocchetti,Marco Brambilla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了隐含信息抽取（IIE）任务，构建了一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的IIE流程，用于从句子中提取关系三元组、验证隐含推理并分析时序关系；实验发现当前LLM在覆盖度上仍有限，且在社会性语境中比人类更保守，在事实性短文本中人类反而更保守。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类能自然理解隐含意义，但大语言模型（LLM）是否具备类似能力尚不明确，需系统研究其隐含信息理解与抽取能力。

Method: 提出隐含信息抽取（IIE）任务，设计基于LLM的IIE流水线：包括结构化知识图谱构建、关系三元组抽取、隐含推理验证与时序关系分析，并在两个数据集上对比两个LLM与众包人工判断结果。

Result: 人类对模型抽取的多数三元组表示认同，但也频繁补充新信息，表明当前LLM的IIE覆盖度不足；模型在社会性丰富语境中比人类更保守，而人类在简短、事实导向语境中更保守。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在隐含信息抽取方面存在覆盖局限性和语境依赖的推理偏差，需进一步提升其对隐含语义的全面建模与适应性推理能力。

Abstract: The interpretation of implicit meanings is an integral aspect of human communication. However, this framework may not transfer to interactions with Large Language Models (LLMs). To investigate this, we introduce the task of Implicit Information Extraction (IIE) and propose an LLM-based IIE pipeline that builds a structured knowledge graph from a context sentence by extracting relational triplets, validating implicit inferences, and analyzing temporal relations. We evaluate two LLMs against crowdsourced human judgments on two datasets. We find that humans agree with most model triplets yet consistently propose many additions, indicating limited coverage in current LLM-based IIE. Moreover, in our experiments, models appear to be more conservative about implicit inferences than humans in socially rich contexts, whereas humans become more conservative in shorter, fact-oriented contexts. Our code is available at https://github.com/Antonio-Dee/IIE_from_LLM.

</details>


### [60] [GenericAgent: A Token-Efficient Self-Evolving LLM Agent via Contextual Information Density Maximization (V1.0)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17091)
*Jiaqing Liang,Jinyi Han,Weijia Li,Xinyi Wang,Zhoujia Zhang,Zishang Jiang,Ying Liao,Tingyun Li,Ying Huang,Hao Shen,Hanyu Wu,Fang Guo,Keyi Wang,Zhonghua Hong,Zhiyu Lu,Lipeng Ma,Sihang Jiang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GenericAgent（GA），一种以最大化上下文信息密度为核心原则的通用自演化大语言模型智能体系统，通过精简工具集、分层按需记忆、自我演化机制和上下文压缩截断四部分，在长周期任务中显著提升性能并降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长周期LLM智能体受限于上下文长度，随着交互增长，冗余信息挤占关键决策信息；同时跨任务经验难以复用，导致性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出GenericAgent（GA）系统，包含：1）最小原子化工具集；2）分层按需记忆机制；3）将验证过的成功轨迹自动转化为标准操作流程（SOP）和可执行代码的自我演化机制；4）动态上下文截断与压缩层。

Result: GA在任务完成率、工具使用效率、记忆有效性、自我演化能力及网页浏览等多维度上持续超越主流智能体系统，同时显著减少token用量和交互次数，并具备随时间持续演化的特性。

Conclusion: 长周期智能体性能的关键不在于上下文长度上限，而在于有限上下文内决策相关信息的密度；GA通过信息密度最大化设计实现了更高效、可持续的智能体行为。

Abstract: Long-horizon large language model (LLM) agents are fundamentally limited by context. As interactions become longer, tool descriptions, retrieved memories, and raw environmental feedback accumulate and push out the information needed for decision-making. At the same time, useful experience gained from tasks is often lost across episodes. We argue that long-horizon performance is determined not by context length, but by how much decision-relevant information is maintained within a finite context budget. We present GenericAgent (GA), a general-purpose, self-evolving LLM agent system built around a single principle: context information density maximization. GA implements this through four closely connected components: a minimal atomic tool set that keeps the interface simple, a hierarchical on-demand memory that only shows a small high-level view by default, a self-evolution mechanism that turns verified past trajectories into reusable SOPs and executable code, and a context truncation and compression layer that maintains information density during long executions. Across task completion, tool use efficiency, memory effectiveness, self-evolution, and web browsing, GA consistently outperforms leading agent systems while using significantly fewer tokens and interactions, and it continues to evolve over time. Project: https://github.com/lsdefine/GenericAgent

</details>


### [61] [How Tokenization Limits Phonological Knowledge Representation in Language Models and How to Improve Them](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17105)
*Disen Liao,Freda Shi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了分词对语言模型语音知识表征能力的影响，发现基于子词的分词方式会系统性削弱局部（如押韵）和全局（如音节划分）语音特征的编码；为此提出了音节划分-分词对齐距离（STAD）指标来量化这种影响，并设计了一种轻量级IPA微调方法以增强模型的语音感知能力，在多项语音相关任务中取得一致提升，同时基本保持数学与通用推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分词是语言模型的第一步，但现有方法从未考虑单词的发音；本文旨在探究分词方式如何影响纯文本语言模型对语音学知识的表征能力。

Method: 通过一系列探针实验分析分词对语音特征编码的影响；提出音节划分-分词对齐距离（STAD）作为评估指标；并设计基于国际音标（IPA）的轻量级微调方法注入语音感知能力。

Result: 发现子词分词系统性削弱局部与全局语音特征编码；STAD越高，语音表征越差；IPA微调在三个语音相关任务中均带来一致提升，且仅使GSM8K和MMLU分别下降1.1%和0.9%。

Conclusion: 分词方式显著影响语言模型的语音表征能力；STAD可作为语音感知型分词的简易诊断工具；IPA微调是一种有效、低损地增强语音能力的方法。

Abstract: Tokenization is the first step in every language model (LM), yet it never takes the sounds of words into account. We investigate how tokenization influences text-only LMs' ability to represent phonological knowledge. Through a series of probing experiments, we show that subword-based tokenization systematically weakens the encoding of both local (e.g., rhyme) and global (e.g., syllabification) phonological features. To quantify this effect, we introduce the syllabification-tokenization alignment distance (STAD), a metric that measures the misalignment between a model's tokenization and the natural syllable boundaries of words, and find that higher misalignment correlates with poorer phonological representations, providing a simple diagnostic for phonology-aware tokenization. To address these limitations, we propose a lightweight IPA-based fine-tuning method that infuses phonological awareness into LMs, leading to consistent improvements across three phonology-related tasks while largely preserving math and general reasoning ability, with 1.1\% and 0.9\% drops on GSM8K and MMLU, respectively.

</details>


### [62] [Beyond Word Boundaries: A Hebrew Coreference Benchmark and an Evaluation Protocol for Morphologically Complex Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17108)
*Refael Shaked Greenfeld,Reut Tsarfaty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个现代希伯来语共指消解数据集KibutzR，针对形态丰富语言（MRLs）中词与指代边界不一致的问题，设计了细粒度（词级、子词级、多词级）标注和边界感知评估协议；实验表明当前大语言模型在希伯来语上性能显著下降，且小编码器反而优于大解码器，揭示了MRLs上CR建模的新挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有共指消解（CR）方法和评估协议基于英语中‘词=指代单位’的假设，但在形态丰富语言（MRLs）如现代希伯来语中，一个词可能含多个附着代词或跨词指代，该假设失效，尤其影响LLM对原始文本的端到端处理。

Method: 构建首个现代希伯来语CR数据集KibutzR，支持词级、子词级和多词级指代表达标注；提出适配MRLs的分割感知（segmentation-aware）评估协议，显式处理词/语素边界错位问题；系统评测主流LLM在分词与未分词文本上的CR性能。

Result: 当代LLM在希伯来语CR任务上显著低于英语水平；未分词原始文本进一步导致性能下降；出乎意料地发现小型编码器模型表现远超大型解码器模型，呈现与英语相反的性能趋势。

Conclusion: 形态丰富语言的共指消解需突破‘词即指代单位’的隐含假设，KibutzR数据集与新评估协议为希伯来语提供了可靠基准，并可推广至其他MRLs，推动面向真实文本结构的CR建模研究。

Abstract: Coreference Resolution (CR) is a fundamental NLP task critical for long-form tasks as information extraction, summarization, and many business applications. However, CR methods originally designed for English struggle with Morphologically Rich Languages (MRLs), where mention boundaries do not necessarily align with word boundaries, and a single token may consist of multiple anaphors. CR modeling and evaluation protocols standardly assume that, as in English, words and mentions mostly align. However, this assumption breaks down in MRLs, particularly in the context of LLMs' raw-text processing and end-to-end tasks. To assess and address this challenge, we introduce {\em KibutzR}, the first comprehensive CR dataset for Modern Hebrew, an MRL rich with complex words and pronominal clitics. We deliver an annotated dataset that identifies mentions at word, sub-word and multi-word levels, and propose an evaluation protocol that directly addresses word/morpheme boundary discrepancies. Our experiments show that contemporary LLMs perform significantly worse on Hebrew than on English, and that performance degrades on raw unsegmented text. Crucially, we show an inverse performance-trend in Hebrew relative to English, where smaller encoders perform far better than contemporary decoder models, leaving ample space for investigation and improvement. We deliver a new benchmark for Hebrew coreference resolution and a segmentation-aware evaluation protocol to inform future work on other MRLs.

</details>


### [63] [The Provenance Gap in Clinical AI: Evidence-Traceable Temporal Knowledge Graphs for Rare Disease Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17114)
*Md Shamim Ahmed,Maja Dusanic,Moritz Nikolai Kirschner,Elisabeth Nyoungui,Jana Zschüntzsch,Lukas Galke Poech,Richard Röttger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HEG-TKG系统，通过构建分层证据驱动的时间知识图谱，显著提升大型语言模型在罕见神经肌肉疾病诊断中的引用可验证性，实现100%证据可验证并保持临床特征覆盖率，同时保障患者数据隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大语言模型虽能生成临床准确的输出，但其引用常为虚构（即‘溯源鸿沟’），亟需提升临床建议的可验证性与可信度。

Method: 构建HEG-TKG系统：基于4512篇PubMed文献与人工整理资料，构建含质量分级和1280个疾病轨迹里程碑的分层时间知识图谱；结合RAG与结构化推理，在相同合成模型下进行三组对照实验。

Result: HEG-TKG实现100%证据可验证性（203条内联引用），临床特征覆盖与基线持平；指南式RAG产生零可验证引用；临床医生评估证实显著优势（Cohen's d = 1.81, p < 0.001）；对注入错误达80%抗性且100%可追溯检测。

Conclusion: HEG-TKG有效弥合临床LLM的溯源鸿沟，兼顾准确性、可验证性与数据隐私，为高风险医疗AI部署提供可行路径。

Abstract: Frontier large language models generate clinically accurate outputs, but their citations are often fabricated. We term this the Provenance Gap. We tested five frontier LLMs across 36 clinician-validated scenarios for three rare neuromuscular disease pairs. No model produced a clinically relevant PubMed identifier without prompting. When explicitly asked to cite, the best model achieved 15.3% relevant PMIDs; the majority resolved to real publications in unrelated fields. We present HEG-TKG (Hierarchical Evidence-Grounded Temporal Knowledge Graphs), a system that grounds clinical claims in temporal knowledge graphs built from 4,512 PubMed records and curated sources with quality-tier stratification and 1,280 disease-trajectory milestones. In a controlled three-arm comparison using the same synthesis model, HEG-TKG matches baseline clinical feature coverage while achieving 100% evidence verifiability with 203 inline citations. Guideline-RAG, given overlapping source documents as raw text, produces zero verifiable citations. LLM judges cannot distinguish fabricated from verified citations without PubMed audit data. Independent clinician evaluation confirms the verifiability advantage (Cohen's d = 1.81, p < 0.001) with no degradation on safety or completeness. A counterfactual experiment shows 80% resistance to injected clinical errors with 100% detectability via citation trace. The system deploys on-premise via open-source models so patient data never leaves institutional infrastructure.

</details>


### [64] [Please refuse to answer me! Mitigating Over-Refusal in Large Language Models via Adaptive Contrastive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17132)
*Yupeng Qi,Ziyu Lyu,Lixin Cui,Lu Bai,Feng Xia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练、模型无关的自适应对比解码（AdaCD）方法，通过分析不同安全级别系统提示对大语言模型拒绝行为的影响，缓解过度拒绝问题，同时保持对恶意查询的高拒绝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在降低对无害查询的拒绝率的同时，维持对恶意查询的高拒绝率，即无法兼顾低误拒与高真拒。

Method: AdaCD方法包含两步：1）对比使用/不使用极端安全系统提示时的输出分布，以精炼拒绝token分布；2）引入自适应对比解码策略，动态调节拒绝/非拒绝token的概率，从而提升非拒绝token被选中的可能性。

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的实验表明，AdaCD平均将过度拒绝查询的拒绝率降低10.35%，同时使恶意查询的拒绝率微增0.13%。

Conclusion: AdaCD是一种高效、通用且无需训练的过拒绝缓解方案，在保障安全性的同时显著改善模型对无害查询的响应质量。

Abstract: Safety-aligned large language models (LLMs) often generate refusal responses to harmless queries due to the over-refusal problem. However, existing methods for mitigating over-refusal cannot maintain a low refusal ratio for harmless queries while keeping a high refusal ratio for malicious ones. In this paper, we analyze how system prompts with varying safety levels affect LLM refusal behaviors when facing over-refusal queries. A key observation is that, when LLMs suffer from the over-refusal issue, non-refusal tokens remain present in the next-token candidate list, but the model systematically fails to select them, despite the generation of refusal tokens. Based on this observation, we propose a training-free and model-agnostic approach, Adaptive Contrastive Decoding (AdaCD), to mitigate over-refusal while maintaining LLM safety. First, AdaCD compares the output distributions of the LLM with or without an extreme safety system prompt to refine the refusal token distribution. Second, we introduce an adaptive contrastive decoding strategy that dynamically incorporates or removes the refusal token distribution, adaptively boosting the probability of selecting refusal or non-refusal tokens. Experimental results on five benchmark datasets show that, on average, AdaCD reduces the refusal ratio for over-refusal queries by 10.35%, yet still increases the refusal ratio for malicious queries by 0.13%. Code is available at https://github.com/OutdoorManofML/AdaCD.

</details>


### [65] [RoIt-XMASA: Multi-Domain Multilingual Sentiment Analysis Dataset for Romanian and Italian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17134)
*Andrei-Marius Avram,Aureliu Valentin Antonie,Cosmin-Mircea Croitoru,Vlad Andrei Muntean,Dumitru-Clementin Cercel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了RoIt-XMASA多语言数据集，并设计了一种多目标对抗训练框架，以解决跨语言和跨领域情感分析问题，在XLM-R和Llama-3.1-8B上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决跨语言和跨领域情感分析中的挑战，扩展现有数据集并提升模型泛化能力。

Method: 构建RoIt-XMASA多语言数据集（含意大利语和罗马尼亚语），提出基于损失反转与元学习系数的多目标对抗训练框架。

Result: XLM-R在该方法下F1达66.23%，较基线提升4.64%；Llama-3.1-8B少样本F1为58.43%，揭示提示工程与微调间的性能-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效提升跨语言跨领域情感分析性能，且多语言数据集为后续研究提供了新资源。

Abstract: We present RoIt-XMASA, a multilingual dataset that extends the Cross-lingual Multi-domain Amazon Sentiment Analysis to Italian and Romanian, comprising 36,000 labeled reviews across three domains (books, movies, and music) and 202,141 unlabeled samples. To address cross-lingual and cross-domain challenges, we propose a multi-target adversarial training framework that employs loss reversal with meta-learned coefficients to dynamically balance sentiment discrimination with domain and language invariance. XLM-R achieves an F1-score of 66.23% with our approach, outperforming the baseline by 4.64%. Few-shot evaluation shows that Llama-3.1-8B achieves 58.43% F1-score, revealing a meaningful trade-off between the efficiency of prompting-based approaches and the higher performance of task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [66] [The Consensus Trap: Rescuing Multi-Agent LLMs from Adversarial Majorities via Token-Level Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17139)
*Jiayuan Liu,Shiyi Du,Weihua Du,Mingyu Guo,Vincent Conitzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的多智能体协作机制——Token-Level Round-Robin（RR），通过在共享自回归上下文中逐token交替生成，替代传统的响应级多数投票（MAJ），从而在存在恶意提示注入攻击时仍保持鲁棒推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于响应级聚合（如多数投票）的多智能体系统在开放环境中易受隐蔽上下文污染（如定向提示注入）影响，尤其当被污染智能体形成局部多数时，系统会因忽略中间推理逻辑而崩溃。

Method: 提出Token-Level Round-Robin协作机制：多个LLM智能体在统一上下文中按轮次逐token生成，形式化为离散时间动力系统；理论证明该机制将聚合从线性计数转变为非线性算子乘积，使诚实模型能通过逻辑交织实现对恶意污染的恢复性压制。

Result: 实验证明，在多种推理基准上，当被污染智能体数量超过半数时，多数投票（MAJ）性能急剧下降，而RR机制仍维持高准确率，显著超越临界阈值。

Conclusion: 响应级聚合存在结构性缺陷，而token级轮转协作通过动态、非线性的逻辑交织，从根本上提升了多智能体系统的鲁棒性与抗攻击能力。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) architectures increasingly rely on response-level aggregation, such as Majority Voting (MAJ), to raise reasoning ceilings. However, in open environments, agents are highly susceptible to stealthy contextual corruption, such as targeted prompt injections. We reveal a critical structural vulnerability in current multi-agent systems: response-level aggregation collapses when corrupted agents form a local majority. Because voting aggregates fully-formed conclusions, it is blind to flawed intermediate logic. To overcome this systematic limitation, we propose the Token-Level Round-Robin (RR) Collaboration, where agents sequentially interleave generation within a shared auto-regressive context. We formalize this process as a discrete-time dynamical system, proving that token-level interleaving transitions aggregation from a brittle counting of final votes (a linear sum) to a dynamic, interwoven chain of logic (a non-linear operator product). Through this theoretical lens, we prove that the honest model's restorative pull can overpower adversarial corruptions, even when corrupted agents form a majority. We conduct an exhaustive empirical evaluation across diverse reasoning benchmarks and demonstrate that while MAJ collapses when corrupted agents reach a majority, RR maintains robust accuracy well beyond this critical threshold.

</details>


### [67] [SciImpact: A Multi-Dimensional, Multi-Field Benchmark for Scientific Impact Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17141)
*Hangxiao Zhu,Yuyu Zhang,Ping Nie,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SciImpact，一个大规模、多维度的科学影响力预测基准，涵盖19个学科领域，整合了引用、奖项、媒体关注、专利引用和工具采用等多种影响力指标；通过评估11种大语言模型，发现多任务监督微调可显著提升小模型性能，甚至超越更大参数量的闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要依赖基于引用的指标评估科研影响力，缺乏对其他多维影响力（如奖项、媒体关注、专利引用等）的系统建模与评估，亟需构建更全面的基准以推动多维度科学影响力预测研究。

Method: 构建了名为SciImpact的大规模多维科学影响力预测基准，覆盖19个学科，整合异构数据源与定向网络爬虫，形成215,928组对比论文对；在该基准上系统评估11种主流大语言模型，并探索多任务监督微调策略对模型性能的影响。

Result: 实验表明：开箱即用的大模型在不同影响力维度和学科间表现差异显著；经多任务监督微调后，4B参数的小模型性能显著优于30B参数的大模型，甚至超越o4-mini等强闭源模型。

Conclusion: SciImpact是一个具有挑战性且实用的多维、多领域科学影响力预测基准，验证了微调策略对提升小模型跨维度影响力推理能力的有效性，为未来科研影响力建模提供了新范式与评测标准。

Abstract: The rapid growth of scientific literature calls for automated methods to assess and predict research impact. Prior work has largely focused on citation-based metrics, leaving limited evaluation of models' capability to reason about other impact dimensions. To this end, we introduce SciImpact, a large-scale, multi-dimensional benchmark for scientific impact prediction spanning 19 fields. SciImpact captures various forms of scientific influence, ranging from citation counts to award recognition, media attention, patent reference, and artifact adoption, by integrating heterogeneous data sources and targeted web crawling. It comprises 215,928 contrastive paper pairs reflecting meaningful impact differences in both short-term (e.g., Best Paper Award) and long-term settings (e.g., Nobel Prize). We evaluate 11 widely used large language models (LLMs) on SciImpact. Results show that off-the-shelf models exhibit substantial variability across dimensions and fields, while multi-task supervised fine-tuning consistently enables smaller LLMs (e.g., 4B) to markedly outperform much larger models (e.g., 30B) and surpass powerful closed-source LLMs (e.g., o4-mini). These results establish SciImpact as a challenging benchmark and demonstrate its value for multi-dimensional, multi-field scientific impact prediction. Our project homepage is https://flypig23.github.io/sciimpact-homepage/

</details>


### [68] [From Legal Text to Executable Decision Models: Evaluating Structured Representations for Legal Decision Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17153)
*David Graus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用大语言模型（LLM）将荷兰环境与规划法文本自动转换为可执行决策模型的问题，发现引入输入/输出约束能显著提升生成模型的结构相似性与功能正确性，同时释放出LLM自动简化冗余逻辑的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将法律文本转化为可执行决策逻辑长期存在，而现有LLM方法仍依赖大量人工编码与评估，亟需更鲁棒、可解释的自动化路径。

Method: 基于真实世界数据集（95个生产级决策模型+对应荷兰法律条文），对比四种输入形式（原始文本、语义角色标注增强、I/O约束增强、二者联合增强），采用图核与图统计进行结构评估，并通过预设测试场景执行验证功能等价性。

Result: I/O约束带来最显著提升（结构相似性+37–54%）；生成模型在51–53%测试场景中实现功能等价；LLM自动消除了45–55%的冗余pass-through节点；结构相似性与功能等价性不完全相关。

Conclusion: 中间结构化表示（尤其是I/O约束）是提升LLM法律逻辑生成质量的关键，结构与功能评估应并重；所发布数据集与代码支持后续可复现研究。

Abstract: Transforming legal text into executable decision logic is a longstanding challenge in legal informatics. With the rise of LLMs, this task has gained renewed interest, but remains challenging due to requiring extensive manual coding and evaluation. We use a unique real-world dataset that pairs production-grade decision models with legal text from the Dutch Environment and Planning Act. These models power the Omgevingsloket government platform, where citizens check permit requirements for environmental activities. We study whether intermediate structured representations can improve LLM-based generation of executable decision models from legal text. We compare four input conditions: raw legal text, text enriched with semantic role labels, text enriched with input and output constraints, and text enriched with both. We evaluate along two dimensions: structural evaluation, through similarity to gold decision models with graph kernels and graphs' descriptive statistics, and outcome evaluation, through functional equivalence by executing models on pre-configured test scenarios. Our findings show that I/O constraints provide the dominant improvement (+37-54% similarity over baseline), while semantic role labels show modest improvements. Outcome evaluation shows that generated models match the gold standard on 51-53% of test scenarios, even though generated models are typically smaller and simpler. We find LLMs eliminate redundant pass-through logic that comprises up to 45-55% of nodes. Importantly, structural similarity and outcome equivalence are complementary: structural similarity does not guarantee outcome equivalence, and vice versa. To facilitate reproducibility, we publicly release our dataset of 95 production decision models with associated legal text and all experimental code.

</details>


### [69] [Modeling Multi-Dimensional Cognitive States in Large Language Models under Cognitive Crowding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17174)
*Lin Zhong,Siyu Zhu,Zizhen Yuan,Jinhao Cui,Xinyang Zhao,Lingzhi Wang,Hao Chen,Qing Liao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CognitiveBench基准和HyCoLLM模型，解决大语言模型在多维认知状态建模中的性能瓶颈，通过超曲几何建模与对齐优化显著提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型仅能处理单一认知维度（如情绪或立场），难以建模心理学定义的多个交互认知维度（情绪、思维风格、立场、意图）间的复杂关系。

Method: 构建首个涵盖四个认知维度统一标注的基准CognitiveBench；利用Gromov δ-超双曲性分析揭示其强层次结构；提出HyCoLLM模型，在双曲空间建模认知状态，并通过双曲引导对齐微调（Hyperbolic Guided Alignment Tuning）对齐LLM表征。

Result: 实验表明，LLMs在多维联合建模上性能骤降；HyCoLLM显著提升多维认知理解能力，8B参数模型超越GPT-4o等强基线。

Conclusion: 认知状态具有本质层次性，其指数级表征需求与LLM的欧氏多项式表征能力不匹配，导致‘认知拥挤’；采用双曲空间建模是突破该瓶颈的有效路径。

Abstract: Modeling human cognitive states is essential for advanced artificial intelligence. Existing Large Language Models (LLMs) mainly address isolated tasks such as emotion analysis or stance detection, and fail to capture interactions among cognitive dimensions defined in psychology, including emotion, thinking style, stance, and intention. To bridge this gap, we construct CognitiveBench, the first benchmark with unified annotations across the above four dimensions. Experiments on CognitiveBench show that although LLMs perform well on single dimension tasks, their performance drops sharply in joint multi-dimensional modeling. Using Gromov $δ$-hyperbolicity analysis, we find that CognitiveBench exhibits a strong hierarchical structure. We attribute the performance bottleneck to ``Cognitive Crowding'', where hierarchical cognitive states require exponential representational space, while the Euclidean space of LLMs grows only polynomially, causing representation overlap and degraded performance. To address this mismatch, we propose HyCoLLM, which models cognitive states in hyperbolic space and aligns LLM representations via Hyperbolic Guided Alignment Tuning. Results show that HyCoLLM substantially improves multi-dimensional cognitive understanding, allowing 8B parameter model to outperform strong baselines, including GPT-4o.

</details>


### [70] [Cognitive Policy-Driven LLM for Diagnosis and Intervention of Cognitive Distortions in Emotional Support Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17178)
*Lin Zhong,Renjin Zhu,Shujuan Ma,Jinhao Cui,Lingzhi Wang,Hao Chen,Qing Liao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CogBiasESC数据集和CoPoLLM框架，首次在情感支持对话中引入认知扭曲建模与干预，显著提升诊断准确性、干预效果与安全控制能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感支持对话模型忽视求助者表达中的认知扭曲问题，仅提供基础情绪安慰，无法从深层认知层面缓解心理困扰。

Method: 构建首个标注认知扭曲类型、强度与安全风险等级的CogBiasESC数据集；提出认知策略驱动的大语言模型框架CoPoLLM，用于认知扭曲的诊断与干预，并从理论上分析其安全性优势。

Result: CoPoLLM在认知扭曲诊断准确率、干预策略有效性及安全风险控制三方面均显著优于15个SOTA基线模型。

Conclusion: 将认知行为疗法原理融入大语言模型的情感支持对话中，可有效提升模型对心理问题的深层理解与安全干预能力，为心理健康AI应用提供新范式。

Abstract: Emotional Support Conversation (ESC) plays a critical role in mental health assistance by providing accessible psychological support in real-world applications. Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong empathetic abilities in ESC tasks. Yet, existing methods overlook the issue of cognitive distortions in help-seekers' expressions. As a result, current models can only provide basic emotional comfort, rather than helping help-seekers address their psychological distress at a deeper cognitive level. To address this challenge, we construct the CogBiasESC dataset, the first dataset that expands existing ESC datasets by adding labels for cognitive distortions, includes their type, intensity, and safe risk level. Furthermore, we propose the Cognitive Policy-driven Large Language Model framework (CoPoLLM) to enhance LLMs' ability to diagnose and intervene cognitive distortions in help-seekers. We also analyze the safety advantages of CoPoLLM from a theoretical perspective. Experimental results show that CoPoLLM significantly outperforms 15 state-of-the-art baselines in terms of distortion diagnosis accuracy, intervention strategy effectiveness, and safety risk control.

</details>


### [71] [Beyond Overlap Metrics: Rewarding Reasoning and Preferences for Faithful Multi-Role Dialogue Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17188)
*Xiaoyong Mei,Tingting Zuo,Da Chen,Guangyu Hu,Xiangyu Wen,Chao Duan,Mingyan Zhang,Fudan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合认知式推理与奖励优化的多角色对话摘要新框架，通过结构化推理蒸馏和双原则奖励（兼顾指标与人类偏好）提升事实一致性与人类对齐性，在多个基准上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法过度依赖ROUGE等自动指标，易导致表面模仿而非真正提升事实忠实度和人类偏好对齐。

Method: 首先从大教师模型中蒸馏结构化推理轨迹（如逐步推理、中间反思），作为辅助监督进行分阶段有监督微调；然后采用GRPO算法，结合基于指标和人类对齐标准（关键信息覆盖、隐含推理、事实忠实性、简洁性）的双原则奖励进行优化。

Result: 在多语言多角色对话基准（如CSDS、SAMSum）上，ROUGE和BERTScore与强基线持平，但在语义一致性、事实忠实性和模型偏好对齐方面显著提升。

Conclusion: 推理感知与偏好感知的训练范式对构建可靠对话摘要系统具有重要价值。

Abstract: Multi-role dialogue summarization requires modeling complex interactions among multiple speakers while preserving role-specific information and factual consistency. However, most existing methods optimize for automatic metrics such as ROUGE and BERTScore, which favor surface-level imitation of references rather than genuine gains in faithfulness or alignment with human preferences. We propose a novel framework that couples explicit cognitive-style reasoning with reward-based optimization for multi-role dialogue summarization. Our method first distills structured reasoning traces (e.g., step-by-step inferences and intermediate reflections) from a large teacher model and uses them as auxiliary supervision to initialize a reasoning-aware summarizer via staged supervised fine-tuning. It then applies GRPO with a dual-principle reward that blends metric-based signals with human-aligned criteria targeting key information coverage, implicit inference, factual faithfulness, and conciseness. Experiments on multilingual multi-role dialogue benchmarks show that our method matches strong baselines on ROUGE and BERTScore. Specifically, results on CSDS confirm the framework's stability in semantic consistency, while in-depth analysis on SAMSum demonstrates clear gains in factual faithfulness and model-based preference alignment. These findings underscore the value of reasoning-aware and preference-aware training for reliable dialogue summarization. Checkpoints and datasets are available at https://huggingface.co/collections/NebulaPixel/summorchestra-multirole-summary.

</details>


### [72] [Learning to Control Summaries with Score Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17197)
*Hongye Liu,Liang Ding,Ricardo Henao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的损失函数，利用细粒度的模型评估分数（如FineSurE）来对摘要生成进行多维度质量控制，在提升整体摘要质量的同时，支持按需优先优化特定质量维度（如简洁性或完整性）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有摘要研究虽关注多维度质量联合优化，但缺乏对单个质量维度（如简洁性、完整性、忠实性）的可控生成能力，尤其难以处理各维度间的固有冲突（如简洁性与完整性的权衡）。

Method: 设计一种新型损失函数，将模型输出与基于FineSurE等模型的细粒度评估得分对齐，从而实现质量提升与维度级可控性兼顾。

Result: 在LLaMA、Qwen和Mistral三个预训练模型上验证有效，摘要整体质量达SOTA水平，并显著提升了对各质量维度（如简洁性、完整性）的独立调控能力。

Conclusion: 该方法成功弥合了摘要质量提升与细粒度可控性之间的鸿沟，为可控文本生成提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in summarization research focus on improving summary quality across multiple criteria, such as completeness, conciseness, and faithfulness, by jointly optimizing these dimensions. However, these efforts largely overlook the challenge of controlling summary generation with respect to individual criteria, especially in the presence of their inherent trade-offs. For example, enhancing conciseness can compromise completeness, and vice versa. In this work, we address this gap by proposing a loss function that aligns model outputs with fine-grained, model-based evaluation scores (e.g., from FineSurE), enabling both improvement in summary quality and dimension-specific control. Our approach improves the overall quality of summaries while maintaining the ability to selectively prioritize one criterion over others. Experiments on three pretrained models (LLaMA, Qwen, and Mistral) demonstrate that our method achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art summarizers, while uniquely offering strong controllability over individual quality dimensions.

</details>


### [73] [Calibrating Model-Based Evaluation Metrics for Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17200)
*Hongye Liu,Dhanajit Brahma,Ricardo Henao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需参考摘要、人工标注或昂贵模型指标的通用摘要评估框架，并引入组等渗回归分箱（GIRB）校准方法，显著提升评估分数的可靠性与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的摘要评估方法存在计算成本高、评分校准差、依赖多参考摘要等问题，限制了其可靠性和实用性。

Method: 提出一种无需参考摘要和人工标注的通用评估框架，并设计组等渗回归分箱（GIRB）方法对原始预测分数进行校准，适用于连续值（如摘要）和离散值（如问答）任务。

Result: 在七个数据集上的实验表明，该方法持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提框架与GIRB校准方法能有效提升摘要评估的可靠性与泛化能力，降低对资源密集型组件的依赖。

Abstract: Recent advances in summary evaluation are based on model-based metrics to assess quality dimensions, such as completeness, conciseness, and faithfulness. However, these methods often require large language models, and predicted scores are frequently miscalibrated, limiting their reliability. Moreover, evaluating the average quality across different summaries for a single document typically requires access to multiple reference summaries. Here, we propose a general framework that generates individual and average proxy scores without relying on reference summaries, human annotations, or expensive model-based metrics. We also propose group isotonic regression binning (GIRB), a calibration method that adjusts the raw predictions to better align with ground-truth evaluation metrics. While we focus on continuous-value scenarios, such as summarization, the method is applicable to discrete-value tasks, such as question answering. Experiments on seven datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms existing baselines.

</details>


### [74] [A Multi-Agent Approach for Claim Verification from Tabular Data Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17225)
*Rudra Ranajee Saha,Laks V. S. Lakshmanan,Raymond T. Ng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MACE的多智能体框架，用于表格数据中的声明验证，通过零样本思维链实现规划、执行与验证三阶段任务，无需复杂微调，在多个数据集上达到SOTA或接近最优性能，同时显著降低模型参数量和提升可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的声明验证方法存在依赖复杂预训练/微调、子任务分解导致解释性差、泛化能力不足等问题。

Method: 提出多智能体框架MACE，包含 Planner（生成推理策略）、Executor（执行具体计算）和 Verifier（逻辑验证）三个零样本Chain-of-Thought智能体，协同完成端到端验证并输出可解释的验证轨迹。

Result: 在两个数据集上达到SOTA，在另外两个上与最优模型性能相当；仅用27–92B参数模型即实现80–100%的235B参数模型性能。

Conclusion: MACE在性能、内存效率和推理透明性之间实现了良好平衡，为表格数据声明验证提供了高效、轻量且可解释的新范式。

Abstract: We present a novel approach for claim verification from tabular data documents. Recent LLM-based approaches either employ complex pretraining/fine-tuning or decompose verification into subtasks, often lacking comprehensive explanations and generalizability. To address these limitations, we propose a Multi-Agentic framework for Claim verification (MACE) consisting of three specialized agents: Planner, Executor, and Verifier. Instead of elaborate finetuning, each agent employs a zero-shot Chain-of-Thought setup to perform its tasks. MACE produces interpretable verification traces, with the Planner generating explicit reasoning strategies, the Executor providing detailed computation steps, and the Verifier validating the logic. Experiments demonstrate that MACE achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on two datasets and performs on par with the best models on two others, while achieving 80--100\% of best performance with substantially smaller models: 27--92B parameters versus 235B. This combination of competitive performance, memory efficiency, and transparent reasoning highlights our framework's effectiveness.

</details>


### [75] [DORA Explorer: Improving the Exploration Ability of LLMs Without Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17244)
*Priya Gurjar,Md Farhan Ishmam,Kenneth Marino*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DORA Explorer框架，通过生成多样化动作候选、基于token对数概率评分并使用可调探索参数选择动作，无需训练即可提升LLM代理在序列决策任务中的探索能力，在多臂老虎机和文本冒险环境（如TextWorld）中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理在序列决策中输出多样性不足，导致探索不充分、收敛至次优解及陷入循环，尤其在需主动探索的环境中问题突出；传统采样方法（如温度缩放）仅提供词元级随机性，难以保障序列级多样性。

Method: 提出无训练框架DORA Explorer（Diversity-Oriented Ranking of Actions），包含三步：1）生成多样化动作候选；2）利用token对数概率对候选动作打分；3）通过可调探索参数选择最终动作。在Multi-Armed Bandit和TALES环境下进行评估。

Result: DORA在MAB任务上达到与UCB算法相当的性能，在TALES基准（如TextWorld）中显著提升Qwen2.5-7B准确率（从29.2%提升至45.5%），且在不同LLM上表现稳健。

Conclusion: DORA Explorer是一种简单、通用、无需训练的探索增强方法，有效弥补了当前LLM代理在序列决策中探索能力的不足，为提升智能体鲁棒性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Despite the rapid progress, LLMs for sequential decision-making (i.e., LLM agents) still struggle to produce diverse outputs. This leads to insufficient exploration, convergence to sub-optimal solutions, and becoming stuck in loops. Such limitations can be problematic in environments that require active exploration to gather information and make decisions. Sampling methods such as temperature scaling introduce token-level randomness but fail to produce enough diversity at the sequence level. We analyze LLM exploration in the classic Multi-Armed Bandit (MAB) setting and the Text Adventure Learning Environment Suite (TALES). We find that current decoding strategies and prompting methods like Chain-of-Thought and Tree-of-Thought are insufficient for robust exploration. To address this, we introduce DORA Explorer (Diversity-Oriented Ranking of Actions), a training-free framework for improving exploration in LLM agents. DORA generates diverse action candidates, scores them using token log-probabilities, and selects actions using a tunable exploration parameter. DORA achieves UCB-competitive performance on MAB and consistent gains across TALES, e.g., improving Qwen2.5-7B's performance from 29.2% to 45.5% in TextWorld. Our project is available at: https://dora-explore.github.io/.

</details>


### [76] [Seeing Isn't Believing: Mitigating Belief Inertia via Active Intervention in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17252)
*Hanlin Wang,Chak Tou Leong,Jian Wang,Wenjie Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Estimate-Verify-Update (EVU)机制，通过主动干预代理信念以缓解‘信念惯性’问题，在具身任务中显著提升决策效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型驱动的具身智能体常忽视环境反馈，固守错误先验信念（即‘信念惯性’），导致次优决策。

Method: 提出EVU机制：估计预期结果、用显式推理比对观测、基于验证证据主动更新信念；该机制生成显式文本化信念状态，兼容提示工程与训练方法。

Result: 在三个具身基准上实验表明，EVU持续显著提升任务成功率；分析证实其有效缓解信念惯性。

Conclusion: EVU作为一种统一的主动信念干预机制，增强了具身智能体的鲁棒性与环境适应能力。

Abstract: Recent advancements in large language models (LLMs) have enabled agents to tackle complex embodied tasks through environmental interaction. However, these agents still make suboptimal decisions and perform ineffective actions, as they often overlook critical environmental feedback that differs from their internal beliefs. Through a formal probing analysis, we characterize this as belief inertia, a phenomenon where agents stubbornly adhere to prior beliefs despite explicit observations. To address this, we advocate active belief intervention, moving from passive understanding to active management. We introduce the Estimate-Verify-Update (EVU) mechanism, which empowers agents to predict expected outcomes, verify them against observations through explicit reasoning, and actively update prior beliefs based on the verification evidence. EVU is designed as a unified intervention mechanism that generates textual belief states explicitly, and can be integrated into both prompting-based and training-based agent reasoning methods. Extensive experiments across three embodied benchmarks demonstrate that EVU consistently yields substantial gains in task success rates. Further analyses validate that our approach effectively mitigates belief inertia, advancing the development of more robust embodied agents. Our code is available at https://github.com/WangHanLinHenry/EVU.

</details>


### [77] [Are Emotion and Rhetoric Neurons in LLM? Neuron Recognition and Adaptive Masking for Emotion-Rhetoric Prediction Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17255)
*Li Zheng,Xin Zhang,Shuyi He,Fei Li,Chong Teng,Jiangming Yang,Donghong Ji,Zhuang Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向大语言模型（LLMs）情绪与修辞表征机制的神经元级分析与调控框架，通过多维筛选识别情绪和修辞相关神经元，并设计自适应掩码方法实现可验证的因果干预，在多个数据集上验证了其对情绪任务增强和非目标句引导的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多依赖外部优化，缺乏对LLM内部情绪与修辞表征机制的深入探索，尤其缺少对情绪与修辞神经元及其内在关联的系统性分析；传统神经元掩码方法存在反直觉现象，难以可靠验证神经元功能。

Method: 提出一种融合多维筛选的神经元识别框架，并设计包含动态过滤、衰减掩码和反馈优化的自适应掩码方法，以支持神经元功能的可靠因果验证。

Result: 成功识别出6类情绪和4类核心修辞对应的神经元，并通过神经元调控实现了非目标句子的定向诱导及情绪任务性能提升；在5个常用数据集上验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 本工作为LLM中情绪与修辞表达的细粒度可控生成提供了新范式，推动了从表层优化向内部表征机制挖掘的演进。

Abstract: Accurate comprehension and controllable generation of emotion and rhetoric are pivotal for enhancing the reasoning capabilities of large language models (LLMs). Existing studies mostly rely on external optimizations, lacking in-depth exploration of internal representation mechanisms, thus failing to achieve fine-grained steering at the neuron level. A handful of works on neurons are confined to emotions, neglecting rhetoric neurons and their intrinsic connections. Traditional neuron masking also exhibits counterintuitive phenomena, making reliable verification of neuron functionality infeasible. To address these issues, we systematically investigate the neurons representation mechanisms and inherent associations of 6 emotion categories and 4 core rhetorical devices. We propose a neuron identification framework that integrates multi-dimensional screening, and design an adaptive masking method incorporating dynamic filtering, attenuation masking, and feedback optimization, enabling reliable causal validation of neuron functionality.Through neuron regulation, we achieve directed induction of non-target sentences and enhancement of emotion tasks via rhetoric neurons. Experiments on 5 commonly used datasets validate the effectiveness of our method, providing a novel paradigm for the fine-grained steering of emotion and rhetoric expressions in LLMs.

</details>


### [78] [REZE: Representation Regularization for Domain-adaptive Text Embedding Pre-finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17257)
*Seungmin Lee,Jeonghwan Lee,Hyunkuk Lim,Sejoon Kim,Mingi Sung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出REZE框架，通过在特征空间中分解锚点-正样本对的关系并进行自适应软收缩，控制预微调过程中的表征偏移，从而在保留语义结构的同时抑制任务引入的噪声。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本嵌入模型在专用领域适配时，常采用对比式预微调（PFT），但其在异构任务上直接微调易引入任务诱导偏差，导致嵌入几何失真与性能下降。

Method: REZE框架在锚点-正样本对的关系空间中进行特征分解，识别任务可变方向，并沿各主成分实施自适应软收缩以抑制噪声、保留不变语义结构。

Result: 在多个嵌入骨干模型和专业基准上，REZE优于标准预微调及各向同性后处理正则化方法，在多数设置下表现更优且稳定性更强；嵌入空间分析证实其能实现与原始流形对齐的受控表征偏移。

Conclusion: 表征偏移控制是异构监督下鲁棒嵌入预微调的关键原则，REZE为此提供了有效且无推理开销的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent text embedding models are often adapted to specialized domains via contrastive pre-finetuning (PFT) on a naive collection of scattered, heterogeneous tasks. However, this approach often introduces task-induced bias alongside domain knowledge, leading to uncontrolled representation shifts that distort the pretrained embedding geometry and cause substantial performance degradation.
