Evaluated on a publicly available corpus of 25,000 annotated legal documents with a 75/25 training-test partition, the proposed system achieves 97.26% classification accuracy and a macro F1-score of 96.82%, surpassing established baselines including fine-tuned BERT, Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) with FastText, CNN with random embeddings, and a Term Frequency-Inverse Document Frequency (TF-IDF) k-Nearest Neighbour (KNN) classifier. The model also attains the highest Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic (AUC-ROC) curve of 97.83% among all compared systems while operating with only 5.1 million parameters and an inference latency of 0.31 ms per document - more than 13 times faster than BERT. Ablation experiments confirm the individual contribution of each pipeline component, and the confusion matrix reveals that residual errors are confined to semantically adjacent citation categories. These findings indicate that carefully designed convolutional architectures represent a scalable, resource-efficient alternative to heavyweight transformers for intelligent legal document analysis.

</details>


### [119] [Before You Interpret the Profile: Validity Scaling for LLM Metacognitive Self-Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17707)
*Jon-Paul Cacioli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文将临床人格评估中的效度量表框架应用于大语言模型（LLM）的元认知探针数据，构建了6个效度指标，发现部分前沿模型在响应效度上存在系统性缺陷，并揭示了思维链训练可能引发两类相反的反应偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型评估缺乏对响应有效性的筛查机制，而临床人格测评（如PAI、MMPI-3）已建立成熟的效度量表体系，本文旨在将其迁移至LLM评估中以提升结果可信度。

Method: 基于20个前沿模型在524项元认知探针任务上的表现，操作化定义L、K、F、Fp、RBS、TRIN六个效度指数；采用分层分类法识别无效模型；通过相关性与效应量比较有效/无效模型的置信度敏感性；进行因子分析提取潜在维度；辅以配套研究开发可移植筛查协议并验证其与选择性预测的一致性。

Result: 6个效度指数成功区分出4个构念层面无效、2个升高型无效的模型；有效模型表现出显著正向的项目敏感置信度（均r = .18），无效模型则呈负向（均r = −.20，d = 2.17, p = .001）；两个潜在维度解释94.6%的方差；思维链训练引发两种相反的反应扭曲。

Conclusion: LLM响应效度问题真实存在且可量化，临床效度量表框架可有效迁移；需在LLM评估中常规嵌入效度筛查；本工作为构建可信AI评估体系提供了方法论基础与实证支持。

Abstract: Clinical personality assessment screens response validity before interpreting substantive scales. LLM evaluation does not. We apply the validity scaling framework from the PAI and MMPI-3 to metacognitive probe data from 20 frontier models across 524 items. Six validity indices are operationalised: L (maintaining confidence on errors), K (betting on errors), F (withdrawing consensus-endorsed items), Fp (withdrawing correct answers), RBS (inverted monitoring), and TRIN (fixed responding). A tiered classification system identifies four models as construct-level invalid and two as elevated. Valid-profile models produce item-sensitive confidence (mean r = .18, 14 of 16 significant). Invalid-profile models do not (mean r = -.20, d = 2.17, p = .001). Chain-of-thought training produces two opposite response distortions. Two latent dimensions account for 94.6% of index variance. Companion papers extract a portable screening protocol (Cacioli, 2026e) and validate it against selective prediction (Cacioli, 2026f). All data and code: https://github.com/synthiumjp/validity-scaling-llm

</details>


### [120] [DeInfer: Efficient Parallel Inferencing for Decomposed Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17709)
*You-Liang Huang,Xinhao Huang,Chengxi Liao,Zeyi Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DeInfer，一种专为分解大语言模型（LLM）并行推理设计的高性能推理系统，通过多项优化提升性能并兼容前沿优化技术，实验表明其在并行推理中具有显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLM）分解工作主要关注下游任务性能提升，却忽视了模型规模扩大时并行推理性能差的关键问题。

Method: 提出DeInfer推理系统，包含多项针对分解LLM并行推理的优化设计，并确保与当前最优优化技术兼容。

Result: 大量实验验证了DeInfer的优越性能，表明其能显著促进分解LLM的并行推理。

Conclusion: DeInfer有效缓解了大规模分解LLM并行推理的性能瓶颈，为高效部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Existing works on large language model (LLM) decomposition mainly focus on improving performance on downstream tasks, but they ignore the poor parallel inference performance when trying to scale up the model size. To mitigate this important performance issue, this paper introduces DeInfer, a high-performance inference system dedicated to parallel inference of decomposed LLMs. It consists of multiple optimizations to maximize performance and be compatible with state-of-the-art optimization techniques. Extensive experiments are carried out to evaluate DeInfer's performance, where the results demonstrate its superiority, suggesting it can greatly facilitate the parallel inference of decomposed LLMs.

</details>


### [121] [Screen Before You Interpret: A Portable Validity Protocol for Benchmark-Based LLM Confidence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17714)
*Jon-Paul Cacioli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种借鉴临床人格评估中的有效性筛查原则（如PAI、MMPI-3）的便携式协议，用于评估大语言模型（LLM）置信度信号在项目层面的有效性；该协议通过三个核心指标（L、Fp、RBS）、一个结构指标（TRIN）和一个项目敏感性统计量，基于单个2×2列联表计算，并采用三级分类（无效、不确定、有效）判定模型置信度质量；在20个前沿LLM、524个项目上的验证显示，有效模型平均相关系数r=0.18，无效模型为r=-0.20（效应量d=2.48），且跨基准（如MMLU）与外部数据验证了其泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏标准方法来检验LLM置信度信号是否具备项目层面的信息，而该信号被广泛用于拒答、路由及安全关键决策，亟需可靠的有效性评估手段。

Method: 将临床人格评估（PAI、MMPI-3）中的有效性筛查原则迁移至LLM置信度分析，构建基于2×2列联表的便携式协议，含L、Fp、RBS三个核心指数、TRIN结构指标和项目敏感性统计，并采用融合四种临床传统的三级分类系统。

Result: 在20个前沿LLM、524项任务上验证：4个模型被归为Invalid，2个为Indeterminate；Valid模型平均r=0.18（15/16显著），Invalid模型平均r=-0.20（d=2.48）；跨基准（MMLU+口头置信）与外部数据（Yang et al., 2024）验证协议具有可迁移性。

Conclusion: 该临床启发式有效性筛查协议为LLM置信度评估提供了可解释、可复现、跨基准适用的标准化工具，填补了当前置信度信号质量验证的方法空白。

Abstract: LLM confidence signals are used for abstention, routing, and safety-critical decisions. No standard practice exists for checking whether a confidence signal carries item-level information before building on it. We transfer the validity screening principle from clinical personality assessment (PAI, MMPI-3) as a portable protocol for benchmark-based LLM confidence data. The protocol specifies three core indices (L, Fp, RBS), a structural indicator (TRIN), and an item-sensitivity statistic, computed from a single 2x2 contingency table. A three-tier classification system (Invalid, Indeterminate, Valid) draws on four clinical traditions. Validated on 20 frontier LLMs across 524 items, four models are classified Invalid, two Indeterminate. Valid-profile models show mean r = .18 (15/16 significant). Invalid-profile models show mean r = -.20 (d = 2.48). Cross-benchmark validation on 18 models using MMLU with verbalized confidence and on external data from Yang et al. (2024) confirms the screen transfers across benchmarks and probe formats. All data and code: https://github.com/synthiumjp/validity-scaling-llm

</details>


### [122] [Concurrent Criterion Validation of a Validity Screen for LLM Confidence Signals via Selective Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17716)
*Jon-Paul Cacioli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了“有效性筛选器”（Validity Screen），该工具通过将大语言模型（LLM）的置信度信号划分为Valid、Indeterminate、Invalid三类，有效预测其在选择性预测任务中的表现（如AUROC），三类间表现呈单调差异，且该分类解释了47%的性能方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决大语言模型置信度信号与实际预测性能不一致的问题，亟需一种能可靠区分模型可信度等级的方法，以支持安全、可控的选择性预测应用。

Method: 采用Cacioli（2026d, 2026e）提出的有效性筛选器，对20个前沿LLM（7个家族）在6个认知任务共524个样本上的置信度信号进行三分类；通过Type 2 AUROC评估选择性预测性能，并使用Cohen's d、方差解释率、split-half交叉验证等统计方法检验分类效度。

Result: Valid类模型平均AUROC为.624，Invalid类为.357，Cohen's d = 2.81（p = .002）；三类AUROC呈严格单调：Invalid < Indeterminate < Valid；分类解释47%的AUROC方差；split-half验证显示效应稳健（中位d = 1.77，P(d > 0) = 1.0）；DeepSeek-R1在覆盖率降至10%时准确率骤降（85.3%→11.3%），凸显筛选必要性。

Conclusion: 有效性筛选器是一种有效、稳健且具有实际意义的置信度质量评估工具，其三类划分能显著预测选择性预测性能，应被纳入LLM部署前的可靠性评估流程。

Abstract: The validity screen (Cacioli, 2026d, 2026e) classifies LLM confidence signals as Valid, Indeterminate, or Invalid. We test whether these classifications predict selective prediction performance. Twenty frontier LLMs from seven families were evaluated on 524 items across six cognitive tracks. Valid models show mean Type 2 AUROC = .624 (SD = .048). Invalid models show mean AUROC = .357 (SD = .231). Cohen's d = 2.81, p = .002. The tiers order monotonically: Invalid (.357) < Indeterminate (.554) < Valid (.624). Split-half cross-validation yields median d = 1.77, P(d > 0) = 1.0 across 1,000 splits. The three-tier classification accounts for 47% of the variance in AUROC. DeepSeek-R1 drops from 85.3% accuracy at full coverage to 11.3% at 10% coverage. The screen predicts the criterion. For selective prediction, the screen matters.

</details>


### [123] [Do LLMs Use Cultural Knowledge Without Being Told? A Multilingual Evaluation of Implicit Pragmatic Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17718)
*Mehwish Nasim,Sanjeevan Selvaganapathy,Neel Ganapathi Sabhahit,Marie Griesbach,Pranav Bhandari,Janina Lütke Stockdiek,Lennart Schäpermeier,Usman Naseem,Christian Grimme*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大型语言模型（LLM）在文化隐含情境下调整语言使用（如礼貌、集体/个体倾向、不确定性表达）的能力，发现其对隐式文化线索的敏感性远低于显式指令（仅约20%迁移），且表现因语言和文化维度而异；尤其指出模型更依赖语言形式而非文化内涵，提示文化适应需从部署方式（显式引导）入手，而非仅靠知识增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型是否能在文化仅通过情境隐含时，自发调整其语言使用（即语用层面的文化适应能力），而不仅限于回答显式文化相关问题。

Method: 构建60个跨文化的对话场景，覆盖英语、德语、印地语、尼泊尔语、乌尔都语五种语言；设计三种提示条件（中性基线、显式文化指令、隐式情境线索），并基于12个语用特征（如对权威的尊重、个体/群体框架、不确定性管理）评分；提出'语用情境敏感性（PCS）'指标衡量显式到隐式迁移程度，并进行跨模型、跨语言统计分析。

Result: 四款主流LLM在五种语言上的平均PCS仅为0.196，表明隐式文化响应能力薄弱；权威相关线索迁移最强（0.299），个体/群体框架最弱（0.120）；不确定性表达（如模糊限制语）在显式指令下反而被对齐训练抑制；印地语与乌尔都语对照分析显示模型响应主要取决于语言结构而非文化关联。

Conclusion: LLM的文化语用能力本质上是‘显式vs隐式部署’问题，而非单纯的事实知识缺失；提升文化适应性需在交互设计中主动引入显式文化引导，而非依赖模型从情境中自发推断。

Abstract: Many benchmarks show that large language models can answer direct questions about culture. We study a different question: do they also change how they speak when culture is only implied by the situation? We evaluate 60 culturally grounded conversational scenarios across five languages in three conditions: a neutral baseline (Prompt A), an explicit cultural instruction (Prompt B), and implicit situational cueing (Prompt C). We score responses on 12 pragmatic features covering deference to authority, individual-versus-group framing, and uncertainty management. We define Pragmatic Context Sensitivity (PCS) as the fraction of the Prompt A->B shift that reappears under Prompt A->C. Across four deployed LLMs and five languages (English, German, Hindi, Nepali, Urdu), the primary stable-only PCS mean is 0.196 (SD = 0.113), indicating that the models recover only about one-fifth of the pragmatic shift they can produce when instructed explicitly. Transfer is strongest for authority-related cues (0.299) and weakest for individual-versus-group framing (0.120). Uncertainty-related behaviour is mixed: hedging density exhibits negative explicit gaps in all five languages, suggesting that alignment training actively suppresses the target behaviour. Because Hindi and Urdu share core grammar yet index distinct cultural communities, we use them as a natural control; a paired analysis finds no reliable baseline difference (t = 0.96, p = 0.339, dz = 0.06), suggesting that models respond primarily to linguistic structure rather than to the cultural associations a language carries. We argue that multilingual cultural pragmatics is an explicit-versus-implicit deployment problem, not only a factual knowledge problem.

</details>


### [124] [RePrompT: Recurrent Prompt Tuning for Integrating Structured EHR Encoders with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17725)
*Arya Hadizadeh Moghaddam,Drew Ross,Mohsen Nayebi Kerdabadi,Dongjie Wang,Zijun Yao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RePrompT框架，通过时间感知的提示调优将结构化EHR编码器与大语言模型（LLM）结合，在不修改LLM架构的前提下，保留时序信息并融入人群级模式，显著提升临床预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在处理结构化电子健康记录（EHR）时存在两大问题：一是将时序EHR转为文本会丢失时间结构和代码标识，削弱对共现与纵向规律的建模；二是LLM通常独立处理每位患者，无法利用人群级学习到的任务对齐表征。

Method: 提出RePrompT框架：1）通过提示调优集成结构化EHR编码器；2）递归融合历史就诊的潜在状态以保持纵向信息；3）引入由队列训练、任务对齐的EHR编码器生成的可训练prompt token，注入人群级信息。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV数据集上，RePrompT在多个临床预测任务中持续优于纯EHR模型和纯LLM基线。

Conclusion: RePrompT有效弥合了LLM与结构化EHR建模之间的鸿沟，证明了在保持LLM通用性的同时，通过轻量级提示工程融入领域知识与人群统计信息的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong promise for mining Electronic Health Records (EHRs) by reasoning over longitudinal clinical information to capture context-rich patient trajectories. However, leveraging LLMs for structured EHRs (e.g., standardized diagnosis and medication codes) presents two key challenges. First, translating time-stamped EHR sequences into plain text can obscure both temporal structure and code identities, weakening the ability to capture code co-occurrence and longitudinal regularities. Second, unlike cohort-trained predictive models that learn a shared, task-aligned representation space across patients, LLMs are often applied in a case-isolated inference setting where each patient is processed independently without leveraging population-level patterns. To address these challenges, we introduce RePrompT, a time-aware LLM framework that integrates structured EHR encoders through prompt tuning, without modifying underlying architectures. Specifically, RePrompT recurrently incorporates latent states from prior visits to preserve longitudinal information, and injects population-level information through trainable prompt tokens derived from a cohort-trained, task-aligned EHR encoder. Experiments on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV demonstrate that RePrompT consistently outperforms both EHR-based and LLM-based baselines across multiple clinical prediction tasks.

</details>


### [125] [MHSafeEval: Role-Aware Interaction-Level Evaluation of Mental Health Safety in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17730)
*Suhyun Lee,Palakorn Achananuparp,Neemesh Yadav,Ee-Peng Lim,Yang Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出R-MHSafe角色感知心理健康安全分类法和MHSafeEval闭环代理评估框架，用于检测大语言模型在多轮心理辅导交互中产生的累积性、角色依赖性安全风险，显著提升了对现有静态基准遗漏的安全失效模式的覆盖与诊断粒度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心理健康领域大语言模型安全评估方法多基于孤立响应或静态数据集，难以捕捉交互过程中动态演化和累积的临床危害，亟需更贴近真实咨询场景的细粒度、轨迹级评估方法。

Method: 构建R-MHSafe角色感知安全分类体系（依据AI咨询师在交互中扮演的加害者、煽动者、促成者或纵容者等角色，结合临床危害类型），并设计MHSafeEval闭环代理评估框架，通过对抗式多轮对话模拟实现轨迹级安全风险发现。

Result: 在多个前沿大语言模型上开展大规模评估，揭示出大量被传统静态基准遗漏的角色依赖性和累积性安全失效，并验证该框架显著提升失效模式覆盖率与诊断精细度。

Conclusion: 角色感知与轨迹级评估是提升心理健康AI安全评测有效性与临床相关性的关键路径，R-MHSafe与MHSafeEval为可信赖AI心理咨询系统提供了新范式与实用工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly explored as scalable tools for mental health counseling, yet evaluating their safety remains challenging due to the interactional and context-dependent nature of clinical harm. Existing evaluation frameworks predominantly assess isolated responses using coarse-grained taxonomies or static datasets, limiting their ability to diagnose how harms emerge and accumulate over multi-turn counseling interactions. In this work, we introduce R-MHSafe, a role-aware mental health safety taxonomy that characterizes clinically significant harm in terms of the interactional roles an AI counselor adopts, including perpetrator, instigator, facilitator, or enabler, combined with clinically grounded harm categories. Then, we propose MHSafeEval, a closed-loop, agent-based evaluation framework that formulates safety assessment as trajectory-level discovery of harm through adversarial multi-turn interactions, guided by role-aware modeling. Using R-MHSafe and MHSafeEval, we conduct a large-scale evaluation across state-of-the-art LLMs. Our results reveal substantial role-dependent and cumulative safety failures that are systematically missed by existing static benchmarks, and show that our framework significantly improves failure-mode coverage and diagnostic granularity.

</details>


### [126] [Mira-Embeddings-V1: Domain-Adapted Semantic Reranking for Recruitment via LLM-Synthesized Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17738)
*Zhaohua Liang,Zhilin Wang,Renjie Cao,Yining Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出mira-embeddings-v1，一种面向招聘领域的语义重排序系统，利用大语言模型（LLM）合成训练数据重塑嵌入空间，并通过轻量级BoundaryHead修正职位边界混淆，在不依赖大规模人工标注数据的前提下显著提升召回率与精确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 候选人在招聘中需在有限审阅预算下实现高召回；现有上游检索器返回的短名单需更优重排序以提升合格候选人排名。

Method: 构建五阶段提示流程生成多样化正样本与难负样本；采用两轮LoRA微调：先JD-JD对比学习，再JD-CV三元组对齐；引入BoundaryHead MLP区分同名但职责范围不同的职位。

Result: 在300个真实JD本地池上，Recall@50从68.89%提升至77.55%，Precision@10从35.77%升至39.62%；在全球44,138候选人池上，Recall@200达0.7047（基线为0.5969）。

Conclusion: LLM合成监督信号结合边界感知重排序，可在无需重型交叉编码器前提下，稳健提升招聘场景下的重排序性能。

Abstract: Candidate sourcing for recruiters is best viewed as a two-stage retrieval and reranking pipeline with recall as the primary objective under a limited review budget. An upstream production retriever first returns a candidate shortlist for each job description (JD), and our goal is to rerank that shortlist so that qualified candidates appear as high as possible. We present mira-embeddings-v1, a semantic reranking system for the recruitment domain that reshapes the embedding space with LLM-synthesized training data and corrects boundary confusions with a lightweight reranking head. Starting from real JDs, we build a five-stage prompt pipeline to generate diverse positive and hard negative samples that sculpt the semantic space from multiple angles. We then apply a two-round LoRA adaptation: JD--JD contrastive training followed by JD--CV triplet alignment on a heterogeneous text dataset. Importantly, these gains require no large-scale manually labeled industrial training pairs: a modest set of real JDs is expanded into supervision through LLM synthesis. Finally, a BoundaryHead MLP reranks the Top-K results to distinguish between roles that share the same title but differ in scope. On a local pool of 300 real JDs with candidates from an upstream production retriever, mira-embeddings-v1 improves Recall@50 from 68.89% (baseline) to 77.55% while lifting Precision@10 from 35.77% to 39.62%. On a supportive global pool over 44,138 candidates judged by a Qwen3-32B rubric, it achieves Recall@200 of 0.7047 versus 0.5969 for the baseline. These results show that LLM-synthesized supervision with boundary-aware reranking yields robust gains without a heavy cross-encoder.

</details>


### [127] [HiRAS: A Hierarchical Multi-Agent Framework for Paper-to-Code Generation and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17745)
*Hanhua Hong,Yizhi LI,Jiaoyan Chen,Sophia Ananiadou,Xiaoli Li,Jung-jae Kim,Chenghua Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层多智能体框架HiRAS，用于端到端实验复现，并改进了Paper2Code基准的评估协议为P2C-Ex，显著提升了性能与鲁棒性，降低了幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在自动化计算研究（尤其是实验复现）中受限于固定顺序的代理流水线和弱全局协调，导致鲁棒性和性能不足。

Method: 提出分层研究代理系统HiRAS，包含监督式管理代理以协调细粒度阶段的专业化代理；同时构建新评估协议Paper2Code-Extra（P2C-Ex），引入仓库级信息并更贴近原参考指标。

Result: 在开放源代码骨干模型上相较先前SOTA获得>10%相对性能提升，显著降低评估中的幻觉现象。

Conclusion: HiRAS框架与P2C-Ex评估协议共同提升了实验复现任务的自动化水平、鲁棒性与可信度。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have highlighted their potential to automate computational research, particularly reproducing experimental results. However, existing approaches still use fixed sequential agent pipelines with weak global coordination, which limits their robustness and overall performance. In this work, we propose Hierarchical Research Agent System (HiRAS), a hierarchical multi-agent framework for end-to-end experiment reproduction that employs supervisory manager agents to coordinate specialised agents across fine-grained stages. We also identify limitations in the reference-free evaluation of the Paper2Code benchmark and introduce Paper2Code-Extra (P2C-Ex), a refined protocol that incorporates repository-level information and better aligns with the original reference-based metric. We conduct extensive evaluation, validating the effectiveness and robustness of our proposed methods, and observing improvements, including >10\% relative performance gain beyond the previous state-of-the-art using open-source backbone models and significantly reduced hallucination in evaluation. Our work is available on GitHub: https://github.com/KOU-199024/HiRAS.

</details>


### [128] [Reverse Constitutional AI: A Framework for Controllable Toxic Data Generation via Probability-Clamped RLAIF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17769)
*Yuan Fang,Yiming Luo,Aimin Zhou,Fei Tan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Reverse Constitutional AI（R-CAI）的自动化、可控对抗数据生成框架，用于红队测试大语言模型的安全性；通过反转无害宪法为毒性宪法，并结合AI反馈强化学习中的概率裁剪技术，实现了高质量、多维度毒性数据的高效合成，兼顾毒性强度与语义连贯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型安全性评估中，系统性合成高质量毒性数据的研究仍不足，且传统方法依赖孤立的越狱提示，缺乏可控性和可扩展性。

Method: 提出R-CAI框架：1）将无害宪法逆向构建为毒性宪法；2）采用批判–修订迭代流程生成对抗样本；3）在AI反馈强化学习中引入概率裁剪机制，防止奖励黑客行为并保持语义连贯性。

Result: 实验表明R-CAI能生成多样、高质量的毒性数据；概率裁剪使语义连贯性提升15%，同时不削弱对抗强度。

Conclusion: R-CAI是一种完全自动化的红队测试数据生成框架，支持对对齐语言模型进行系统性安全评估。

Abstract: Ensuring the safety of large language models (LLMs) requires robust red teaming, yet the systematic synthesis of high-quality toxic data remains under-explored. We propose Reverse Constitutional AI (R-CAI), a framework for automated and controllable adversarial data generation that moves beyond isolated jailbreak prompts. By inverting a harmless constitution into a constitution of toxicity and iteratively refining model outputs through a critique--revision pipeline, R-CAI enables scalable synthesis of multi-dimensional adversarial data without human annotation. Optimizing solely for toxicity-related rewards, however, can lead to reward hacking and degraded semantic coherence. To address this challenge, we introduce probability clamping within reinforcement learning from AI feedback, which stabilizes adversarial optimization while preserving adversarial intent. Experiments demonstrate that R-CAI generates diverse, high-quality toxic data and that probability clamping substantially improves semantic coherence (15%) without sacrificing adversarial strength. Overall, R-CAI provides a fully automated framework for red teaming data generation and systematic safety evaluation of aligned language models.

</details>


### [129] [SPENCE: A Syntactic Probe for Detecting Contamination in NL2SQL Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17771)
*Mohammadtaher Safarzadeh,Hitesh Laxmichand Patel,Afshin Orojlooyjadid,Graham Horwood,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SPENCE框架，通过系统生成NL2SQL测试查询的句法变体，检测并量化大语言模型在自然语言转SQL任务中因训练数据污染导致的性能虚高问题；实验发现旧基准（如Spider）污染严重，而新基准（如BIRD）基本未受污染，强调需采用时序感知、句法驱动的鲁棒评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NL2SQL基准（如Spider）的评测结果可能因训练数据污染（含相同或相似查询）而虚高，缺乏对模型真实泛化能力的可信评估。

Method: 提出SPENCE框架：基于四类NL2SQL数据集（Spider、SParC、CoSQL、BIRD），系统生成测试查询的句法变体；采用执行准确率作为评估指标；测量不同句法偏离程度下的准确率变化，并用Kendall's tau（带Bootstrap置信区间）量化排序敏感性；结合基准发布时间分析污染时序模式。

Result: 发现明显时间梯度：较老的Spider基准呈现最强负相关（高污染迹象），而较新的BIRD基准几乎无敏感性（低污染）；各LLM在句法扰动下执行准确率普遍下降，且下降幅度与基准发布时间强相关。

Conclusion: NL2SQL评测必须考虑训练-测试时间线与句法鲁棒性，SPENCE为识别和量化数据污染提供了可控、可解释的评估范式，推动更可信的基准设计与模型评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance on natural language to SQL (NL2SQL) benchmarks, yet their reported accuracy may be inflated by contamination from benchmark queries or structurally similar patterns seen during training. We introduce SPENCE (Syntactic Probing and Evaluation of NL2SQL Contamination Effects), a controlled syntactic probing framework for detecting and quantifying such contamination. SPENCE systematically generates syntactic variants of test queries for four widely used NL2SQL datasets-Spider, SParC, CoSQL, and the newer BIRD benchmark. We use SPENCE to evaluate multiple high-capacity LLMs under execution-based scoring. For each model, we measure changes in execution accuracy across increasing levels of syntactic divergence and quantify rank sensitivity using Kendall's tau with bootstrap confidence intervals. By aligning these robustness trends with benchmark release dates, we observe a clear temporal gradient: older benchmarks such as Spider exhibit the strongest negative values and thus the highest likelihood of training leakage, whereas the more recent BIRD dataset shows minimal sensitivity and appears largely uncontaminated. Together, these findings highlight the importance of temporally contextualized, syntactic-probing evaluation for trustworthy NL2SQL benchmarking.

</details>


### [130] [Forget What Matters, Keep the Rest: Selective Unlearning of Informative Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17785)
*Seunghee Koh,Sunghyun Baek,Youngdong Kim,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预测分布熵的词元级遗忘正则化方法ETW，用于大语言模型的高效遗忘学习，兼顾遗忘效果与模型效用保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘方法在应用遗忘损失时未考虑词元语义重要性，导致模型效用下降；而现有词元级正则器依赖真实标签置信度或外部语言解析器，难以捕捉上下文信息和模型整体预测状态。

Method: 提出Entropy-guided Token Weighting（ETW），利用预测分布的熵作为词元信息量的代理指标：高熵对应信息性词元，低熵对应结构词元，并据此加权遗忘损失。

Result: ETW在遗忘效果上优于现有词元级方法，同时更有效地保持了模型效用。

Conclusion: 基于熵的词元加权策略能更合理地反映词元的信息重要性，为大语言模型的可控遗忘提供了一种轻量、自适应且上下文感知的新范式。

Abstract: Unlearning in large language models (LLMs) has emerged as a promising safeguard against adversarial behaviors. When the forgetting loss is applied uniformly without considering token-level semantic importance, model utility can be unnecessarily degraded. Recent studies have explored token-wise loss regularizers that prioritize informative tokens, but largely rely on ground-truth confidence or external linguistic parsers, which limits their ability to capture contextual information or the model's overall predictive state. Intuitively, function words like "the" primarily serve syntactic roles and are highly predictable with little ambiguity, but informative words admit multiple plausible alternatives with greater uncertainty. Based on this intuition, we propose Entropy-guided Token Weighting (ETW), a token-level unlearning regularizer that uses entropy of the predictive distribution as a proxy for token informativeness. We demonstrate that informative tokens tend to have higher entropy, whereas structural tokens tend to have lower entropy. This behavior enables ETW to achieve more effective unlearning while better preserving model utility than existing token-level approaches.

</details>


### [131] [Bridging the Reasoning Gap in Vietnamese with Small Language Models via Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17794)
*Bui The Trung,Do Minh Duc,Nguyen Van Vinh,Bui Nguyen Quoc Trinh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了在资源受限设备上部署小型语言模型（SLM）进行越南语小学数学推理的问题，提出Vi-S1K数据集和Vi-Elementary-Bench基准，并发现监督微调（SFT）能显著提升解释质量，而简化测试时缩放策略优于复杂代理式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小型语言模型（SLMs）在越南语等非英语语言中存在‘推理差距’，尤其在资源受限设备上难以维持连贯思维链，亟需提升其本地化推理能力。

Method: 构建越南语小学数学高保真推理数据集Vi-S1K和双资源评估基准Vi-Elementary-Bench；采用LLM-as-a-Judge协议评估；对比监督微调（SFT）、ReAct与Chain-of-Thought（CoT）+Self-Consistency等提示策略效果。

Result: 基础模型具备强潜在知识（准确率4.05/5.00），但存在严重‘格式化差距’；SFT使解释质量提升77%；ReAct带来‘认知税’而降低性能，纯CoT+Self-Consistency更优。

Conclusion: 对于边缘端推理，SFT结合简化的测试时缩放策略优于复杂代理工作流，确立了SLM的实用部署层级。

Abstract: The democratization of ubiquitous AI hinges on deploying sophisticated reasoning capabilities on resource-constrained devices. However, Small Language Models (SLMs) often face a "reasoning gap", particularly in non-English languages like Vietnamese, where they struggle to maintain coherent chains of thought. This paper investigates Test-Time Scaling strategies for the Qwen3-1.7B architecture within the context of Vietnamese Elementary Mathematics. We introduce Vi-S1K, a high-fidelity reasoning dataset localized via a Gemini 2.5 Flash-Lite powered pipeline, and Vi-Elementary-Bench, a dual-resource benchmark for rigorous evaluation. Using an LLM-as-a-Judge protocol, we reveal that the base model possesses robust latent knowledge (Accuracy: 4.05/5.00) but suffers from a severe "formatting gap" in communication. Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) acts as a critical "reasoning unlocker", yielding a 77% improvement in Explanation Quality and bridging the gap between raw calculation and pedagogical coherence. Furthermore, our analysis of prompting strategies uncovers a significant trade-off: structured frameworks like ReAct impose a "cognitive tax" on the 1.7B parameter capacity, degrading performance relative to pure Chain-of-Thought (CoT) combined with Self-Consistency. These findings establish a deployment hierarchy for SLMs, demonstrating that SFT combined with simplified test-time scaling is superior to complex agentic workflows for edge-based reasoning.

</details>


### [132] [PDDL-Mind: Large Language Models are Capable on Belief Reasoning with Reliable State Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17819)
*Wang Bill Zhu,Qiutong Tony Yi,Robin Jia,Jesse Thomason*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PDDL-Mind框架，通过将叙事转化为PDDL形式的显式状态与动作，并验证状态转移逻辑一致性，提升大语言模型在心智理论（ToM）任务中的表现，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在心智理论（ToM）基准测试中远低于人类水平，作者认为主因是隐式状态跟踪不可靠，而非高层推理能力不足。

Method: 提出神经符号框架PDDL-Mind，将叙事描述翻译为PDDL表示的显式状态与动作，并利用预定义领域验证动作引发的状态转移，从而为LLM提供逻辑一致、显式的环境状态表征。

Result: 在MMToM-QA、MuMA和FanToM三个ToM基准上，PDDL-Mind相比当前最优方法取得超过5%的绝对准确率提升。

Conclusion: 显式建模和验证世界状态可有效缓解LLM在ToM任务中因隐式状态跟踪不可靠导致的性能瓶颈，神经符号方法是提升ToM能力的有效路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) perform substantially below human level on existing theory-of-mind (ToM) benchmarks, even when augmented with chain-of-thought prompting or probabilistic belief updates. We argue that these failures primarily arise from unreliable implicit state tracking rather than limitations in high-level reasoning. We introduce PDDL-Mind, a neuro-symbolic framework that decouples environment state evolution from belief inference. By translating narrative descriptions into explicit states and actions expressed in Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL), and by verifying action-induced state transitions against a predefined domain, PDDL-Mind provides LLMs with a logically consistent and explicit representation of world states for ToM tasks. Experiments on MMToM-QA, MuMA and FanToM show that PDDL-Mind achieves over 5% absolute accuracy gain over the best existing state-of-the-art method on ToM benchmark questions.

</details>


### [133] [Learning to Seek Help: Dynamic Collaboration Between Small and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17827)
*Hang Zeng,Xiangyu Liu,Yong Hu,Chaoyue Niu,Jiarui Zhang,Shaojie Tang,Fan Wu,Guihai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种动态协作框架，让小型语言模型（SLM）主动决定在多步推理中如何向大型语言模型（LLM）请求帮助，而LLM则提供自适应反馈；实验表明该策略具有可扩展性、优于静态流程，并能泛化到未见过的LLM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡LLM的强大能力与高成本、低隐私，以及SLM的高效私密但能力有限之间的矛盾，实现二者优势互补。

Method: 设计动态协作框架：SLM学习主动决策何时及如何向LLM请求协助，LLM提供自适应反馈而非被动响应；系统分析协作策略如何受SLM/LLM能力及效率、隐私约束影响。

Result: 发现明显缩放效应：更强SLM更自依赖，更强LLM使交互更少但更有信息量；动态协作显著优于静态流水线和独立推理，且能鲁棒迁移到未见过的LLM。

Conclusion: 动态协作是协同利用SLM与LLM优势的有效范式，兼顾性能、效率与隐私，并具备良好泛化性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer strong capabilities but raise cost and privacy concerns, whereas small language models (SLMs) facilitate efficient and private local inference yet suffer from limited capacity. To synergize the complementary strengths, we introduce a dynamic collaboration framework, where an SLM learns to proactively decide how to request an LLM during multi-step reasoning, while the LLM provides adaptive feedback instead of acting as a passive tool. We further systematically investigate how collaboration strategies are shaped by SLM and LLM capabilities as well as efficiency and privacy constraints. Evaluation results reveal a distinct scaling effect: stronger SLMs become more self-reliant, while stronger LLMs enable fewer and more informative interactions. In addition, the learned dynamic collaboration strategies significantly outperform static pipelines and standalone inference, and transfer robustly to unseen LLMs.

</details>


### [134] [How Non-Linguistic Is the Indus Sign System? A Synthetic-Baseline Scorecard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17828)
*Ashish Nair*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多指标判别框架，通过将印度河谷文字语料与两类计算机生成的非语言基线（纹章系统和行政编码系统）进行对比，检验其是否编码口语；结果显示印度河谷文字在四个关键统计特征上处于两类基线之间，既不符合典型非语言系统，也无法被任一非语言模型完全复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决关于印度河谷文字（公元前2600–1900年）是否编码口语长达数十年的争议，回应Farmer-Sproat-Witzel（2004）提出的质疑。

Method: 构建多指标判别框架，设计两类Zipf分布、位置约束和二元依赖关系校准的非语言基线（纹章型与行政型），并用四个核心指标（文本简短性、公式化重复、单次词率、位置刚性）评估；分析ICIT/Yajnadevam数字化的1916条去重铭文。

Result: 印度河谷语料在四项指标上均不完全匹配任一非语言基线，处于二者中间；亦无法被七种真实非语言语料（含Sproat 2014数据集）完整复现；复现了Zipf斜率-1.49和条件熵3.23比特等关键统计结果。

Conclusion: 印度河谷文字的统计特征难以用现有非语言模型充分解释，其语言属性不能被排除，支持其可能编码某种口语系统的观点。

Abstract: Whether the Indus Valley sign system (c. 2600-1900 BCE) encodes spoken language has been debated for decades. This paper introduces a multi-metric discrimination framework that tests the observed Indus corpus against two kinds of computer-generated non-linguistic baseline -- one mimicking a heraldic emblem system, the other an administrative coding system -- each calibrated with Zipfian frequency distributions, positional constraints, and bigram dependencies derived from six attested non-linguistic corpora. The scorecard evaluates four properties central to the Farmer-Sproat-Witzel (2004) critique: text brevity, repeated formulaic phrases, hapax legomenon rate, and positional rigidity. Applying this framework to 1,916 deduplicated inscriptions (584 unique signs, 11,110 tokens) from the ICIT/Yajnadevam digitization, we find that the Indus corpus does not match either baseline cleanly. Across the four metrics examined, the Indus corpus occupies an intermediate position relative to the two baseline families, matching neither cleanly. Neither a heraldic nor an administrative generator can reproduce all four properties at once. We also compare against seven real-world non-linguistic corpora including Sproat's (2014) datasets, finding that no attested non-linguistic system reproduces the full Indus statistical profile either. We replicate key prior results including a Zipf slope of -1.49 and conditional entropy of 3.23 bits. All code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [135] [QuickScope: Certifying Hard Questions in Dynamic LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17842)
*Taylor Lundy,Narun K. Raman,Kevin Leyton-Brown*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为QuickScope的新方法，利用改进的贝叶斯优化算法COUP，在动态LLM基准中高效识别模型的难点问题，提升采样效率并降低噪声导致的误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态LLM基准虽灵活但评估成本高，尤其在需精准定位模型弱点而非仅计算平均分时；现有方法难以兼顾效率与可靠性。

Method: 基于贝叶斯优化算法COUP，进行多项实质性修改以适配LLM评估流程，并构建支持自定义数据集和效用函数的工具框架，可定向搜索低准确率或相对复杂度异常高的难题。

Result: 在多个基准上的实验表明，QuickScope比标准基线更样本高效地发现真正困难的问题，同时显著减少因输出噪声导致的假阳性。

Conclusion: QuickScope为动态LLM基准中的难点识别提供了实用、鲁棒且可定制的新范式，有助于更深入理解模型能力边界。

Abstract: LLM benchmarks are increasingly dynamic: instead of containing a fixed set of questions, they define templates and parameters that can generate an effectively unlimited number of question variants. This flexibility is valuable, but it makes evaluation expensive -- especially when the goal is not just determining an average score, but reliably identifying a model's weak spots. This paper introduces a new methodology for identifying hard questions in dynamic benchmarks. It leverages COUP, a recent Bayesian optimization algorithm (Graham, Velez & Leyton-Brown, 2026), after introducing several substantive modifications to make the algorithm suitable for practical LLM pipelines. We also wrap it in a tool that supports flexible choices of datasets and utility functions, enabling users to target the kinds of questions they care about (e.g., low-accuracy questions; questions that are unusually hard relative to their measured complexity). In experiments across a range of benchmarks, we show that our method, dubbed $\texttt{QuickScope}$, discovers truly difficult questions more sample efficiently than standard baselines, while also reducing false positives from noisy outcomes.

</details>


### [136] [On the Emergence of Syntax by Means of Local Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17857)
*Zichao Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅通过局部交互就能自发涌现句法处理能力的神经元细胞自动机（NCA），其在仅受1比特边界信号监督下，成功学习算术表达式文法的成员判定问题，并自组织形成名为Proto-CKY的空间结构表征，具备超越正则语言的表达力、结构泛化能力及与语法结构高度对齐的内部组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究句法处理能否仅从纯粹局部交互中自发涌现。

Method: 使用一个仅含18,658参数的二维神经元细胞自动机（NCA），以1比特边界信号为唯一监督信号，在算术表达式文法的成员判定任务上进行训练；分析其自组织形成的内部空间表征（Proto-CKY）是否满足句法处理的三项操作标准。

Result: NCA自发形成Proto-CKY表征，满足：1）表达力超越正则语言；2）结构泛化能力强于训练分布；3）内部组织与语法结构定量对齐（Pearson r≈0.71）；该现象在四种上下文无关文法上均独立出现，且扰动后可自发再生。

Conclusion: 句法处理能力可在极简、无显式句法归纳偏置的物理系统中自发涌现；Proto-CKY是CKY算法的物理原型，二者差异蕴含底层物理基质的信息。

Abstract: Can syntactic processing emerge spontaneously from purely local interaction? We present a concrete instance on a minimal system: an 18,658-parameter two-dimensional neural cellular automaton (NCA), supervised by nothing more than a 1-bit boundary signal, is trained on the membership problem of an arithmetic-expression grammar. After training, its internal $L \times L$ grid spontaneously self-organizes into an ordered, spatially extended representation that we name Proto-CKY. This representation satisfies three operational criteria for syntactic processing: expressive power beyond the regular languages, structural generalization beyond the training distribution, and an internal organization quantitatively aligned with grammatical structure (Pearson $r \approx 0.71$). It emerges independently on four context-free grammars and regenerates spontaneously after perturbation. Proto-CKY is functionally aligned with the CKY algorithm but formally distinct from it: it is a physical prototype, a concrete instantiation of a mathematical ideal on a physical substrate, and the systematic distance between the two carries information about the substrate itself.

</details>


### [137] [Latent Abstraction for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17866)
*Ha Lan N. T,Minh-Anh Nguyen,Dung D. Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LAnR框架，通过在单一LLM的隐空间内联合完成编码、检索与生成，摒弃传统自然语言查询和分离式检索器，利用[PRED]标记隐状态生成稠密向量进行检索，并基于答案token熵自适应判断检索是否充分，从而提升事实性、准确率与推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统依赖自然语言查询和严格分离的检索器-生成器架构，无法充分利用LLM的表征能力；同时，检索终止判断缺乏统一、轻量的机制。

Method: 提出LAnR（Latent Abstraction for RAG）：用单个LLM在其隐空间中完成全部流程；以[PRED]标记的隐藏状态生成稠密检索向量，匹配同模型编码的文档向量；引入轻量MLP控制头，基于隐藏状态预测检索是否充分（依据答案token熵信号）。

Result: 在六个单跳与多跳QA基准上超越现有RAG方法；减少检索调用次数，提升推理效率；实现更紧耦合的模型集成。

Conclusion: LAnR验证了在LLM隐空间内统一检索与生成的可行性与优势，为下一代RAG范式提供了新思路——摆脱显式文本查询与模块割裂，转向端到端隐式协同。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become a standard approach for enhancing large language models (LLMs) with external knowledge, mitigating hallucinations, and improving factuality. However, existing systems rely on generating natural language queries at each hop and maintaining a strict architectural separation between retriever and generator, preventing them from leveraging the full representational capacity of the LLM. We propose \textbf{LAnR} (Latent Abstraction for RAG), a unified framework in which a single LLM jointly performs encoding, retrieval, and generation entirely within its own latent space. Rather than generating textual queries, LAnR produces dense retrieval vectors from the hidden states of a designated \texttt{[PRED]} token and uses them to match against encoded document representations from the same model. Furthermore, LAnR adaptively decides when sufficient evidence has been retrieved using a lightweight MLP control head over those same hidden states, eliminating both the separate retriever and explicit token-level stopping reasoning. This design is motivated by our empirical observation that answer token entropy reliably signals retrieval sufficiency. Extensive experiments on six QA benchmarks spanning single-hop and multi-hop settings demonstrate that LAnR outperforms existing RAG methods, while achieving improved inference efficiency through reduced number of retrieval calls and tighter model integration.

</details>


### [138] [GraSP: Graph-Structured Skill Compositions for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17870)
*Tianle Xia,Lingxiang Hu,Yiding Sun,Ming Xu,Lan Xu,Siying Wang,Wei Xu,Jie Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GraSP，一种可执行的技能图架构，通过将扁平技能集编译为带前提-效果边的有向无环图（DAG），实现结构化技能编排，显著提升LLM智能体在多任务基准上的性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体技能生态虽丰富，但单纯增加技能数量不再提升性能，甚至有害；瓶颈已从技能供给转向技能编排——亟需支持技能选择、组合与因果执行的结构化机制。

Method: 提出GraSP架构，包含技能图编译层：将技能映射为类型化DAG，边表示前提-效果依赖；引入节点级验证执行机制和五种类型化局部修复算子，将重规划复杂度从O(N)降至O(d^h)。

Result: 在ALFWorld、ScienceWorld、WebShop和InterCode四大基准及8种LLM骨干上，GraSP全面超越ReAct、Reflexion、ExpeL等基线，奖励最高提升+19分，环境步数最多减少41%；优势随任务复杂度上升，并对技能过检索与质量下降鲁棒。

Conclusion: 结构化技能编排（而非扩大技能库）是提升LLM智能体可靠执行能力的关键；GraSP验证了显式因果建模与图结构执行的有效性。

Abstract: Skill ecosystems for LLM agents have matured rapidly, yet recent benchmarks show that providing agents with more skills does not monotonically improve performance -- focused sets of 2-3 skills outperform comprehensive documentation, and excessive skills actually hurt. The bottleneck has shifted from skill availability to skill orchestration: agents need not more skills, but a structural mechanism to select, compose, and execute them with explicit causal dependencies. We propose GraSP, the first executable skill graph architecture that introduces a compilation layer between skill retrieval and execution. GraSP transforms flat skill sets into typed directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) with precondition-effect edges, executes them with node-level verification, and performs locality-bounded repair through five typed operators -- reducing replanning from O(N) to O(d^h). Across ALFWorld, ScienceWorld, WebShop, and InterCode with eight LLM backbones, GraSP outperforms ReAct, Reflexion, ExpeL, and flat skill baselines in every configuration, improving reward by up to +19 points over the strongest baseline while cutting environment steps by up to 41%. GraSP's advantage grows with task complexity and is robust to both skill over-retrieval and quality degradation, confirming that structured orchestration -- not larger skill libraries -- is the key to reliable agent execution.

</details>


### [139] [Latent Preference Modeling for Cross-Session Personalized Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17886)
*Yejin Yoon,Minseo Kim,Taeuk Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper introduces MPT, a benchmark for studying personalized tool calling in LLM-based agents, and proposes PRefine, a memory-augmented method that improves tool-calling accuracy by modeling user preferences as evolving hypotheses via a generate-verify-refine loop.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Users often provide under-specified inputs to LLM-based agents, making tool use challenging due to incomplete API arguments; thus, personalized tool calling is needed.

Method: The authors introduce the MPT benchmark (265 multi-session dialogues) covering Preference Recall, Induction, and Transfer, and propose PRefine—a test-time memory-augmented method using a generate-verify-refine loop to extract and refine reusable constraints from dialogue history.

Result: PRefine improves tool-calling accuracy while using only 1.24% of the tokens required by full-history prompting.

Conclusion: Robust personalization in agentic systems relies on memory that captures the reasons behind user choices—not just the choices themselves.

Abstract: Users often omit essential details in their requests to LLM-based agents, resulting in under-specified inputs for tool use. This poses a fundamental challenge for tool-augmented agents, as API execution typically requires complete arguments, highlighting the need for personalized tool calling. To study this problem, we introduce MPT, a benchmark comprising 265 multi-session dialogues that cover three challenges: Preference Recall, Preference Induction, and Preference Transfer. We also propose PRefine, a test-time memory-augmented method that represents user preferences as evolving hypotheses. Through a generate--verify--refine loop, it extracts reusable constraints from history and improves tool-calling accuracy while using only 1.24% of the tokens required by full-history prompting. These results indicate that robust personalization in agentic systems depends on memory that captures the reasons behind user choices, not just the choices themselves.

</details>


### [140] [Automatic Slide Updating with User-Defined Dynamic Templates and Natural Language Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17894)
*Kun Zhou,Jiakai He,Wenmian Yang,Zhensheng Wang,Yiquan Zhang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DynaSlide基准和SlideAgent框架，旨在通过自然语言指令动态更新用户自定义模板的演示文稿，支持数据驱动报告的自动化维护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化方法多依赖固定模板填充，难以支持用户自定义、多样化的幻灯片动态更新需求，尤其在业务报告场景中更新复杂分析型幻灯片仍高度依赖人工。

Method: 构建大规模真实世界基准DynaSlide（含20,036组源幻灯片-指令-目标幻灯片三元组），并提出SlideAgent代理框架，融合多模态幻灯片解析、自然语言指令语义对齐及面向表格/图表/文本结论的工具增强推理能力。

Result: SlideAgent在DynaSlide上实现了内容准确更新的同时保持原始布局与样式，成为强基线；设计了端到端与组件级评估协议，揭示了关键挑战与研究方向。

Conclusion: Dynamic Slide Update是一项新且具实际价值的任务；DynaSlide与SlideAgent为该方向提供了首个系统性基准与可行技术路径，推动数据驱动幻灯片自动化向BYO-template范式演进。

Abstract: Presentation slides are a primary medium for data-driven reporting, yet keeping complex, analytics-style decks up to date remains labor-intensive. Existing automation methods mostly follow fixed template filling and cannot support dynamic updates for diverse, user-authored slide decks. We therefore define "Dynamic Slide Update via Natural Language Instructions on User-provided Templates" and introduce DynaSlide, a large-scale benchmark with 20,036 real-world instruction-execution triples (source slide, user instruction, target slide) grounded in a shared external database and built from business reporting slides under bring-your-own-template (BYO-template) conditions. To tackle this task, we propose SlideAgent, an agent-based framework that combines multimodal slide parsing, natural language instruction grounding, and tool-augmented reasoning for tables, charts, and textual conclusions. SlideAgent updates content while preserving layout and style, providing a strong reference baseline on DynaSlide. We further design end-to-end and component-level evaluation protocols that reveal key challenges and opportunities for future research. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/XiaoZhou2024/SlideAgent.

</details>


### [141] [Heterogeneity in Formal Linguistic Competence of Language Models: Is Data the Real Bottleneck?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17930)
*H S V N S Kowndinya Renduchintala,Sumit Bhatia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过在预训练数据中注入少量（1%）针对特定语言现象的合成数据，显著提升了小型语言模型（GPT-2 Small）在BLiMP基准中多个低性能语言现象上的表现，表明LLM的语言能力缺陷主要源于训练数据中相关构式稀缺，而非模型架构固有局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在形式语言能力上表现差异的原因：是架构限制还是训练语料中特定语法结构稀缺？

Method: 在FineWeb子集（1亿token）上预训练GPT-2 Small模型，并向其中注入仅占1%的、针对9个BLiMP低性能范式的合成数据，评估干预效果。

Result: 8/9个目标语言现象（如only_npi_scope）准确率大幅提升（例如从20.9%升至69.4%），整体性能基本保持或略有提升；唯principle_A_c_command仍低于随机水平。

Conclusion: LLM在部分语言现象上的低性能主要归因于训练数据中对应构式稀缺，而非模型能力上限；优化数据组成对提升语言建模能力具有关键意义。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit a puzzling disparity in their formal linguistic competence: while they learn some linguistic phenomena with near-perfect mastery, they often perform below chance on others, even after training on trillions of tokens. In this work, we investigate whether these failures stem from inherent architectural limitations or simply the scarcity of these specific grammatical constructions in web-scale corpora. We pre-train simple GPT-2 Small (124M) models on a 100M-token random sample of the FineWeb corpus and intervene by injecting a minimal amount (1%) of synthetic data targeting specific linguistic phenomena. We find that this targeted intervention substantially improves model performance in 8 out of the 9 worst-performing BLiMP paradigms - notably the accuracy on a specific paradigm, only_npi_scope, surges from 20.9% to 69.4%. Furthermore, we observe that these interventions generally preserve or slightly improve aggregate performance. However, while we also identify a resistant phenomenon, principle_A_c_command, whose performance remains below chance even after our data augmentation, our findings do serve as an optimistic existence proof that even small language models can substantially improve on those linguistic phenomena on which models typically perform poorly, provided the pre-training data contains sufficient exposure to them. This suggests that efforts towards human-scale language modeling may benefit greatly by focusing on data composition. The code to reproduce our results is open-sourced at https://github.com/kowndinya-renduchintala/heterogeneity-in-formal-linguistic-competence.

</details>


### [142] [Domain-oriented RAG Assessment (DoRA): Synthetic Benchmarking for RAG-based Question Answering on Defense Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17943)
*Bao Gia Doan,Aditya Joshi,Pantelis Elinas,Aarya Bodhankar,Oscar Leslie,Tom Marchant,Flora Salim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DoRA，一个面向领域的RAG评估基准，基于国防文档构建，包含6.5K个合成、意图驱动的问答实例，用于更真实地评估RAG系统在领域迁移下的性能与事实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放域RAG基准因预训练数据重叠和归因要求弱，易高估实际部署性能。

Method: 构建DoRA基准：基于真实国防文档，设计五类意图驱动的问答任务（find/explain/summarize/generate/provide），配以可审计的证据段落；在固定稠密检索器下进行端到端评估，并对Llama3.1-8B-Instruct开展DoRA监督微调（SFT）。

Result: DoRA SFT模型相比基线模型在QA任务成功率上最高提升26%，RAG忠实性得分中幻觉率降低47%。

Conclusion: DoRA支持污染感知的回归测试，能更可靠地评估RAG系统在领域偏移下的真实性能与事实一致性。

Abstract: Open-domain RAG benchmarks over public corpora can overestimate deployment performance due to pretraining overlap and weak attribution requirements. We present DoRA (Domain-oriented RAG Assessment), a domain-grounded benchmark built from defense documents that pairs synthetic, intent-conditioned QA (question answering) with auditable evidence passages for attribution. DoRA covers five question types (find, explain, summarize, generate, provide) and contains 6.5K curated instances. In end-to-end evaluation with a fixed dense retriever, general-purpose Language Models (LMs) perform similarly, while a model trained on DoRA (DoRA SFT) yields large gains over the base model (Llama3.1-8B-Instruct): up to 26% improvement in QA task success, while reducing the hallucination rate by 47% in RAG faithfulness scores, supporting contamination-aware regression testing under domain shift.

</details>


### [143] [ReCoQA: A Benchmark for Tool-Augmented and Multi-Step Reasoning in Real Estate Question and Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17944)
*Yindong Zhang,Wenmian Yang,Yiquan Zhang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了ReCoQA基准数据集和HIRE-Agent分层框架，旨在解决多源信息导航难题，通过真实房地产场景验证了分层协作对复杂推理任务的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准难以反映结合数据库查询与外部API调用的混合工作流，导致开发能处理碎片化、多源信息的智能体面临挑战。

Method: 构建包含29,270个实例的ReCoQA基准，提供可机器验证的中间步骤监督（如意图标签、SQL查询、API调用）；提出HIRE-Agent分层框架，采用理解-规划-执行架构，由前端解析器、规划主管和执行专家协同工作。

Result: 实验表明HIRE-Agent是强基线，证实了分层协作对复杂现实世界推理任务的必要性。

Conclusion: 分层协作架构对于处理需融合异构证据的复杂推理任务至关重要，ReCoQA为该方向提供了可靠评估基准。

Abstract: Developing agents capable of navigating fragmented, multi-source information remains challenging, primarily due to the scarcity of benchmarks reflecting hybrid workflows combining database querying with external APIs. To bridge this gap, we introduce ReCoQA, a large-scale benchmark of 29,270 real-estate instances featuring machine-verifiable supervision for intermediate steps, including structured intent labels, SQL queries, and API calls. Complementarily, we propose HIRE-Agent, a hierarchical framework instantiating an understand-plan-execute architecture as a strong baseline. By orchestrating a Front-end parser, a planning Supervisor, and execution Specialists, HIRE-Agent effectively integrates heterogeneous evidence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HIRE-Agent constitutes a strong baseline and substantiates the necessity of hierarchical collaboration for complex, real-world reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [144] [Process Reward Models Meet Planning: Generating Precise and Scalable Datasets for Step-Level Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17957)
*Raffaele Pisano,Roberto Navigli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于PDDL规划问题的可扩展PRM数据集生成方法，显著提升了PRM在数学与非数学推理任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PRM数据集构建成本高、易出错，且主要局限于数学领域，缺乏可扩展性和泛化性。

Method: 提出基于PDDL逻辑规划问题生成PRM训练数据的新方法，并构建了包含约百万推理步骤的语料库用于训练PRM。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，加入PDDL数据后，PRM在数学与非数学推理任务上的性能均显著提升。

Conclusion: PDDL规划问题是一种可扩展、鲁棒且细粒度的PRM训练数据来源，能有效弥补当前以数学为主的PRM数据局限。

Abstract: Process Reward Models (PRMs) have emerged as a powerful tool for providing step-level feedback when evaluating the reasoning of Large Language Models (LLMs), which frequently produce chains of thought (CoTs) containing errors even when the final answer is correct. However, existing PRM datasets remain expensive to construct, prone to annotation errors, and predominantly limited to the mathematical domain. This work introduces a novel and scalable approach to PRM dataset generation based on planning logical problems expressed in the Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL). Using this method, we generate a corpus of approximately one million reasoning steps across various PDDL domains and use it to train PRMs. Experimental results show that augmenting widely-used PRM training datasets with PDDL-derived data yields substantial improvements in both mathematical and non-mathematical reasoning, as demonstrated across multiple benchmarks. These findings indicate that planning problems constitute a scalable and effective resource for generating robust, precise, and fine-grained training data for PRMs, going beyond the classical mathematical sources that dominate this field.

</details>


### [145] [Modeling Multiple Support Strategies within a Single Turn for Emotional Support Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17972)
*Jie Zhu,Huaixia Dou,Junhui Li,Lifan Guo,Feng Chen,Jinsong Su,Chi Zhang,Fang Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出多策略话语生成方法，用于情感支持对话（ESC），允许单个话语中包含多个支持策略，并通过强化学习引导的认知推理提升策略选择和响应生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的情感支持对话常在一个话语中使用多种策略，而先前工作通常假设每个话语只对应一种策略，因此需要重新审视ESC任务。

Method: 提出两种生成方法：All-in-One（单步解码预测所有策略-响应对）和One-by-One（迭代生成策略-响应对），并结合强化学习引导的认知推理优化策略选择与响应构成。

Result: 在ESConv数据集上，所提方法在话语级和对话级设置下均显著提升支持质量和对话成功率；首次提供实证证据证明单话语多策略是可行且有益的。

Conclusion: 允许多策略共存于单个话语中能更真实地建模情感支持对话，所提方法有效提升了ESC系统的实用性与表现。

Abstract: Emotional Support Conversation (ESC) aims to assist individuals experiencing distress by generating empathetic and supportive dialogue. While prior work typically assumes that each supporter turn corresponds to a single strategy, real-world supportive communication often involves multiple strategies within a single utterance. In this paper, we revisit the ESC task by formulating it as multi-strategy utterance generation, where each utterance may contain one or more strategy-response pairs. We propose two generation methods: All-in-One, which predicts all strategy-response pairs in a single decoding step, and One-by-One, which iteratively generates strategy-response pairs until completion. Both methods are further enhanced with cognitive reasoning guided by reinforcement learning to improve strategy selection and response composition. We evaluate our models on the ESConv dataset under both utterance-level and dialogue-level settings. Experimental results show that our methods effectively model multi-strategy utterances and lead to improved supportive quality and dialogue success. To our knowledge, this work provides the first systematic empirical evidence that allowing multiple support strategies within a single utterance is both feasible and beneficial for emotional support conversations. All code and data will be publicly available at https://github.com/aliyun/qwen-dianjin.

</details>


### [146] [ltzGLUE: Luxembourgish General Language Understanding Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17976)
*Alistair Plum,Felicia Körner,Anne-Marie Lutgen,Laura Bernardy,Fred Philippy,Emilia Milano,Nils Rehlinger,Cédric Lothritz,Tharindu Ranasinghe,Barbara Plank,Christoph Purschke*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper introduces ltzGLUE, the first NLU benchmark for Luxembourgish, adapting the English GLUE framework; it includes new and reused NLU tasks (e.g., NER, topic/intent classification) and evaluates pre-trained LTZ language models.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Luxembourgish (LTZ) is an official European language often overlooked in NLU research despite growing availability of NLU resources for other European languages; there is a need for a standardized benchmark to assess and advance LTZ language understanding.

Method: The authors construct ltzGLUE by designing new LTZ-specific NLU tasks and adapting existing ones, covering binary/multi-class classification (e.g., named entity recognition, topic classification, intent classification); they evaluate multiple pre-trained LTZ language models on this benchmark.

Result: ltzGLUE is established as the first official NLU benchmark for Luxembourgish; empirical evaluation reveals the current performance landscape of pre-trained LTZ models across diverse NLU tasks.

Conclusion: ltzGLUE fills a critical gap in multilingual NLU by providing a standardized evaluation framework for Luxembourgish, enabling future development and comparison of LTZ language models.

Abstract: This paper presents ltzGLUE, the first Natural Language Understanding (NLU) benchmark for Luxembourgish (LTZ) based on the popular GLUE benchmark for English. Although NLU tasks are available for many European languages nowadays, LTZ is one of the official national languages that is often overlooked. We construct new tasks and reuse existing ones to introduce the first official NLU benchmark and accompanying evaluation of encoder models for the language. Our tasks include common natural language processing tasks in binary and multi-class classification settings, including named entity recognition, topic classification, and intent classification. We evaluate various pre-trained language models for LTZ to present an overview of the current capabilities of these models on the LTZ language.

</details>


### [147] [Employing General-Purpose and Biomedical Large Language Models with Advanced Prompt Engineering for Pharmacoepidemiologic Study Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17988)
*Xinyao Zhang,Nicole Sonne Heckmann,Manuela Del Castillo Suero,Francesco Paolo Speca,Maurizio Sessa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究比较了通用大语言模型（如GPT-4o、DeepSeek-R1）与生物医学微调模型在药物流行病学研究设计支持任务中的表现，发现通用模型结合Least-to-Most提示策略效果更优，尤其在相关性和推理逻辑方面；但所有模型在本体编码映射任务上均表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估大语言模型在药物流行病学研究设计中的可靠性，特别是通用模型与生物医学专用模型的性能差异尚不明确。

Method: 使用46份来自HMA-EMA和Sentinel系统的协议，对比GPT-4o、DeepSeek-R1及两个生物医学微调模型（Bio-Medical-Llama-3-8B、qwen2-1.5B-medical_qa），采用Least-to-Most（LTM）和主动提示策略，在相关性、论证逻辑性、多编码系统本体映射三方面进行评估。

Result: GPT-4o-LTM在HMA-EMA协议中8/9问题达到中位相关性得分4；通用模型在相关性和逻辑论证上显著优于生物医学微调模型；所有模型在本体编码映射任务上能力有限，LTM策略最能提升推理稳定性。

Conclusion: 当前开箱即用的通用大语言模型在支持药物流行病学研究设计方面优于生物医学微调模型，提示工程（尤其是LTM）对性能影响显著。

Abstract: Background: The potential of large language models (LLMs) to automate and support pharmacoepidemiologic study design is an emerging area of interest, yet their reliability remains insufficiently characterized. General-purpose LLMs often display inaccuracies, while the comparative performance of specialized biomedical LLMs in this domain remains unknown. Methods: This study evaluated general-purpose LLMs (GPT-4o and DeepSeek-R1) versus biomedically fine-tuned LLMs (QuantFactory/Bio-Medical-Llama-3-8B-GGUF and Irathernotsay/qwen2-1.5B-medical_qa-Finetune) using 46 protocols (2018-2024) from the HMA-EMA Catalogue and Sentinel System. Performance was assessed across relevance, logic of justification, and ontology-code agreement across multiple coding systems using Least-to-Most (LTM) and Active Prompting strategies. Results: GPT-4o and DeepSeek-R1 paired with LTM prompting achieved the highest relevance and logic of justification scores, with GPT-4o-LTM reaching a median relevance score of 4 in 8 of 9 questions for HMA-EMA protocols. Biomedical LLMs showed lower relevance overall and frequently generated insufficient justification. All LLMs demonstrated limited proficiency in ontology-code mapping, although LTM provided the most consistent improvements in reasoning stability. Conclusion: Off-the-shelf general-purpose LLMs currently offer superior support for pharmacoepidemiologic design compared to biomedical LLMs. Prompt strategy strongly influenced LLM performance.

</details>


### [148] [How Creative Are Large Language Models in Generating Molecules?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18031)
*Wen Tao,Yiwei Wang,Peng Zhou,Bryan Hooi,Wanlong Fang,Tianle Zhang,Xiao Luo,Yuansheng Liu,Alvin Chan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次将分子生成所需的能力重新定义为‘创造力’，并系统评估了大语言模型（LLM）在该任务中的创造性行为，区分了聚合型与发散型两种创造力，并分析其在理化性质、ADMET及生物活性等多约束任务中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子生成本质上是需满足多重化学与生物学约束的非二元问题，要求模型具备识别非显而易见解、避免局部最优的能力——即功能性创造力；而当前尚不清楚LLM在此场景中展现何种创造力及如何评估。

Method: 通过在理化性质、ADMET和生物活性任务上开展系统性实证评估，从聚合型创造力（convergent）和发散型创造力（divergent）两个互补维度刻画LLM的创造性行为，并分析不同因素对其影响。

Result: LLM在分子生成中展现出独特创造性模式，例如在增加约束条件下约束满足率反而提升；研究首次确立了创造力作为分子生成的核心功能属性。

Conclusion: 创造力是分子生成的内在功能需求而非美学概念；本工作为理解LLM在分子发现中的适用边界与作用机制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Molecule generation requires satisfying multiple chemical and biological constraints while searching a large and structured chemical space. This makes it a non-binary problem, where effective models must identify non-obvious solutions under constraints while maintaining exploration to improve success by escaping local optima. From this perspective, creativity is a functional requirement in molecular generation rather than an aesthetic notion. Large language models (LLMs) can generate molecular representations directly from natural language prompts, but it remains unclear what type of creativity they exhibit in this setting and how it should be evaluated. In this work, we study the creative behavior of LLMs in molecular generation through a systematic empirical evaluation across physicochemical, ADMET, and biological activity tasks. We characterize creativity along two complementary dimensions, convergent creativity and divergent creativity, and analyze how different factors shape these behaviors. Our results indicate that LLMs exhibit distinct patterns of creative behavior in molecule generation, such as an increase in constraint satisfaction when additional constraints are imposed. Overall, our work is the first to reframe the abilities required for molecule generation as creativity, providing a systematic understanding of creativity in LLM-based molecular generation and clarifying the appropriate use of LLMs in molecular discovery pipelines.

</details>


### [149] [SignDPO: Multi-level Direct Preference Optimisation for Skeleton-based Gloss-free Sign Language Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18034)
*Muxin Pu,Xiao-Ming Wu,Mei Kuan Lim,Chun Yong Chong,Wei Li,Chen Change Loy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SignDPO是一种新型多级直接偏好优化（DPO）框架，用于提升基于骨架的手语翻译模型在空间、时间和语言维度上的对齐能力，通过分层扰动、自引导机制和自动语言级偏好生成，在多个基准上超越现有无词表方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于骨架的手语翻译模型依赖最大似然估计，受限于模仿式范式，缺乏对细粒度时空特征的判别敏感性，易导致语义漂移。

Method: 提出三方面设计：1）分层扰动策略构建空间与时间维度的非偏好样本；2）基于解码器交叉注意力的自引导机制定位并扰动语义关键骨骼区域；3）微调专用扰动模型实现无需人工标注的语言级偏好自动生成。

Result: 在CSL-Daily、How2Sign和OpenASL三个基准上显著优于现有无词表方法，性能媲美主流有词表方法。

Conclusion: 多级偏好对齐是弥合高熵骨架轨迹与离散语言语义之间鸿沟的有效范式。

Abstract: We present SignDPO, a novel multi-level Direct Preference Optimisation (DPO) framework designed to enhance the alignment of skeleton-based Sign Language Translation. While current skeleton-based models have made significant progress using Maximum Likelihood Estimation, they are primarily constrained by an imitation-based paradigm that lacks discriminative sensitivity to the fine-grained spatio-temporal nuances of sign language, often leading to semantic drift. To address this, SignDPO shifts the optimisation goal from simple sequence mimicry to structured preference alignment across spatial, temporal, and linguistic dimensions. Our framework involves three key designs. First, we introduce a hierarchical perturbation strategy to construct spatial and temporal non-preferred samples at both global and local granularities automatically. Second, we propose a self-guiding mechanism that leverages decoder cross-attention scores to identify and perturb semantically salient skeletal regions, forcing the model to distinguish genuine sign signals from structural distortions. Third, we establish an automated language-level preference generator by fine-tuning a dedicated perturbation model, capturing complex output-level failure modes without manual annotation. Extensive experiments on three widely adopted benchmarks, CSL-Daily, How2Sign, and OpenASL, demonstrate that SignDPO consistently outperforms state-of-the-art gloss-free methods and even rivals established gloss-based ones. Our results suggest that multi-level preference alignment is a powerful paradigm for bridging the gap between high-entropy skeletal trajectories and discrete linguistic semantics.

</details>


### [150] [JudgeMeNot: Personalizing Large Language Models to Emulate Judicial Reasoning in Hebrew](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18041)
*Itay Razumenko,Arnon Sturm,Nir Grinberg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种合成-有机监督流水线，将原始司法判决转化为指令微调数据，以实现对个体法官的高效个性化大语言模型微调，尤其适用于低资源场景。实验表明，该方法在词汇、风格和语义相似性上均显著优于现有基线，且生成结果与真实法官推理难以区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型取得显著进展，但如何为个体决策者（如法官）进行个性化仍是一个未解难题，尤其在低资源设置下。

Method: 提出合成-有机监督流水线，将原始司法判决转化为指令微调数据，并采用因果语言建模结合合成指令微调进行参数高效微调。

Result: 在三个不同任务和设置中，该方法显著优于所有基线，在词汇、风格和语义相似性指标上均有提升；模型输出与人类法官推理高度一致，难以区分。

Conclusion: 该方法验证了在低资源环境下对个体法官进行高效、可行的个性化建模的潜力。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in large language models, personalizing them for individual decision-makers remains an open problem. Here, we introduce a synthetic-organic supervision pipeline that transforms raw judicial decisions into instruction-tuning data, enabling parameter-efficient fine-tuning of personalized models for individual judges in low-resource settings. We compare our approach to state-of-the-art personalization techniques across three different tasks and settings. The results show that Causal Language Modeling followed by synthetically generated instruction-tuning significantly outperforms all other baselines, providing significant improvements across lexical, stylistic, and semantic similarity. Notably, our model-generated outputs are indistinguishable from the reasoning of human judges, highlighting the viability of efficient personalization, even in low-resource settings.

</details>


### [151] [Modeling Human Perspectives with Socio-Demographic Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18069)
*Leixin Zhang,Cagri Coltekin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Socio-Contrastive Learning的方法，用于联合建模标注者视角并学习社会人口学表征，以更好地解释人类主观性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作通常只关注单一或有限组合的社会人口学因素，而现实中标注者的视角受复杂社会背景影响，需更细粒度的属性来建模。

Method: 提出Socio-Contrastive Learning，融合社会人口学特征与文本表征，通过对比学习联合建模标注者视角。

Result: 该方法在预测标注者视角上优于标准拼接方法，并支持对社会人口学因素与视角差异关系的分析与可视化。

Conclusion: 细粒度社会人口学信息能有效提升对主观标注差异的建模能力，所提方法为理解人类视角多样性提供了新途径。

Abstract: Humans often hold different perspectives on the same issues. In many NLP tasks, annotation disagreement can reflect valid subjective perspectives. Modeling annotator perspectives and understanding their relationship with other human factors, such as socio-demographic attributes, have received increasing attention. Prior work typically focuses on single demographic factors or limited combinations. However, in real-world settings, annotator perspectives are shaped by complex social contexts, and finer-grained socio-demographic attributes can better explain human perspectives. In this work, we propose Socio-Contrastive Learning, a method that jointly models annotator perspectives while learning socio-demographic representations. Our method provides an effective approach for the fusion of socio-demographic features and textual representations to predict annotator perspectives, outperforming standard concatenation-based methods. The learned representations further enable analysis and visualization of how demographic factors relate to variation in annotator perspectives. Our code is available at GitHub: https://github.com/Leixin-Zhang/Socio_Contrastive_Learning

</details>


### [152] [Mix and Match: Context Pairing for Scalable Topic-Controlled Educational Summarisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18087)
*Nathikan Yodthapa,Thanapong Intharah,Sahan Bulathwela*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对小语言模型（sLMs）的主题控制式摘要生成的数据增强方法，通过跨文档上下文配对构建对比样本，提升模型对主题与摘要间关系的学习能力，并在SciTLDR数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升小语言模型在主题控制式摘要任务上的性能，缓解其因参数量和训练数据受限而导致的表现不足问题。

Method: 提出一种成对数据增强方法，将不同文档的上下文组合形成对比训练样本；在SciTLDR数据集基础上融合维基百科衍生的主题信息进行实验评估。

Result: 随着增强规模增大，模型在胜率和语义对齐度上持续提升，且真实训练数据量保持不变；T5-base模型借助该方法达到与更大模型相当的性能，但参数量和真实样本数显著减少。

Conclusion: 所提 pairwise 数据增强策略能有效提升小语言模型在主题控制摘要任务中的表现，为资源受限场景下的可控文本生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Topic-controlled summarisation enables users to generate summaries focused on specific aspects of source documents. This paper investigates a data augmentation strategy for training small language models (sLMs) to perform topic-controlled summarisation. We propose a pairwise data augmentation method that combines contexts from different documents to create contrastive training examples, enabling models to learn the relationship between topics and summaries more effectively. Using the SciTLDR dataset enriched with Wikipedia-derived topics, we systematically evaluate how augmentation scale affects model performance. Results show consistent improvements in win rate and semantic alignment as the augmentation scale increases, while the amount of real training data remains fixed. Consequently, a T5-base model trained with our augmentation approach achieves competitive performance relative to larger models, despite using significantly fewer parameters and substantially fewer real training examples.

</details>


### [153] [Culture-Aware Humorous Captioning: Multimodal Humor Generation across Cultural Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18091)
*Run Xu,Lu Li,Rongzhao Zhang,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出文化感知幽默图像描述生成任务，通过多阶段对齐框架（包括西方高资源初始化、基于裁判的GRPO多维偏好对齐与东方小样本适配）提升模型在指定文化背景下的图像相关性、语境适配性与幽默质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图文大模型虽能生成幽默描述，但难以稳定控制显式文化语境，导致图像相关性、语境恰当性与幽默质量难以兼顾。

Method: 提出文化感知幽默图像描述生成新任务；构建六维评估框架（图像相关性、语境适配性、语义丰富性、合理性、幽默性、创造性）；设计分阶段对齐框架：1）西方文化高资源监督初始化；2）基于裁判的GRPO多维偏好对齐，引入降级感知原型排斥约束以抑制奖励黑客；3）东方文化小样本微调。

Result: 在六维评估框架下整体性能更强，尤其显著提升语境适配性，并更好平衡图像相关性与文化约束下的幽默质量。

Conclusion: 分阶段对齐策略能有效提升模型在多元文化背景下的可控幽默生成能力，为跨文化多模态生成提供新范式。

Abstract: Recent multimodal large language models have shown promising ability in generating humorous captions for images, yet they still lack stable control over explicit cultural context, making it difficult to jointly maintain image relevance, contextual appropriateness, and humor quality under a specified cultural background. To address this limitation, we introduce a new multimodal generation task, culture-aware humorous captioning, which requires a model to generate a humorous caption conditioned on both an input image and a target cultural context. Captions generated under different cultural contexts are not expected to share the same surface form, but should remain grounded in similar visual situations or humorous rationales.To support this task, we establish a six-dimensional evaluation framework covering image relevance, contextual fit, semantic richness, reasonableness, humor, and creativity. We further propose a staged alignment framework that first initializes the model with high-resource supervision under the Western cultural context, then performs multi-dimensional preference alignment via judge-based GRPO with a Degradation-aware Prototype Repulsion Constraint to mitigate reward hacking in open-ended generation, and finally adapts the model to the Eastern cultural context with a small amount of supervision. Experimental results show that our method achieves stronger overall performance under the proposed evaluation framework, with particularly large gains in contextual fit and a better balance between image relevance and humor under cultural constraints.

</details>


### [154] [Efficient Low-Resource Language Adaptation via Multi-Source Dynamic Logit Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18106)
*Chen Zhang,Jiuheng Lin,Zhiyuan Liao,Yansong Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TriMix框架，在测试时融合三个不同来源的logits：小模型的低资源语言能力、高资源语言指令调优的任务能力，以及大模型的扩展优势，无需低资源语言任务标注，仅需小模型持续预训练，显著优于单模型基线和Proxy Tuning。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言（LRLs）中大语言模型适配受限于任务数据和计算资源稀缺；Proxy Tuning在LRL场景下常因大模型LRL能力弱而失效。

Method: 提出TriMix——一种测试时logit融合框架，动态平衡小模型（持续预训练获得LRL能力）、高资源语言指令调优模型（任务能力）和大模型（缩放效益）三者的logits；无需LRL任务标注，仅依赖小模型持续预训练。

Result: 在四个模型家族和八种低资源语言上的实验表明，TriMix持续优于单模型基线和Proxy Tuning；分析发现优先采用小LRL专用模型的logits是关键，挑战了‘大模型主导’的常规假设。

Conclusion: TriMix是一种高效、轻量且有效的低资源语言适配方法，强调小模型LRL能力的核心作用，为资源受限场景下的LLM适配提供了新范式。

Abstract: Adapting large language models (LLMs) to low-resource languages (LRLs) is constrained by the scarcity of task data and computational resources. Although Proxy Tuning offers a logit-level strategy for introducing scaling effects, it often fails in LRL settings because the large model's weak LRL competence might overwhelm the knowledge of specialized smaller models. We thus propose TriMix, a test-time logit fusion framework that dynamically balances capabilities from three different sources: LRL competence from a continually pretrained small model, task competence from high-resource language instruction tuning, and the scaling benefits of large models. It is data- and compute-efficient, requiring no LRL task annotations, and only continual pretraining on a small model. Experiments across four model families and eight LRLs show that TriMix consistently outperforms single-model baselines and Proxy Tuning. Our analysis reveals that prioritizing the small LRL-specialized model's logits is crucial for success, challenging the prevalent large-model-dominant assumption.

</details>


### [155] [FLiP: Towards understanding and interpreting multimodal multilingual sentence embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18109)
*Santosh Kesiraju,Bolaji Yusuf,Šimon Sedláček,Oldřich Plchot,Petr Schwarz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出因子化线性投影（FLiP）模型，用于解析预训练句子嵌入空间的词汇内容，在多语言、多模态和API驱动的嵌入空间中实现超75%的词汇召回率，并揭示编码器的模态与语言偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为深入理解预训练句子嵌入空间的内在结构，尤其是其承载的词汇信息，以及揭示不同编码器在模态和语言上的隐含偏差。

Method: 提出因子化线性投影（FLiP）模型，训练其从LaBSE（多语言）、SONAR（多模态）和Gemini（API-based）等句子嵌入空间中恢复原始词汇内容，并在多种高/中资源语言上进行评估。

Result: FLiP在多个嵌入空间中可恢复超过75%的词汇内容，显著优于非因子化的基线方法；并作为诊断工具，成功揭示了各句子编码器的模态偏好与语言偏差。

Conclusion: FLiP是一种有效的内在可解释性工具，无需下游任务即可提供关于句子编码器表示能力的实质性洞见，有助于指导模型选择与改进。

Abstract: This paper presents factorized linear projection (FLiP) models for understanding pretrained sentence embedding spaces. We train FLiP models to recover the lexical content from multilingual (LaBSE), multimodal (SONAR) and API-based (Gemini) sentence embedding spaces in several high- and mid-resource languages. We show that FLiP can recall more than 75% of lexical content from the embeddings, significantly outperforming existing non-factorized baselines. Using this as a diagnostic tool, we uncover the modality and language biases across the selected sentence encoders and provide practitioners with intrinsic insights about the encoders without relying on conventional downstream evaluation tasks. Our implementation is public https://github.com/BUTSpeechFIT/FLiP.

</details>


### [156] [Retrieval-Augmented Multimodal Model for Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18112)
*Yiheng Li,Weihai Lu,Hanyi Yu,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RAMM模型，通过多模态大语言模型、抽象叙事对齐模块和语义表征对齐模块，解决跨实例叙事一致性建模与领域知识缺乏两大挑战，提升多模态多领域假新闻检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以捕捉跨新闻样本的叙事一致性，且缺乏面向新领域或数据稀缺领域的推理知识。

Method: 提出检索增强的多模态假新闻检测模型RAMM，包含多模态大语言模型主干、抽象叙事对齐模块（提取跨域抽象叙事一致性）和语义表征对齐模块（实现类人实例类比推理）。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上验证了RAMM的有效性，显著提升了多模态多领域假新闻检测性能。

Conclusion: RAMM通过引入外部叙事知识与类比推理机制，有效缓解了跨实例一致性建模与跨领域泛化难题，为多模态假新闻检测提供了新思路。

Abstract: In recent years, multimodal multidomain fake news detection has garnered increasing attention. Nevertheless, this direction presents two significant challenges: (1) Failure to Capture Cross-Instance Narrative Consistency: existing models usually evaluate each news in isolation, fail to capture cross-instance narrative consistency, and thus struggle to address the spread of cluster based fake news driven by social media; (2) Lack of Domain Specific Knowledge for Reasoning: conventional models, which rely solely on knowledge encoded in their parameters during training, struggle to generalize to new or data-scarce domains (e.g., emerging events or niche topics). To tackle these challenges, we introduce Retrieval-Augmented Multimodal Model for Fake News Detection (RAMM). First, RAMM employs a Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) as its backbone to capture cross-modal semantic information from news samples. Second, RAMM incorporates an Abstract Narrative Alignment Module. This component adaptively extracts abstract narrative consistency from diverse instances across distinct domains, aggregates relevant knowledge, and thereby enables the modeling of high-level narrative information. Finally, RAMM introduces a Semantic Representation Alignment Module, which aligns the model's decision-making paradigm with that of humans - specifically, it shifts the model's reasoning process from direct inference on multimodal features to an instance-based analogical reasoning process. Extensive experimental results on three public datasets validate the efficacy of our proposed approach. Our code is available at the following link: https://github.com/li-yiheng/RAMM

</details>


### [157] [Decisive: Guiding User Decisions with Optimal Preference Elicitation from Unstructured Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18122)
*Akriti Jain,Anish Mulay,Divyansh Verma,Aishani Pandey,Pritika Ramu,Aparna Garimella*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Decisive框架，结合文档驱动推理与贝叶斯偏好推断，通过自适应成对权衡提问学习用户隐式偏好，在减少用户负担的同时提升决策准确性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有决策方法（如大语言模型和传统决策支持系统）要么信息过载，要么难以准确捕捉用户细微偏好，亟需更高效、个性化且透明的交互式决策支持方案。

Method: 提出Decisive框架：1）从源文档中提取客观选项评分矩阵；2）通过自适应选择的成对权衡问题，利用贝叶斯方法动态推断用户潜在偏好向量；3）以信息增益最大化为目标进行交互式偏好 elicitation。

Result: 在多个领域实验表明，Decisive显著优于通用大语言模型及现有决策框架，决策准确率最高提升20%。

Conclusion: Decisive实现了低用户负担、高准确性、强透明性与个性化推荐的统一，为交互式智能决策提供了新范式。

Abstract: Decision-making is a cognitively intensive task that requires synthesizing relevant information from multiple unstructured sources, weighing competing factors, and incorporating subjective user preferences. Existing methods, including large language models and traditional decision-support systems, fall short: they often overwhelm users with information or fail to capture nuanced preferences accurately. We present Decisive, an interactive decision-making framework that combines document-grounded reasoning with Bayesian preference inference. Our approach grounds decisions in an objective option-scoring matrix extracted from source documents, while actively learning a user's latent preference vector through targeted elicitation. Users answer pairwise tradeoff questions adaptively selected to maximize information gain over the final decision. This process converges efficiently, minimizing user effort while ensuring recommendations remain transparent and personalized. Through extensive experiments, we demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms both general-purpose LLMs and existing decision-making frameworks achieving up to 20% improvement in decision accuracy over strong baselines across domains.

</details>


### [158] [TLoRA: Task-aware Low Rank Adaptation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18124)
*Weicheng Lin,Yi Zhang,Jiawei Dang,Liang-Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TLoRA，一种任务感知的低秩自适应框架，通过数据驱动初始化和敏感性驱动的资源分配，在保持高性能的同时显著减少可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA变体通常只优化初始化、秩分配或缩放因子中的一个因素，且常以增加训练复杂度或降低效率为代价。

Method: TLoRA联合优化初始化与资源分配：首先对预训练权重与输入激活协方差的乘积做SVD，用其主导子空间初始化LoRA的A矩阵并冻结；仅训练B矩阵；再基于层敏感性指标在固定参数预算下自适应分配各层的秩和缩放因子。

Result: 在自然语言理解、常识推理、数学推理、代码生成和对话生成等多类任务上，TLoRA持续取得优异性能，同时大幅减少可训练参数量。

Conclusion: TLoRA提供了一种统一、高效且任务感知的LoRA优化范式，兼顾性能、参数效率与训练简洁性。
