### [196] [Positioning radiata pine branches requiring pruning by drone stereo vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16480)
*Yida Lin,Bing Xue,Mengjie Zhang,Sam Schofield,Richard Green*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于立体视觉的无人机系统，用于检测和定位辐射松枝条以支持自主修剪，通过比较多种分割与深度估计方法，在自建数据集上验证了深度学习方法在低代价立体视觉中进行林木枝条定位的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持无人机在林业中实现自主修剪，需要一种低成本、高精度的枝条检测与定位方案。

Method: 采用两阶段流程：1）使用YOLOv8、YOLOv9和Mask R-CNN在自建71对ZED Mini立体图像数据集上进行枝条分割；2）对比传统SGBM（含WLS滤波）与多种深度学习立体匹配方法（PSMNet、ACVNet等），并提出基于质心的三角测量与MAD离群值剔除算法计算枝条距离。

Result: 在1–2米距离的定性评估中，深度学习生成的视差图比SGBM产生更一致、更可靠的深度估计。

Conclusion: 深度学习驱动的立体视觉方案在低成本硬件上具备可行性和优势，适用于林木枝条的自动定位任务。

Abstract: This paper presents a stereo-vision-based system mounted on a drone for detecting and localising radiata pine branches to support autonomous pruning. The proposed pipeline comprises two stages: branch segmentation and depth estimation. For segmentation, YOLOv8, YOLOv9, and Mask R-CNN variants are compared on a custom dataset of 71 stereo image pairs captured with a ZED Mini camera. For depth estimation, both a traditional method (SGBM with WLS filtering) and deep-learning-based methods (PSMNet, ACVNet, GWCNet, MobileStereoNet, RAFT-Stereo, and NeRF-Supervised Deep Stereo) are evaluated. A centroid-based triangulation algorithm with MAD outlier rejection is proposed to compute branch distance from the segmentation mask and disparity map. Qualitative evaluation at distances of 1-2 m indicates that the deep learning-based disparity maps produce more coherent depth estimates than SGBM, demonstrating the feasibility of low-cost stereo vision for automated branch positioning in forestry.

</details>


### [197] [Erasing Thousands of Concepts: Towards Scalable and Practical Concept Erasure for Text-to-Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16481)
*Hoigi Seo,Byung Hyun Lee,Jaehyun Cho,Sungjin Lim,Se Young Chun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ETC框架，通过tMM建模低秩概念分布，并结合MoEraser模块实现对数千个概念的精准、可扩展且鲁棒的概念擦除，显著提升大规模文本到图像扩散模型的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型文本到图像扩散模型存在生成不良内容（如版权内容）的安全风险，而现有概念擦除方法在可扩展性、精度和鲁棒性上难以兼顾，仅能处理数百个概念。

Method: 提出ETC框架：1）用Student's t-分布混合模型（tMM）建模低秩概念分布；2）通过仿射最优传输实现目标概念的精确定位擦除，并以分布边界为锚点保留非目标概念；3）设计MoE结构的MoEraser模块擦除目标嵌入、保留锚嵌入；4）在文本嵌入投影器中注入噪声并微调MoEraser以增强对白盒攻击（如模块移除）的鲁棒性。

Result: 在2000多个跨领域概念及多种扩散模型上实验表明，ETC在大规模概念擦除任务中达到当前最优的可扩展性和精度，并具备强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: ETC是一种高效、可扩展且鲁棒的大规模概念擦除新范式，为提升T2I模型安全性提供了实用可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models deliver remarkable visual fidelity but pose safety risks due to their capacity to reproduce undesirable content, such as copyrighted ones. Concept erasure has emerged as a mitigation strategy, yet existing approaches struggle to balance scalability, precision, and robustness, which restricts their applicability to erasing only a few hundred concepts. To address these limitations, we present Erasing Thousands of Concepts (ETC), a scalable framework capable of erasing thousands of concepts while preserving generation quality. Our method first models low-rank concept distributions via a Student's t-distribution Mixture Model (tMM). It enables pin-point erasure of target concepts via affine optimal transport while preserving others by anchoring the boundaries of target concept distributions without pre-defined anchor concepts. We then train a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE)-based module, termed MoEraser, which removes target embeddings while preserving the anchor embeddings. By injecting noise into the text embedding projector and fine-tuning MoEraser for recovery, our framework achieves robustness to white-box attack such as module removal. Extensive experiments on over 2,000 concepts across heterogeneous domains and diffusion models demerate state-of-the-art scalability and precision in large-scale concept erasure.

</details>


### [198] [A Survey of Spatial Memory Representations for Efficient Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16482)
*Ma. Madecheen S. Pangaliman,Steven S. Sison,Erwin P. Quilloy,Rowel Atienza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文调查了视觉机器人在大环境中导航时空间记忆效率问题，提出α比率（峰值运行内存与保存地图大小之比）来揭示实际部署成本与论文中报告的地图大小之间的差距，并提出了标准化评估协议和α感知预算算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着视觉机器人在更大环境中导航，其空间记忆不断增长，最终耗尽计算资源，特别是在嵌入式平台上（8-16GB共享内存，<30W），无法通过增加硬件解决该问题。

Method: 对1989–2025年间88篇文献、52个系统进行综述；引入α比率指标；在NVIDIA A100 GPU上独立性能分析；提出包含内存增长率、查询延迟、内存-完整性曲线和吞吐量退化率的标准化评估协议；采用Pareto前沿分析并分离基准测试。

Result: 发现神经方法中α值跨度达两个数量级（2.3到215）；3DGS方法在Replica数据集上以90–254MB地图尺寸实现最高绝对精度；场景图提供可预测开销的语义抽象；提供了首个独立测量的α参考值及α感知预算算法。

Conclusion: 内存架构而非范式标签决定部署可行性；需采用更全面、硬件感知的评估标准；所提α指标与预算算法可帮助开发者在实现前评估目标硬件上的部署可行性。

Abstract: As vision-based robots navigate larger environments, their spatial memory grows without bound, eventually exhausting computational resources, particularly on embedded platforms (8-16GB shared memory, $<$30W) where adding hardware is not an option. This survey examines the spatial memory efficiency problem across 88 references spanning 52 systems (1989-2025), from occupancy grids to neural implicit representations. We introduce the $α= M_{\text{peak}} / M_{\text{map}}$, the ratio of peak runtime memory (the total RAM or GPU memory consumed during operation) to saved map size (the persistent checkpoint written to disk), exposing the gap between published map sizes and actual deployment cost. Independent profiling on an NVIDIA A100 GPU reveals that $α$ spans two orders of magnitude within neural methods alone, ranging from 2.3 (Point-SLAM) to 215 (NICE-SLAM, whose 47,MB map requires 10GB at runtime), showing that memory architecture, not paradigm label, determines deployment feasibility. We propose a standardized evaluation protocol comprising memory growth rate, query latency, memory-completeness curves, and throughput degradation, none of which current benchmarks capture. Through a Pareto frontier analysis with explicit benchmark separation, we show that no single paradigm dominates within its evaluation regime: 3DGS methods achieve the best absolute accuracy at 90-254,MB map size on Replica, while scene graphs provide semantic abstraction at predictable cost. We provide the first independently measured $α$ reference values and an $α$-aware budgeting algorithm enabling practitioners to assess deployment feasibility on target hardware prior to implementation.

</details>


### [199] [Dynamic Eraser for Guided Concept Erasure in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16483)
*Qinghui Gong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Dynamic Semantic Steering (DSS)框架，用于在文本到图像扩散模型中实现可解释、可控且无需训练的概念擦除，显著提升擦除率并保持图像保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理阶段的概念擦除方法存在过校正、语义粒度粗、上下文理解差及语义漂移或表征崩溃等问题，亟需更安全、精准、可控的擦除方案。

Method: DSS包含两部分：1）敏感语义边界建模（SSBM），自动发现安全语义锚点；2）敏感语义引导（SSG），利用交叉注意力特征精确定位敏感内容，并通过良置目标导出的闭式解进行修正。

Result: DSS平均擦除率达91.0%，大幅超越SOTA方法（18.6%–85.9%），同时对生成图像保真度影响极小。

Conclusion: DSS是一种轻量、免训练、可解释且高可控性的概念擦除新范式，有效平衡敏感内容抑制与良性语义保留。

Abstract: Concept erasure in Text-To-Image (T2I) diffusion models is vital for safe content generation, but existing inference-time methods face significant limitations. Feature-correction approaches often cause uncontrolled over-correction, while token-level interventions struggle with semantic granularity and context. Moreover, both types of methods are prone to severe semantic drift or even complete representation collapse. To address these challenges, we present Dynamic Semantic Steering (DSS), a lightweight, training-free framework for interpretable and controllable concept erasure. DSS introduces: 1) Sensitive Semantic Boundary Modeling (SSBM) to automate the discovery of safe semantic anchors, and 2) Sensitive Semantic Guidance (SSG), which leverages cross-attention features for precise detection and performs correction via a closed-form solution derived from a well-posed objective. This ensures optimal suppression of sensitive content while preserving benign semantics. DSS achieves an average erasure rate of 91.0\%, significantly outperforming SOTA methods (from 18.6\% to 85.9\%) with minimal impact on output fidelity.

</details>


### [200] [DexWorldModel: Causal Latent World Modeling towards Automated Learning of Embodied Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16484)
*Yueci Deng,Guiliang Liu,Kui Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出因果潜在世界模型（CLWM），通过DINOv3特征建模、双状态测试时训练记忆和推测式异步推理，显著提升生成式世界动作模型在机器人操作任务中的泛化性、内存效率与部署速度，并在仿真与真实机器人上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式世界-动作模型受限于冗余像素级重建、O(T)内存增长及顺序推理延迟，难以部署于实际机器人操作任务。

Method: 提出CLWM框架：1）以DINOv3特征为生成目标解耦交互语义与视觉噪声；2）设计双状态测试时训练（TTT）记忆实现O(1)内存占用；3）引入推测式异步推理（SAI）掩盖扩散去噪延迟；4）构建EmbodiChain在线训练框架，注入物理约束轨迹流以建立效率定律。

Result: 在复杂双臂仿真中达到SOTA性能，并在物理机器人上实现前所未有的零样本sim-to-real迁移效果，甚至超越在真实数据上微调的基线方法。

Conclusion: CLWM通过语义特征建模、恒定内存机制与异步推理协同优化，有效突破生成式世界模型在鲁棒性、可扩展性与实时性方面的关键瓶颈，为具身智能部署提供新范式。

Abstract: Deploying generative World-Action Models for manipulation is severely bottlenecked by redundant pixel-level reconstruction, $\mathcal{O}(T)$ memory scaling, and sequential inference latency. We introduce the Causal Latent World Model (CLWM), which employs DINOv3 features as generative targets to disentangle interaction semantics from visual noise, yielding highly robust domain generalization. To overcome memory scaling, CLWM features a Dual-State Test-Time Training (TTT) Memory that guarantees a strict $\mathcal{O}(1)$ footprint for long-horizon tasks. To overcome deployment latency, we propose Speculative Asynchronous Inference (SAI) to mask partial diffusion denoising behind physical execution, cutting blocking latency by about $50\%$. To scale robust policies, we present EmbodiChain, an online framework that establishes the Efficiency Law by injecting an infinite flow of physics-grounded trajectories during training. Extensive experiments validate that CLWM achieves state-of-the-art performance in complex dual-arm simulation and unprecedented zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on physical robots, outperforming baselines explicitly finetuned on real-world data.

</details>


### [201] [Saccade Attention Networks: Using Transfer Learning of Attention to Reduce Network Sizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16485)
*Marc Estafanous*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Saccade Attention Network的稀疏注意力机制，通过学习关键特征位置来大幅减少输入序列长度，从而降低计算量约80%，同时保持相近性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer网络因注意力矩阵的二次复杂度而受限于序列长度；而人类在观察图像时采用类似‘扫视（saccades）’的稀疏注意力机制，启发作者设计更高效的注意力方式。

Method: 构建Saccade Attention Network，利用大型预训练模型指导学习图像中关键特征的位置，并仅将这些关键特征作为后续网络的输入，从而缩短序列长度、减小模型规模。

Result: 实验表明该方法可减少近80%的计算量，同时维持与原模型相近的性能表现。

Conclusion: 基于生物启发的稀疏注意力机制（saccades）可有效缓解Transformer的计算瓶颈，在显著降低计算成本的同时保持模型性能。

Abstract: One of the limitations of transformer networks is the sequence length due to the quadratic nature of the attention matrix. Classical self attention uses the entire sequence length, however, the actual attention being used is sparse. Humans use a form of sparse attention when analyzing an image or scene called saccades. Focusing on key features greatly reduces computation time. By using a network (Saccade Attention Network) to learn where to attend from a large pre-trained model, we can use it to pre-process images and greatly reduce network size by reducing the input sequence length to just the key features being attended to. Our results indicate that you can reduce calculations by close to 80% and produce similar results.

</details>


### [202] [Aletheia: Physics-Conditioned Localized Artifact Attention (PhyLAA-X) for End-to-End Generalizable and Robust Deepfake Video Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16486)
*Devendra Ghori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PhyLAA-X，一种结合物理先验（光流涡度、镜面反射偏度、rPPG功率谱）与局部伪影注意力机制的深度伪造检测方法，在跨生成器、高压缩和对抗攻击下显著提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测器在域内表现优异，但在跨生成器迁移、高压缩和对抗扰动下性能下降，主因是语义伪影学习与物理不变性（如光流不连续、镜面反射异常、心率调制反射）脱节。

Method: 提出PhyLAA-X：将三种可微分物理特征体（光流涡度、镜面反射偏度、上采样rPPG功率谱）通过交叉注意力门控注入LAA-X注意力计算，并引入共振一致性损失；集成于高效时空模型（EfficientNet-B4+BiLSTM等），采用不确定性感知自适应加权。

Result: 在FaceForensics++(c23)、Celeb-DF v2、DFDC上分别达97.2%/0.992、94.9%/0.981、90.8%/0.966；跨生成器设置下比LAA-Net高4.1–7.3%，PGD-10攻击下仍保持79.4%准确率；单骨干消融显示PhyLAA-X单独带来4.2%跨数据集AUC增益。

Conclusion: 融合物理先验与注意力机制可有效提升深度伪造检测的泛化性与鲁棒性，PhyLAA-X为面向真实场景的检测系统提供了新范式，并已开源完整代码与数据。

Abstract: State-of-the-art deepfake detectors achieve near-perfect in-domain accuracy yet degrade under cross-generator shifts, heavy compression, and adversarial perturbations. The core limitation remains the decoupling of semantic artifact learning from physical invariants: optical-flow discontinuities, specular-reflection inconsistencies, and cardiac-modulated reflectance (rPPG) are treated either as post-hoc features or ignored.
  We introduce PhyLAA-X, a novel physics-conditioned extension of Localized Artifact Attention (LAA-X). PhyLAA-X injects three end-to-end differentiable physics-derived feature volumes - optical-flow curl, specular-reflectance skewness, and spatially-upsampled rPPG power spectra - directly into the LAA-X attention computation via cross-attention gating and a resonance consistency loss. This forces the network to learn manipulation boundaries where semantic inconsistencies and physical violations co-occur - regions inherently harder for generative models to replicate consistently.
  PhyLAA-X is embedded across an efficient spatiotemporal ensemble (EfficientNet-B4+BiLSTM, ResNeXt-101+Transformer, Xception+causal Conv1D) with uncertainty-aware adaptive weighting. On FaceForensics++ (c23), Aletheia reaches 97.2% accuracy / 0.992 AUC-ROC; on Celeb-DF v2, 94.9% / 0.981; on DFDC, 90.8% / 0.966 - outperforming the strongest published baseline (LAA-Net [1]) by 4.1-7.3% in cross-generator settings and maintaining 79.4% accuracy under epsilon = 0.02 PGD-10 attacks. Single-backbone ablations confirm PhyLAA-X alone delivers a 4.2% cross-dataset AUC gain. The full production system is open-sourced at https://github.com/devghori1264/Aletheia (v1.2, April 2026) with pretrained weights, the adversarial corpus (referred to as ADC-2026 in this work), and complete reproducibility artifacts.

</details>


### [203] [Geometry-Aware CLIP Retrieval via Local Cross-Modal Alignment and Steering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16487)
*Nirmalendu Prakash,Narmeen Fatimah Oozeer,Xin Su,Phillip Howard,Shaan Shah,Zoe Wanying He,Shuang Wu,Shivam Raval,Roy Ka-Wei Lee,Meenakshi Khosla,Amir Abdullah*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于邻域对齐的CLIP检索改进方法，包括匈牙利匹配重排序和查询条件下的局部引导，提升属性绑定与组合式检索性能，无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP检索虽具良好的全局跨模态对齐能力，但常因局部几何不一致（如近邻排序错误）导致系统性混淆和结果松散，现有方法多聚焦点级相关性优化或微调，未能有效解决局部结构问题。

Method: 提出两种邻域级技术：(1) 基于匈牙利匹配的邻域重排序，以奖励结构一致性；(2) 查询条件下的局部引导，利用查询周围对比邻域导出的方向重塑检索结果。二者均在推理阶段运行，无需重训练。

Result: 在属性绑定和组合式检索任务上显著提升检索性能；验证了局部结构对检索质量与可控性的关键作用。

Conclusion: 检索质量与可控性高度依赖局部邻域结构，通过推理时邻域对齐策略（重排序与局部引导）可有效提升CLIP检索效果，且无需模型再训练。

Abstract: CLIP retrieval is typically framed as a pointwise similarity problem in a shared embedding space. While CLIP achieves strong global cross-modal alignment, many retrieval failures arise from local geometric inconsistencies: nearby items are incorrectly ordered, leading to systematic confusions (e.g., pentagon vs. hexagon) and produces diffuse, weakly controlled result sets. Prior work largely optimizes for point wise relevance or finetuning to mitigate these problems. We instead view retrieval as a problem of neighborhood alignment. Our work introduces (1) neighborhood-level re-ranking via Hungarian matching, which rewards structural consistency; (2) query-conditioned local steering, where directions derived from contrastive neighborhoods around the query reshape retrieval. We show that these techniques improve retrieval performance on attribute-binding and compositional retrieval tasks. Together, these methods operate on local neighborhoods but serve different roles: re-ranking rewards alignment whereas local steering controls neighborhood structure. This shows that retrieval quality and controllability depend critically on local structure, which can be exploited at inference time without retraining.

</details>


### [204] [An Uncertainty-Aware Loss Function Incorporating Fuzzy Logic: Application to MRI Brain Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16490)
*Hanuman Verma,Akshansh Gupta,Pranabesh Maji,Saurav Mandal,Vijay Kumar Pandey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合模糊逻辑的新型损失函数，用于提升脑部MRI图像分割的准确性，尤其在处理像素分类中的不确定性方面表现优异。该损失函数融合了分类交叉熵（CCE）与基于模糊逻辑的模糊熵，在IBSR和OASIS数据集上验证表明其优于传统CCE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决脑部MRI图像分割中因组织边界模糊、成像噪声等导致的像素分类不确定性问题。

Method: 提出一种融合分类交叉熵（CCE）与模糊熵的新损失函数，利用模糊逻辑建模像素归属的不确定性，并在U-Net和U-Net++架构上进行验证。

Result: 在IBSR和OASIS数据集上，使用该损失函数训练的模型在多项指标上均优于仅用CCE训练的模型，且提升了分割性能与预测可靠性。

Conclusion: 所提模糊损失函数能有效建模和利用分割不确定性，不仅提高分割精度，还增强模型预测的可信度，为医学图像分割提供了新思路。

Abstract: Accurate brain image segmentation, particularly for distinguishing various tissues from magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) images, plays a pivotal role in finding the neurological dis ease and medical image computing. In deep learning approaches, loss functions are very crucial for optimizing the model. In this study, we introduce a novel loss function integrating fuzzy logic to deals uncertainty issues in brain image segmentation into various tissues. It integrates the well-known categorical cross-entropy (CCE) loss function and fuzzy entropy based on fuzzy logic. By employing fuzzy logic, this loss function accounts for the inherent uncertainties in pixel classifications. The proposed loss function has been evaluated on two publicly available benchmark datasets, IBSR and OASIS, using two widely recognised architectures, U-Net and U-Net++. Experimental results demonstrate that the trained model with proposed loss function provided better results in comparison to the CCE optimisation function in terms of various performance metrics. Additionally, it effectively enhances segmentation performance while handling meaningful uncer tainty during training. The findings suggest that this approach not only improves segmentation outcomes but also contributes to the reliability of model predictions.

</details>


### [205] [A Lightweight Transformer for Pain Recognition from Brain Activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16491)
*Stefanos Gkikas,Christian Arzate Cruz,Yu Fang,Lu Cao,Muhammad Umar Khan,Thomas Kassiotis,Giorgos Giannakakis,Raul Fernandez Rojas,Randy Gomez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的Transformer架构，通过统一的标记化机制融合多种fNIRS信号表示，实现多视角联合建模，无需模态特异性调整，且保持计算高效，适用于实时疼痛识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疼痛具有复杂的临床和社会负担，亟需可靠的自动化评估方法。

Method: 设计轻量级Transformer模型，采用统一tokenization机制融合多种fNIRS表征（如原始波形和功率谱密度），通过结构化分段策略保留时空及时频特征，并映射到共享潜在空间。

Result: 在AI4Pain数据集上验证，该模型在疼痛识别任务中达到有竞争力的性能，同时计算开销小，支持GPU和CPU上的实时推理。

Conclusion: 所提方法在不增加模型复杂度的前提下，有效融合多视角fNIRS信息，为便携式、实时疼痛监测提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Pain is a multifaceted and widespread phenomenon with substantial clinical and societal burden, making reliable automated assessment a critical objective. This paper presents a lightweight transformer architecture that fuses multiple fNIRS representations through a unified tokenization mechanism, enabling joint modeling of complementary signal views without requiring modality-specific adaptations or increasing architectural complexity. The proposed token-mixing strategy preserves spatial, temporal, and time-frequency characteristics by projecting heterogeneous inputs onto a shared latent representation, using a structured segmentation scheme to control the granularity of local aggregation and global interaction. The model is evaluated on the AI4Pain dataset using stacked raw waveform and power spectral density representations of fNIRS inputs. Experimental results demonstrate competitive pain recognition performance while remaining computationally compact, making the approach suitable for real-time inference on both GPU and CPU hardware.

</details>


### [206] [LayerCache: Exploiting Layer-wise Velocity Heterogeneity for Efficient Flow Matching Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16492)
*Guandong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LayerCache，一种针对Flow Matching模型的层感知缓存框架，通过分组独立缓存和自适应JVP跨度选择，在保持高质量图像生成的同时显著降低推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow Matching模型虽生成质量高，但因需在大型Transformer上迭代去噪而推理成本高昂；作者发现不同层组速度动态差异显著，但现有缓存方法将Transformer视为整体，无法利用该异质性。

Method: 提出LayerCache框架：将Transformer划分为层组，每组在每去噪步独立决策是否缓存；引入基于组内稳定性度量的自适应JVP跨度K选择机制；建模为三维调度问题（时间步、层组、JVP跨度），用贪心预算分配算法求解。

Result: 在Qwen-Image（1024×1024，50步）上，LayerCache相比MeanCache提升PSNR 5.38 dB（达37.46 dB）、SSIM至0.9834、LPIPS降低70%（至0.0178），并实现1.37倍加速，全面主导质量-速度Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: LayerCache通过细粒度层感知缓存与自适应JVP控制，有效挖掘Transformer内部动态异质性，在不牺牲生成质量前提下大幅提升Flow Matching模型推理效率，为高效扩散式生成建模提供了新范式。

Abstract: Flow Matching models achieve state-of-the-art image generation quality but incur substantial inference cost due to iterative denoising through large Transformer networks. We observe that different layer groups within a Transformer exhibit markedly heterogeneous velocity dynamics: shallow layers are highly stable and amenable to aggressive caching, while deep layers undergo large velocity changes that demand full computation. Existing caching methods, however, treat the entire Transformer as a monolithic unit, applying a single caching decision per timestep and thus failing to exploit this heterogeneity. Based on this finding, we propose LayerCache, a layer-aware caching framework that partitions the Transformer into layer groups and makes independent, per-group caching decisions at each denoising step. LayerCache introduces an adaptive JVP span K selection mechanism that leverages per-group stability measurements to balance estimation accuracy and computational savings. We formulate a three-dimensional scheduling problem over timesteps, layer groups, and JVP span, and solve it with a greedy budget allocation algorithm. On Qwen-Image (1024x1024, 50 steps), LayerCache achieves PSNR 37.46 dB (+5.38 dB over MeanCache), SSIM 0.9834, and LPIPS 0.0178 (a 70% reduction over MeanCache) at 1.37x speedup, dominating all prior caching methods on the quality-speed Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [207] [HQA-VLAttack: Towards High Quality Adversarial Attack on Vision-Language Pre-Trained Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16499)
*Han Liu,Jiaqi Li,Zhi Xu,Xiaotong Zhang,Xiaoming Xu,Fenglong Ma,Yuanman Li,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为HQA-VLAttack的黑盒对抗攻击框架，用于视觉-语言预训练模型，通过结合语义一致的文本扰动与对比学习优化的图像扰动，显著提升攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒攻击方法要么查询开销大，要么仅降低正样本图文对相似度而忽略负样本对相似度变化，影响攻击效果。

Method: HQA-VLAttack包含文本和图像两个攻击阶段：文本阶段使用反拟合词向量生成语义一致的替代词；图像阶段先基于层重要性初始化对抗样本，再用对比学习优化扰动，以同时降低正样本相似度、提升负样本相似度。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，HQA-VLAttack在攻击成功率上显著优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: HQA-VLAttack是一种简单而高效的方法，能生成高质量对抗样本，在黑盒设置下有效提升对视觉-语言预训练模型的攻击性能。

Abstract: Black-box adversarial attack on vision-language pre-trained models is a practical and challenging task, as text and image perturbations need to be considered simultaneously, and only the predicted results are accessible. Research on this problem is in its infancy, and only a handful of methods are available. Nevertheless, existing methods either rely on a complex iterative cross-search strategy, which inevitably consumes numerous queries, or only consider reducing the similarity of positive image-text pairs but ignore that of negative ones, which will also be implicitly diminished, thus inevitably affecting the attack performance. To alleviate the above issues, we propose a simple yet effective framework to generate high-quality adversarial examples on vision-language pre-trained models, named HQA-VLAttack, which consists of text and image attack stages. For text perturbation generation, it leverages the counter-fitting word vector to generate the substitute word set, thus guaranteeing the semantic consistency between the substitute word and the original word. For image perturbation generation, it first initializes the image adversarial example via the layer-importance guided strategy, and then utilizes contrastive learning to optimize the image adversarial perturbation, which ensures that the similarity of positive image-text pairs is decreased while that of negative image-text pairs is increased. In this way, the optimized adversarial images and texts are more likely to retrieve negative examples, thereby enhancing the attack success rate. Experimental results on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that HQA-VLAttack significantly outperforms strong baselines in terms of attack success rate.

</details>


### [208] [Semantically Stable Image Composition Analysis via Saliency and Gradient Vector Flow Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16500)
*Armin Dadras,Robert Sablatnig,Franziska Proksa,Markus Seidl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VFCNet，通过融合显著性与边缘信息构建梯度矢量流（GVF）场，结合注意力机制与DINOv3多尺度特征提取，在PICD基准上达到SOTA性能，并发现仅用DINOv3自监督特征加简单分类器即可超越专用构图模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠评估摄影构图需兼顾空间布局判别性与语义内容鲁棒性的特征，现有方法难以兼顾。

Method: 提出VFCNet：将显著性与边缘信息融合为梯度矢量流（GVF）场；采用双流GVF表征并以注意力机制融合；利用DINOv3骨干网络提取多尺度流动特征。

Result: 在PICD基准上CDA-1达0.683、CDA-2达0.629，分别较此前最优方法提升33.1%和36.1%；验证了DINOv3自监督特征+简单分类器优于复杂专用构图模型。

Conclusion: 基于视觉注意流建模构图是有效路径；低层几何结构引导的GVF表征配合先进自监督视觉特征，可显著提升构图质量评估性能。

Abstract: The reliable computational assessment of photographic composition requires features that are discriminative of spatial layout yet robust to semantic content. This paper proposes a low-level representation grounded in the assumption that composition can be understood as the flow of visual attention across geometric structure. We introduce VFCNet, which fuses saliency and edge information into a gradient vector flow (GVF) field. The model computes dual-stream GVF representations, integrates them via attention, and extracts multi-scale flow features with a DINOv3 backbone. VFCNet achieves state-of-the-art performance on the PICD benchmark (CDA-1: 0.683, CDA-2: 0.629), improving by 33.1\% and 36.1\% over the previous best method. We also show that a simple classifier on self-supervised DINOv3 features substantially outperforms more sophisticated, composition-specialized models. Code is available at https://github.com/ADadras/VFCNet

</details>


### [209] [Topology-Aware Layer Pruning for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16502)
*Pengcheng Zheng,Chaoning Zhang,Ya Wen,Wang Liu,Qigan Sun,Jiarong Mo,Jiaquan Zhang,Jewon Lee,Tae-Ho Kim,Kuien Liu,Tianyu Li,Caiyan Qin,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的拓扑感知层剪枝框架，利用单纯复形和zigzag持续同调量化层间拓扑一致性，实现自适应剪枝，保留关键表征跃迁，在多种多模态基准上显著优于现有剪枝方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM层剪枝方法依赖局部相似性或静态代理信号，无法捕捉跨深度的全局、动态表征演化，易误删过渡关键层，且LVLM计算与内存开销大，难以部署于资源受限场景。

Method: 将各层隐状态建模为点云，用单纯复形描述其演化过程，并借助zigzag持续同调量化层间拓扑一致性，据此指导自适应层剪枝。

Result: 在多个多模态基准上，该方法在广泛稀疏率下均一致优于现有剪枝方法。

Conclusion: 拓扑感知的层剪枝框架能更有效地识别并保留对表征演化至关重要的网络层，提升LVLM在资源受限下的高效部署能力。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in natural language understanding and reasoning, while recent extensions that incorporate visual inputs enable them to process multimodal information. Despite these advances, Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) incur substantial computational and memory costs, hindering deployment in resource-constrained scenarios. Existing layer pruning methods typically rely on local similarity metrics or static proxy signals, failing to capture the global and dynamic evolution of representations across model depth, which often leads to the removal of transition-critical layers. To address this limitation, we propose a topology-aware layer pruning framework for LVLMs. Specifically, we represent layer wise hidden states as point clouds and models their evolution using \textit{simplicial complexes}. By leveraging \textit{zigzag persistent homology}, we quantify inter-layer topological consistency and enable adaptive pruning that preserves critical representational transitions. Extensive experiments on diverse multimodal benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms existing pruning methods across a wide range of sparsity ratios. Our code is available at https://github.com/zpc456/TopoVLM.

</details>


### [210] [Motif-Video 2B: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16503)
*Junghwan Lim,Wai Ting Cheung,Minsu Ha,Beomgyu Kim,Taewhan Kim,Haesol Lee,Dongpin Oh,Jeesoo Lee,Taehyun Kim,Minjae Kim,Sungmin Lee,Hyeyeon Cho,Dahye Choi,Jaeheui Her,Jaeyeon Huh,Hanbin Jung,Changjin Kang,Dongseok Kim,Jangwoong Kim,Youngrok Kim,Hyukjin Kweon,Hongjoo Lee,Jeongdoo Lee,Junhyeok Lee,Eunhwan Park,Yeongjae Park,Bokki Ryu,Dongjoo Weon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Motif-Video 2B模型，通过架构专业化（如共享交叉注意力、三段式主干网络）和高效训练策略（动态token路由、冻结视频编码器特征对齐），在小预算（<10M视频、<100K H200 GPU小时）下实现高质量文本到视频生成，性能超越更大模型Wan2.1 14B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在有限数据（<10M clips）和算力（<100,000 H200 GPU小时）下能否实现强文本到视频生成质量，指出模型容量的组织方式（而不仅是规模）是关键。

Method: 提出Motif-Video 2B：1）共享交叉注意力增强长视频序列下的文本控制；2）三段式主干网络分别处理早期融合、联合表征学习和细节细化；3）结合动态token路由与冻结预训练视频编码器的早期特征对齐进行高效训练。

Result: 在VBench上达到83.76%，超越Wan2.1 14B，同时参数量少7倍、训练数据大幅减少；分析显示其后期block具有比单流基线更清晰的跨帧注意力结构。

Conclusion: 架构专业化与效率导向训练策略可显著缩小甚至超越大规模视频模型的质量差距，验证了‘小而精’的设计路径可行性。

Abstract: Training strong video generation models usually requires massive datasets, large parameter counts, and substantial compute. In this work, we ask whether strong text-to-video quality is possible at a much smaller budget: fewer than 10M clips and less than 100,000 H200 GPU hours. Our core claim is that part of the answer lies in how model capacity is organized, not only in how much of it is used. In video generation, prompt alignment, temporal consistency, and fine-detail recovery can interfere with one another when they are handled through the same pathway. Motif-Video 2B addresses this by separating these roles architecturally, rather than relying on scale alone. The model combines two key ideas. First, Shared Cross-Attention strengthens text control when video token sequences become long. Second, a three-part backbone separates early fusion, joint representation learning, and detail refinement. To make this design effective under a limited compute budget, we pair it with an efficient training recipe based on dynamic token routing and early-phase feature alignment to a frozen pretrained video encoder. Our analysis shows that later blocks develop clearer cross-frame attention structure than standard single-stream baselines. On VBench, Motif-Video~2B reaches 83.76\%, surpassing Wan2.1 14B while using 7$\times$ fewer parameters and substantially less training data. These results suggest that careful architectural specialization, combined with an efficiency-oriented training recipe, can narrow or exceed the quality gap typically associated with much larger video models.

</details>


### [211] [From Handwriting to Structured Data: Benchmarking AI Digitisation of Handwritten Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16504)
*Nicholas Pather,Joshua Fouché,Sitwala Mundia,Karl-Günter Technau,Thokozile Malaba,Alex Welte,Ushma Mehta,Bruce A. Bassett*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了17种多模态大语言模型在复杂手写医疗表单数字化任务中的性能，发现最新模型（如GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 3.1）在准确率、F1分数和错误率等方面表现优异，提示其有望实现全自动手写文档数字化，尤其适用于中低收入国家。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动数字化结构化手写文档效率低、成本高，亟需自动化解决方案，尤其在资源受限地区。

Method: 对17个前沿多模态大语言模型（包括Google、OpenAI和开源模型）在真实、高难度医疗表单数据集上进行基准测试，涵盖日期、印刷体、手写内容及高度可变性；同时分析prompt优化对各项指标的影响。

Result: 最新模型（Gemini 3.1、GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6）在离散字段准确率达~85%，加权F1≈90%；Gemini 3.1在自由文本错误率（WER=0.50, CER=0.31）和分类指标上最优；prompt优化使宏平均指标提升超60%，但加权指标仅提升2–5%。

Conclusion: 多模态大语言模型的快速进步为全自动、高鲁棒性手写文档数字化提供了可行路径，具有重要现实意义，特别是在中低收入国家的医疗场景中。

Abstract: Manual digitisation of structured handwritten documents is slow and costly. We benchmark 17 leading frontier multi-modal large language models and open-source models against a very challenging real-world medical form that mixes dates; structured, printed text; hand-written responses and significant variability challenges. None of the smaller or older models perform well but the latest Google and OpenAI models reach accuracies around $85\%$ with weighted F1 scores $\simeq 90\%$ across the discrete or predefined fields despite the very challenging nature of the responses. Clear task specific strengths emerge: GPT 5.4 excels in noisy date extraction as well as reliability with the lowest hallucination rate ($6\%$). Claude Sonnet 4.6 had the best average performance across formatted fields (dates and numerical values), while Gemini 3.1 delivered the best overall performance, with the lowest free text error rates (WER = $0.50$ and CER = $0.31$) and the strongest results across discrete classification metrics. We further show that prompt optimisation dramatically improves macro precision, recall and F1 by over $60\%$, but has little impact on weighted metrics (only $\sim2-5\%$ improvement). These results provide evidence that the rapid improvements of multimodal large language models offer a compelling pathway toward fully automated digitisation of complex handwritten workflows that is particularly relevant in low- and middle-income countries.

</details>


### [212] [Predicting Blastocyst Formation in IVF: Integrating DINOv2 and Attention-Based LSTM on Time-Lapse Embryo Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16505)
*Zahra Asghari Varzaneh,Niclas Wölner-Hanssen,Reza Khoshkangini,Thomas Ebner,Magnus Johnsson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合DINOv2视觉模型与增强型LSTM（含多头注意力机制）的混合模型，利用每日少量胚胎图像预测囊胚形成，准确率达96.4%，且对帧缺失鲁棒，适用于缺乏完整时序成像系统的IVF实验室。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胚胎移植前需筛选最优胚胎，但传统方法依赖人工分析大量时序图像；而许多诊所缺乏完整时序成像系统，仅能获取每日少量图像，导致囊胚形成预测困难。

Method: 提出一种融合DINOv2（用于提取单帧胚胎图像特征）与增强型LSTM（加入多头注意力机制，建模时间序列发育动态）的混合深度学习模型，并在704例真实胚胎视频数据上进行训练与验证。

Result: 模型在囊胚形成预测任务中达到96.4%的准确率，显著优于现有方法；同时对图像帧缺失具有较强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该模型可有效提升胚胎筛选效率与可靠性，为资源受限的IVF实验室提供实用、鲁棒的辅助决策工具。

Abstract: The selection of the optimal embryo for transfer is a critical yet challenging step in in vitro fertilization (IVF), primarily due to its reliance on the manual inspection of extensive time-lapse imaging data. A key obstacle in this process is predicting blastocyst formation from the limited number of daily images available. Many clinics also lack complete time-lapse systems, so full videos are often unavailable. In this study, we aimed to predict which embryos will develop into blastocysts using limited daily images from time-lapse recordings. We propose a novel hybrid model that combines DINOv2, a transformer-based vision model, with an enhanced long short-term memory (LSTM) network featuring a multi-head attention layer. DINOv2 extracts meaningful features from embryo images, and the LSTM model then uses these features to analyze embryo development over time and generate final predictions. We tested our model on a real dataset of 704 embryo videos. The model achieved 96.4% accuracy, surpassing existing methods. It also performs well with missing frames, making it valuable for many IVF laboratories with limited imaging systems. Our approach can assist embryologists in selecting better embryos more efficiently and with greater confidence.

</details>


### [213] [Medical thinking with multiple images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16506)
*Zonghai Yao,Benlu Wang,Yifan Zhang,Junda Wang,Iris Xia,Zhipeng Tang,Shuo Han,Feiyun Ouyang,Zhichao Yang,Arman Cohan,Hong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MedThinkVQA——一个面向多图像临床推理的专家标注基准，强调跨视图证据整合；现有大模型在此任务上表现远低于人类专家，主要瓶颈在于图像理解与跨视图对齐，而非推理长度本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实临床诊断常需整合多个医学影像（如不同切面或模态），而现有医学QA基准多基于单图，无法评估模型的多图像协同推理能力。

Method: 构建专家标注的MedThinkVQA基准，含8067例（720例测试），平均每例6.62张图像；引入中间监督与步骤级评估；系统评测主流闭源与开源多模态大模型，并开展消融实验（如提供专家提示 vs 自生成中间结果）及错误归因分析。

Result: 最强闭源模型（Claude-4.6-Opus等）准确率仅57.2%；开源最强模型（Qwen3.5-397B-A17B）为52.2%；超70%错误源于图像解读与跨视图整合；增强视觉基础（如专家提示）显著提升性能，而推理步数增加在基础薄弱时收益有限甚至有害。

Conclusion: 多图像临床推理的核心挑战不是推理链长度，而是可靠、可对齐、可组合的多视图视觉证据 grounding 机制；未来工作应聚焦于提升早期视觉感知与跨图像语义对齐能力。

Abstract: Large language models perform well on many medical QA benchmarks, but real clinical reasoning often requires integrating evidence across multiple images rather than interpreting a single view. We introduce MedThinkVQA, an expert-annotated benchmark for thinking with multiple images, where models must interpret each image, combine cross-view evidence, and answer diagnostic questions with intermediate supervision and step-level evaluation. The dataset contains 8,067 cases, including 720 test cases, with an average of 6.62 images per case, substantially denser than prior work, whose expert-level benchmarks use at most 1.43 images per case. On the test set, the best closed-source models, Claude-4.6-Opus, Gemini-3-Pro, and GPT-5.2-xhigh, reach only 57.2%, 55.3%, and 54.9% accuracy, while GPT-5-mini and GPT-5-nano reach 39.7% and 30.8%. Strong open-source models lag behind, led by Qwen3.5-397B-A17B at 52.2% and Qwen3.5-27B at 50.6%. Further analysis identifies grounded multi-image reasoning as the main bottleneck: models often fail to extract, align, and compose evidence across views before higher-level inference can help. Providing expert single-image cues and cross-image summaries improves performance, whereas replacing them with self-generated intermediates reduces accuracy. Step-level analysis shows that over 70% of errors arise from image reading and cross-view integration. Scaling results further show that additional inference-time computation helps only when visual grounding is already reliable; when early evidence extraction is weak, longer reasoning yields limited or unstable gains and can amplify misread cues. These results suggest that the key challenge is not reasoning length alone, but reliable mechanisms for grounding, aligning, and composing distributed evidence across real-world multimodal clinical inputs.

</details>


### [214] [Medial Axis Aware Learning of Signed Distance Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16512)
*Samuel Weidemaier,Christoph Norden-Smoch,Martin Rumpf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于变分法和相场近似的新型方法，用于从无向点云中高精度重建全局有符号距离函数（SDF），显式建模梯度跳跃集（即中轴），并通过神经网络联合学习SDF与相场函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以兼顾SDF的全局精度与中轴结构的显式建模，尤其对无向点云效果有限。

Method: 构建高阶变分模型，显式引入SDF梯度的跳跃集（对应表面中轴），结合Eikonal方程和零水平集约束；采用Ambrosio-Tortorelli型相场近似处理跳跃集，用神经网络分别参数化SDF和相场函数。

Result: 在无向点云上实现了高精度的全局SDF重建，近场与远场均表现优异，定量与定性对比均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 该方法通过变分建模与相场近似有效融合几何先验（中轴）与深度学习，为点云隐式重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: We propose a novel variational method to compute a highly accurate global signed distance function (SDF) to a given point cloud. To this end, the jump set of the gradient of the SDF, which coincides with the medial axis of the surface, is explicitly taken into account through a higher-order variational formulation that enforces linear growth along the gradient direction away from this discontinuity set. The eikonal equation and the zero-level set of the SDF are enforced as constraints. To make this variational problem computationally tractable, a phase field approximation of Ambrosio-Tortorelli type is employed. The associated phase field function implicitly describes the medial axis. The method is implemented for surfaces represented by unoriented point clouds using neural network approximations of both the SDF and the phase field. Experiments demonstrate the method's accuracy both in the near field and globally. Quantitative and qualitative comparisons with other approaches show the advantages of the proposed method.

</details>


### [215] [SynthPID: P&ID digitization from Topology-Preserving Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16513)
*Suraj Prasad,Pinak Mahapatra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SynthPID，一种基于真实图纸拓扑结构生成的665张合成P&ID数据集，并结合专为高分辨率图设计的patch-based Relationformer模型，在仅用合成数据训练下达到63.8%边检测mAP，接近使用真实数据训练的性能上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: P&ID数字化缺乏足够标注数据，现有公开基准仅有12张图像；此前基于模板的合成方法因忽略真实工艺拓扑结构，导致模型性能低下（仅约33%边检测精度）。

Method: 提出SynthPID合成数据集，其管道拓扑源自真实P&ID；设计适配高分辨率工程图的patch-based Relationformer模型；开展控制实验与缩放研究验证生成质量对性能的关键影响。

Result: 仅用SynthPID训练的模型在PID2Graph OPEN100测试集上达到63.8 ± 3.1%边mAP，比模板法提升约30个百分点，逼近真实数据训练的oracle性能（相差仅8个百分点）；缩放研究表明400张后性能趋于饱和，种子多样性成为瓶颈。

Conclusion: 合成数据的质量（尤其是拓扑真实性）比数量或模型架构更能决定P&ID解析性能；SynthPID为工业文档理解提供了可复现、可扩展的数据基础。

Abstract: Automating the digitization of Piping and Instrumentation Diagrams (P&IDs) into structured process graphs would unlock significant value in plant operations, yet progress is bottlenecked by a fundamental data problem: engineering drawings are proprietary, and the entire community shares a single public benchmark of just 12 annotated images. Prior attempts at synthetic augmentation have fallen short because template-based generators scatter symbols at random, producing graphs that bear little resemblance to real process plants and, accordingly, yield only approximately 33% edge detection accuracy under synth-only training. We argue the failure is structural rather than visual and address it by introducing SynthPID, a corpus of 665 synthetic P&IDs whose pipe topology is seeded directly from real drawings. Paired with a patch-based Relationformer adapted for high-resolution diagrams, a model trained on SynthPID alone achieves 63.8 +/- 3.1% edge mAP on PID2Graph OPEN100 without seeing a single real P&ID during training, closing within 8 pp of the real-data oracle. These gains hold up under a controlled comparison against the template-based regime, confirming that generation quality drives performance rather than model choice. A scaling study reveals that gains flatten beyond roughly 400 synthetic images, pointing to seed diversity as the binding constraint.

</details>


### [216] [BARD: Bridging AutoRegressive and Diffusion Vision-Language Models Via Highly Efficient Progressive Block Merging and Stage-Wise Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16514)
*Baoyou Chen,Hanchen Xia,Peng Tu,Haojun Shi,Shan Mu,Weihao Yuan,Siyu Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BARD框架，将预训练自回归视觉语言模型（VLM）高效转换为大块扩散VLM（dVLM），通过渐进式块合并、阶段内扩散蒸馏、混合噪声调度和内存友好训练，在保持性能的同时实现最高3倍解码吞吐提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归VLM解码效率低，而直接转为大块扩散VLM会导致性能严重下降，亟需一种高质量、高效率的转换方法。

Method: 提出BARD框架：1）渐进式监督块合并；2）以固定小块扩散模型为锚点的阶段内dVLM蒸馏；3）混合噪声调度与去噪过程中的token修订；4）支持长多模态序列的内存友好训练。

Result: BARD-VL在≤4.4M数据下成功迁移Qwen3-VL能力，在4B/8B规模开源dVLM中SOTA，并实现最高3×解码吞吐加速。

Conclusion: 在扩散范式内部进行蒸馏比跨范式（自回归→扩散）蒸馏更有效且稳定；BARD提供了一种简单、通用、高效的自回归VLM向大块dVLM转换路径。

Abstract: Autoregressive vision-language models (VLMs) deliver strong multimodal capability, but their token-by-token decoding imposes a fundamental inference bottleneck. Diffusion VLMs offer a more parallel decoding paradigm, yet directly converting a pretrained autoregressive VLM into a large-block diffusion VLM (dVLM) often leads to substantial quality degradation. In this work, we present BARD, a simple and effective bridging framework that converts a pretrained autoregressive VLM into a same-architecture, decoding-efficient dVLM. Our approach combines progressive supervised block merging, which gradually enlarges the decoding block size, with stage-wise intra-dVLM distillation from a fixed small-block diffusion anchor to recover performance lost at larger blocks. We further incorporate a mixed noise scheduler to improve robustness and token revision during denoising, and memory-friendly training to enable efficient training on long multimodal sequences. A key empirical finding is that direct autoregressive-to-diffusion distillation is poorly aligned and can even hurt performance, whereas distillation within the diffusion regime is consistently effective. Experimental results show that, with $\leq 4.4M$ data, BARD-VL transfers strong multimodal capability from Qwen3-VL to a large-block dVLM. Remarkably, BARD-VL establishes a new SOTA among comparable-scale open dVLMs on our evaluation suite at both 4B and 8B scales. At the same time, BARD-VL achieves up to \textbf{3$\times$} decoding throughput speedup compared to the source model.

</details>


### [217] [Penny Wise, Pixel Foolish: Bypassing Price Constraints in Multimodal Agents via Visual Adversarial Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16515)
*Jiachen Qian,Zhaolu Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PriceBlind攻击框架，揭示多模态大模型在金融交易场景中因视觉主导幻觉（VDH）导致的决策风险，并验证其在主流MLLM上的高攻击成功率及防御方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）被用于高风险金融交易，但其对抗鲁棒性尚未被充分研究；作者发现视觉主导幻觉（VDH）问题——即微小视觉扰动可压倒文本价格证据，导致代理做出非理性决策。

Method: 提出PriceBlind白盒对抗攻击框架，利用CLIP编码器的模态差距，设计语义解耦损失（Semantic-Decoupling Loss），使图像嵌入对齐低成本价值相关锚点，同时保持像素级保真度。

Result: 在E-ShopBench上白盒攻击成功率（ASR）达约80%；迁移至GPT-4o、Gemini-1.5-Pro和Claude-3.5-Sonnet时ASR为35–41%；鲁棒编码器与Verify-then-Act防御能显著降低ASR，但伴随一定干净准确率下降。

Conclusion: VDH是MLLM在价格敏感任务中的关键脆弱性，PriceBlind揭示了其严重性；防御方案虽有效，但需权衡鲁棒性与性能，凸显面向金融场景的多模态安全评估与加固的必要性。

Abstract: The rapid proliferation of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) has enabled mobile agents to execute high-stakes financial transactions, but their adversarial robustness remains underexplored. We identify Visual Dominance Hallucination (VDH), where imperceptible visual cues can override textual price evidence in screenshot-based, price-constrained settings and lead agents to irrational decisions. We propose PriceBlind, a stealthy white-box adversarial attack framework for controlled screenshot-based evaluation. PriceBlind exploits the modality gap in CLIP-based encoders via a Semantic-Decoupling Loss that aligns the image embedding with low-cost, value-associated anchors while preserving pixel-level fidelity. On E-ShopBench, PriceBlind achieves around 80% ASR in white-box evaluation; under a simplified single-turn coordinate-selection protocol, Ensemble-DI-FGSM transfers with roughly 35-41% ASR across GPT-4o, Gemini-1.5-Pro, and Claude-3.5-Sonnet. We also show that robust encoders and Verify-then-Act defenses reduce ASR substantially, though with some clean-accuracy trade-off.

</details>


### [218] [Operationalizing Fairness in Text-to-Image Models: A Survey of Bias, Fairness Audits and Mitigation Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16516)
*Megan Smith,Venkatesh Thirugnana Sambandham,Florian Richter,Laura Crompton,Matthias Uhl,Torsten Schön*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: This paper provides a systematic review of fairness in text-to-image generation, clarifies conceptual ambiguities around bias and fairness, proposes a taxonomy, distinguishes target vs. threshold fairness, surveys mitigation strategies, and introduces a new operational framework for fairness testing.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The field lacks clear, operational definitions of 'bias' and 'fairness', leading to conceptual ambiguity in evaluating and mitigating societal stereotypes in text-to-image models.

Method: Systematic literature review, taxonomy construction, conceptual analysis (distinguishing target vs. threshold fairness), and survey of bias mitigation strategies (e.g., prompt engineering, diffusion process manipulation).

Result: A structured taxonomy of bias types and fairness notions; identification of the gap between target fairness and threshold fairness; comprehensive survey of existing mitigation techniques.

Conclusion: A new framework is proposed to operationalize fairness through rigorous, target-based testing—shifting from descriptive metrics toward accountable generative AI development.

Abstract: Text-to-Image (T2I) generation models have been widely adopted across various industries, yet are criticized for frequently exhibiting societal stereotypes. While a growing body of research has emerged to evaluate and mitigate these biases, the field at present contends with conceptual ambiguity, for example terms like "bias" and "fairness" are not always clearly distinguished and often lack clear operational definitions. This paper provides a comprehensive systematic review of T2I fairness literature, organizing existing work into a taxonomy of bias types and fairness notions. We critically assess the gap between "target fairness" (normative ideals in T2I outputs) and "threshold fairness" (normative standards with actionable decision rules). Furthermore, we survey the landscape of mitigation strategies, ranging from prompt engineering to diffusion process manipulation. We conclude by proposing a new framework for operationalizing fairness that moves beyond descriptive metrics towards rigorous, target-based testing, offering an approach for more accountable generative AI development.

</details>


### [219] [SmoGVLM: A Small, Graph-enhanced Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16517)
*Debjyoti Mondal,Rituraj Singh,Subhadarshi Panda*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SmoGVLM is a small, graph-enhanced vision-language model that integrates structured knowledge via Graph Neural Networks to reduce hallucination and improve grounding in knowledge-intensive reasoning; it achieves up to 16.24% performance gain and outperforms larger VLMs.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large vision-language models suffer from hallucination and poor grounding in knowledge-intensive reasoning; there is a need for more efficient, smaller-scale multimodal models with better factual consistency.

Method: Proposes SmoGVLM, a small graph-enhanced VLM that fuses structured knowledge with visual and textual modalities using Graph Neural Networks; evaluates across model sizes (1.3B to 13B).

Result: A small SmoGVLM achieves up to 16.24% performance gain and surpasses larger VLMs and strong fine-tuned baselines.

Conclusion: Structured knowledge augmentation significantly enhances efficiency and reasoning capability of smaller VLMs, offering a promising direction for compact, reliable multimodal systems.

Abstract: Large vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong performance on multimodal tasks but often suffer from hallucination and poor grounding in knowledge-intensive reasoning. We propose SmoGVLM, a small, graph-enhanced VLM that integrates structured knowledge with visual and textual modalities, using Graph Neural Networks. We investigate the effects of our method across a range of model sizes, from tiny (1.3B) to large (13B) models. Our results demonstrate that, when trained using our approach, a small model can achieve performance gains upto 16.24%, and surpass its larger counterparts, outperforming larger VLMs and strong fine-tuned baselines. These results highlight the potential of structured knowledge augmentation for efficient, smaller-scale multimodal reasoning systems.

</details>


### [220] [Fast Online 3D Multi-Camera Multi-Object Tracking and Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16522)
*Linh Van Ma,Tran Thien Dat Nguyen,Moongu Jeon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多单目相机的快速在线3D多目标跟踪与姿态估计联合方法，仅需2D检测结果，无需3D标注数据或复杂深度模型，实现了高效、鲁棒的Bayes最优滤波。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有3D多目标跟踪方法依赖昂贵3D训练数据和计算密集型深度模型的问题，并提升在多相机间歇性断连场景下的鲁棒性。

Method: 设计一种高效的Bayes最优多目标跟踪滤波器实现，仅利用多个单目相机提供的2D边界框和姿态检测结果进行在线联合跟踪与姿态估计。

Result: 算法显著快于当前最先进方法，在保持精度的同时，仅使用公开预训练的2D检测模型；并在多相机动态连接/断开场景下表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该方法在效率、精度与部署便捷性之间取得良好平衡，为实际多相机系统中的实时3D感知提供了可行新路径。

Abstract: This paper proposes a fast and online method for jointly performing 3D multi-object tracking and pose estimation using multiple monocular cameras. Our algorithm requires only 2D bounding box and pose detections, eliminating the need for costly 3D training data or computationally expensive deep learning models. Our solution is an efficient implementation of a Bayes-optimal multi-object tracking filter, enhancing computational efficiency while maintaining accuracy. We demonstrate that our algorithm is significantly faster than state-of-the-art methods without compromising accuracy, using only publicly available pre-trained 2D detection models. We also illustrate the robust performance of our algorithm in scenarios where multiple cameras are intermittently disconnected or reconnected during operation.

</details>


### [221] [Privacy-Preserving Semantic Segmentation without Key Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16523)
*Mare Hirose,Shoko Imaizumi,Hitoshi Kiya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型的隐私保护语义分割方法，支持每个客户端和每张图像使用独立密钥进行加密，训练和推理均在加密图像上进行，并通过在训练中也应用图像加密来缓解性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决语义分割任务中数据隐私保护与模型性能之间的矛盾，支持客户端本地密钥生成与端到端加密处理。

Method: 采用客户端与模型创建者各自生成密钥对图像加密；训练和推理全程在加密图像上进行；在训练阶段同步应用图像加密以提升模型鲁棒性。

Result: 在Cityscapes数据集和SETR（基于视觉Transformer）模型上验证了该方法的有效性，兼顾了隐私保护与分割性能。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了细粒度密钥控制下的端到端隐私保护语义分割，且无需牺牲显著精度。

Abstract: This paper proposes a novel privacy-preserving semantic segmentation method that can use independent keys for each client and image. In the proposed method, the model creator and each client encrypt images using locally generated keys, and model training and inference are conducted on the encrypted images. To mitigate performance degradation, an image encryption method is applied to model training in addition to the generation of test images. In experiments, the effectiveness of the proposed method is confirmed on the Cityscapes dataset under the use of a vision transformer-based model, called SETR.

</details>


### [222] [Expert-Annotated Embryo Image Dataset with Natural Language Descriptions for Evidence-Based Patient Communication in IVF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16528)
*Nicklas Neu,Thomas Ebner,Jasmin Primus,Bernhard Schenkenfelder,Raphael Zefferer,Mathias Brunbauer,Florian Kromp*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个专家标注的胚胎图像-自然语言描述数据集，旨在通过视觉-语言模型实现可解释、透明的AI辅助胚胎选择，支持循证决策和医患沟通。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI胚胎筛选方法受限于临床数据适配性差、依赖时序培养箱、缺乏可解释性，且患者对专家决策质疑增多，亟需基于证据的透明化决策支持。

Method: 构建专家标注的胚胎图像与自然语言形态学描述（含细胞周期、发育阶段及形态特征）配对数据集，用于微调基础视觉-语言模型，进而自动生成描述并链接文献证据。

Result: 提供首个面向胚胎评估的视觉-语言基准数据集，支持可解释AI模型训练，实现从图像到科学证据的自动推理链。

Conclusion: 该数据集推动语言驱动、可解释、透明的胚胎评估研究，长期有望提升临床决策质量与患者结局。

Abstract: Embryo selection is one of multiple crucial steps in in-vitro fertilization, commonly based on morphological assessment by clinical embryologists. Although artificial intelligence methods have demonstrated their potential to support embryo selection by automated embryo ranking or grading methods, the overall impact of AI-based solutions is still limited. This is mainly due to the required adaptation of automated solutions to custom clinical data, reliance on time lapse incubators and a lack of interpretability to understand AI reasoning. The modern, informed patient is questioning expert decisions, particularly if the treatment is not successful. Thus, evidence-based decision justification in tasks like embryo selection would support transparent decision making and respectful patient communication. To support this aim, we hereby present an expert-annotated dataset consisting of embryo images and corresponding morphological description using natural language. The description contains relevant information on embryonic cell cycle, developmental stage and morphological features. This dataset enables the finetuning of modern foundational vision-language models to learn and improve over time with high accuracy. Predicted embryo descriptions can then be leveraged to automatically extract scientific evidence from literature, facilitating well-informed, evidence-based decision-making and transparent communication with patients. Our proposed dataset supports research in language-based, interpretable, and transparent automated embryo assessment and has the potential to enhance the decision-making process and improve patient outcomes significantly over time.

</details>


### [223] [Beyond Attack Success Rate: A Multi-Metric Evaluation of Adversarial Transferability in Medical Imaging Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16532)
*Emily Curl,Kofi Ampomah,Md Erfan,Sayanton Dibbo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了医学图像分析中不同深度学习模型（CNNs和ViTs）对多种对抗攻击的鲁棒性，发现仅依赖攻击成功率（ASR）指标存在严重不足，需结合感知质量（PSNR、SSIM）和扰动强度（L2）等多维度指标进行综合评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗脆弱性评估过度依赖二值化的攻击成功率（ASR），忽视扰动强度、图像感知质量及跨架构迁移性，尤其在ViT等新架构兴起背景下，单一ASR难以全面刻画对抗行为。

Method: 在四个医学图像数据集（PathMNIST、DermaMNIST、RetinaMNIST、CheXpert）上，对七种模型（含CNN与ViT）和七种攻击方法，在五个扰动预算下，同步测量ASR、PSNR、SSIM和L2扰动大小，开展系统性实证研究。

Result: PSNR、SSIM与L2扰动强相关，但与ASR几乎无关；该现象在CNN和ViT上均一致；ASR无法反映扰动代价或迁移能力，单独使用易导致鲁棒性误判。

Conclusion: ASR不足以支撑医学AI的对抗风险评估；应采用涵盖攻击效能、方法特性与实施开销的多指标评估框架，以保障临床部署安全性。

Abstract: While deep learning systems are becoming increasingly prevalent in medical image analysis, their vulnerabilities to adversarial perturbations raise serious concerns for clinical deployment. These vulnerability evaluations largely rely on Attack Success Rate (ASR), a binary metric that indicates solely whether an attack is successful. However, the ASR metric does not account for other factors, such as perturbation strength, perceptual image quality, and cross-architecture attack transferability, and therefore, the interpretation is incomplete. This gap requires consideration, as complex, large-scale deep learning systems, including Vision Transformers (ViTs), are increasingly challenging the dominance of Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs). These architectures learn differently, and it is unclear whether a single metric, e.g., ASR, can effectively capture adversarial behavior. To address this, we perform a systematic empirical study on four medical image datasets: PathMNIST, DermaMNIST, RetinaMNIST, and CheXpert. We evaluate seven models (VGG-16, ResNet-50, DenseNet-121, Inception-v3, DeiT, Swin Transformer, and ViT-B/16) against seven attack methods at five perturbation budgets, measuring ASR, Peak Signal-to-Noise Ratio (PSNR), Structural Similarity Index Measure (SSIM), and $L_2$ perturbation magnitude. Our findings show a consistent pattern: perceptual and distortion metrics are strongly associated with one another and exhibit minimal correlation with ASR. This applies to both CNNs and ViTs. The results demonstrate that ASR alone is an inadequate indicator of adversarial robustness and transferability. Consequently, we argue that a thorough assessment of adversarial risk in medical AI necessitates multi-metric frameworks that encompass not only the attack efficacy but also its methodology and associated overheads.

</details>


### [224] [PoInit-of-View: Poisoning Initialization of Views Transfers Across Multiple 3D Reconstruction Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16540)
*Weijie Wang,Songlong Xing,Zhengyu Zhao,Nicu Sebe,Bruno Lepri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PoInit-of-View方法，通过在Structure-from-Motion（SfM）初始化阶段引入跨视角梯度不一致性，实现对多种3D重建系统的可迁移投毒攻击，显著降低重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D重建投毒方法仅对整个流水线进行端到端梯度回传，未挖掘SfM初始化这一几何核心模块的特有脆弱性。

Method: 提出PoInit-of-View，优化输入视图的对抗扰动，刻意制造对应3D点投影间的跨视角梯度不一致性，从而破坏关键点检测、特征匹配、位姿估计与三角化过程；并提供理论分析连接不一致性与对应关系崩溃。

Result: 在黑盒迁移设置（如3DGS→NeRF）下，PSNR提升25.1%，SSIM提升16.5%，显著优于单视图基线，在多个系统和数据集上验证有效。

Conclusion: SfM初始化是3D重建系统中一个关键且易受攻击的薄弱环节，针对性投毒可实现高效、可迁移的重建性能退化。

Abstract: Poisoning input views of 3D reconstruction systems has been recently studied. However, we identify that existing studies simply backpropagate adversarial gradients through the 3D reconstruction pipeline as a whole, without uncovering the new vulnerability rooted in specific modules of the 3D reconstruction pipeline. In this paper, we argue that the structure-from-motion (SfM) initialization, as the geometric core of many widely used reconstruction systems, can be targeted to achieve transferable poisoning effects across diverse 3D reconstruction systems. To this end, we propose PoInit-of-View, which optimizes adversarial perturbations to intentionally introduce cross-view gradient inconsistencies at projections of corresponding 3D points. These inconsistencies disrupt keypoint detection and feature matching, thereby corrupting pose estimation and triangulation within SfM, eventually resulting in low-quality rendered views. We also provide a theoretical analysis that connects cross-view inconsistency to correspondence collapse. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our PoInit-of-View on diverse 3D reconstruction systems and datasets, surpassing the single-view baseline by 25.1% in PSNR and 16.5% in SSIM in black-box transfer settings, such as 3DGS to NeRF.

</details>


### [225] [BOOKAGENT: Orchestrating Safety-Aware Visual Narratives via Multi-Agent Cognitive Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16541)
*Bo Gao,Chang Liu,Yuyang Miao,Siyuan Ma,Ser-Nam Lim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BookAgent，一种面向儿童安全的多智能体协作框架，用于端到端生成高质量、安全合规的 illustrated storybook，通过动态图文对齐与全局一致性修复提升叙事连贯性、视觉一致性和安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法多为分阶段生成，缺乏整体多模态对齐；且缺少面向儿童的安全约束整合到叙事规划与序列级多模态验证中。

Method: 提出BookAgent多智能体框架，支持从用户草稿出发的端到端故事书合成，包含联合规划、脚本生成、插图绘制与全局不一致性修复；引入页面级图文动态校准与时间维度上的角色身份和叙事逻辑验证-修正机制。

Result: 在叙事连贯性、视觉一致性与安全合规性上显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: BookAgent为复杂多模态创作中可靠智能体的设计提供了新范式，并将开源实现。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Large Generative Models (LGMs) have revolutionized multi-modal generation. However, generating illustrated storybooks remains an open challenge, where prior works mainly decompose this task into separate stages, and thus, holistic multi-modal grounding remains limited. Besides, while safety alignment is studied for text- or image-only generation, existing works rarely integrate child-specific safety constraints into narrative planning and sequence-level multi-modal verification. To address these limitations, we propose BookAgent, a safety-aware multi-agent collaboration framework designed for high-quality, safety-aware visual narratives. Different from prior story visualization models that assume a fixed storyline sequence, BookAgent targets end-to-end storybook synthesis from a user draft by jointly planning, scripting, illustrating, and globally repairing inconsistencies. To ensure precise multi-modal grounding, BookAgent dynamically calibrates page-level alignment between textual scripts and visual layouts. Furthermore, BookAgent calibrates holistic consistency from the temporal dimension, by verifying-then-rectifying global inconsistencies in character identity and storytelling logic. Extensive experiments demonstrate that BookAgent significantly outperforms current methods in narrative coherence, visual consistency, and safety compliance, offering a robust paradigm for reliable agents in complex multi-modal creation. The implementation will be publicly released at https://github.com/bogao-code/BookAgent/tree/main.

</details>


### [226] [A B-Spline Function Based 3D Point Cloud Unwrapping Scheme for 3D Fingerprint Recognition and Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16546)
*Mohammad Mogharen Askarin,Jiankun Hu,Min Wang,Xuefei Yin,Xiuping Jia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于B样条曲线拟合的3D指纹展开方法，将3D点云转换为灰度图像后使用传统2D识别方法，显著提升识别精度，EER低至0.2072%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决3D指纹识别中因拓扑高度变化导致的纹线提取困难和配准受限问题，提升接触式识别的卫生性、安全性与鲁棒性。

Method: 采用B样条曲线拟合对3D点云指纹进行展开以抑制高度变化、缓解配准限制；再将展开后的点云按相对高度映射为灰度图像，输入传统2D指纹识别流程。

Result: 在三组实验中EER分别为0.2072%、0.26%和0.22%，优于现有方法；跨会话实验中EER为1.50%，亦优于3D指纹展平技术。

Conclusion: 所提展开方法有效提升了3D指纹识别精度与鲁棒性，为高精度、低误差的非接触式生物认证提供了新思路。

Abstract: Three-dimensional (3D) fingerprint recognition and identification offer several advantages over traditional two-dimensional (2D) recognition systems. The contactless nature of 3D fingerprints enhances hygiene and security, reducing the risk of contamination and spoofing. In addition to surface ridge and valley patterns, 3D fingerprints capture depth, curvature, and shape information, enabling the development of more precise and robust authentication systems. Despite recent advancements, significant challenges remain. The topological height of fingerprint pixels complicates the extraction of ridge and valley patterns. Furthermore, registration issues limit the acquisition process, requiring consistent direction and orientation across all samples. To address these challenges, this paper introduces a method that unwraps 3D fingerprints, represented as 3D point clouds, using B-spline curve fitting to mitigate height variation and reduce registration limitations. The unwrapped point cloud is then converted into a grayscale image by mapping the relative heights of the points. This grayscale image is subsequently used for recognition through conventional 2D fingerprint identification methods. The proposed approach demonstrated superior performance in 3D fingerprint recognition, achieving Equal Error Rates (EERs) of 0.2072%, 0.26%, and 0.22% across three experiments, outperforming existing methods. Additionally, the method surpassed 3D fingerprint flattening technique in both recognition and identification during cross-session experiments, achieving an EER of 1.50% when fingerprints with varying registrations were included.

</details>


### [227] [Co-generation of Layout and Shape from Text via Autoregressive 3D Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16552)
*Zhenggang Tang,Yuehao Wang,Yuchen Fan,Jun-Kun Chen,Yu-Ying Yeh,Kihyuk Sohn,Zhangyang Wang,Qixing Huang,Alexander Schwing,Rakesh Ranjan,Dilin Wang,Zhicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的顺序式文本到场景生成范式，通过3D自回归扩散模型3D-ARD+统一处理场景布局与物体生成，实现对复杂空间关系和语义描述的高保真建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到场景生成方法通常只关注布局或物体生成，难以同时兼顾；且生成结果常与文本中关于形状、外观和空间关系的复杂描述不一致。

Method: 提出3D自回归扩散模型3D-ARD+，分两步生成：先在场景空间中自回归生成粗粒度3D潜变量（条件于文本与已生成场景），再在物体空间中扩散生成细粒度3D潜变量（用于解码几何与外观）；并构建含230K室内场景-文本对的大规模数据集。

Result: 在挑战性场景上评估7B参数的3D-ARD+，验证其能准确遵循文本中非平凡的空间布局与语义约束生成并摆放物体。

Conclusion: 3D-ARD+实现了布局与物体生成的统一建模，显著提升了文本到3D场景生成的保真度与可控性，为交互式场景创建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent text-to-scene generation approaches largely reduced the manual efforts required to create 3D scenes. However, their focus is either to generate a scene layout or to generate objects, and few generate both. The generated scene layout is often simple even with LLM's help. Moreover, the generated scene is often inconsistent with the text input that contains non-trivial descriptions of the shape, appearance, and spatial arrangement of the objects. We present a new paradigm of sequential text-to-scene generation and propose a novel generative model for interactive scene creation. At the core is a 3D Autoregressive Diffusion model 3D-ARD+, which unifies the autoregressive generation over a multimodal token sequence and diffusion generation of next-object 3D latents. To generate the next object, the model uses one autoregressive step to generate the coarse-grained 3D latents in the scene space, conditioned on both the current seen text instructions and already synthesized 3D scene. It then uses a second step to generate the 3D latents in the smaller object space, which can be decoded into fine-grained object geometry and appearance. We curate a large dataset of 230K indoor scenes with paired text instructions for training. We evaluate 7B 3D-ARD+, on challenging scenes, and showcase the model can generate and place objects following non-trivial spatial layout and semantics prescribed by the text instructions.

</details>


### [228] [PA-TCNet: Pathology-Aware Temporal Calibration with Physiology-Guided Target Refinement for Cross-Subject Motor Imagery EEG Decoding in Stroke Patients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16554)
*Xiangkai Wang,Yun Zhao,Dongyi He,Qingling Xia,Gen Li,Nizhuan Wang,Ningxiao Peng,Bin Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PA-TCNet框架，通过病理感知的节律状态建模与生理引导的目标域校准，提升卒中患者跨被试运动想象EEG解码性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卒中患者因病灶导致EEG时序动态异常及被试间异质性强，现有迁移方法易受慢波活动和不稳伪标签干扰，难以泛化。

Method: 提出PA-TCNet：1）Pathology-aware Rhythmic State Mamba（PRSM）模块分离慢节律背景与快瞬态扰动，并将病理上下文注入状态传播；2）Physiology-Guided Target Calibration（PGTC）模块基于源域传感器运动区模板施加生理一致性约束，动态优化目标域伪标签。

Result: 在XW-Stroke和2019-Stroke两个独立卒中EEG数据集上，留一被试实验平均准确率分别达66.56%和72.75%，优于现有SOTA方法。

Conclusion: 联合建模病理时序动态与生理约束的伪监督可为卒中后个性化MI-BCI康复提供更鲁棒的跨被试初始化。

Abstract: Stroke patient cross-subject electroencephalography (EEG) decoding of motor imagery (MI) brain-computer interface (BCI) is essential for motor rehabilitation, yet lesion-related abnormal temporal dynamics and pronounced inter-patient heterogeneity often undermine generalization. Existing adaptation methods are easily misled by pathological slow-wave activity and unstable target-domain pseudo-labels. To address this challenge, we propose PA-TCNet, a pathology-aware temporal calibration framework with physiology-guided target refinement for stroke motor imagery decoding. PA-TCNet integrates two coordinated components. The Pathology-aware Rhythmic State Mamba (PRSM) module decomposes EEG spatiotemporal features into slowly varying rhythmic context and fast transient perturbations, injecting the fused pathological context into selective state propagation to more effectively capture abnormal temporal dynamics. The Physiology-Guided Target Calibration (PGTC) module constructs source-domain sensorimotor region-of-interest templates, imposing physiological consistency constraints and dynamically refining target-domain pseudo-labels, thereby improving adaptation reliability. Leave-one-subject-out experiments on two independent stroke EEG datasets, XW-Stroke and 2019-Stroke, yielded mean accuracies of 66.56\% and 72.75\%, respectively, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. These results indicate that jointly modeling pathological temporal dynamics and physiology-constrained pseudo-supervision can provide more robust cross-subject initialization for personalized post-stroke MI-BCI rehabilitation. The implemented code is available at https://github.com/wxk1224/PA-TCNet.

</details>


### [229] [See Through the Noise: Improving Domain Generalization in Gaze Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16562)
*Yanming Peng,Shijing Wang,Yaping Huang,Yi Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了标签噪声对泛化型视线估计性能的负面影响，并提出了一种名为See-Through-Noise（SeeTN）的新框架，通过构建语义嵌入空间、区分噪声/干净样本并进行亲和正则化，提升模型在跨域场景下的鲁棒性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有泛化型视线估计方法常忽视由标注困难导致的标签噪声问题，而该噪声会显著损害模型泛化性能。

Method: 提出SeeTN框架：1）基于原型的变换构建保持拓扑一致性的语义嵌入空间；2）通过特征-标签亲和一致性判别噪声样本；3）在语义流形中引入亲和正则化，实现从干净样本向噪声样本的知识迁移。

Result: SeeTN在不牺牲源域精度的前提下，显著提升了跨域泛化性能，有效缓解了源域标签噪声的负面影响。

Conclusion: 显式建模和处理标签噪声对提升泛化型视线估计的鲁棒性和实用性至关重要。

Abstract: Generalizable gaze estimation methods have garnered increasing attention due to their critical importance in real-world applications and have achieved significant progress. However, they often overlook the effect of label noise, arising from the inherent difficulty of acquiring precise gaze annotations, on model generalization performance. In this paper, we are the first to comprehensively investigate the negative effects of label noise on generalization in gaze estimation. Further, we propose a novel solution, called See-Through-Noise (SeeTN) framework, which improves generalization from a novel perspective of mitigating label noise. Specifically, we propose to construct a semantic embedding space via a prototype-based transformation to preserve a consistent topological structure between gaze features and continuous labels. We then measure feature-label affinity consistency to distinguish noisy from clean samples, and introduce a novel affinity regularization in the semantic manifold to transfer gaze-related information from clean to noisy samples. Our proposed SeeTN promotes semantic structure alignment and enforces domain-invariant gaze relationships, thereby enhancing robustness against label noise. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our SeeTN effectively mitigates the adverse impact of source-domain noise, leading to superior cross-domain generalization without compromising the source-domain accuracy, and highlight the importance of explicitly handling noise in generalized gaze estimation.

</details>


### [230] [Classification of systolic murmurs in heart sounds using multiresolution complex Gabor dictionary and vision transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16563)
*Mahmoud Fakhry,Abeer FathAllah Brery*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多分辨率复Gabor基函数与视觉Transformer的自动收缩期杂音分类系统，实现了95.96%的高分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 收缩期杂音强度、音调和音质变化大，需精确识别以支持心脏疾病诊断，但传统方法难以应对杂音变异性。

Method: 采用复正交匹配追踪（C-OMP）将杂音片段投影到冗余的多分辨率复Gabor基函数字典上，生成可变分辨率时频特征矩阵；多个片段共享同一字典以抑制变异性；随后用CNN进行分块标记化，再通过含多头注意力与一维卷积的Vision Transformer编码器完成分类。

Result: 在CirCor DigiScope数据集上对四类收缩期杂音分类达到95.96%准确率。

Conclusion: 融合多分辨率特征提取与Transformer架构显著提升了心音杂音识别的准确性与鲁棒性，为临床辅助诊断提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Systolic murmurs are extra heart sounds that occur during the contraction phase of the cardiac cycle, often indicating heart abnormalities caused by turbulent blood flow. Their intensity, pitch, and quality vary, requiring precise identification for the accurate diagnosis of cardiac disorders. This study presents an automatic classification system for systolic murmurs using a feature extraction module, followed by a classification model. The feature extraction module employs complex orthogonal matching pursuit to project single or multiple murmur segments onto a redundant dictionary composed of multiresolution complex Gabor basis functions (GBFs). The resulting projection weights are split and reshaped into variable-resolution time--frequency feature matrices. Processing multiple segments of a single recording using a shared dictionary mitigates murmur variability. This is achieved by learning the weights for each segment while enforcing that they correspond to the same set of basis functions in the dictionary, promoting consistent time--frequency feature matrices. The classification model is built based on a vision transformer to process multiple input matrices of different resolutions by passing each through a convolutional neural network for patch tokenization. All embedding tokens are then concatenated to form a matrix and forwarded to an encoder layer that includes multihead attention, residual connections, and a convolutional network with a kernel size of one. This integration of multiresolution feature extraction with transformer-based feature classification enhances the accuracy and reliability of heart murmur identification. An experimental analysis of four types of systolic murmurs from the CirCor DigiScope dataset demonstrates the effectiveness of the system, achieving a classification accuracy of $95.96\%$.

</details>


### [231] [Multilevel neural networks with dual-stage feature fusion for human activity recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16577)
*Abeer FathAllah Brery,Ascensión Gallardo-Antolín,Israel Gonzalez-Carrasco,Mahmoud Fakhry*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于人体活动识别（HAR）的双层网络架构，结合晚期融合与中间层融合，通过实验验证其在两个公开数据集上优于仅用晚期融合或基线模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HAR方法多依赖单一或简单组合模型，难以充分挖掘不同神经网络（如CNN、LSTM、ConvLSTM）的互补优势；结构设计（尤其是特征融合方式）对性能影响关键，但多级融合策略尚未系统探索。

Method: 构建双层网络架构，引入双阶段特征融合：晚期融合（融合第一层各子网络输出）和中间融合（跨层级融合第一层与第二层特征）；系统评估15种CNN/LSTM/ConvLSTM组合架构，在两种基准数据集上进行对比实验。

Result: 融合晚期与中间融合的架构显著提升识别准确率，优于仅用晚期融合的架构及各类基线模型。

Conclusion: 双阶段特征融合的双层架构能更有效地整合多模型互补特征，是提升HAR性能的有效范式。

Abstract: Human activity recognition (HAR) refers to the process of identifying human actions and activities using data collected from sensors. Neural networks, such as convolutional neural networks (CNNs), long short-term memory (LSTM) networks, convolutional LSTM, and their hybrid combinations, have demonstrated exceptional performance in various research domains. Developing a multilevel individual or hybrid model for HAR involves strategically integrating multiple networks to capitalize on their complementary strengths. The structural arrangement of these components is a critical factor influencing the overall performance. This study explores a novel framework of a two-level network architecture with dual-stage feature fusion: late fusion, which combines the outputs from the first network level, and intermediate fusion, which integrates the features from both the first and second levels. We evaluated $15$ different network architectures of CNNs, LSTMs, and convolutional LSTMs, incorporating late fusion with and without intermediate fusion, to identify the optimal configuration. Experimental evaluation on two public benchmark datasets demonstrates that architectures incorporating both late and intermediate fusion achieve higher accuracy than those relying on late fusion alone. Moreover, the optimal configuration outperforms baseline models, thereby validating its effectiveness for HAR.

</details>


### [232] [Camo-M3FD: A New Benchmark Dataset for Cross-Spectral Camouflaged Pedestrian Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16582)
*Henry O. Velesaca,Andrea Mero,Guillermo A. Castillo,Angel D. Sappa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Camo-M3FD数据集，首个面向跨光谱伪装行人检测的基准，填补了安全关键场景下伪装人体检测的研究空白；通过融合可见光与热成像，提升在复杂背景与低可见度下的检测鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伪装目标检测（COD）基准聚焦于生物物种，缺乏面向自动驾驶、安防等安全关键场景的伪装行人检测数据与方法；多光谱检测虽缓解低能见度问题，但对伪装行人（与背景高度相似）的检测仍未被充分研究。

Method: 基于M3FD数据集构建Camo-M3FD——包含配准的可见光-热成像图像对的新基准；采用定量指标筛选高前景-背景相似性样本；提供高质量像素级掩码；建立基于前沿COD模型的标准化评估框架。

Result: 实验表明热成像信号对定位至关重要，但仅靠热图不足以恢复结构细节；多光谱融合显著提升检测精度；所提基准推动了跨光谱伪装行人检测方法的发展。

Conclusion: Camo-M3FD是首个专为安全关键型伪装行人检测设计的跨光谱基准，证实多光谱融合对解决伪装挑战的必要性，为鲁棒行人检测系统提供了基础支撑。

Abstract: Pedestrian detection is fundamental to autonomous driving, robotics, and surveillance. Despite progress in deep learning, reliable identification remains challenging due to occlusions, cluttered backgrounds, and degraded visibility. While multispectral detection-combining visible and thermal sensors-mitigates poor visibility, the challenge of camouflaged pedestrians remains largely unexplored. Existing Camouflaged Object Detection (COD) benchmarks focus on biological species, leaving a gap in safety-critical human detection where targets blend into their surroundings. To address this, we introduce Camo-M3FD (derived from the M3FD dataset), a novel benchmark for cross-spectral camouflaged pedestrian detection, consisting of registered visible-thermal image pairs. The dataset is curated using quantitative metrics to ensure high foreground-background similarity. We provide high-quality pixel-level masks and establish a standardized evaluation framework using state-of-the-art COD models. Our results demonstrate that while thermal signals provide indispensable localization cues, multispectral fusion is essential for refining structural details. Camo-M3FD serves as a foundational resource for developing robust and safety-critical detection systems. The dataset is available on GitHub: https://cod-espol.github.io/Camo-M3FD/

</details>


### [233] [Real-Time Visual Attribution Streaming in Thinking Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16587)
*Seil Kang,Woojung Han,Junhyeok Kim,Jinyeong Kim,Youngeun Kim,Seong Jae Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种摊销框架，用于多模态思维模型中的实时视觉归因流式传输，通过学习从注意力特征中直接估计语义区域的因果效应，在保持因果忠实性的同时实现高效、实时的视觉证据呈现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以兼顾因果归因的忠实性（需高成本反向传播或扰动）与实时性（注意力图虽快但缺乏因果有效性），亟需一种既忠实又高效的视觉归因机制。

Method: 提出一种摊销框架，利用注意力特征中蕴含的丰富信号，学习直接估计语义区域对模型输出的因果影响，避免重复计算。

Result: 在五个基准数据集和四种思维模型上，该方法实现了与穷举式因果方法相当的归因忠实性，并支持实时视觉归因流式输出。

Conclusion: 通过轻量级学习而非暴力计算，可在多模态思维模型中实现真实、实时的视觉归因，为可解释AI提供了新路径。

Abstract: We present an amortized framework for real-time visual attribution streaming in multimodal thinking models. When these models generate code from a screenshot or solve math problems from images, their long reasoning traces should be grounded in visual evidence. However, verifying this reliance is challenging: faithful causal methods require costly repeated backward passes or perturbations, while raw attention maps offer instant access, they lack causal validity. To resolve this, we introduce an amortized approach that learns to estimate the causal effects of semantic regions directly from the rich signals encoded in attention features. Across five diverse benchmarks and four thinking models, our approach achieves faithfulness comparable to exhaustive causal methods while enabling visual attribution streaming, where users observe grounding evidence as the model reasons, not after.
