Our results demonstrate that real-time, faithful attribution in multimodal thinking models is achievable through lightweight learning, not brute-force computation.

</details>


### [234] [MambaKick: Early Penalty Direction Prediction from HAR Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16588)
*Henry O. Velesaca,David Freire-Obregon,Abel Reyes-Angulo,Steven Araujo,Angel Sappa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MambaKick框架，利用预训练的人类动作识别（HAR）嵌入和轻量级Mamba时序模型预测点球射门方向，在真实体育视频中实现低延迟意图预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 守门员需在极短时间内预判罚球方向，现有方法依赖显式运动学重建或手工特征，不够高效鲁棒。

Method: 从接触中心的短视频片段中提取预训练HAR嵌入，结合Mamba模型进行时序建模，并融合场地侧向、惯用脚等上下文元数据。

Result: 在三分类和二分类任务上分别达到53.1%和64.5%准确率，优于或匹配多种强嵌入基线。

Conclusion: 结合预训练HAR表征与高效状态空间时序建模，是实现实时体育视频低延迟意图预测的有效可行路径。

Abstract: Penalty kicks in soccer are decided under extreme time constraints, where goalkeepers benefit from anticipating shot direction from the kickers motion before or around ball contact. In this paper, MambaKick is presented as a learning-based framework for penalty direction prediction that leverages pretrained human action recognition (HAR) embeddings extracted from contact-centered short video segments and combines them with a lightweight temporal predictor. Rather than relying on explicit kinematic reconstruction or handcrafted biomechanical features, the approach reuses transferable spatiotemporal representations and utilizes selective state-spare models (Mamba) for efficient sequence aggregation. Simple contextual metadata (e.g., field side and footedness) are also considered as complementary cues that may reduce ambiguity in real-world footage. Across a range of HAR backbones, MambaKick consistently improves or matches strong embedding baselines, achieving up to 53.1% accuracy for three classes and 64.5% for two classes under the proposed methodology. Overall, the results indicate that combining pretrained HAR representations with efficient state-space temporal modeling is a practical direction for low-latency intention prediction in real-world sports video. The code will be available at GitHub: https://github.com/hvelesaca/MambaKick/

</details>


### [235] [IncepDeHazeGAN: Novel Satellite Image Dehazing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16609)
*Tejeswar Pokuri,Shivarth Rai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为IncepDeHazeGAN的新型生成对抗网络，结合Inception模块与多层特征融合，用于单图像去雾任务，并通过Grad-CAM可解释性技术验证其关注区域与适应性，实验表明其在多个数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升雾天或阴霾条件下遥感图像的视觉质量与清晰度，解决单图像去雾问题。

Method: 提出IncepDeHazeGAN模型，融合Inception模块（实现多尺度特征提取）和多层特征融合机制（增强特征复用），并采用Grad-CAM进行可解释性分析。

Result: 在多个标准数据集上取得当前最优（state-of-the-art）的去雾性能。

Conclusion: IncepDeHazeGAN通过结构创新与可解释性验证，有效提升了单图像去雾效果，具有良好的泛化能力与实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Dehazing is a technique in computer vision for enhancing the visual quality of images captured in cloudy or foggy conditions. Dehazing helps to recover clear, high-quality images from haze-affected remote sensing data. In this study, we introduce IncepDeHazeGAN, a novel Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) involving Inception block and multi-layer feature fusion for the task of single-image dehazing. Utilizing the Inception block allows for multi-scale feature extraction. On the other hand, the multi-layer feature fusion design achieves efficient reuse of features as the features extracted at different convolution layers are fused several times. Grad-CAM XAI technique has been applied to our network, highlighting the regions focused on by the network for dehazing and its adaptation to different haze conditions. Experiments demonstrate that our network achieves state-of-the-art results in several datasets.

</details>


### [236] [AVRT: Audio-Visual Reasoning Transfer through Single-Modality Teachers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16617)
*Edson Araujo,Saurabhchand Bhati,M. Jehanzeb Mirza,Brian Kingsbury,Samuel Thomas,Rogerio Feris,James R. Glass,Hilde Kuehne*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AVRT框架，利用单模态教师模型生成高质量音视频推理轨迹，并通过LLM合并器融合视觉与音频推理轨迹，再经监督微调和强化学习两阶段训练，显著提升多模态推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本推理模型难以迁移到音视频等多模态场景，主因是缺乏高质量、特定组合的多模态推理数据。

Method: 提出AVRT框架：1）分别用视觉和音频专用教师模型生成独立推理轨迹；2）用LLM合并器融合轨迹；3）先进行监督微调（SFT）冷启动，再进行大规模强化学习训练。

Result: 在7个音视频及音频基准测试中，3B和7B参数模型达到同规模模型最优性能，包括OmniBench、DailyOmni（音视频）和MMAR（纯音频），且跨模态训练能正向迁移至单模态任务。

Conclusion: AVRT建立了一种新颖有效的多模态推理模型训练范式，解决了多模态推理数据稀缺问题，并验证了跨模态训练对单模态任务的泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in reasoning models have shown remarkable progress in text-based domains, but transferring those capabilities to multimodal settings, e.g., to allow reasoning over audio-visual data, still remains a challenge, in part because of the limited availability of high-quality reasoning data in targeted multimodal combinations. To address this problem, we introduce AVRT, a novel framework that generates high-quality audio-visual reasoning traces from single-modality teacher models. We generate independent vision- and audio-reasoning traces via models specialized to reason over their respective modalities and merge the resulting traces with an LLM merger model. The resulting multimodal traces are used in a supervised fine-tuning (SFT) cold start to adapt the target model to audio-visual reasoning traces first, before training it in a second reinforcement learning stage on larger-scale data. Evaluated on seven audio-visual and audio benchmarks, our 3B and 7B parameter models achieve state-of-the-art results among models of comparable size including OmniBench and DailyOmni for audio-visual and MMAR for audio-only reasoning, showing that cross-modal training also transfers to single-modality tasks and establishing a new training pipeline for multimodal reasoning models.

</details>


### [237] [Amortized Inverse Kinematics via Graph Attention for Real-Time Human Avatar Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16629)
*Muhammad Saif Ullah Khan,Chen-Yu Wang,Tim Prokosch,Michael Lorenz,Bertram Taetz,Didier Stricker*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出IK-GAT，一种轻量级图注意力网络，用于从稀疏3D关节点位置单次前向推理出全身关节朝向，克服传统迭代IK求解器速度慢、噪声敏感等缺点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统逆运动学（IK）方法在从稀疏3D关节点位置恢复完整关节朝向时存在欠定性（尤其是骨骼扭转模糊）、迭代优化慢且对噪声敏感的问题，难以满足实时人体动画需求。

Method: 提出IK-GAT模型：基于骨骼父子图进行图注意力消息传递；采用骨对齐的世界坐标系旋转表示（显式建模扭转轴）；使用连续6D旋转表示和SO(3)测地线损失训练，并可加入前向运动学一致性正则项。

Result: IK-GAT仅374K参数、CPU上超650 FPS，优于VPoser迭代方法，且对初始姿态和输入噪声鲁棒；输出可直接驱动绑定角色或转换为SMPL类模型姿态参数。

Conclusion: IK-GAT为实时、在线人体动画提供了一种高效、鲁棒、端到端的单帧关节朝向估计新范式。

Abstract: Inverse kinematics (IK) is a core operation in animation, robotics, and biomechanics: given Cartesian constraints, recover joint rotations under a known kinematic tree. In many real-time human avatar pipelines, the available signal per frame is a sparse set of tracked 3D joint positions, whereas animation systems require joint orientations to drive skinning. Recovering full orientations from positions is underconstrained, most notably because twist about bone axes is ambiguous, and classical IK solvers typically rely on iterative optimization that can be slow and sensitive to noisy inputs. We introduce IK-GAT, a lightweight graph-attention network that reconstructs full-body joint orientations from 3D joint positions in a single forward pass. The model performs message passing over the skeletal parent-child graph to exploit kinematic structure during rotation inference. To simplify learning, IK-GAT predicts rotations in a bone-aligned world-frame representation anchored to rest-pose bone frames. This parameterization makes the twist axis explicit and is exactly invertible to standard parent-relative local rotations given the kinematic tree and rest pose. The network uses a continuous 6D rotation representation and is trained with a geodesic loss on SO(3) together with an optional forward-kinematics consistency regularizer. IK-GAT produces animation-ready local rotations that can directly drive a rigged avatar or be converted to pose parameters of SMPL-like body models for real-time and online applications. With 374K parameters and over 650 FPS on CPU, IK-GAT outperforms VPoser-based per-frame iterative optimization without warm-start at significantly lower cost, and is robust to initial pose and input noise

</details>


### [238] [Tri-Modal Fusion Transformers for UAV-based Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16630)
*Craig Iaboni,Pramod Abichandani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于无人机目标检测的三模态（RGB、热红外、事件相机）融合框架，采用双流分层视觉Transformer与新型跨模态融合模块（MAGE和BiTE），并在首个同步三模态UAV数据集上进行了系统验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有UAV目标检测方法在光照变化、运动模糊和场景动态性下鲁棒性不足；RGB线索易受抑制，而热红外（LWIR）和事件相机分别在低光对比度和微秒级时序边缘方面具有互补优势，但三者统一融合尚未被系统研究。

Method: 提出双流分层视觉Transformer架构，引入Modality-Aware Gated Exchange（MAGE）进行通道与空间门控交换，以及Bidirectional Token Exchange（BiTE）实现带深度可分离优化的双向token级注意力；构建首个10,489帧同步RGB-热-事件UAV数据集，并开展61组受控消融实验。

Result: 三模态融合显著优于所有双模态基线；融合深度影响显著；轻量级CSSA变体以极小开销恢复大部分性能增益；首次建立三模态UAV检测系统基准与模块化骨干网络。

Conclusion: 三模态协同可有效提升UAV目标检测在复杂环境下的鲁棒性；所提MAGE+BiTE融合机制与数据集为后续多模态感知研究提供了坚实基础与标准化平台。

Abstract: Reliable UAV object detection requires robustness to illumination changes, motion blur, and scene dynamics that suppress RGB cues. Thermal long-wave infrared (LWIR) sensing preserves contrast in low light, and event cameras retain microsecond-level temporal edges, but integrating all three modalities in a unified detector has not been systematically studied. We present a tri-modal framework that processes RGB, thermal, and event data with a dual-stream hierarchical vision transformer. At selected encoder depths, a Modality-Aware Gated Exchange (MAGE) applies inter-sensor channel and spatial gating, and a Bidirectional Token Exchange (BiTE) module performs bidirectional token-level attention with depthwise-pointwise refinement, producing resolution-preserving fused maps for a standard feature pyramid and two-stage detector.
  We introduce a 10,489-frame UAV dataset with synchronized and pre-aligned RGB-thermal-event streams and 24,223 annotated vehicles across day and night flights. Through 61 controlled ablations, we evaluate fusion placement, mechanism (baseline MAGE+BiTE, CSSA, GAFF), modality subsets, and backbone capacity. Tri-modal fusion improves over all dual-modal baselines, with fusion depth having a significant effect and a lightweight CSSA variant recovering most of the benefit at minimal cost. This work provides the first systematic benchmark and modular backbone for tri-modal UAV-based object detection.

</details>


### [239] [A Benchmark Study of Segmentation Models and Adaptation Strategies for Landslide Detection from Satellite Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16663)
*Md Kowsher,Weiwei Zhan,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了CNN、Transformer和大预训练基础模型在高分辨率卫星图像滑坡检测任务中的性能，并比较了全量微调与参数高效微调（如LoRA、AdaLoRA）的效果，发现Transformer模型表现优异，而参数高效微调可在大幅减少可训练参数（达95%）的同时保持相近精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 滑坡检测对灾害响应和风险评估至关重要，但当前主流分割模型及其微调策略在此任务上的相对有效性尚缺乏系统评估。

Method: 基于GDCLD数据集，在统一训练与评估协议下，对比卷积神经网络、Transformer分割模型及大预训练基础模型；同时比较全量微调与参数高效微调方法（LoRA、AdaLoRA），并分析分布偏移下的泛化能力。

Result: Transformer模型在分割性能上表现强劲；参数高效微调可将可训练参数减少最多95%，精度与全量微调相当；在分布偏移场景下，模型验证集与留出测试集性能存在差异。

Conclusion: Transformer架构更适合滑坡检测任务，且参数高效微调是一种高效实用的部署策略，兼顾性能与计算资源节约。

Abstract: Landslide detection from high resolution satellite imagery is a critical task for disaster response and risk assessment, yet the relative effectiveness of modern segmentation architectures and finetuning strategies for this problem remains insufficiently understood. In this work, we present a systematic benchmarking study of convolutional neural networks, transformer based segmentation models, and large pre-trained foundation models for landslide detection. Using the Globally Distributed Coseismic Landslide Dataset (GDCLD) dataset, we evaluate representative CNN- and transformer-based segmentation models alongside large pretrained foundation models under consistent training and evaluation protocols. In addition, we compare full fine-tuning with parameter-efficient fine-tuning methods, including LoRA and AdaLoRA, to assess their performance efficiency tradeoffs. Experimental results show that transformer-based models achieve strong segmentation performance, while parameter efficient finetuning reduces trainable parameters by up to 95% with comparable accuracy to full finetuning. We further analyze generalization under distribution shift by comparing validation and held-out test performance.

</details>


### [240] [Appearance-free Action Recognition: Zero-shot Generalization in Humans and a Two-Pathway Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16675)
*Prerana Kumar,Martin A. Giese*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究人类和视觉模型在零样本情况下对无外观（appearance-free）真实世界动作视频的泛化能力，发现人类表现显著高于随机水平；提出一种受格式塔共同命运原则启发的双通路3D CNN模型，在无外观视频上泛化能力强于现有模型，验证了运动表征的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究人类与视觉模型是否能在未经显式训练的情况下，零样本泛化到保留运动但去除静态形状线索的真实世界动作视频，以理解动作识别中运动与形态线索的计算贡献。

Method: 通过实验室心理物理实验测试22名被试在自然视频（UCF5）训练后对两类无外观视频（AFD5密集噪声视频和随机点视频）的零样本识别能力；构建融合RGB（形态）流与光流（运动）流的双通路3D CNN模型，并引入受Gestalt共同命运启发的相干性门控机制。

Result: 人类在两类无外观视频上识别准确率显著高于随机水平；所提双通路模型在无外观数据集上泛化性能优于当前主流视频分类模型，且运动通路对无外观泛化至关重要，形态通路主要提升自然视频识别性能。

Conclusion: 运动表征是零样本泛化至无外观动作视频的关键，多通路架构（尤其强调运动通路）更符合人类动作识别机制，为建模鲁棒动作识别提供了新思路。

Abstract: Action recognition is a fundamental ability for social species. Yet, its underlying computations are not well understood. Classical psychophysical studies using simplified stimuli have shown that humans can perceive body motion even under degradation of relevant shape cues. Recent work using real-world action videos and their appearance-free counterparts (that preserve motion but lack static shape cues) included explicit training of humans and models on the appearance-free videos. Whether humans and vision models generalize in a zero-shot manner to appearance-free transformations of real-world action videos is not yet known. To measure this generalization in humans, we conducted a laboratory-based psychophysics experiment. 22 participants were trained to recognize five action categories using naturalistic videos (UCF5 dataset), and tested zero-shot on two types of appearance-free transformations: (i) dense-noise motion videos from an existing dataset (AFD5) and (ii) random-dot appearance-free videos. We find that participants recognize actions in both types of appearance-free videos well above chance, albeit with reduced accuracy compared to naturalistic videos. To model this behavior, we developed a two-pathway 3D CNN-based model combining an RGB (form) stream and an optical flow (motion) stream, including a coherence-gating mechanism inspired by Gestalt common-fate grouping. Our model generalizes to both appearance-free datasets and outperforms contemporary video classification models, narrowing the gap to human performance. We find that the motion pathway is critical for generalization to appearance-free videos, while the form pathway improves performance on naturalistic videos. Our findings highlight the importance of motion-based representations for generalization to appearance-free videos, and support the use of multi-stream architectures to model video-based action recognition.

</details>


### [241] [C-GenReg: Training-Free 3D Point Cloud Registration by Multi-View-Consistent Geometry-to-Image Generation with Probabilistic Modalities Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16680)
*Yuval Haitman,Amit Efraim,Joseph M. Francos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: C-GenReg是一种无需训练的3D点云配准框架，通过将几何配准问题迁移至图像域，利用世界尺度生成先验与注册导向的视觉基础模型（VFMs）协同工作，实现零样本、跨域泛化强的配准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的3D点云配准方法难以跨感知模态、采样差异和环境泛化。

Method: C-GenReg利用世界基础模型将输入点云生成多视角一致的RGB图像，再用预训练VFM提取像素级对应关系，并通过深度图提升回3D空间；进一步提出'Match-then-Fuse'概率冷融合机制，融合生成RGB分支与原始几何分支的对应后验分布。

Result: 在室内（3DMatch、ScanNet）和室外（Waymo）基准上展现出强零样本性能与卓越跨域泛化能力；首次成功在无图像数据的真实室外LiDAR数据上运行生成式配准框架。

Conclusion: C-GenReg是零样本、即插即用的配准框架，所有模块均预训练且无需微调，兼顾几何与语义信息，提升了鲁棒性与置信度校准能力。

Abstract: We introduce C-GenReg, a training-free framework for 3D point cloud registration that leverages the complementary strengths of world-scale generative priors and registration-oriented Vision Foundation Models (VFMs). Current learning-based 3D point cloud registration methods struggle to generalize across sensing modalities, sampling differences, and environments. Hence, C-GenReg augments the geometric point cloud registration branch by transferring the matching problem into an auxiliary image domain, where VFMs excel, using a World Foundation Model to synthesize multi-view-consistent RGB representations from the input geometry. This generative transfer, preserves spatial coherence across source and target views without any fine-tuning. From these generated views, a VFM pretrained for finding dense correspondences extracts matches. The resulting pixel correspondences are lifted back to 3D via the original depth maps. To further enhance robustness, we introduce a "Match-then-Fuse" probabilistic cold-fusion scheme that combines two independent correspondence posteriors, that of the generated-RGB branch with that of the raw geometric branch. This principled fusion preserves each modality inductive bias and provides calibrated confidence without any additional learning. C-GenReg is zero-shot and plug-and-play: all modules are pretrained and operate without fine-tuning. Extensive experiments on indoor (3DMatch, ScanNet) and outdoor (Waymo) benchmarks demonstrate strong zero-shot performance and superior cross-domain generalization. For the first time, we demonstrate a generative registration framework that operates successfully on real outdoor LiDAR data, where no imagery data is available.

</details>


### [242] [LOD-Net: Locality-Aware 3D Object Detection Using Multi-Scale Transformer Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16696)
*Mustaqeem Khan,Aidana Nurakhmetova,Wail Gueaieb,Abdulmotaleb El Saddik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成在3DETR中的多尺度注意力（MSA）机制，通过上采样生成高分辨率特征图以提升小物体和语义相关物体的检测性能，在ScanNetv2上mAP@25和mAP@50分别提升约1%和4.78%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点云数据稀疏且缺乏全局结构，导致3D目标检测困难。

Method: 在3DETR架构中引入多尺度注意力（MSA）机制，并加入上采样操作以生成高分辨率特征图，增强局部几何与全局上下文建模能力。

Result: 在ScanNetv2数据集上，3DETR+MSA模型相比基线在mAP@25提升近1%，mAP@50提升4.78%；而对轻量版3DETR-m应用MSA效果有限，表明需适配轻量模型的上采样策略。

Conclusion: 将分层特征提取与注意力机制结合可有效提升3D场景理解能力，尤其在处理小物体和语义关联物体时优势明显。

Abstract: 3D object detection in point cloud data remains a challenging task due to the sparsity and lack of global structure inherent in the input. In this work, we propose a novel Multi-Scale Attention (MSA) mechanism integrated into the 3DETR architecture to better capture both local geometry and global context. Our method introduces an upsampling operation that generates high-resolution feature maps, enabling the network to better detect smaller and semantically related objects. Experiments conducted on the ScanNetv2 dataset demonstrate that our 3DETR + MSA model improves detection performance, achieving a gain of almost 1% in mAP@25 and 4.78% in mAP@50 over the baseline. While applying MSA to the 3DETR-m variant shows limited improvement, our analysis reveals the importance of adapting the upsampling strategy for lightweight models. These results highlight the effectiveness of combining hierarchical feature extraction with attention mechanisms in enhancing 3D scene understanding.

</details>


### [243] [iDocV2: Leveraging Self-Supervision and Open-Set Detection for Improving Pattern Spotting in Historical Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16726)
*Jose M. Saavedra,Crhistopher Stears,Marcelo Pizarro,Cristóbal Loyola,Luis Aros*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于改进编码器iDoc和开放集检测器的新模型，用于提升历史文档中图形模式检索的精度与速度，尤其在小尺寸非方形查询上达到新SOTA精度0.612，并将搜索速度提升10倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文档检索与模式识别方法在精度（尤其小非方形查询仅0.427）和速度（达7秒）方面存在不足，难以应对数字图书大规模普及的需求。

Method: 提出基于自监督训练的改进编码器iDoc和开放集检测器，并引入非极大值抑制（NMS）降低误检率。

Result: 在小非方形查询上精度达0.612（SOTA），整体模式识别精度提升，搜索速度提高10倍（相比SOTA模型）。

Conclusion: 所提模型在保持竞争力的同时显著提升效率与特定场景精度，验证了编码器改进与检测策略优化的有效性。

Abstract: Considering the imminent massification of digital books, it has become critical to facilitate searching collections through graphical patterns. Current strategies for document retrieval and pattern spotting in historical documents still need to be improved. State-of-the-art strategies achieve an overall precision of $0.494$ for pattern spotting, where the precision for small non-square queries reaches 0.427. In addition, the processing time is excessive, requiring up to 7 seconds for searching in the DocExplore dataset due to a dense-based strategy used by SOTA models. Therefore, we propose a new model based on a better encoder (iDoc), trained under a self-supervised strategy, and an open-set detector to accelerate searching. Our model achieves competitive results with state-of-the-art pattern spotting and document retrieval, improving speed by 10x. Furthermore, our model reaches a new SOTA performance on the small non-square queries, achieving a new precision of 0.612.Different from the previous version, this leverages non-maximum suppression to reduce false positives.

</details>


### [244] [Agentic Large Language Models for Training-Free Neuro-Radiological Image Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16729)
*Ayhan Can Erdur,Daniel Scholz,Jiazhen Pan,Benedikt Wiestler,Daniel Rueckert,Jan C. Peeken*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的智能体（agentic）框架，利用大语言模型（LLM）协同专业外部工具，实现端到端自动脑MRI分析，涵盖预处理、病灶分割与纵向评估，并发布新基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型缺乏原生3D空间推理能力，难以直接处理CT/MRI等体数据；而新兴的智能体AI可通过调用专用工具绕过该限制，但其在复杂放射科多步工作流中的可行性尚未充分探索。

Method: 构建训练-free的智能体流水线，集成多个商用LLM（GPT-5.1、Gemini 3 Pro、Claude Sonnet 4.5）与即插即用的领域工具（如颅骨剥离、配准、病灶分割），支持单智能体与多智能体（按领域分工）两种架构，并基于BraTS数据构建并开源图像-提示-答案评测数据集。

Result: 系统成功完成从单次扫描分割、体积报告到需多时间点对比的纵向疗效评估等递进式任务；多智能体协作在复杂任务中表现优于单智能体；所有LLM均无需微调即可达成高精度分析。

Conclusion: 智能体AI通过工具调用可有效解决高难度神经放射影像分析任务，无需模型训练或参数更新，为医学AI提供了一条轻量、灵活、可扩展的新路径。

Abstract: State-of-the-art large language models (LLMs) show high performance in general visual question answering. However, a fundamental limitation remains: current architectures lack the native 3D spatial reasoning required for direct analysis of volumetric medical imaging, such as CT or MRI. Emerging agentic AI offers a new solution, eliminating the need for intrinsic 3D processing by enabling LLMs to orchestrate and leverage specialized external tools. Yet, the feasibility of such agentic frameworks in complex, multi-step radiological workflows remains underexplored. In this work, we present a training-free agentic pipeline for automated brain MRI analysis. Validating our methodology on several LLMs (GPT-5.1, Gemini 3 Pro, Claude Sonnet 4.5) with off-the-shelf domain-specific tools, our system autonomously executes complex end-to-end workflows, including preprocessing (skull stripping, registration), pathology segmentation (glioma, meningioma, metastases), and volumetric analysis. We evaluate our framework across increasingly complex radiological tasks, from single-scan segmentation and volumetric reporting to longitudinal response assessment requiring multi-timepoint comparisons. We analyze the impact of architectural design by comparing single-agent models against multi-agent "domain-expert" collaborations. Finally, to support rigorous evaluation of future agentic systems, we introduce and release a benchmark dataset of image-prompt-answer tuples derived from public BraTS data. Our results demonstrate that agentic AI can solve highly neuro-radiological image analysis tasks through tool use without the need for training or fine-tuning.

</details>


### [245] [Active World-Model with 4D-informed Retrieval for Exploration and Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16733)
*Elaheh Vaezpour,Amirhosein Javadi,Tara Javidi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AW4RE，一种以感知意识为中心的生成式世界模型，用于在部分可观测环境中优化传感决策，通过4D信息检索、动作条件几何支持和时序一致性以及条件生成补全来估计动作条件下的观测过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理感知在大型动态环境中面临部分可观测性挑战，传统强化学习和POMDP方法难以应对真实世界探索成本高及仿真到现实迁移中未观测视角的问题。

Method: 提出AW4RE模型，融合4D信息引导的证据检索、动作条件下的几何支撑与时序一致性建模、以及条件生成式补全，构建传感器原生的代理环境以模拟传感查询。

Result: 实验表明，AW4RE在极端视角变化、时间间隔和稀疏几何支撑下，相比几何感知生成基线，能产生更扎实、更一致的预测结果。

Conclusion: AW4RE为部分可观测物理感知任务提供了一种有效的生成式世界建模框架，提升了传感决策的鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Physical awareness, especially in a large and dynamic environment, is shaped by sensing decisions that determine observability across space, time, and scale, while observations impact the quality of sensing decisions. This loopy information structure makes physical awareness a fundamentally challenging decision problem with partial observations. While in the past decade we have witnessed the unprecedented success of reinforcement learning (RL) in problems with full observability, decision problems with partial observation, such as POMDPs, remain largely open: real-world explorations are excessively costly, while sim-to-real pipeline suffer from unobserved viewpoints. We introduce AW4RE (Active World-model with 4D-informed Retrieval for Exploration), an awareness-centric generative world model that provides a sensor-native surrogate environment for exploring sensing queries. Conditioned on a queried sensing action, AW4RE estimates the action-conditioned observation process. This is done by combining 4D-informed evidence retrieval, action-conditioned geometric support with temporal coherence, and conditional generative completion. Experiments demonstrate that AW4RE produces more grounded and consistent predictions than geometry-aware generative baselines under extreme viewpoint shifts, temporal gaps, and sparse geometric support.

</details>


### [246] [Reducing Peak Memory Usage for Modern Multimodal Large Language Model Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16734)
*Junwan Kim,Hyunkyung Bae*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在预填充阶段进行结构感知的键值缓存压缩机制，以控制多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）推理过程中的内存增长，在保持生成性能的同时显著降低峰值内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多模态大语言模型处理高分辨率图像和长视频，视觉token数量激增，导致KV缓存内存消耗成为推理瓶颈；现有压缩方法仅在输入处理完毕后执行，无法缓解预填充阶段的高内存峰值。

Method: 利用MLLMs固有的结构规律性和表征冗余性，设计一种顺序式输入压缩机制，在预填充过程中动态执行结构感知的KV缓存压缩，强制维持固定内存预算。

Result: 显著降低峰值内存使用，同时仅造成轻微性能下降，提升多模态推理的实用性与内存效率。

Conclusion: 结构感知的在线缓存压缩可有效平衡内存开销与生成质量，为大规模多模态模型的高效部署提供新路径。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have recently demonstrated strong capabilities in understanding and generating responses from diverse visual inputs, including high-resolution images and long video sequences. As these models scale to richer visual representations, inference increasingly relies on storing large numbers of vision tokens in the key-value (KV) cache, making memory consumption a central bottleneck. Existing methods address this issue by identifying redundancy in vision tokens and compressing the cache, but such compression is typically applied only after all inputs are processed, resulting in high peak memory usage during the prefill stage. In this work, we show that MLLMs exhibit inherent structural regularities and representational redundancy that can be exploited to control memory growth throughout inference. Based on this insight, we propose a sequential input-compression mechanism that enforces a fixed memory budget by performing structure-aware key-value cache compression during the prefill process. This approach substantially reduces peak memory usage while maintaining generative performance with only minimal degradation, enabling more practical and memory-efficient multimodal inference.

</details>


### [247] [Automated Palynological Analysis System: Integrating Deep Metric Learning and $U^{2}$-Net Detection in $H\infty$ bright field microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16743)
*J. Staforelli-Vivanco,R. Jofré,B. Muñoz,V. Salamanca,P. Coelho,I. Sanhueza,L. Viafora,C. Toro,J. Troncoso,M. Rondanelli-Reyes,I. Lamas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合H∞鲁棒机械控制与深度学习的高通量显微镜系统，用于智利比奥比奥地区花粉的自动计数、分类与形态分析，显著提升效率与准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蜜蜂花粉学（melissopalynology）耗时且主观，单个样本需4–6小时，亟需自动化、高精度、高通量的替代方案。

Method: 构建集成H∞鲁棒机械控制的自动化显微系统；采用U²-Net进行显著目标检测；以DINOv2视觉Transformer为骨干，结合深度度量学习训练分类模型；引入梯度加权注意力机制实现可解释的纹理与诊断特征标注。

Result: 系统分类召回率达95.8%，处理速度较人工专家分析提升6倍。

Conclusion: 该系统有效克服了传统花粉分析的效率与主观性瓶颈，为区域蜂源花粉溯源与生态监测提供了可靠、可扩展的技术支撑。

Abstract: Traditional melissopalynology is a time-consuming and subjective process, often taking 4-6 hours per sample. We present an automated, high-throughput microscopy system that integrates $H\infty$ robust mechanical control with advanced deep learning pipelines for the precise counting, classification, and morphological analysis of pollen grains from Bio Bio region in south central territory in Chile. Our system employs $U^{2}$-Net for salient object detection and a DINOv2 Vision Transformer backbone trained via Deep Metric Learning for classification. By integrating Gradient-Weighted Attention, the model provides human-interpretable texture and diagnostic feature annotations. The system achieves a 95.8$\%$ classification recall and a 6x processing speedup compared to manual expert analysis.

</details>


### [248] [Incoherent Deformation, Not Capacity: Diagnosing and Mitigating Overfitting in Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16747)
*Ahmad Droby*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了动态3D高斯泼溅（Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting）在单目视频中泛化差的主因：高斯分裂导致容量过载，以及形变场缺乏一致性；提出弹性能量正则化（EER）等方法显著缩小训练-测试PSNR差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态3D高斯泼溅在训练视角上表现优异，但在测试视角泛化能力差，存在显著的训练-测试PSNR差距，亟需理解其根本原因并提升泛化性。

Method: 通过系统消融实验分析自适应密度控制各组件（尤其是分裂操作）的影响；提出弹性能量正则化（EER）约束高斯形变场的局部平滑性，并结合GAD阈值策略与PTDrop高斯丢弃机制；在D-NeRF、Deformable-3DGS及真实单目视频（HyperNeRF）上验证有效性。

Result: 分裂操作贡献超80%的PSNR泛化差距；EER单独降低差距40.8%，联合GAD与PTDrop达57%；EER使高斯平均应变降低99.72%；方法在不同形变架构和真实数据上均有效，证实泛化失败主因是形变不一致而非参数量。

Conclusion: 动态3D高斯泼溅的过拟合源于形变场的不一致性，而非模型容量本身；引入形变相干性正则化（如EER）是提升泛化的关键路径。

Abstract: Dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting methods achieve strong training-view PSNR on monocular video but generalize poorly: on the D-NeRF benchmark we measure an average train-test PSNR gap of 6.18 dB, rising to 11 dB on individual scenes. We report two findings that together account for most of that gap.
  Finding 1 (the role of splitting). A systematic ablation of the Adaptive Density Control pipeline (split, clone, prune, frequency, threshold, schedule) shows that splitting is responsible for over 80% of the gap: disabling split collapses the cloud from 44K to 3K Gaussians and the gap from 6.18 dB to 1.15 dB. Across all threshold-varying ablations, gap is log-linear in count (r = 0.995, bootstrap 95% CI [0.99, 1.00]), which suggests a capacity-based explanation.
  Finding 2 (the role of deformation coherence). We show that the capacity explanation is incomplete. A local-smoothness penalty on the per-Gaussian deformation field -- Elastic Energy Regularization (EER) -- reduces the gap by 40.8% while growing the cloud by 85%. Measuring per-Gaussian strain directly on trained checkpoints, EER reduces mean strain by 99.72% (median 99.80%) across all 8 scenes; on 8/8 scenes the median Gaussian under EER is less strained than the 1st-percentile (best-behaved) Gaussian under baseline. Alongside EER, we evaluate two further regularizers: GAD, a loss-rate-aware densification threshold, and PTDrop, a jitter-weighted Gaussian dropout. GAD+EER reduces the gap by 48%; adding PTDrop and a soft growth cap reaches 57%. We confirm that coherence generalizes to (a) a different deformation architecture (Deformable-3DGS, +40.6% gap reduction at re-tuned lambda), and (b) real monocular video (4 HyperNeRF scenes, reducing the mean PSNR gap by 14.9% at the same lambda as D-NeRF, with near-zero quality cost). The overfitting in dynamic 3DGS is driven by incoherent deformation, not parameter count.

</details>


### [249] [TriTS: Time Series Forecasting from a Multimodal Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16748)
*Xiang Ao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TriTS是一种新型跨模态解耦框架，将一维时间序列投影到时间、频率和二维视觉空间，结合周期感知重塑、视觉Mamba和多分辨率小波混合模块，在长期时间序列预测中实现SOTA性能，同时降低参数量和推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期时间序列预测（LTSF）面临真实信号时序动态高度纠缠、纯一维建模难以充分表征的挑战，现有视觉Transformer方法存在计算复杂度高（O(N²)）的问题。

Method: 提出TriTS框架：1）周期感知重塑策略+视觉Mamba（Vim）建模跨周期全局视觉纹理；2）多分辨率小波混合（MR-WM）模块在频域显式解耦非平稳信号的趋势与噪声；3）保留时域流式线性分支保障数值稳定性；4）动态融合三模态表征。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能，显著优于现有基于视觉的预测器，同时大幅降低参数量和推理延迟。

Conclusion: TriTS通过跨模态解耦与高效建模，突破了一维时序表征瓶颈，为长期时间序列预测提供了兼具高性能、低开销与强适应性的新范式。

Abstract: Time series forecasting plays a pivotal role in critical sectors such as finance, energy, transportation, and meteorology. However, Long-term Time Series Forecasting (LTSF) remains a significant challenge because real-world signals contain highly entangled temporal dynamics that are difficult to fully capture from a purely 1D perspective. To break this representation bottleneck, we propose TriTS, a novel cross-modal disentanglement framework that projects 1D time series into orthogonal time, frequency, and 2D-vision spaces.To seamlessly bridge the 1D-to-2D modality gap without the prohibitive $O(N^2)$ computational overhead of Vision Transformers (ViTs), we introduce a Period-Aware Reshaping strategy and incorporate Visual Mamba (Vim). This approach efficiently models cross-period dependencies as global visual textures while maintaining linear computational complexity. Complementing this, we design a Multi-Resolution Wavelet Mixing (MR-WM) module for the frequency modality, which explicitly decouples non-stationary signals into trend and noise components to achieve fine-grained time-frequency localization. Finally, a streaming linear branch is retained in the time domain to anchor numerical stability. By dynamically fusing these three complementary representations, TriTS effectively adapts to diverse data contexts. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that TriTS achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance, fundamentally outperforming existing vision-based forecasters by drastically reducing both parameter count and inference latency.

</details>


### [250] [Frozen Vision Transformers for Dense Prediction on Small Datasets: A Case Study in Arrow Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16758)
*Maxwell Shepherd*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种仅需48张标注图像（5084个箭孔）即可实现室内射箭靶面箭孔自动检测、定位与计分的系统，结合颜色校正、冻结的自监督ViT（DINOv3）与AnyUp特征上采样、轻量CenterNet检测头，在小数据下达到媲美甚至优于大数据监督方法的性能（F1=0.893，定位误差1.41 mm）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决室内射箭靶面箭孔检测任务中标注数据稀缺、高精度定位难的问题，探索小样本密集预测的新范式。

Method: 采用三阶段流程：1）基于颜色的靶面标准坐标系映射（透视校正）；2）冻结DINOv3 ViT-L/16主干+AnyUp引导特征上采样以恢复亚毫米精度；3）轻量CenterNet式热图检测头；仅训练3.8M参数（占总数308M的~1.2%）。

Result: 交叉验证平均F1为0.893±0.011，平均定位误差1.41±0.06 mm；箭孔得分平均误差1.8%，群心定位中位误差4.00 mm；消融显示CenterNet偏移回归头无益于检测且损害定位精度。

Conclusion: 冻结基础模型+极简适配可在小数据密集预测任务中取得高性能，为类似场景提供实用新范式。

Abstract: We present a system for automated detection, localization, and scoring of arrow punctures on 40\,cm indoor archery target faces, trained on only 48 annotated photographs (5{,}084 punctures). Our pipeline combines three components: a color-based canonical rectification stage that maps perspective-distorted photographs into a standardized coordinate system where pixel distances correspond to known physical measurements; a frozen self-supervised vision transformer (DINOv3 ViT-L/16) paired with AnyUp guided feature upsampling to recover sub-millimeter spatial precision from $32 \times 32$ patch tokens; and lightweight CenterNet-style detection heads for arrow-center heatmap prediction. Only 3.8\,M of 308\,M total parameters are trainable. Across three cross-validation folds, we achieve a mean F1 score of $0.893 \pm 0.011$ and a mean localization error of $1.41 \pm 0.06$\,mm, comparable to or better than prior fully-supervised approaches that require substantially more training data. An ablation study shows that the CenterNet offset regression head, typically essential for sub-pixel refinement, provides negligible detection improvement while degrading localization in our setting. This suggests that guided feature upsampling already resolves the spatial precision lost through patch tokenization. On downstream archery metrics, the system recovers per-image average arrow scores with a median error of 1.8\% and group centroid positions to within a median of 4.00\,mm. These results demonstrate that frozen foundation models with minimal task-specific adaptation offer a practical paradigm for dense prediction in small-data regimes.

</details>


### [251] [FairNVT: Improving Fairness via Noise Injection in Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16780)
*Qiaoyue Tang,Sepidehsadat Hosseini,Mengyao Zhai,Thibaut Durand,Greg Mori*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FairNVT 是一种轻量级去偏框架，通过适配器学习任务相关与敏感属性嵌入，注入校准高斯噪声并融合，结合正交约束与公平性正则化，在表征和预测层面同步提升公平性，同时保持任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去偏方法常将表征公平性与预测公平性分开处理，而本文认为二者本质关联：抑制表征层的敏感信息有助于实现更公平的预测。

Method: FairNVT 使用轻量级适配器分别学习任务相关嵌入和敏感属性嵌入；对敏感嵌入添加校准的高斯噪声，并与任务嵌入融合；引入正交约束和公平性正则化联合减少敏感信息泄露。

Result: 在涵盖视觉与语言的三个数据集上，FairNVT 降低了敏感属性攻击者准确率，提升了人口均等性（demographic parity）与机会均等性（equalized odds）指标，同时维持了高任务性能。

Conclusion: FairNVT 有效实现了表征与预测层面的协同去偏，是一种通用、高效且兼容多种预训练Transformer编码器的公平性增强框架。

Abstract: This paper presents FairNVT, a lightweight debiasing framework for pretrained transformer-based encoders that improves both representation and prediction level fairness while preserving task accuracy. Unlike many existing debiasing approaches that address these notions separately, we argue they are inherently connected: suppressing sensitive information at the representation level can facilitate fairer predictions. Our approach learns task-relevant and sensitive embeddings via lightweight adapters, applies calibrated Gaussian noise to the sensitive embedding, and fuses it with the task representation. Together with orthogonality constraints and fairness regularization, these components jointly reduce sensitive-attribute leakage in the learned embeddings and encourage fairer downstream predictions. The framework is compatible with a wide range of pretrained transformer encoders. Across three datasets spanning vision and language, FairNVT reduces sensitive-attribute attacker accuracy, improves demographic-parity and equalized-odds metrics, and maintains high task performance.

</details>


### [252] [EdgeVTP: Exploration of Latency-efficient Trajectory Prediction for Edge-based Embedded Vision Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16783)
*Seungjin Kim,Reza Jafarpourmarzouni,Christopher Neff,Hamed Tabkhi,Vinit Katariya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EdgeVTP是一种面向嵌入式边缘设备的车辆轨迹预测方法，通过交互感知图建模、轻量Transformer主干和单次曲线解码器，在保证低且确定性端到端延迟的同时，达到SOTA精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署在路侧边缘设备上的车辆轨迹预测需满足有界、确定性的端到端延迟要求，而现有方法难以兼顾实时性与精度。

Method: 提出EdgeVTP：采用交互感知图建模（带硬性邻居数量限制的局部图）、轻量Transformer主干、以及基于紧凑曲线参数（锚定于最后观测位置）的一次性解码器，替代传统自回归航点预测。

Result: 在三个高速公路基准数据集和两类Jetson边缘平台上，EdgeVTP实现了最低实测端到端延迟（含图构建与后处理），并在其中两个数据集上达到SOTA精度，在其余基准上误差具有竞争力。

Conclusion: EdgeVTP验证了嵌入式优先设计可在严格实时约束下实现高精度轨迹预测，为车路协同系统提供了实用化解决方案。

Abstract: Vehicle trajectory prediction is central to highway perception, but deployment on roadside edge devices necessitates bounded, deterministic end-to-end latency. We present EdgeVTP, an embedded-first trajectory predictor that combines interaction-aware graph modeling with a lightweight transformer backbone and a one-shot curve decoder. By predicting future motion as compact curve parameters (anchored at the last observed position) rather than horizon-scaled autoregressive waypoints, EdgeVTP reduces decoding overhead while producing smooth trajectories. To keep runtime predictable in crowded scenes, we explicitly bound interaction complexity via a locality graph with a hard neighbor cap. Across three highway benchmarks and two Jetson-class platforms, EdgeVTP achieves the lowest measured end-to-end latency under a protocol that includes graph construction and post-processing, while attaining state-of-the-art (SotA) prediction accuracy on two of the three datasets and competitive error on other benchmarks. Our code is available at https://github.com/SeungjinStevenKim/EdgeVTP.

</details>


### [253] [Bridging Coarse and Fine Recognition: A Hybrid Approach for Open-Ended Multi-Granularity Object Recognition in Interactive Educational Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16785)
*Hanling Yi,Feng Lin,Mao Luo,Yifan Yang,Xiaotian Yu,Rong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HyMOR框架，结合多模态大语言模型（MLLM）与CLIP模型，实现跨粒度的开放对象识别：MLLM负责开放、粗粒度识别，CLIP专注细粒度领域对象（如动植物）；在自建教材图像数据集TBO及多项评测中显著提升细粒度与通用识别性能，增强多模态内容生成与交互学习能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM擅长开放、粗粒度对象识别但细粒度能力弱；CLIP类模型细粒度强但类别覆盖窄；需融合二者优势以支持教育、游戏等需多粒度感知的下游应用。

Method: 提出Hybrid Multi-granularity open-ended Object Recognition（HyMOR）框架：协同使用MLLM（主干用于开放/粗粒度识别）与CLIP模型（专用于细粒度领域对象识别），并构建教材导向数据集TBO（20,942图像，8,816类别）支撑评估。

Result: HyMOR将细粒度识别差距缩小至比CLIP仅低0.2%；通用对象识别比基线MLLM提升2.5%（SBert相似度）；在全部评测数据集上平均SBert提升23.2%。

Conclusion: HyMOR通过MLLM与CLIP协同实现多粒度对象识别，在保持开放性的同时显著增强细粒度能力，为多模态内容生成与交互式教育/游戏应用提供了更鲁棒的感知基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have enabled open-ended object recognition, yet they struggle with fine-grained tasks. In contrast, CLIP-style models excel at fine-grained recognition but lack broad coverage of general object categories. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{HyMOR}, a \textbf{Hy}brid \textbf{M}ulti-granularity open-ended \textbf{O}bject \textbf{R}ecognition framework that integrates an MLLM with a CLIP model. In HyMOR, the MLLM performs open-ended and coarse-grained object recognition, while the CLIP model specializes in fine-grained identification of domain-specific objects such as animals and plants. This hybrid design enables accurate object understanding across multiple semantic granularities, serving as a robust perceptual foundation for downstream multi-modal content generation and interactive gameplay. To support evaluation in content-rich and educational scenarios, we introduce TBO (TextBook Objects), a dataset containing 20,942 images annotated with 8,816 object categories extracted from textbooks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HyMOR narrows the fine-grained recognition gap with CLIP to 0.2\% while improving general object recognition by 2.5\% over a baseline MLLM, measured by average Sentence-BERT (SBert) similarity. Overall, HyMOR achieves a 23.2\% improvement in average SBert across all evaluated datasets, highlighting its effectiveness in enabling accurate perception for multi-modal game content generation and interactive learning applications.

</details>


### [254] [Improving Radio Interferometry Imaging by Explicitly Modeling Cross-Domain Consistency in Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16794)
*Kai Cheng,Ruoqi Wang,Qiong Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CDCRec，一种多模态射电干涉数据重建方法，通过显式建模可见度域与图像域间的跨域一致性，提升成像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法局限于单一模态（仅脏图或仅可见度）重建，导致丢失跨域互补信息，无法充分建模域间依赖与一致性。

Method: 提出CDCRec，采用分层多任务多阶段框架，显式建模可见度域与图像域之间的跨域一致性，并引入自监督互补建模策略。

Result: 实验表明CDCRec能更好提取跨域相关性，显著提升成像性能，尤其在从受限源域数据中恢复密集信息的干涉域翻译任务上优于当前方法。

Conclusion: 跨域一致性建模对射电干涉数据重建至关重要，CDCRec为多模态天文数据处理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Radio astronomy plays a crucial role in understanding the universe, particularly within the realm of non-thermal astrophysics. Images of celestial objects are derived from the signals (called visibility) measured by radio telescopes. Such imaging results, called dirty images, contain artifacts due to factors such as sparsity and therefore require reconstruction to improve imaging quality. Existing methods typically restrict reconstruction to a unimodal domain, either to the dirty image after imaging or to the sparse visibility prior to imaging. Focusing solely on each unimodal reconstruction results in the loss of complementary in-context information in either the visibility or image domain, leading to an incomplete modeling of mutual dependency and consistency. To address these challenges, we propose CDCRec, a multimodal radio interferometric data reconstruction method that explicitly models cross-domain consistency. We design a hierarchical multi-task and multi-stage framework to enhance the exploration of interplays between domains during reconstruction. Our experimental results demonstrate that CDCRec improves imaging performance through enhanced cross-domain correlation extraction. In particular, our self-supervised complementary modeling strategy is better than current methods at interferometric domain translations that rely heavily on recovering dense information from constrained source-domain data.

</details>


### [255] [Generative Semantic Communication via Alternating Dual-Domain Posterior Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16796)
*Shunpu Tang,Qianqian Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种交替双域后验采样（ADDPS）方法，用于生成式语义通信（SemCom）中的语义解码，通过交替在潜在域和图像域上施加一致性约束，克服了现有单域引导方法的局限性，实现了最优感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式语义通信接收器依赖最大后验（MAP）估计，无法保持数据分布，限制感知质量；扩散模型中单域引导存在噪声敏感或解码器偏差问题，简单融合双域又导致伪后验过自信。

Method: 将语义解码建模为贝叶斯反问题，理论证明后验采样可最优保持数据分布；据此提出交替双域后验采样（ADDPS），在扩散采样过程中交替执行潜在域与图像域的一致性约束。

Result: 在FFHQ数据集上的实验表明，ADDPS相比现有方法显著提升了重建图像的感知质量。

Conclusion: 后验采样是实现最优感知质量的理论基础，ADDPS通过交替双域策略有效融合两域优势，在不引发梯度冲突的前提下提升语义解码性能。

Abstract: Generative semantic communication (SemCom) harnesses pretrained generative priors to improve the perceptual quality of wireless image transmission. Existing generative SemCom receivers, however, rely on maximum a posteriori (MAP) estimation, which fundamentally cannot preserve the data distribution and thus limits achievable perceptual quality. Moreover, current diffusion-based approaches using single-domain guidance face significant limitations: latent-domain guidance is sensitive to channel noise, while image-domain guidance inherits decoder bias. Simply combining both domains simultaneously yields an overconfident pseudo-posterior. In this paper, we formulate semantic decoding as a Bayesian inverse problem and prove that posterior sampling achieves optimal perceptual quality by preserving the data distribution. Building on this insight, we propose alternating dual-domain posterior sampling (ADDPS), a diffusion-based SemCom receiver that alternately enforces latent-domain and image-domain consistency during the sampling process. This alternating strategy decomposes joint posterior sampling into simpler subproblems, avoiding gradient conflicts while retaining the complementary strengths of both domains. Experiments on FFHQ demonstrate that the proposed ADDPS achieves superior perceptual quality compared with existing methods.

</details>


### [256] [Frequency-Decomposed INR for NIR-Assisted Low-Light RGB Image Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16800)
*Ligen Shi,Zengyu Pang,Chang Liu,Shuchen Sun,Jun Qiu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于频率解耦隐式神经表示（FDINR）的近红外（NIR）辅助低光照图像恢复方法，通过多尺度小波分解和双分支隐式神经网络，结合RGB与NIR在不同频段的互补先验，并引入不确定性加权损失，有效缓解噪声、结构退化、色彩失真与伪影问题，同时支持任意分辨率重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低光照下可见光图像存在严重噪声和高频结构退化，传统空间域刚性融合易导致色彩失真和伪影。

Method: 基于RGB-NIR跨模态频率统计先验，采用多尺度小波变换显式解耦图像频率成分，构建双分支隐式神经表示框架；设计跨模态差异化频率监督机制（RGB指导低频亮度/色彩，NIR约束高频纹理），并引入不确定性自适应加权损失函数。

Result: FD-INR在恢复亮度一致性与结构细节方面表现优异；得益于隐式连续表征，在任意分辨率重建任务上显著优于现有方法，提升了低光感知可靠性。

Conclusion: FDINR通过频率域解耦建模与自适应跨模态监督，为低光照图像恢复提供了更鲁棒、更灵活的新范式。

Abstract: Addressing the issues of severe noise and high frequency structural degradation in visible images under low-light conditions, this paper proposes a Near Infrared (NIR) aided low light image restoration method based on Frequency Decoupled Implicit Neural Representation (FDINR). Based on the statistical prior of RGB-NIR cross-modal frequency correlations, specifically that low-frequency RGB signals are more reliable, whereas high frequency NIR signals exhibit higher correlation, we explicitly decompose images into distinct frequency components via multi-scale wavelet transforms and construct a dual-branch implicit neural representation framework. Within this framework, we design a cross modal differentiated frequency supervision mechanism, leveraging low light RGB to guide the reconstruction of low frequency luminance and color, and utilizing high-SNR NIR signals to constrain the generation of high frequency texture details, thereby achieving complementary advantages in the frequency domain. Furthermore, an uncertainty-based adaptive weighting loss function is introduced to automatically balance the contributions of different frequency tasks, solving the problems of color distortion and artifacts caused by rigid fusion in the spatial domain common in traditional methods. Experimental results demonstrate that FD-INR not only effectively restores image luminance consistency and structural details but also, benefitting from its implicit continuous representation, outperforms existing methods in arbitrary-resolution reconstruction tasks, significantly enhancing the reliability of low light perception.

</details>


### [257] [Channel Attention-Guided Cross-Modal Knowledge Distillation for Referring Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16806)
*Chen Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通道注意力引导的跨模态知识蒸馏方法，用于轻量化 referring image segmentation（RIS）模型，在不增加推理参数的前提下显著提升学生模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RIS方法依赖大规模多模态编码器，参数量大，难以部署于计算资源受限场景。

Method: 提出通道注意力引导的跨模态知识蒸馏，将教师网络中视觉-语言高阶细粒度关联及各通道语义成分关系迁移至学生网络；区别于像素级关系蒸馏，兼顾知识迁移与学生独立学习能力。

Result: 在两个公开数据集上验证，该方法推理时不引入额外参数，学生模型性能显著提升。

Conclusion: 所提蒸馏方法有效缓解学习偏差传递，在保持轻量化的同时提升RIS模型精度，适用于资源受限场景。

Abstract: Referring image segmentation (RIS) requires accurate segmentation of target regions in images according to language descriptions, which is a cross-modal task integrating vision and language. Existing RIS methods typically employ large-scale vision and language encoding models to improve performance, but their enormous parameter size severely restricts deployment in scenarios with limited computing resources. To solve this problem, this paper proposes a channel attention-guided cross-modal knowledge distillation method, which transfers the high-order fine-grained correlations between vision and language learned by the teacher network, as well as the correlations between semantic components represented by each channel, to the student network. Compared with the traditional pixel-wise relational distillation, this method not only enables the student to learn the knowledge of the teacher, but also retains part of its independent learning ability, alleviating the transfer of learning bias. Experimental results on two public datasets show that the proposed distillation method does not introduce additional parameters during inference and can achieve significant performance improvement for the student model.

</details>


### [258] [Modeling Biomechanical Constraint Violations for Language-Agnostic Lip-Sync Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16808)
*Hao Chen,Junnan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BioLip框架，利用口周关键点的时间抖动（temporal lip jitter）这一生物力学不一致特征检测唇形同步深度伪造，具有跨语言、种族和录制条件的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有唇音同步深度伪造检测器依赖像素级伪影或音视频对应关系，无法跨语言泛化，因其捕捉的是数据相关模式而非普适物理规律。

Method: 提出基于口面部生物力学约束缺失导致的时间唇部抖动（temporal lip jitter）这一新判据，并构建轻量级BioLip框架，仅使用MediaPipe提取的64个口周关键点坐标进行检测。

Result: 所提方法在多种语言、种族及录制条件下均表现出稳定且一致的检测性能，验证了时间唇部抖动作为通用伪造线索的有效性。

Conclusion: 时间唇部抖动是一种由生成模型忽略真实生物力学约束所引发的普适性伪造信号，可支撑更鲁棒、泛化性更强的唇音同步深度伪造检测。

Abstract: Current lip-sync deepfake detectors rely on pixel-level artifacts or audio-visual correspondence, failing to generalize across languages because these cues encode data-dependent patterns rather than universal physical laws. We identify a more fundamental principle: generative models do not enforce the biomechanical constraints of authentic orofacial articulation, producing measurably elevated temporal lip variance -- a signal we term temporal lip jitter -- that is empirically consistent across the speaker's language, ethnicity, and recording conditions. We instantiate this principle through BioLip, a lightweight framework operating on 64 perioral landmark coordinates extracted by MediaPipe.

</details>


### [259] [Hierarchical Vision Transformer Enhanced by Graph Convolutional Network for Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16823)
*Haibin Jiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合层次化Vision Transformer与图卷积网络（GCN）的新型图像分类模型GCN-HViT，以解决ViT中补丁尺寸选择、位置编码空间建模不足以及GCN缺乏全局结构建模能力等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT在图像分类中取得突破，但存在补丁尺寸选择困难、1D位置嵌入无法准确捕捉空间结构、GCN缺乏全局建模能力、ViT缺乏局部结构建模能力等关键挑战。

Method: 提出GCN-HViT：1）设计层次化ViT，实现单层内全局补丁交互与跨层大小补丁的层级关系建模；2）引入GCN作为局部特征提取器，生成2D位置嵌入并建模局部补丁交互。

Result: 在3个真实数据集上实验表明，GCN-HViT达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: GCN-HViT有效融合了ViT的全局建模能力和GCN的局部结构建模能力，提升了图像分类性能，并为解决ViT和GCN各自的局限性提供了新思路。

Abstract: Vision Transformer (ViT) has brought new breakthroughs to the field of image classification by introducing the self-attention mechanism and Graph Convolutional Networks(GCN) have been proposed and successfully applied in data representation and analysis. However, there are key challenges which limit their further development: (1) The patch size selected by ViT is crucial for accurate predictions, which raises a natural question: How to select the size of patches properly or how to comprehensively combine small patches and larger patches; (2) While the spatial structure information is important in vision tasks, the 1D position embeddings fails to capture the spatial structure information of patches more accurately; (3) The GCN can capture the local connectivity relationships between image nodes, but it lacks the ability to capture global graph structural information. On the contrary, the self-attention mechanism of ViT can draw the global relation on image patches, but it is unable to model the local structure of image. To overcome such limitations, we propose the Hierarchical Vision Transformer Enhanced by Graph Convolutional Network (GCN-HViT) for image classification. Specifically, the Hierarchical ViT we designed can model patch-wise information interactions on a global scale within each level and model hierarchical relationships between small patches and large patches across multiple levels. In addition, the proposed GCN method functions as a local feature extractor to obtain the local representation of each image patch which serves as a 2D position embedding of each patch in the 2D space. Meanwhile, it models patch-wise information interactions on a local scale within each level. Extensive experiments on 3 real-world datasets demonstrate that GCN-HViT achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [260] [Lorentz Framework for Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16836)
*Zahid Hasan,Masud Ahmed,Nirmalya Roy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双曲洛伦兹模型的语义分割新框架，克服了传统庞加莱球模型的数值不稳定性与优化难题，支持像素级和掩码分类，并能自然提供不确定性估计、置信度图、边界检测、层次/文本检索及零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双曲语义分割方法多依赖于存在数值不稳定、优化困难和计算挑战的庞加莱球模型，亟需更稳定高效的替代方案。

Method: 提出架构无关的洛伦兹空间语义分割框架，融合文本嵌入与视觉线索指导像素级层次表征学习；无需黎曼优化器，支持标准梯度优化；引入洛伦兹锥嵌入中的新型不确定性与置信度指标，并开展梯度分析以理解洛伦兹优化机制。

Result: 在ADE20K、COCO-Stuff-164k、Pascal-VOC和Cityscapes数据集上，结合DeepLabV3、SegFormer、Mask2Former等主流模型验证了方法有效性与泛化性；实现了稳定高效训练、免费不确定性估计、置信图生成、边界 delineation、层次与文本检索及零样本能力。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹双曲嵌入为鲁棒、不确定性感知的语义分割提供了有前景的新范式，兼具理论可解释性与工程实用性。

Abstract: Semantic segmentation in hyperbolic space enables compact modeling of hierarchical structure while providing inherent uncertainty quantification. Prior approaches predominantly rely on the Poincaré ball model, which suffers from numerical instability, optimization, and computational challenges. We propose a novel, tractable, architecture-agnostic semantic segmentation framework (pixel-wise and mask classification) in the hyperbolic Lorentz model. We employ text embeddings with semantic and visual cues to guide hierarchical pixel-level representations in Lorentz space. This enables stable and efficient optimization without requiring a Riemannian optimizer, and easily integrates with existing Euclidean architectures. Beyond segmentation, our approach yields free uncertainty estimation, confidence map, boundary delineation, hierarchical and text-based retrieval, and zero-shot performance, reaching generalized flatter minima. We introduce a novel uncertainty and confidence indicator in Lorentz cone embeddings. Further, we provide analytical and empirical insights into Lorentz optimization via gradient analysis. Extensive experiments on ADE20K, COCO-Stuff-164k, Pascal-VOC, and Cityscapes, utilizing state-of-the-art per-pixel classification models (DeepLabV3 and SegFormer) and mask classification models (mask2former and maskformer), validate the effectiveness and generality of our approach. Our results demonstrate the potential of hyperbolic Lorentz embeddings for robust and uncertainty-aware semantic segmentation. Code is available at https://github.com/mxahan/Lorentz_semantic_segmentation.

</details>


### [261] [When Earth Foundation Models Meet Diffusion: An Application to Land Surface Temperature Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16841)
*Yiheng Chen,Zihui Ma,Peishi Jiang,Yilong Dai,Qikai Hu,Xinyue Ye,Lingyao Li,Rita Sousa,Runlong Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EFDiff框架，利用地球基础模型Prithvi-EO-2.0引导扩散模型，实现地表温度（LST）在32倍极端降质下的超分辨率重建，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地表温度（LST）超分辨率对环境监测至关重要，但粗粒度热观测严重缺乏细尺度结构信息，导致重建困难。

Method: 提出Earth Foundation Model-guided Diffusion（EFDiff），利用Prithvi-EO-2.0将高分辨率多光谱反射率编码为地理空间嵌入，并通过交叉注意力机制注入去噪网络，指导从高度退化观测中重建细尺度结构；设计EFDiff-ε和EFDiff-x₀两种变体以平衡感知真实性和像素保真度。

Result: 在涵盖242,416个共配准Landsat热-反射图像块的全球多样性基准上，EFDiff在32×超分任务中持续超越基线方法；验证了EFM交叉注意力条件优于HLS通道拼接。

Conclusion: EFDiff是一种有效利用预训练地球基础模型引导生成式重建的新范式，不仅适用于LST超分辨率，还可推广至其他可借助地理空间表征的遥感重建任务。

Abstract: Land surface temperature (LST) super-resolution is important for environmental monitoring. However, it remains challenging as coarse thermal observations severely underdetermine fine-scale structure. In this paper, we propose Earth Foundation Model-guided Diffusion (EFDiff), a novel framework for super-resolution under extreme spatial degradation. EFDiff uses the Prithvi-EO-2.0 Earth foundation model to encode high-resolution multispectral reflectance into geospatial embeddings, which are injected into the denoising network via cross-attention to guide fine-scale reconstruction from highly degraded observations. We study two variants, EFDiff-$ε$ and EFDiff-$x_0$, which offer complementary trade-offs between perceptual realism and pixel-level fidelity. We evaluate EFDiff under an extreme $32\times$ scale gap using a globally diverse benchmark comprising 242,416 co-registered Landsat thermal-reflectance patches. Results show that EFDiff consistently outperforms baseline methods and that cross-attention conditioning by EFM is more effective than HLS channel concatenation. Although we present EFDiff in the context of LST super-resolution, the framework is broadly applicable to remote sensing problems in which pretrained geospatial representations can guide generative reconstruction.

</details>


### [262] [TowerDataset: A Heterogeneous Benchmark for Transmission Corridor Segmentation with a Global-Local Fusion Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16848)
*Xu Cui,Xinyan Liu,Chen Yang,Zhaobo Qi,Beichen Zang,Weigang Zhang,Antoni B. Chan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TowerDataset数据集和全局-局部融合框架，以解决输电走廊点云细粒度语义分割中真实数据稀缺、长程结构建模难等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共数据集样本少、类别粗、场景短，难以反映真实巡检中的长程依赖、长尾分布及关键部件细微差异，导致模型评估不充分、全局与局部信息融合能力不明。

Method: 构建包含661个真实场景、24.66亿点的TowerDataset数据集，并提出全局-局部融合框架：全局分支采用NoCrop训练与原型对比学习捕获长程拓扑，局部分支分块处理保留几何细节，再通过几何验证融合优化预测。

Result: 在TowerDataset及两个公开基准上的实验表明，新数据集具有挑战性，所提框架在复杂异构输电走廊场景中展现出更强的鲁棒性和对稀有/易混淆部件的识别能力。

Conclusion: TowerDataset填补了输电走廊细粒度分割领域高质量真实数据的空白，所提全局-局部融合框架有效兼顾长程结构与局部几何细节，为智能电力巡检提供了可靠技术支撑。

Abstract: Fine-grained semantic segmentation of transmission-corridor point clouds is fundamental for intelligent power-line inspection. However, current progress is limited by realistic data scarcity and the difficulty of modeling global corridor structure and local geometric details in long, heterogeneous scenes. Existing public datasets usually provide only a few coarse categories or short cropped scenes which overlook long-range structural dependencies, severe long-tail distributions, and subtle distinctions among safety-critical components. As a result, current methods are difficult to evaluate under realistic inspection settings, and their ability to preserve and integrate complementary global and local cues remains unclear.
  To address the above challenges, we introduce TowerDataset, a heterogeneous benchmark for transmission-corridor segmentation. TowerDataset contains 661 real-world scenes and about 2.466 billion points. It preserves long corridor extents, defines a fine-grained 22-class taxonomy, and provides standardized splits and evaluation protocols.
  In addition, we present a global-local fusion framework which preserves and fuses whole-scene and local-detail information. A whole-scene branch with NoCrop training and prototypical contrastive learning captures long-range topology and contextual dependencies. A block-wise local branch retains fine geometric structures. Both predictions are then fused and refined by geometric validation. This design allows the model to exploit both global relationships and local shape details when recognizing rare and confusing components. Experiments on TowerDataset and two public benchmarks demonstrate the challenge of the proposed benchmark and the robustness of our framework in real, complex, and heterogeneous transmission-corridor scenes. The dataset will be released soon at https://huggingface.co/datasets/tccx18/Towerdataset/tree/main.

</details>


### [263] [CATP: Confidence-Aware Token Pruning for Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16854)
*Yuhan Gao,Shuhao Kang,Xin He,Bing Li,Xu Cheng,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向伪装目标检测（COD）的分层置信度感知令牌剪枝框架（CATP），通过剪枝易区分令牌并补偿特征信息，在显著降低计算开销的同时保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer-based COD检测器虽精度高，但计算开销大，限制了实际部署。

Method: 提出分层置信度感知令牌剪枝框架（CATP），在不同层级识别并剪枝背景与目标内部易区分令牌，聚焦边界关键令牌；引入双路径特征补偿机制，聚合被剪枝令牌的上下文信息以增强特征。

Result: 在多个COD基准上实验表明，该方法大幅降低计算复杂度，同时维持高检测精度。

Conclusion: CATP为COD模型在真实场景中的高效部署提供了有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Camouflaged Object Detection (COD) aims to segment targets that share extreme textural and structural similarities with their complex environments. Leveraging their capacity for long-range dependency modeling, Transformer-based detectors have become the mainstream approach and achieve state-of-the-art (SoTA) accuracy, yet their substantial computational overhead severely limits practical deployment. To address this, we propose a hierarchical Confidence-Aware Token Pruning framework (CATP) tailored for COD. Our approach hierarchically identifies and discards easily distinguishable tokens from both background and object interiors, focusing computations on critical boundary tokens. To compensate for information loss from pruning, we introduce a dual-path feature compensation mechanism that aggregates contextual knowledge from pruned tokens into enriched features. Extensive experiments on multiple COD benchmarks demonstrate that our method significantly reduces computational complexity while maintaining high accuracy, offering a promising research direction for the efficient deployment of COD models in real-world scenarios. The code will be released.

</details>


### [264] [When W4A4 Breaks Camouflaged Object Detection: Token-Group Dual-Constraint Activation Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16855)
*Tianqi Li,Wenyu Fang,Xin He,Xue Geng,Xu Cheng,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出COD-TDQ方法，针对伪装目标检测（COD）任务中低比特量化时因背景token主导激活范围而导致边界细节丢失的问题，通过Token分组与双重约束范围投影，在不重训练前提下显著提升W4A4量化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伪装目标检测（COD）对细微纹理和边界线索敏感，但在边缘设备上需低比特量化以满足内存和延迟限制；然而现有量化方法在COD任务中易因背景token激活值重尾分布导致关键边界信息被量化为零，性能急剧下降。

Method: 提出COD-TDQ：包含Direct-Sum Token-Group（DSTG）抑制跨token范围主导，以及Dual-Constraint Range Projection（DCRP）约束每组token的步长-离散比和零bin质量，实现4位激活下的鲁棒量化。

Result: 在四个COD基准和CFRN、ESCNet两个模型上，COD-TDQ相比SOTA量化方法Sα得分平均提升超0.12，且无需重训练。

Conclusion: COD任务存在独特的量化瓶颈——token局部范围主导问题；COD-TDQ通过任务感知的双约束token分组量化策略，有效缓解该瓶颈，为边缘端COD部署提供了高效可行方案。

Abstract: Camouflaged object detection (COD) segments objects that intentionally blend with the background, so predictions depend on subtle texture and boundary cues. COD is often needed under tight on-device memory and latency budgets, making low-bit inference highly desirable. However, COD is unusually hard to quantify aggressively. We study post-training W4A4 quantization of Transformer-based COD and find a task-specific cliff: heavy-tailed background tokens dominate a shared activation range, inflating the step size and pushing weak-but-structured boundary cues into the zero bin. This exposes a token-local bottleneck -- remove cross-token range domination and bound the zero-bin mass under 4-bit activations. To address this, we introduce COD-TDQ, a COD-aware Token-group Dual-constraint activation Quantization method. COD-TDQ addresses this token-local bottleneck with two coupled steps: Direct-Sum Token-Group (DSTG) assigns token-group scales to suppress cross-token range domination, and Dual-Constraint Range Projection (DCRP) projects each token-group clip range to keep the step-to-dispersion ratio and the zero-bin mass bounded. Across four COD benchmarks and two baseline models (CFRN and ESCNet), COD-TDQ consistently achieves an Sαscore more than 0.12 higher than that of the state-of-the-art quantization method without retraining. The code will be released.

</details>


### [265] [Q-DeepSight: Incentivizing Thinking with Images for Image Quality Assessment and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16858)
*Xudong Li,Jiaxi Tan,Ziyin Zhou,Yan Zhong,Zihao Huang,Jingyuan Zheng,Yan Zhang,Xiawu Zheng,Rongrong Ji*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-DeepSight，一种结合图像与语言的多模态链式推理框架，用于图像质量评估，支持定位质量问题并提供可操作反馈；通过感知课程奖励和证据梯度过滤提升训练效果，并在多个基准上达到SOTA；还提出无需训练的Perceptual-in-Generation（PiG）方法实现评估-增强闭环。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLLM的图像质量评估方法采用单次、纯语言范式，缺乏视觉证据支撑，难以提供可靠、可操作的局部反馈，无法满足生成模型与图像修复中对感知批评者的需求。

Method: 提出Q-DeepSight框架，采用交错式多模态链式推理（iMCoT），结合工具增强的证据获取（如裁剪缩放）；引入感知课程奖励（PCR）缓解奖励稀疏性，以及证据梯度过滤（EGF）优化视觉 grounding 的信用分配；进一步设计无需训练的Perceptual-in-Generation（PiG）框架，利用诊断结果指导迭代图像增强。

Result: 在自然图像、修复图像及AI生成图像等多样化基准上达到SOTA性能；成功验证PiG框架可有效实现评估驱动的图像增强闭环。

Conclusion: Q-DeepSight通过模拟人类证据驱动的判断过程，显著提升了IQA模型的可解释性、定位能力与实用性，为感知驱动的图像生成与修复提供了新范式。

Abstract: Image Quality Assessment (IQA) models are increasingly deployed as perceptual critics to guide generative models and image restoration. This role demands not only accurate scores but also actionable, localized feedback. However, current MLLM-based methods adopt a single-look, language-only paradigm, which departs from human evidence-seeking judgment and yields weakly grounded rationales, limiting their reliability for in-the-loop refinement. We propose Q-DeepSight, a think-with-image framework that emulates this human-like process. It performs interleaved Multimodal Chain-of-Thought (iMCoT) with tool-augmented evidence acquisition (e.g., crop-and-zoom) to explicitly determine where quality degrades and why. To train these long iMCoT trajectories via reinforcement learning, we introduce two techniques: Perceptual Curriculum Reward (PCR) to mitigate reward sparsity and Evidence Gradient Filtering (EGF) to improve credit assignment for visually-grounded reasoning. Q-DeepSight achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse benchmarks, including natural, restored, and AI-generated content. Furthermore, we demonstrate its practical value with Perceptual-in-Generation (PiG), a training-free framework where Q-DeepSight's diagnoses guide iterative image enhancement, effectively closing the loop between assessment and refinement.

</details>


### [266] [Adaptive Forensic Feature Refinement via Intrinsic Importance Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16879)
*Jiazhen Yang,Junjun Zheng,Kejia Chen,Xiangheng Kong,Jie Lei,Zunlei Feng,Bingde Hu,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出I2P框架，通过自适应识别关键表征层并约束任务驱动参数更新，提升合成图像检测在未知生成源上的泛化能力，同时保护视觉基础模型的跨模态预训练结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于视觉基础模型（VFM）的合成图像检测方法在适应策略上较粗粒度，缺乏对最优表征层级的显式建模，且直接微调易损害支持开集泛化的跨模态预训练结构。

Method: 将VFM适配重构为联合优化问题：一方面自适应识别最利于携带伪造判别信息的关键表征层；另一方面将任务驱动的参数更新限制在低敏感性参数子空间内。

Result: I2P框架在跨分布泛化性能上优于现有方法，能更有效地检测来自未知生成源的合成图像，同时较好保持VFM的原始跨模态结构和迁移能力。

Conclusion: I2P通过内在重要性感知机制，在任务特异性与预训练结构保护之间取得更好平衡，为VFM在合成图像检测中的鲁棒适配提供了新范式。

Abstract: With the rapid development of generative models and multimodal content editing technologies, the key challenge faced by synthetic image detection (SID) lies in cross-distribution generalization to unknown generation sources. In recent years, visual foundation models (VFM), which acquire rich visual priors through large scale image-text alignment pretraining, have become a promising technical route for improving the generalization ability of SID. However, existing VFM-based methods remain relatively coarse-grained in their adaptation strategies. They typically either directly use the final layer representations of VFM or simply fuse multi layer features, lacking explicit modeling of the optimal representational hierarchy for transferable forgery cues. Meanwhile, although directly fine-tuning VFM can enhance task adaptation, it may also damage the cross-modal pretrained structure that supports open-set generalization. To address this task specific tension, we reformulate VFM adaptation for SID as a joint optimization problem: it is necessary both to identify the critical representational layer that is more suitable for carrying forgery discriminative information and to constrain the disturbance caused by task knowledge injection to the pretrained structure. Based on this, we propose I2P, an SID framework centered on intrinsic importance perception. I2P first adaptively identifies the critical layer representations that are most discriminative for SID, and then constrains task-driven parameter updates within a low sensitivity parameter subspace, thereby improving task specificity while preserving the transferable structure of pretrained representations as much as possible.

</details>


### [267] [Bias-constrained multimodal intelligence for equitable and reliable clinical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16884)
*Cheng Li,Weijian Huang,Jiarun Liu,Hao Yang,Qi Yang,Song Wu,Ye Li,Hairong Zheng,Shanshan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BiasCareVL，一种偏见感知的多模态学习框架，通过在模型设计中直接引入偏见控制机制（如自适应不确定性建模与人机协同优化），提升医疗视觉-语言系统在分布不平衡下的公平性与鲁棒性；在344万样本、15种影像模态上训练，在8个公开临床基准上全面超越20种SOTA方法，并在部分任务上超越人类专家水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗影像与临床文本融合催生了通用医疗AI，但普遍存在疾病流行度失衡、解剖区域分布偏差、成像协议异质性及人群人口学差异等偏见问题，严重威胁模型在真实临床场景中的公平性与可靠性。

Method: 提出BiasCareVL框架，将偏见控制内嵌于模型设计：采用自适应不确定性建模动态调节主导数据模式的影响，并支持可选的人机协同精调机制，以在分布不平衡下实现公平推理；统一表征空间支持多种临床任务（VQA、分类、分割、报告生成）；基于3.44百万跨模态样本训练。

Result: 在涵盖皮肤科、肿瘤科、放射科和病理科的8个公开基准上，全面优于20种SOTA方法；多类皮肤病变诊断准确率提升超10%，小肿瘤分割Dice系数提升超20%；与认证放射科医生对比，诊断性能超越人类且耗时显著减少。

Conclusion: BiasCareVL通过前置式偏见建模，实现了更公平、可靠、通用的医疗多模态AI；开源该框架旨在推动医疗AI向透明、可复现、公平及临床可信的方向发展。

Abstract: The integration of medical imaging and clinical text has enabled the emergence of generalist artificial intelligence (AI) systems for healthcare. However, pervasive biases, such as imbalanced disease prevalence, skewed anatomical region distributions, heterogeneous imaging protocols, and demographic disparities, pose significant challenges to the fairness and reliability of vision-language systems in real-world clinical settings. Here we present BiasCareVL, a bias-aware multimodal learning framework that introduces bias control directly into model design, rather than treating it as a post hoc correction. BiasCareVL incorporates adaptive uncertainty modeling with optional human-in-the-loop refinement to regulate the influence of dominant data patterns and to promote equitable reasoning under distributional imbalance. Trained on 3.44 million samples spanning over 15 imaging modalities, the framework supports diverse clinical tasks, including visual question answering, disease classification, segmentation, and report generation within a unified representation space. Across eight public benchmarks covering dermatology, oncology, radiology, and pathology, BiasCareVL consistently outperforms 20 state-of-the-art methods, with pronounced gains in clinically challenging scenarios, including over 10% accuracy improvement in multi-class skin lesion diagnosis and more than 20% Dice improvement in small tumor segmentation. Furthermore, BiasCareVL achieves diagnostic performance exceeding human accuracy with substantially reduced time requirements when evaluated with board-certified radiologists. By open-sourcing BiasCareVL, we aim to promote a transparent, reproducible, and equitable future for AI in healthcare, paving the way for general-purpose, trustworthy, and clinically reliable AI systems.

</details>


### [268] [CrossFlowDG: Bridging the Modality Gap with Cross-modal Flow Matching for Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16892)
*Antonios Kritikos,Nikolaos Spanos,Athanasios Voulodimos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CrossFlowDG框架，通过无噪声的跨模态流匹配，在联合欧氏潜在空间中连续变换图像嵌入，使其向对应类别的文本嵌入对齐，从而提升域泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于对比学习的多模态域泛化方法存在模态间几何分离问题（即模态鸿沟），导致图像和文本嵌入虽语义对应但结构不一致，影响泛化能力。

Method: 提出CrossFlowDG框架，利用无噪声跨模态流匹配技术，在图像与CLIP文本嵌入共享的欧氏潜在空间中，将域偏差图像嵌入显式地输运至对应类别的域不变文本嵌入；采用VMamba作为图像编码器，CLIP文本编码器。

Result: 在四个主流域泛化基准上取得竞争性结果，在TerraIncognita数据集上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 跨模态流匹配可有效弥合图像与文本嵌入间的几何差距，为域泛化提供更鲁棒、语义一致的对齐方式。

Abstract: Domain generalization (DG) aims to maintain performance under domain shift, which in computer vision appears primarily as stylistic variations that cause models to overfit to domain-specific appearance cues rather than class semantics. To overcome this, recent methods use textual representations as stable, domain-invariant anchors. However, multimodal approaches that rely on cosine similarity-based contrastive alignment leave a modality gap where image and text embeddings remain geometrically separated despite semantic correspondence. We propose CrossFlowDG, a novel DG framework that addresses this residual gap using noise-free, cross-modal flow matching. By learning a continuous transformation in the joint Euclidean latent space, our framework explicitly transports domain-biased image embeddings toward domain-invariant text embeddings of the correct class. Using the efficient VMamba image encoder and CLIP's text encoder, CrossFlowDG is tested against four common DG benchmarks, and achieves competitive performance on several benchmarks and state-of-the-art on TerraIncognita. Code is available at: https://github.com/ajkrit/CrossFlowDG

</details>


### [269] [EasyVideoR1: Easier RL for Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16893)
*Chuanyu Qin,Chenxu Yang,Qingyi Si,Naibin Gu,Dingyu Yao,Zheng Lin,Peng Fu,Nan Duan,Jiaqi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EasyVideoR1，一个专为视频理解任务设计的高效强化学习框架，解决了现有RL框架在视频模态上的不足，通过离线预处理、任务感知奖励系统、混合训练范式、图像-视频联合训练及异步多基准评估等创新，显著提升了训练效率与模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型向原生多模态架构演进，将强化学习从可验证奖励（RLVR）扩展到视频理解变得重要但尚未充分探索，主要受限于视频任务类型多样、高维视觉输入重复解码与预处理的计算开销，以及超参数敏感导致的评估不可复现性。

Method: 提出EasyVideoR1框架，包含：(1) 带离线预处理与张量缓存的完整视频RL训练流程；(2) 覆盖11类视频/图像任务的模块化、可扩展任务感知奖励系统；(3) 结合高质量离线轨迹与在线策略探索的混合训练范式；(4) 支持独立像素预算配置的图像-视频联合训练；(5) 异步多基准评估框架，覆盖22个主流视频理解基准。

Result: EasyVideoR1实现1.47×吞吐量提升，支持22个视频理解基准的高保真复现评估，官方报告准确率与复现结果高度一致，并在多个挑战性任务上展现出更强学习能力。

Conclusion: EasyVideoR1为视频理解领域的RL训练提供了系统性、高效且可复现的开源基础设施，推动了多模态大模型在视频推理能力上的实质性进展。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) has demonstrated remarkable effectiveness in improving the reasoning capabilities of large language models. As models evolve into natively multimodal architectures, extending RLVR to video understanding becomes increasingly important yet remains largely unexplored, due to the diversity of video task types, the computational overhead of repeatedly decoding and preprocessing high-dimensional visual inputs, and the difficulty of reproducible evaluation across numerous sensitive hyperparameters. Existing open-source RL training frameworks provide solid infrastructure for text and image scenarios but lack systematic optimizations tailored for video modality. In this work, we present \textbf{EasyVideoR1}, a complete and efficient reinforcement learning framework specifically designed for training large vision-language models on video understanding tasks. EasyVideoR1 makes the following contributions: (1) a full video RL training pipeline with offline preprocessing and tensor caching that eliminates redundant video decoding and yields a 1.47 $\times$ throughput improvement; (2) a comprehensive, task-aware reward system covering 11 distinct video and image problem types with unified routing and modular extension; (3) a mixed offline-online data training paradigm that combines curated high-quality trajectories with on-policy exploration, benefiting the learning of more challenging tasks; (4) joint image-video training with independently configurable pixel budgets, allowing the two modalities to mutually reinforce each other; and (5) an asynchronous multi-benchmark evaluation framework covering 22 mainstream video understanding benchmarks, with reproduced accuracy closely aligned with officially reported scores.

</details>


### [270] [Physics-Informed Tracking (PIT)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16895)
*Emil Hovad,Allan Peter Engsig-Karup*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Physics-Informed Tracking (PIT)的视频粒子跟踪框架，结合神经网络自动编码器与可微分物理模块，通过无监督的Physics-Informed Landmark Loss (PILL)或有监督的PILLS损失实现高精度、物理一致的单粒子轨迹估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频粒子跟踪方法常缺乏对物理规律的显式建模，导致在噪声干扰或标注缺失时性能下降；本文旨在利用已知动力学先验提升无/有监督下的跟踪鲁棒性与精度。

Method: 提出PIT框架：1）用神经网络自动编码器生成粒子热图定位（landmark）；2）嵌入可微分物理模块约束多帧landmark形成符合动力学的轨迹；3）设计无监督损失PILL（对比预测轨迹与热图峰值）和有监督损失PILLS（对比预测与仿真真值的位置、速度、反弹）；4）采用分离瓶颈结构的自动编码器，分别处理跟踪结构与背景重建。

Result: 在26因子设计实验（n=4，共64配置）中，PILLS在干净与噪声条件下均实现亚像素级跟踪精度，尤其在双线性解码器和物理优化解码器输出上表现稳定。

Conclusion: PIT框架通过将物理先验嵌入学习过程，显著提升了视频粒子跟踪的精度与泛化能力，PILLS在有监督设置下效果最优，PILL则为无标签场景提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We propose Physics-Informed Tracking (PIT), a video-based framework for tracking a single particle from video, where a neural network autoencoder localizes a particle as a heatmap peak (landmark) and a differentiable physics module embedded in the autoencoder constrains several landmarks over time (a trajectory) to satisfy known dynamics. The novel Physics-Informed Landmark Loss (PILL) compares this predicted trajectory back against the landmarks, enforcing physical consistency without labels. Its supervised variant (PILLS) instead compares the prediction against ground-truth position, velocity, and bounce from simulation, enabling end-to-end backpropagation. To support supervised and unsupervised learning, we use an autoencoder with a split bottleneck that separates A) tracking-related structure via landmark heatmaps from B) background noise and subsequent image reconstruction. We evaluate a replicated 26 factorial design (n = 4 replicates, 64 configurations), showing that PILLS consistently achieves sub-pixel tracking accuracy for the bilinear and physics-refined decoder outputs under both clean and noisy conditions.

</details>


### [271] [LAGS: Low-Altitude Gaussian Splatting with Groupwise Heterogeneous Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16910)
*Yikun Wang,Yujie Wan,Wei Zuo,Shuai Wang,Yik-Chung Wu,Chengzhong Xu,Huseyin Arslan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向低空高斯溅射（LAGS）3D重建的组别异构图神经网络（GW-HGNN）资源分配方法，通过建模图像组对重建贡献的非均匀性，在保证渲染质量的同时显著降低通信与计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低空资源分配方案未考虑多视角图像多样性，导致在以重建质量为优先的LAGS任务中效率低下。

Method: 提出组别异构图神经网络（GW-HGNN），将LAGS损失和通信约束转化为图学习代价，实现双层消息传递，显式建模不同图像组对重建的非均匀贡献。
