Motivation: 现有单车辆轨迹建图方法存在视角不足问题，难以观测被遮挡区域；多车融合虽能提供互补视角，但面临计算开销大、视角冗余及位姿误差和遮挡噪声等问题。

Method: 将多车建图建模为'先选择后融合'问题：1）最优车辆选择（OVS）模块基于遮挡区域的自我中心不确定性筛选少量关键辅助车辆；2）跨车注意力（CVA）与语义感知噪声滤波器（SNF）实现位姿鲁棒对齐与伪影抑制；3）在BEV层面进行融合。

Result: 在nuScenes和Argoverse2数据集上，相比MapTRv2分别提升+10.5和+9.3 mAP；相比MVMap和HRMapNet在nuScenes上分别提升+6.2和+3.8 mAP。

Conclusion: 不确定性引导的辅助车辆选择对高效、高精度多车矢量化建图至关重要，所提OptiMVMap在更少视角下实现了更完整、拓扑更准确的地图。

Abstract: Offline vectorized maps constitute critical infrastructure for high-precision autonomous driving and mapping services. Existing approaches rely predominantly on single ego-vehicle trajectories, which fundamentally suffer from viewpoint insufficiency: while memory-based methods extend observation time by aggregating ego-trajectory frames, they lack the spatial diversity needed to reveal occluded regions. Incorporating views from surrounding vehicles offers complementary perspectives, yet naive fusion introduces three key challenges: computational cost from large candidate pools, redundancy from near-collinear viewpoints, and noise from pose errors and occlusion artifacts.
  We present OptiMVMap, which reformulates multi-vehicle mapping as a select-then-fuse problem to address these challenges systematically. An Optimal Vehicle Selection (OVS) module strategically identifies a compact subset of helpers that maximally reduce ego-centric uncertainty in occluded regions, addressing computation and redundancy challenges. Cross-Vehicle Attention (CVA) and Semantic-aware Noise Filter (SNF) then perform pose-tolerant alignment and artifact suppression before BEV-level fusion, addressing the noise challenge. This targeted pipeline yields more complete and topologically faithful maps with substantially fewer views than indiscriminate aggregation. On nuScenes and Argoverse2, OptiMVMap improves MapTRv2 by +10.5 mAP and +9.3 mAP, respectively, and surpasses memory-augmented baselines MVMap and HRMapNet by +6.2 mAP and +3.8 mAP on nuScenes. These results demonstrate that uncertainty-guided selection of helper vehicles is essential for efficient and accurate multi-vehicle vectorized mapping. The code is released at https://github.com/DanZeDong/OptiMVMap.

</details>


### [309] [ScenarioControl: Vision-Language Controllable Vectorized Latent Scenario Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17147)
*Lili Gao,Yanbo Xu,William Koch,Samuele Ruffino,Luke Rowe,Behdad Chalaki,Dmitriy Rivkin,Julian Ost,Roger Girgis,Mario Bijelic,Felix Heide*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了ScenarioControl，首个用于生成驾驶场景的视觉-语言控制机制，能够根据文本或图像输入生成多样且真实的3D驾驶场景序列，并支持多视角、长时序的场景延续。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶场景生成方法缺乏对文本或图像等多模态输入的细粒度、高保真控制能力，难以兼顾场景多样性、真实性和时序一致性。

Method: 提出在联合表征道路结构与动态智能体的向量化潜在空间中生成场景；设计跨全局控制机制，融合交叉注意力与轻量级全局上下文分支，实现对道路布局和交通状况的精细控制。

Result: ScenarioControl在控制遵循性与生成保真度上全面优于所有对比方法；支持多主体视角与长时序场景延续；并开源了带文本标注的向量化地图数据集。

Conclusion: ScenarioControl为可控、可扩展、高保真的自动驾驶仿真场景生成提供了新范式，推动了多模态驱动的虚拟测试环境发展。

Abstract: We introduce ScenarioControl, the first vision-language control mechanism for learned driving scenario generation. Given a text prompt or an input image, Scenario-Control synthesizes diverse, realistic 3D scenario rollouts - including map, 3D boxes of reactive actors over time, pedestrians, driving infrastructure, and ego camera observations. The method generates scenes in a vectorized latent space that represents road structure and dynamic agents jointly. To connect multimodal control with sparse vectorized scene elements, we propose a cross-global control mechanism that integrates crossattention with a lightweight global-context branch, enabling fine-grained control over road layout and traffic conditions while preserving realism. The method produces temporally consistent scenario rollouts from the perspectives different actors in the scene, supporting long-horizon continuation of driving scenarios. To facilitate training and evaluation, we release a dataset with text annotations aligned to vectorized map structures. Extensive experiments validate that the control adherence and fidelity of ScenarioControl compare favorable to all tested methods across all experiments. Project webpage: https://light.princeton.edu/ScenarioControl

</details>


### [310] [Instant Colorization of Gaussian Splats](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17155)
*Daniel Lieber,Alexander Mock,Nils Wandel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将2D图像信息（如颜色、神经特征、分割掩码）高效反向映射到3D高斯点阵场景的方法，用于场景重光照、风格化和3D语义分割等任务，通过可见性加权最小二乘法实现，比基于梯度下降的方法快一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯泼溅（Gaussian Splatting）框架擅长从3D到2D的高效渲染，但缺乏将2D图像信息高效反向映射回3D高斯场景的能力，而该能力对场景编辑与语义理解至关重要。

Method: 利用法方程求解每个高斯椭球上的可见性加权最小二乘问题，并借助现有可微光栅化器高效实现。

Result: 在场景重光照、特征增强和3D语义分割任务上验证了方法有效性，相比基于梯度下降的基线方法实现了最高达10倍的速度提升。

Conclusion: 所提反向映射方法兼顾效率与精度，为基于高斯泼溅的3D场景编辑与理解提供了新范式。

Abstract: Gaussian Splatting has recently become one of the most popular frameworks for photorealistic 3D scene reconstruction and rendering. While current rasterizers allow for efficient mappings of 3D Gaussian splats onto 2D camera views, this work focuses on mapping 2D image information (e.g. color, neural features or segmentation masks) efficiently back onto an existing scene of Gaussian splats. This 'opposite' direction enables applications ranging from scene relighting and stylization to 3D semantic segmentation, but also introduces challenges, such as view-dependent colorization and occlusion handling.
  Our approach tackles these challenges using the normal equation to solve a visibility-weighted least squares problem for every Gaussian and can be implemented efficiently with existing differentiable rasterizers. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach on scene relighting, feature enrichment and 3D semantic segmentation tasks, achieving up to an order of magnitude speedup compared to gradient descent-based baselines.

</details>


### [311] [PPEDCRF: Dynamic-CRF-Guided Selective Perturbation for Background-Based Location Privacy in Video Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17163)
*Bo Ma,Weiqi Yan,Jinsong Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PPEDCRF框架，通过动态条件随机场识别位置敏感背景区域，结合归一化控制惩罚与DP式校准规则，在区域内注入高斯噪声，以保护视频帧中基于背景的位置隐私，同时显著提升图像质量（PSNR）并有效降低检索准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 即使去除GPS元数据，攻击者仍可通过匹配视频帧背景视觉线索与地理标记参考图像实现地理定位，因此需保护基于背景的位置隐私。

Method: 采用动态条件随机场（DCRF）估计位置敏感背景区域，用归一化控制惩罚（NCP）调节扰动强度，并依据差分隐私（DP）风格校准规则仅在推断区域内注入高斯噪声。

Result: 在配对场景检索基准测试中，PPEDCRF将ResNet18 Top-1检索准确率从0.667降至0.361±0.127（σ₀=8），同时保持36.14 dB PSNR，比全局高斯噪声高出约6 dB；跨模型迁移效果总体良好（23/24情形改善），仅MixVPR存在负面迁移。

Conclusion: PPEDCRF的核心优势在于空间聚焦扰动，在相同噪声尺度下显著提升图像视觉质量，而非单纯增强隐私强度；其隐私-效用权衡表现为在同等效用下隐私水平相当，但质量更优。

Abstract: We propose PPEDCRF, a calibrated selective perturbation framework that protects \emph{background-based location privacy} in released video frames against gallery-based retrieval attackers. Even after GPS metadata are stripped, an adversary can geolocate a frame by matching its background visual cues to geo-tagged reference imagery; PPEDCRF mitigates this threat by estimating location-sensitive background regions with a dynamic conditional random field (DCRF), rescaling perturbation strength with a normalized control penalty (NCP), and injecting Gaussian noise only inside the inferred regions via a DP-style calibration rule.
  On a controlled paired-scene retrieval benchmark with eight attacker backbones and three noise seeds, PPEDCRF reduces ResNet18 Top-1 retrieval accuracy from 0.667 to $0.361\pm0.127$ at $σ_0=8$ while preserving $36.14\,$dB PSNR -- an ${\approx}6\,$dB quality advantage over global Gaussian noise. Transfer across the eight-backbone seed-averaged benchmark is broadly supportive (23 of 24 backbone-gallery cells show negative $Δ$), while appendix-scale confirmation identifies MixVPR as a remaining adverse-transfer exception. Matched-operating-point analysis shows that PPEDCRF and global Gaussian noise converge in Top-1 privacy at equal utility, so the practical benefit is spatially concentrated perturbation that preserves higher visual quality at any given noise scale rather than stronger matched-utility privacy. Code: https://github.com/mabo1215/PPEDCRF

</details>


### [312] [LookasideVLN: Direction-Aware Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17190)
*Yuwei Ning,Ganlong Zhao,Yipeng Qin,Si Liu,Yang Liu,Liang Lin,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LookasideVLN新范式，通过利用自然语言中的方向线索提升空中视觉-语言导航（Aerial VLN）的空间推理精度与计算效率，包含动态编码方向关系的自我中心旁视图图（ELG）、轻量级空间地标知识库（SLKB）及对齐多模态信息的旁视MLLM导航代理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Aerial VLN方法仅依赖地标描述，忽视方向线索这一人类导航中关键的空间上下文信息，导致指令理解浅层、计算成本高。

Method: 提出LookasideVLN：（1）构建自我中心旁视图图（ELG）动态编码地标及其方向关系；（2）建立空间地标知识库（SLKB）实现轻量记忆检索；（3）设计旁视MLLM导航代理，融合语言指令、视觉观测与ELG提供的地标-方向信息进行路径规划。

Result: 在多项实验中显著超越当前最优方法CityNavAgent，即使仅使用单层前瞻，验证了利用方向线索在准确性与效率上的双重优势。

Conclusion: 方向线索是提升Aerial VLN性能的关键且高效策略；LookasideVLN为复杂城市环境中基于自然语言的无人机导航提供了更鲁棒、可扩展的新范式。

Abstract: Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation (Aerial VLN) enables unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to follow natural language instructions and navigate complex urban environments. While recent advances have achieved progress through large-scale memory graphs and lookahead path planning, they remain limited by shallow instruction understanding and high computational cost. In particular, existing methods rely primarily on landmark descriptions, overlooking directional cues "a key source of spatial context in human navigation". In this work, we propose LookasideVLN, a new paradigm that exploits directional cues in natural language to achieve both more accurate spatial reasoning and greater computational efficiency. LookasideVLN comprises three core components: (1) an Egocentric Lookaside Graph (ELG) that dynamically encodes instruction-relevant landmarks and their directional relationships, (2) a Spatial Landmark Knowledge Base (SLKB) that provides lightweight memory retrieval from prior navigation experiences, and (3) a Lookaside MLLM Navigation Agent that aligns multimodal information from user instructions, visual observations, and landmark-direction information from ELG for path planning. Extensive experiments show that LookasideVLN significantly outperforms the state-of-the-art CityNavAgent, even with a single-level lookahead, demonstrating that leveraging directional cues is a powerful yet efficient strategy for Aerial VLN.

</details>


### [313] [DreamShot: Personalized Storyboard Synthesis with Video Diffusion Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17195)
*Junjia Huang,Binbin Yang,Pengxiang Yan,Jiyang Liu,Bin Xia,Zhao Wang,Yitong Wang,Liang Lin,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DreamShot，一种基于视频扩散模型的可控多镜头分镜合成框架，通过利用视频生成模型的时空一致性先验，并引入多参考角色条件模块与角色注意力一致性损失，显著提升了分镜序列在叙事连贯性、角色身份一致性和场景连贯性方面的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于文生图扩散模型的分镜合成方法难以维持长程时序一致性、角色身份一致性和叙事流畅性。

Method: 提出DreamShot框架，融合视频扩散先验，支持Text-to-Shot、Reference-to-Shot及基于前序帧的故事延续生成；设计多参考角色条件模块和Role-Attention Consistency Loss以对齐角色身份。

Result: 在场景连贯性、角色一致性与生成效率上均优于当前最优的文生图分镜模型。

Conclusion: DreamShot为可控、视频模型驱动的视觉叙事提供了新范式，推动分镜合成向更真实、连贯的方向发展。

Abstract: Storyboard synthesis plays a crucial role in visual storytelling, aiming to generate coherent shot sequences that visually narrate cinematic events with consistent characters, scenes, and transitions. However, existing approaches are mostly adapted from text-to-image diffusion models, which struggle to maintain long-range temporal coherence, consistent character identities, and narrative flow across multiple shots. In this paper, we introduce DreamShot, a video generative model based storyboard framework that fully exploits powerful video diffusion priors for controllable multi-shot synthesis. DreamShot supports both Text-to-Shot and Reference-to-Shot generation, as well as story continuation conditioned on previous frames, enabling flexible and context-aware storyboard generation. By leveraging the spatial-temporal consistency inherent in video generative models, DreamShot produces visually and semantically coherent sequences with improved narrative fidelity and character continuity. Furthermore, DreamShot incorporates a multi-reference role conditioning module that accepts multiple character reference images and enforces identity alignment via a Role-Attention Consistency Loss, explicitly constraining attention between reference and generated roles. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DreamShot achieves superior scene coherence, role consistency, and generation efficiency compared to state-of-the-art text-to-image storyboard models, establishing a new direction toward controllable video model-driven visual storytelling.

</details>


### [314] [SciDraw-6K: A Multilingual Scientific Illustration Dataset Generated by Google Gemini](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17206)
*Davie Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SciDraw-6K 是一个包含6291张科学插图及其11种语言提示的多语言、领域专用数据集，专为科学可视化（如示意图、机理图等）而构建，支持多语言文生图、领域微调与提示工程研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图数据集多为通用领域，缺乏面向科学插图（如示意图、机理图、概念海报）的高质量、多语言、结构化数据集，限制了科学可视化生成模型的发展。

Method: 利用 Gemini-2.5-flash-image 和 Gemini-3-pro-image-preview 模型合成科学插图，设计覆盖8大科学类别的提示词体系，支持11种语言，并构建标准化的数据清洗与标注流程；发布为公开 Hugging Face 数据集并配套开源代码与在线服务 sci-draw.com。

Result: 构建了 SciDraw-6K 数据集（6,291 张图像 + 多语言 prompt），涵盖8类科学领域，已上线为公共服务 sci-draw.com，并开放数据与代码供研究使用。

Conclusion: SciDraw-6K 填补了科学插图文生图数据集的空白，为多语言、领域自适应的科学可视化生成研究提供了关键基础设施。

Abstract: We present SciDraw-6K, a curated dataset of 6,291 scientific illustrations synthesized by Google Gemini image-generation models, each paired with prompts in eleven languages (English, Simplified Chinese, Traditional Chinese, Japanese, Korean, German, French, Spanish, Brazilian Portuguese, Italian, and Russian). Images span eight broad scientific categories -- biomedical, chemistry, materials, electronics, environment, AI systems, physics, and a long "other" tail -- and are produced primarily by the gemini-2.5-flash-image and gemini-3-pro-image-preview model families. In contrast to general-purpose text-to-image corpora that dominate the literature, SciDraw-6K is purpose-built for the scientific illustration genre: schematic diagrams, mechanism figures, table-of-contents graphics, and conceptual posters. We describe the construction pipeline, report dataset statistics, and document its use as the substrate of sci-draw.com, a public scientific drawing service. The dataset is released to support multilingual text-to-image research, domain-adapted diffusion fine-tuning, and prompt-engineering studies for scientific visualization. Dataset: https://huggingface.co/datasets/SciDrawAI/SciDraw-6K Code: https://github.com/SciDrawAI/scidraw-6k

</details>


### [315] [CDSA-Net:Collaborative Decoupling of Vascular Structure and Background for High-Fidelity Coronary Digital Subtraction Angiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17208)
*Si Li,Chen-Kai Hu,Zhenhuan Lyu,Yuanqing He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CDSA-Net框架，首次显式解耦并联合优化冠状血管结构保持与背景真实重建，通过HGPG机制和ANM模块分别解决边界伪影与灰度失真问题，在定量指标与临床效用上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSA冠脉成像受生理运动干扰，原始图像含大量解剖噪声；现有深度学习方法存在边界伪影和组织灰度失真两大临床不可接受缺陷。

Method: 提出CDSA-Net：1）冠状结构提取网络（CSENet）引入分层几何先验引导（HGPG），融合集成几何先验（IGP）、门控空间调制（GSM）与中心线感知拓扑（CAT）损失；2）冠状背景重建网络（CBResNet）嵌入自适应噪声模块（ANM），建模X射线噪声随机性以消除边界伪影；最终通过从原始造影图中减去重建背景获得减影结果。

Result: 在血管强度相关性和感知质量上显著优于SOTA方法；形态评估效率提升25.6%，血流动力学评估速度提升42.9%；诊断结果与原始造影图一致。

Conclusion: CDSA-Net首次实现血管结构保真与背景真实重建的协同优化，有效克服临床DSA减影中的关键瓶颈，为介入心脏病学提供了新基准。

Abstract: Digital subtraction angiography (DSA) in coronary imaging is fundamentally challenged by physiological motion, forcing reliance on raw angiograms cluttered with anatomical noise. Existing deep learning methods often produced images with two critical clinically unacceptable flaws: persistent boundary artifacts and a loss of native tissue grayscale fidelity that undermined diagnostic confidence. We propose a novel framework termed as CDSA-Net that for the first time explicitly decouples and jointly optimizes vascular structure preservation and realistic background restoration. CDSA-Net introduces two core innovations: (i) A hierarchical geometric prior guidance (HGPG) mechanism, embedded in our coronary structure extraction network (CSENet). It synergistically combines integrated geometric prior (IGP) with gated spatial modulation (GSM) and centerline-aware topology (CAT) loss supervision, ensuring structural continuity. (ii) An adaptive noise module (ANM) within our coronary background restoration network (CBResNet). Unlike standard restoration, ANM uniquely models the stochastic nature of clinical X-ray noise, bridging the domain gap to enable seamless background intensity estimation and the complete elimination of boundary artifacts. The final subtraction is obtained by removing the restored background from the raw angiogram. Quantitatively, it significantly outperformed state-of-the-art methods in vascular intensity correlation and perceptual quality. A 25.6% improvement in morphology assessment efficiency and a 42.9% gain in hemodynamic evaluation speed set a new benchmark for utility in interventional cardiology, while maintaining diagnostic results consistent with raw angiograms. The project code is available at https://github.com/DrThink-ai/CDSA-Net.

</details>


### [316] [DREAM: Dynamic Retinal Enhancement with Adaptive Multi-modal Fusion for Expert Precision Medical Report Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17209)
*Nagur Shareef Shaik,Teja Krishna Cherukuri,Dong Hye Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DREAM是一种新型视网膜图像报告生成框架，通过两阶段自适应多模态融合和对比对齐模块，在小样本医疗场景下实现高保真、临床可信的报告生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大视觉语言模型在数据稀缺的医学领域易过拟合，难以捕捉关键细微病理特征，亟需结合临床知识提升泛化性与可靠性。

Method: 提出DREAM框架：1）Abstractor模块将图像与眼科医生标注的临床关键词映射到共享空间；2）Adaptor模块通过可学习参数动态加权多模态信息进行自适应融合；3）Contrastive Alignment模块在训练中对齐融合表征与真实报告。

Result: 在DeepEyeNet基准上达到BLEU-4为0.241的新SOTA，并在ROCO数据集上展现强泛化能力。

Conclusion: DREAM通过融合临床专业知识与高效多模态建模策略，显著提升了小样本下视网膜报告生成的质量与临床可信度。

Abstract: Automating medical reports for retinal images requires a sophisticated blend of visual pattern recognition and deep clinical knowledge. Current Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) often struggle in specialized medical fields where data is scarce, leading to models that overfit and miss subtle but critical pathologies. To address this, we introduce DREAM (Dynamic Retinal Enhancement with Adaptive Multi-modal Fusion), a novel framework for high-fidelity medical report generation that excels even with limited data. DREAM employs a unique two-stage fusion mechanism that intelligently integrates visual data with clinical keywords curated by ophthalmologists. First, the Abstractor module maps image and keyword features into a shared space, enhancing visual data with pathology-relevant insights. Next, the Adaptor performs adaptive multi-modal fusion, dynamically weighting the importance of each modality using learnable parameters to create a unified representation. To ensure the model's outputs are semantically grounded in clinical reality, a Contrastive Alignment module aligns these fused representations with ground-truth medical reports during training. By combining medical expertise with an efficient fusion strategy, DREAM sets a new state-of-the-art on the DeepEyeNet benchmark, achieving a BLEU-4 score of 0.241, and further demonstrates strong generalization to the ROCO dataset.

</details>


### [317] [EmbodiedHead: Real-Time Listening and Speaking Avatar for Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17211)
*Yu Zhang,Kaiyuan Shen,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EmbodiedHead是一个语音驱动的实时 talking-head 框架，通过结合 Rectified-Flow Diffusion Transformer 与可微渲染器，实现高保真、低步数（仅4步）的视听同步生成，并采用单流音频接口与显式状态建模，解决传统双流方法在因果交互中的前瞻性依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具备实时性、视听行为统一性与高视觉质量的具身化虚拟头像，以支持LLM与用户的自然对话交互。

Method: 提出EmbodiedHead框架：1）首次将Rectified-Flow Diffusion Transformer（DiT）用于 talking-head 生成；2）引入可微分渲染器实现高质量图像合成；3）设计单流音频接口、帧级倾听/说话状态显式建模及Streaming Audio Scheduler，消除听音时伪嘴动；4）采用两阶段训练：系数空间预训练 + 图像域联合精调。

Result: 在说话与倾听两种场景下均达到SOTA的视觉质量与运动保真度，仅需4个采样步即生成高保真结果，显著优于双流基线方法。

Conclusion: EmbodiedHead为LLM提供了高效、自然、高质量的实时视觉化身能力，解决了倾听-说话行为建模与因果交互兼容性的关键挑战，推动具身化人机对话发展。

Abstract: We present EmbodiedHead, a speech-driven talking-head framework that equips LLMs with real-time visual avatars for conversation. A practical embodied avatar must achieve real-time generation, unified listening-speaking behavior, and high rendered visual quality simultaneously. Our framework couples the first Rectified-Flow Diffusion Transformer (DiT) for this task with a differentiable renderer, enabling diverse, high-fidelity generation in as few as four sampling steps. Prior listening-speaking methods rely on dual-stream audio, introducing an interlocutor look-ahead dependency incompatible with causal user--LLM interaction. We instead adopt a single-stream interface with explicit per-frame listening-speaking state conditioning and a Streaming Audio Scheduler, suppressing spurious mouth motion during listening while enabling seamless turn-taking. A two-stage training scheme of coefficient-space pretraining and joint image-domain refinement further closes the gap between motion-level supervision and rendered quality. Extensive experiments demonstrate state-of-the-art visual quality and motion fidelity in both speaking and listening scenarios.

</details>


### [318] [Cross-Modal Attention Analysis and Optimization in Vision-Language Models: A Study on Visual Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17217)
*Lijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对抗性评估框架，用于量化视觉-语言模型（VLMs）对文本的过度依赖（即“文本捷径学习”），通过在图像不变前提下引入语义冲突文本，测量准确率下降（Drop）；在几何图形数据集上验证四种对抗策略，发现经多策略优化的LoRA模型显著降低Drop（27.5%→9.8%），同时保持高正常准确率，并通过注意力与嵌入分析证实其增强视觉利用与跨模态对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型存在“文本捷径学习”问题，即过度依赖文本描述而忽视视觉证据，亟需定量评估和缓解该偏差。

Method: 构建对抗性评估框架，定义Drop指标；在可控几何形状数据集上应用四种文本干扰策略（shape_swap、color_swap、position_swap、random_text）；对比Baseline CLIP、LoRA微调与LoRA Optimized（集成硬负例挖掘、标签平滑、分层学习率等六种技术）三种配置。

Result: LoRA Optimized模型将平均Drop从27.5%显著降至9.8%（相对改善64.4%，p<0.001），正常准确率维持在97%；注意力可视化与嵌入空间分析表明其更关注视觉特征、跨模态对齐更紧密。

Conclusion: 通过系统性训练优化可有效缓解VLM的文本捷径学习，提升其对视觉证据的依赖与鲁棒性，为构建更平衡、可信的跨模态模型提供可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) achieve strong cross-modal performance, yet recent evidence suggests they over-rely on textual descriptions while under-utilizing visual evidence -- a phenomenon termed ``text shortcut learning.'' We propose an adversarial evaluation framework that quantifies this cross-modal dependency by measuring accuracy degradation (Drop) when semantically conflicting text is paired with unchanged images. Four adversarial strategies -- shape\_swap, color\_swap, position\_swap, and random\_text -- are applied to a controlled geometric-shapes dataset ($n{=}1{,}000$). We compare three configurations: Baseline CLIP (ViT-B/32), LoRA fine-tuning, and LoRA Optimized (integrating Hard Negative Mining, Label Smoothing, layer-wise learning rates, Cosine Restarts, curriculum learning, and data augmentation). The optimized model reduces average Drop from 27.5\% to 9.8\% (64.4\% relative improvement, $p{<}0.001$) while maintaining 97\% normal accuracy. Attention visualization and embedding-space analysis confirm that the optimized model attends more to visual features and achieves tighter cross-modal alignment.

</details>


### [319] [Region-Affinity Attention for Whole-Slide Breast Cancer Classification in Deep Ultraviolet Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17222)
*Nagur Shareef Shaik,Teja Krishna Cherukuri,Dong Hye Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种专为深紫外荧光全片图像（DUV-WSI）乳腺癌分类设计的区域亲和注意力机制，无需分块处理，保留空间完整性，并结合对比损失提升判别性，在136例样本上达到92.67%准确率和95.97% AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统病理方法在术中诊断中速度与精度不足；现有基于补丁的深度学习方法破坏空间上下文且预处理开销大；标准注意力机制无法充分建模DUV-WSI中多尺度、诊断相关的区域关系。

Method: 提出区域亲和注意力机制：建模局部邻域距离、构建全亲和矩阵以动态突出诊断关键区域；端到端处理整张WSI（非补丁式）；引入对比损失增强特征判别力。

Result: 在136例DUV-WSI数据集上，准确率达92.67 ± 0.73%，AUC达95.97%，优于现有多种注意力机制。

Conclusion: 区域亲和注意力机制有效挖掘DUV-WSI中的诊断特异性空间关系，显著提升乳腺癌分类性能，具备更强临床适用性。

Abstract: Breast cancer diagnosis demands rapid and precise tools, yet traditional histopathological methods often fall short in intra-operative settings. Deep Ultraviolet (DUV) fluorescence imaging emerges as a transformative approach, offering high-contrast, label-free visualization of whole-slide images (WSIs) with unprecedented detail, surpassing conventional hematoxylin and eosin (H&E) staining in speed and resolution. However, existing deep learning methods for breast cancer classification, predominantly patch-based, fragment spatial context and incur significant preprocessing overhead, limiting their clinical utility. Moreover, standard attention mechanisms, such as Spatial, Squeeze-and-Excitation, Global Context and Guided Context Gating, fail to fully exploit the rich, multi-scale regional relationships inherent in DUV-WSI data, often prioritizing generic feature recalibration over diagnostic specificity. This study introduces a novel Region-Affinity Attention mechanism tailored for DUV-WSI breast cancer classification, processing entire slides without patching to preserve spatial integrity. By modeling local neighbor distances and constructing a full affinity matrix, our method dynamically highlights diagnostically relevant regions, augmented by a contrastive loss to enhance feature discriminability. Evaluated on a dataset of 136 DUV-WSI samples, our approach achieves an accuracy of 92.67 +/- 0.73% and an AUC of 95.97%, outperforming existing attention methods.

</details>


### [320] [Fringe Projection Based Vision Pipeline for Autonomous Hard Drive Disassembly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17231)
*Badrinath Balasubramaniam,Vignesh Suresh,Benjamin Metcalf,Beiwen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于硬盘驱动器（HDD）机器人拆解的端到端自主视觉感知流水线，融合了 fringe projection profilometry（FPP）3D传感、深度补全与轻量级实时实例分割，实现像素级对齐的高精度语义与几何感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未回收的电子废弃物造成巨大经济损失，而硬盘驱动器（HDD）作为高价值电子废弃物，亟需自动化拆解；但现有方法在整体3D感知、场景理解与紧固件定位方面存在碎片化、鲁棒性差和缺失关键定位能力等问题。

Method: 提出一种自主视觉流水线：1）采用条纹投影轮廓术（FPP）进行主动3D传感；2）在FPP失效区域选择性触发深度补全模块（基于Depth Anything V2 Base）；3）将FPP系统复用于实例分割与深度感知，实现无需配准的像素级对齐；4）部署优化轻量级实时实例分割网络；5）采用sim-to-real迁移学习扩充物理数据集。

Result: 实例分割达到box mAP@50=0.960、mask mAP@50=0.957；深度补全RMSE=2.317 mm、MAE=1.836 mm；端到端推理延迟12.86 ms，吞吐达77.7 FPS；构建并开源HDD合成实例分割数据集。

Conclusion: 该感知系统为HDD机器人拆解提供了高保真、像素对齐的语义与空间信息，显著提升自动化拆解可行性，并为工业3D视觉任务提供可复用的技术范式。

Abstract: Unrecovered e-waste represents a significant economic loss. Hard disk drives (HDDs) comprise a valuable e-waste stream necessitating robotic disassembly. Automating the disassembly of HDDs requires holistic 3D sensing, scene understanding, and fastener localization, however current methods are fragmented, lack robust 3D sensing, and lack fastener localization. We propose an autonomous vision pipeline which performs 3D sensing using a Fringe Projection Profilometry (FPP) module, with selective triggering of a depth completion module where FPP fails, and integrates this module with a lightweight, real-time instance segmentation network for scene understanding and critical component localization. By utilizing the same FPP camera-projector system for both our depth sensing and component localization modules, our depth maps and derived 3D geometry are inherently pixel-wise aligned with the segmentation masks without registration, providing an advantage over RGB-D perception systems common in industrial sensing. We optimize both our trained depth completion and instance segmentation networks for deployment-oriented inference. The proposed system achieves a box mAP@50 of 0.960 and mask mAP@50 of 0.957 for instance segmentation, while the selected depth completion configuration with the Depth Anything V2 Base backbone achieves an RMSE of 2.317 mm and MAE of 1.836 mm; the Platter Facing learned inference stack achieved a combined latency of 12.86 ms and a throughput of 77.7 Frames Per Second (FPS) on the evaluation workstation. Finally, we adopt a sim-to-real transfer learning approach to augment our physical dataset. The proposed perception pipeline provides both high-fidelity semantic and spatial data which can be valuable for downstream robotic disassembly. The synthetic dataset developed for HDD instance segmentation will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [321] [Enhancing Zero-shot Personalized Image Aesthetics Assessment with Profile-aware Multimodal LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17233)
*Chun Wang,Chenfeng Wei,Chenyang Liu,Weihong Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出P-MLLM模型，通过用户画像作为上下文信号，在零样本场景下实现个性化图像美学评估（PIAA），无需依赖历史用户评分数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PIAA方法依赖用户历史评分建模个体偏好，在缺乏此类数据的零样本场景下表现受限，亟需不依赖历史评分的个性化建模新范式。

Method: 提出基于用户画像的个性化范式，构建P-MLLM——一种融合选择性视觉融合模块的冻结多模态大语言模型，使视觉信息在画像引导的推理过程中被有选择地、画像感知地整合进隐状态。

Result: 在最新PIAA基准上验证了P-MLLM具备竞争力的零样本性能，且即使使用粗粒度画像信息仍保持有效。

Conclusion: 基于画像的个性化范式为零样本PIAA提供了可行且鲁棒的新路径，凸显了利用用户画像替代历史评分进行美学建模的潜力。

Abstract: Personalized image aesthetics assessment (PIAA) aims to predict an individual user's subjective rating of an image, which requires modeling user-specific aesthetic preferences. Existing methods rely on historical user ratings for this modeling and therefore struggle when such data are unavailable. We address this zero-shot setting by using user profiles as contextual signals for personalization and adopting a profile-based personalization paradigm. We introduce P-MLLM, a profile-aware multimodal LLM that augments a frozen LLM with selective fusion modules for controlled visual integration. These modules selectively integrate visual information into the model's evolving hidden states during profile-conditioned reasoning, allowing visual information to be incorporated in a profile-aware manner. Experiments on recent PIAA benchmarks show that P-MLLM achieves competitive zero-shot performance and remains effective even with coarse profile information, highlighting the potential of profile-based personalization for zero-shot PIAA.

</details>


### [322] [RemoteShield: Enable Robust Multimodal Large Language Models for Earth Observation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17243)
*Rui Min,Liang Yao,Shiyu Miao,Shengxiang Xu,Yuxuan Liu,Chuanyi Zhang,Shimin Di,Fan Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RemoteShield，一种针对遥感多模态大语言模型（MLLM）鲁棒性问题的新方法，通过构建语义等价簇和跨条件偏好学习，提升模型在真实噪声输入（如云雾、文本模糊等）下的稳定性与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感多模态大语言模型在干净数据上训练，面对真实地球观测中常见的视觉退化（如云、雾）和文本变化（如口语化、模糊或缺失指令）时表现脆弱、泛化差。

Method: 构建包含视觉与文本扰动的真实多模态扰动集；提出RemoteShield模型，将每个干净样本与其扰动变体组成语义等价簇，并在簇内通过偏好学习优化模型响应，使其在清洁与扰动输入下输出一致。

Result: 在三个地球观测任务上，RemoteShield在多种现实多模态扰动下显著优于基线模型，展现出更强的鲁棒性和跨条件一致性。

Conclusion: RemoteShield通过语义对齐与偏好学习机制，有效缓解了遥感MLLM对输入扰动的敏感性，为构建面向实际部署的鲁棒遥感AI系统提供了可行路径。

Abstract: A robust Multimodal Large Language Model (MLLM) for Earth Observation should maintain consistent interpretation and reasoning under realistic input variations. However, current Remote Sensing MLLMs fail to meet this requirement. Trained on carefully curated clean datasets, they learn brittle mappings that do not generalize to noisy conditions in operational Earth Observation. Consequently, their performance degrades when confronted with imperfect inputs in deployment. To quantify this vulnerability, we construct a realistic set of multimodal perturbations, including visual degradations such as cloud and fog cover, together with diverse human-centric textual variations ranging from colloquialisms to vague or omitted instructions. Empirical evaluations show that these perturbations significantly impair the visual-semantic reasoning capabilities of leading RS foundation models. To address this limitation, we introduce RemoteShield, a robust Remote Sensing MLLM trained to maintain consistent outputs across realistic input variations. During training, each clean sample is paired with its image-text perturbed variants to form a semantic equivalence cluster. Rather than directly fitting noisy samples, RemoteShield is optimized through preference learning over clean and perturbed conditions within the same cluster. By comparing model responses to clean and corrupted inputs, the model is encouraged to favor stable responses over perturbation-induced failures. This cross-condition alignment helps the model focus on underlying task semantics despite visual degradations and textual noise. Experiments on three Earth Observation tasks show that RemoteShield consistently delivers stronger robustness and cross-condition consistency than representative baselines under realistic multimodal perturbations.

</details>


### [323] [Fractal Characterization of Low-Correlation Signals in AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17268)
*Wenwei Xie,Jie Yin,Lu Ma,Xuansong Zhang,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从信号层面出发，发现低相关性信号可作为AI生成图像与真实图像的区分标志，并基于分形理论提出一种量化该信号的新方法，显著提升了深度伪造检测在开放场景下的鲁棒性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测方法在开放世界场景中鲁棒性不足，亟需探索更本质、更具泛化能力的检测线索。

Method: 基于信号层面分析，发现并利用AI生成图像中特有的低相关性信号；引入分形理论对其建模与量化，提取合成过程导致的统计异常特征。

Result: 所提方法在多个基准数据集上展现出更强的鲁棒性与检测精度，且适用于各类AI生成图像（不限于人脸），验证了信号级特征的有效性与普适性。

Conclusion: 信号级分析是深度伪造检测的新可行方向，低相关性信号及其分形表征为构建泛化性强、原理驱动的检测器提供了新范式。

Abstract: AI-generated imagery has reached near-photorealistic fidelity, yet this technology poses significant threats to information security and societal trust. Existing deepfake detection methods often exhibit limited robustness in open-world scenarios. To address this limitation, this paper investigates intrinsic discrepancies between synthetic and authentic images from a signal-level perspective. Our analysis reveals that low-correlation signals serve as distinctive markers for differentiating AI-generated imagery from real photographs. Building on this insight, we introduce a novel method for quantifying these signals based on fractal theory. By analyzing the fractal characteristics of low-correlation signals, our method effectively captures the subtle statistical anomalies inherent to the synthesis process. Extensive experimental results demonstrate the method's robustness and superior detection performance. This work emphasizes the need to shift research focus to a new signal-level direction for deepfake detection. Theoretically, this proposed approach is not limited to face image identification but can be applied to all AI-generated image detection tasks. This study provides a new research direction for deepfake detection.

</details>


### [324] [Instinct vs. Reflection: Unifying Token and Verbalized Confidence in Multimodal Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17274)
*Yunkai Dang,Yifan Jiang,Yizhu Jiang,Anqi Chen,Wenbin Li,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的置信度估计方法，通过融合模型内在直觉（token-level支持）与外在反思（口头置信评估），解决二者间的不一致问题，并提升校准性与失败预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注纯文本大语言模型的置信估计，依赖计算昂贵的自一致性采样；而多模态场景下缺乏系统性研究，且发现MLLMs存在‘直觉-反思’置信错位现象。

Method: 提出单调置信融合框架，融合双通道信号（隐式token支持与显式口头置信）并引入跨通道一致性建模；再通过保序均值对齐校正全局偏差。

Result: 在多个开源与闭源MLLM上验证了方法有效性，显著提升置信估计的可靠性、校准性及失败预测性能。

Conclusion: 直觉与反思的错位是MLLM置信估计的关键瓶颈，所提双通道融合与保序对齐策略可有效缓解该问题，为MLLM可信部署提供新思路。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated exceptional capabilities in various perception and reasoning tasks. Despite this success, ensuring their reliability in practical deployment necessitates robust confidence estimation. Prior works have predominantly focused on text-only LLMs, often relying on computationally expensive self-consistency sampling. In this paper, we extend this to multimodal settings and conduct a comprehensive evaluation of MLLMs' response confidence estimation. Our analysis reveals a significant instinct-reflection misalignment: the model's implicit token-level support frequently diverges from its verbal self-assessment confidence. To address this misalignment, we propose a monotone confidence fusion framework to merge dual-channel signals and cross-channel consistency to estimate correctness. Subsequently, an order-preserving mean alignment step is applied to correct global bias, which improves calibration while preserving the risk-coverage trade-off for selective prediction. Experiments on diverse open-source and closed-source MLLMs show that our method consistently yields more reliable confidence estimates and improves both calibration and failure prediction. Code will be available at https://github.com/Yunkaidang/Instinct-vs.-Reflection.

</details>


### [325] [PestVL-Net: Enabling Multimodal Pest Learning via Fine-grained Vision-Language Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17278)
*Xueheng Li,Tao Hu,Ke Cao,Runsheng Qi,Huixin Zhang,Rui Li,Jie Zhang,Chengjun Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PestVL-Net，一种结合视觉与语言的细粒度害虫识别框架，利用RWKV视觉主干和专家知识引导的多模态链式推理文本生成，提升真实农业场景下的害虫识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实场景中害虫数据采集困难，且害虫种类繁多、形态复杂，现有方法难以建模其细粒度视觉与高层语义特征，限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出PestVL-Net：视觉通路采用RWKV架构与显著性引导的自适应窗口划分；语言通路基于多模态大模型，融合农业专家知识并采用多模态链式思维（CoT）生成精准语义描述；最后实现视觉与文本表征的深度融合。

Result: 在多个害虫数据集上的实验表明，PestVL-Net性能优于现有方法，展现出在真实害虫管理中的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 该协同式视觉-语言框架有效解决了细粒度害虫识别难题，为可持续农业提供了一种实用、可扩展的技术路径。

Abstract: Effective pest recognition and management are crucial for sustainable agricultural development. However, collecting pest data in real scenarios is often challenging. Compared to other domains, pests exhibit a wide variety of species with complex and diverse morphological characteristics. Existing techniques struggle to effectively model the key visual and high-level semantic features of pests in a fine-grained manner. These limitations hinder the practical application of such methods in real agricultural scenarios. To address these critical challenges, we present a synergistic approach that integrates PestVL-Net, a novel vision-language framework, with two multi-species pest datasets to facilitate fine-grained pest learning. The visual pathway of PestVL-Net utilizes the Recurrent Weighted Key Value (RWKV) architecture, incorporating a saliency-guided adaptive window partitioning scheme to effectively model the fine-grained visual characteristics of pests. Concurrently, the linguistic component generates precise pest semantic descriptions by leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) priors, critically informed by agricultural expert knowledge and structured via multimodal Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning. The deep fusion of these complementary visual and textual representations enables fine-grained multimodal pest learning. Extensive experimental evaluations on multiple pest datasets validate the superior performance of PestVL-Net, highlighting its potential for effective real-world pest management.

</details>


### [326] [Depth Adaptive Efficient Visual Autoregressive Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17286)
*Chunliang Li,Tianze Cao,Sanyuan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DepthVAR，一种无需训练的框架，通过动态分配每个token的计算深度来加速视觉自回归（VAR）模型推理，相比传统硬剪枝方法，在大幅加速（2.3×–3.1×）的同时保持图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VAR模型对每个位置采用固定计算深度，导致高分辨率图像生成效率低下；现有基于频率图的硬剪枝方法存在根本性局限，无法兼顾加速与质量提升。

Method: 提出从整体剪枝token转向按token自适应分配计算深度；设计训练无关的DepthVAR框架，包含循环旋转的自适应深度调度器、层优先掩码驱动的动态推理机制，以及深度加权的码字融合策略。

Result: DepthVAR在多个基准上实现2.3×–3.1×推理加速，图像质量损失极小，计算-性能权衡优于现有硬剪枝方法。

Conclusion: 按token动态分配计算深度是一种更优的VAR加速范式；DepthVAR作为一种即插即用、无需训练的方案，显著提升了VAR模型的推理效率与实用性。

Abstract: Visual Autoregressive (VAR) modeling inefficiently applies a fixed computational depth to each position when generating high-resolution images. While existing methods accelerate inference by pruning tokens using frequency maps, their binary hard-pruning approach is fundamentally limited and fails to improve quality even with better frequency estimation. Observing that VAR models possess significant depth redundancy, we propose a paradigm shift from pruning entire tokens to adaptively allocating per-token computational depth. To this end, we introduce DepthVAR, a training-free framework that dynamically allocates computation. It integrates an adaptive depth scheduler, which assigns computational depth via a cyclic rotated schedule for balanced, non-static refinement, with a dynamic inference process that translates these depths into layer-major masks, selectively applies transformer blocks, and blends the resulting codes to ensure each token's influence is proportional to its processing depth. Extensive experiments show that DepthVAR achieves 2.3$\times$-3.1$\times$ acceleration with minimal quality loss, offering a competitive compute-performance trade-off compared to existing hard-pruning approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/STOVAGtz/DepthVAR

</details>


### [327] [Spectral Forensics of Diffusion Attention Graphs for Copy-Move Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17287)
*H. M. Shadman Tabib,Tasriad Ahmed Tias,Nafis Tahmid*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GraphSpecForge，一种无需训练的复制-移动伪造检测框架，通过分析预训练Stable Diffusion U-Net中自注意力图的谱结构（特别是归一化拉普拉斯谱）来识别伪造，利用 Wasserstein 距离比较图像谱与真实参考分布，实现在多个基准上稳定有效的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复制-移动伪造持续威胁图像真实性，现有方法多依赖监督训练或手工特征；本文旨在探索预训练扩散模型内在注意力机制中可被无监督利用的伪造敏感谱信号。

Method: 基于预训练Stable Diffusion U-Net提取多层自注意力图，构建注意力图并计算其归一化图拉普拉斯矩阵的特征谱；理论论证复制-移动操作导致近似子图复制从而引发谱重分布；以Wasserstein距离度量单图谱与真实参考谱分布的偏离程度，实现图像级异常检测。

Result: 在RecodAI-LUC、MICC-F220、CoMoFoD和COVERAGE四个基准上AUROC分别达0.606、0.752、0.774和0.673；在CoMoFoD上AUPRC=0.833，TPR@1%FPR=32.5%；归一化拉普拉斯谱比原始注意力谱提升+0.057 AUROC；消融与控制实验验证信号特异性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 注意力图的谱特性蕴含可泛化的、与伪造操作强相关的内在统计偏差，GraphSpecForge证明了无需微调大模型即可实现有效零样本伪造检测，为可信视觉分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: Copy-move forgery, where a region within an image is duplicated to hide or fabricate content, remains a persistent threat to visual media integrity. We introduce GraphSpecForge, a training-free framework that detects copy-move forgery by analysing the spectral structure of attention graphs from a pretrained Stable Diffusion U-Net. Our central insight is that copy-move manipulation induces approximate subgraph duplication in the self-attention graph, leading to measurable spectral redistribution in the normalized graph Laplacian. We formalise this link with perturbation-based arguments and build an image-level anomaly detector using Wasserstein distances between per-image Laplacian spectra and an authentic reference distribution. We evaluate GraphSpecForge on four copy-move benchmarks without forgery-specific retraining. On RecodAI-LUC (5,128 images), our best configuration achieves AUROC = 0.606 (95% CI: 0.580-0.638; permutation p = 0.005), and the normalized Laplacian outperforms raw attention spectra by +0.057 AUROC. On MICC-F220, CoMoFoD, and COVERAGE, the same pipeline attains AUROCs of 0.752, 0.774, and 0.673, respectively; on CoMoFoD it also reaches AUPRC = 0.833, balanced accuracy = 0.712, MCC = 0.499, and TPR@1%FPR = 32.5%. Additional ablation and falsification experiments confirm the signal's specificity and sensitivity to manipulation strength, while null-graph controls rule out trivial-statistic explanations.

</details>


### [328] [Frequency-guided Multi-level Reasoning for Scene Graph Generation in Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17298)
*Chenxing Li,Yiping Duan,Xiaoming Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FReMuRe模型，通过关系特异性分支、频率感知双分支谓词嵌入网络及两种新型分类头，显著提升视频场景图生成中长尾关系的召回率与推理鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频场景图生成方法在处理长尾分布关系时存在局限，难以有效建模低频（尾部）关系。

Method: 提出Frequency-guided Relational Multi-level Reasoning (FReMuRe)模型：1）引入关系特异性分支缓解梯度冲突；2）设计频率感知双分支谓词嵌入网络，分别建模高低频关系并经门控融合提升尾类召回；3）提出贝叶斯分类头（估计不确定性）和高斯混合模型分类头（增强类内多样性）。

Result: 在Action Genome数据集上，FReMuRe显著提升了长尾关系的召回率和整体推理鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 从机制层面增强对长尾关系的建模能力是提升视频场景图生成性能的有效途径，所提多级频率引导推理框架具有有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Video Scene Graph Generation aims to obtain structured semantic representations of objects and their relationships in videos for high-level understanding. However, existing methods still have limitations in handling long-tail distributions. This paper proposes the Frequency-guided Relational Multi-level Reasoning (FReMuRe) model, which enhances the modeling ability of long-tail relationships from a mechanism perspective. We introduce relation-specific branches to deal gradient conflicts, yielding more balanced and tail-aware learning. And we design a frequency-aware dual-branch predicate embedding network to model high-frequency and low-frequency relationships separately and improve the recall rate of tail classes through gated fusion. Meanwhile, we propose two types of interchangeable relation classification heads: Bayesian Head for uncertainty estimation and new Gaussian Mixture Model Head to enhance intra-class diversity. Experimental results show that FReMuRe significantly improves the recall rate of long-tail relationships and overall reasoning robustness on the Action Genome dataset.

</details>


### [329] [The First Challenge on Mobile Real-World Image Super-Resolution at NTIRE 2026: Benchmark Results and Method Overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17306)
*Jiatong Li,Zheng Chen,Kai Liu,Jingkai Wang,Zihan Zhou,Xiaoyang Liu,Libo Zhu,Jue Gong,Radu Timofte,Yulun Zhang,Congyu Wang,Zihao Wang,Ke Wu,Xinzhe Zhu,Fengkai Zhang,Zhongbao Yang,Long Sun,Jiangxin Dong,Jinshan Pan,Jiachen Tu,Yaokun Shi,Guoyi Xu,Yaoxin Jiang,Jiajia Liu,Renyuan Situ,Yixin Yang,Zhaorun Zhou,Junyang Chen,Yuqi Li,Chuanguang Yang,Weilun Feng,Chuanyue Yan,Yuedong Tan,Yingli Tian,Zhenzhong Chen,Tongqi Guo,Ruhan Liu,Sangzi Shi,Huazhang Deng,Jie Yang,Wenzhuo Ma,Yuantong Zhang,Daiqin Yang,Tianrun Chen,Deyi Ji,Yuxiao Jiang,Qi Zhu,Lanyun Zhu,Yuwen Pan,Runze Tian,Mingyu Shi,Zhanfeng Feng,Yuanfei Bao,Jiaming Guo,Renjing Pei,Xin Di,Long Peng,Linfeng Jiang,Xueyang Fu,Yang Cao,Zhengjun Zha,Choulhyouc Lee,Shyang-En Weng,Yi-Cheng Liao,Jorge Tyrakowski,Yu-Syuan Xu,Wei-Chen Chiu,Ching-Chun Huang,Yoonjin Im,Jihye Park,Hyungju Chun,Hyunhee Park,MinKyu Park,Xiaoxuan Yu,Jianxing Zhang,Yuxuan Jiang,Chengxi Zeng,Tianhao Peng,Fan Zhang,David Bull,Watchara Ruangsang,Supavadee Aramvith,JiaHao Deng,Wei Zhou,Hongyu Huang,Shaohui Lin,Zihan Wang,Yilin Chen,Yunchen Li,Junbo Qiao,Wei Li,Jiao Xie,Gaoqi He,Wenxi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文综述了NTIRE 2026移动真实世界图像超分辨率挑战赛，聚焦于在未知退化下实现x4上采样、兼顾性能与移动端部署效率的算法方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决真实场景中低分辨率图像在未知退化条件下高效恢复高分辨率图像的问题，并满足移动端部署的计算资源约束。

Method: 组织并评估参赛团队提出的轻量高效超分辨率网络设计，采用图像质量（IQA）与推理速度加权综合指标进行评测。

Result: 共108支队伍注册，16支队伍进入最终有效排名；推动了移动端真实世界超分辨率技术的发展，并总结了当前最新研究趋势。

Conclusion: 该挑战赛促进了兼顾精度与效率的移动端超分辨率方法发展，为后续研究提供了基准与方向。

Abstract: This paper provides a review of the NTIRE 2026 challenge on mobile real-world image super-resolution, highlighting the proposed solutions and the resulting outcomes. The challenge aims to recover high-resolution (HR) images from low-resolution (LR) counterparts generated through unknown degradations with a x4 scaling factor while ensuring the models remain executable on mobile devices. The objective is to develop effective and efficient network designs or solutions that achieve state-of-the-art real-world image super-resolution performance. The track of the challenge evaluates performance using a weighted combination of image quality assessment (IQA) score and speedup ratios. The competition attracted 108 registrants, with 16 teams achieving a valid score in the final ranking. This collaborative effort advances the performance of mobile real-world image super-resolution while offering an in-depth overview of the latest trends in the field.

</details>


### [330] [Generalizable Face Forgery Detection via Separable Prompt Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17307)
*Enrui Yang,Yuezun Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于CLIP文本模态引导的可分离提示学习策略（SePL），通过解耦图像中的伪造相关与无关信息，提升深度伪造人脸检测性能，展现出强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CLIP的伪造检测方法多依赖视觉编码器，忽视了具有指导性的文本模态；本文旨在利用文本模态指导检测任务。

Method: 提出可分离提示学习（SePL）策略，通过两类提示学习解耦伪造相关与无关信息，并设计跨模态对齐策略和专用优化目标。

Result: 在跨数据集和跨方法评估下，SePL取得竞争性甚至更优性能，验证了其强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 文本模态经精心设计可有效指导深度伪造检测，CLIP不仅可用于视觉理解，亦可通过提示工程在文本侧发挥关键作用。

Abstract: Detecting face forgeries using CLIP has recently emerged as a promising and increasingly popular research direction. Owing to its rich visual knowledge acquired through large-scale pretraining, most existing methods typically rely on the visual encoder of CLIP, while paying limited attention to the text modality. Given the instructive nature of the text modality, we posit that it can be leveraged to instruct Deepfake detection with meticulous design. Accordingly, we shift the focus from the visual modality to the text modality and propose a new Separable Prompt Learning strategy (SePL) that enables CLIP to serve as an effective face forgery detector. The core idea of SePL is to disentangle forgery-specific and forgery-irrelevant information in images via two types of prompt learning, with the former enhancing detection. To achieve this disentangle, we describe a cross-modality alignment strategy and a set of dedicated objectives. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, with this simple adaptation, our method achieves competitive and even superior performance compared to other methods under both cross-dataset and cross-method evaluation, highlighting its strong generalizability. The codes have been released at https://github.com/OUC-YER/SePL-DeepfakeDetection

</details>


### [331] [When Background Matters: Breaking Medical Vision Language Models by Transferable Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17318)
*Akash Ghosh,Subhadip Baidya,Sriparna Saha,Xiuying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MedFocusLeak的新型黑盒多模态对抗攻击方法，专为医疗视觉语言模型（VLMs）设计，能在保持图像扰动不可察觉的前提下，诱导模型输出错误但临床看似合理的诊断结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗对抗攻击要么聚焦于次要目标（如模型窃取），要么从自然图像迁移的攻击会产生易被医生察觉的明显失真；而临床VLMs在真实场景下的鲁棒性尚未被充分研究，存在严重安全风险。

Method: MedFocusLeak通过在非诊断性背景区域注入协调扰动，并引入注意力分散机制，使模型注意力偏离病灶区域，从而实现高可迁移性、黑盒、不可见的多模态攻击。

Result: 在六种医学影像模态上验证，MedFocusLeak达到SOTA攻击性能，能跨多种临床VLM生成误导性强但外观真实的诊断输出；并提出统一评估框架与新指标，揭示当前临床VLM推理能力的关键缺陷。

Conclusion: MedFocusLeak暴露了临床VLMs在诊断推理中的根本脆弱性，强调亟需提升其鲁棒性与可信度，尤其在关键医疗决策场景中。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly used in clinical diagnostics, yet their robustness to adversarial attacks remains largely unexplored, posing serious risks. Existing medical attacks focus on secondary objectives such as model stealing or adversarial fine-tuning, while transferable attacks from natural images introduce visible distortions that clinicians can easily detect. To address this, we propose MedFocusLeak, a highly transferable black-box multimodal attack that induces incorrect yet clinically plausible diagnoses while keeping perturbations imperceptible. The method injects coordinated perturbations into non-diagnostic background regions and employs an attention distraction mechanism to shift the model's focus away from pathological areas. Extensive evaluations across six medical imaging modalities show that MedFocusLeak achieves state-of-the-art performance, generating misleading yet realistic diagnostic outputs across diverse VLMs. We further introduce a unified evaluation framework with novel metrics that jointly capture attack success and image fidelity, revealing a critical weakness in the reasoning capabilities of modern clinical VLMs.

</details>


### [332] [E2E-GMNER: End-to-End Generative Grounded Multimodal Named Entity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17319)
*Meng Zhang,Jinzhong Ning,Xiaolong Wu,Hongfei Lin,Yijia Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出E2E-GMNER，一种端到端生成式框架，统一处理多模态命名实体识别、语义类型预测、视觉定位与隐式知识推理，并引入链式思维与高斯风险感知框扰动（GRBP）提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GMNER方法多采用分步流水线架构，导致误差累积和联合优化不足；同时，生成式定位易受标注噪声和离散化误差影响。

Method: 提出端到端生成式框架E2E-GMNER，将GMNER建模为指令微调的条件生成任务，融合链式思维推理机制，并设计高斯风险感知框扰动（GRBP）以软化边界框监督。

Result: 在Twitter-GMNER和Twitter-FMNERG基准上达到SOTA或极具竞争力的性能，验证了端到端联合优化与噪声感知定位监督的有效性。

Conclusion: 统一的端到端生成范式结合自适应推理与鲁棒定位监督，显著提升了GMNER任务的准确性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Grounded Multimodal Named Entity Recognition (GMNER) aims to jointly identify named entity mentions in text, predict their semantic types, and ground each entity to a corresponding visual region in an associated image. Existing approaches predominantly adopt pipeline-based architectures that decouple textual entity recognition and visual grounding, leading to error accumulation and suboptimal joint optimization. In this paper, we propose E2E-GMNER, a fully end-to-end generative framework that unifies entity recognition, semantic typing, visual grounding, and implicit knowledge reasoning within a single multimodal large language model. We formulate GMNER as an instruction-tuned conditional generation task and incorporate chain-of-thought reasoning to enable the model to adaptively determine when visual evidence or background knowledge is informative, reducing reliance on noisy cues. To further address the instability of generative bounding box prediction, we introduce Gaussian Risk-Aware Box Perturbation (GRBP), which replaces hard box supervision with probabilistically perturbed soft targets to improve robustness against annotation noise and discretization errors. Extensive experiments on the Twitter-GMNER and Twitter-FMNERG benchmarks demonstrate that E2E-GMNER achieves highly competitive performance compared with state of the art methods, validating the effectiveness of unified end-to-end optimization and noise-aware grounding supervision. Code is available at:https://github.com/Finch-coder/E2E-GMNER

</details>


### [333] [Towards Joint Quantization and Token Pruning of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17320)
*Xinqing Li,Xin He,Xindong Zhang,Ming-Ming Cheng,Lei Zhang,Yun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种协同量化与剪枝框架QUOTA，统一低比特推理与确定性视觉令牌剪枝，通过在W4A4量化操作下结合激活幅度、注意力线索和低比特风险信号评估令牌重要性，实现高效视觉令牌压缩，在保持95.65%平均性能的同时仅保留30%视觉令牌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vision-Language Models（VLMs）在激进低比特推理下部署困难，主要因预填充阶段长视觉令牌前缀及自回归解码中不断增长的KV缓存导致推理开销大；而现有分阶段量化与剪枝方法因校准与剪枝执行不匹配而鲁棒性差。

Method: 提出协作式量化-剪枝框架QUOTA：将低比特校准信号转化为层间令牌分配计划，并以剪枝方案形式实现；在部署的W4A4算子与量化KV缓存下，融合激活幅度、注意力线索和显式低比特风险信号评估令牌重要性，支持一致的预算化top-k选择。

Result: 在标准VLM基准测试中，相比同低比特下的分阶段基线方法，本方法展现出更高鲁棒性：在仅保留30%视觉令牌时达到95.65%平均性能保留率，优于代表性分阶段组合的约94.3%。

Conclusion: QUOTA框架成功实现了低比特量化与确定性视觉令牌剪枝的统一协同优化，显著提升了VLM在资源受限场景下的部署效率与稳定性。

Abstract: Deploying Vision-Language Models (VLMs) under aggressive low-bit inference remains challenging because inference cost is dominated by the long visual-token prefix during prefill and the growing KV cache during autoregressive decoding. Token pruning and low-bit quantization are complementary for reducing these costs, yet naive stage-wise combinations are often brittle due to a mismatch between quantization calibration and pruning execution. We present a collaborative quantization-and-pruning framework that unifies low-bit inference and deterministic visual-token pruning in a single deployable pipeline. The framework introduces the \textbf{Q}uantization \textbf{U}nified \textbf{O}ffline \textbf{T}oken \textbf{A}llocator (\textbf{QUOTA}), which converts low-bit calibration signals into a layer-wise token allocation schedule and materializes it as a pruning recipe. Token importance is evaluated under deployed W4A4 operators with a quantized KV cache by combining activation magnitude, attention cues, and an explicit low-bit risk signal, enabling consistent budgeted top-$k$ selection. Experiments on standard VLM benchmarks show improved robustness over stage-wise baselines under the same low-bit regime, achieving 95.65\% average retention while retaining only 30\% of visual tokens, compared with about 94.3\% retention for representative stage-wise combinations. The code will be released.

</details>


### [334] [R-FLoRA: Residual-Statistic-Gated Low-Rank Adaptation for Single-Image Face Morphing Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17321)
*Raghavendra Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种单图像人脸融合攻击检测（S-MAD）框架，结合拉普拉斯残差统计与冻结的大规模视觉Transformer特征，通过R-FLoRA和Res-FiLM模块增强对局部融合伪影的敏感性，并引入残差对比对齐损失提升泛化能力，在多个ICAO合规数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸融合攻击严重威胁护照签发、边境管控和数字身份验证中人脸识别系统的可靠性；单图检测面临缺乏可信参考和攻击方法多样性的挑战。

Method: 提出S-MAD框架：融合高频率拉普拉斯残差统计与冻结的视觉Transformer特征；引入残差统计门控低秩适配器（R-FLoRA）和特征级残差融合（Res-FiLM）；设计残差对比对齐损失以正则化融合token空间。

Result: 在四个ICAO合规数据集、涵盖七种融合生成技术的实验中，该方法在检测精度和跨域泛化性上持续超越九种最新S-MAD算法；模型参数少、推理高效且可解释。

Conclusion: 所提方法兼顾高检测性能、强泛化能力、实时效率与可解释性，适用于真实生物特征验证系统。

Abstract: Face morphing attacks pose a substantial risk to the reliability of face recognition systems used in passport issuance, border control, and digital identity verification. Detecting morphing attacks from a single facial image remains challenging owing to the lack of a trusted reference and the diversity of attack generation methods. This paper presents a new Single-Image Face Morphing Attack Detection (S-MAD) framework that integrates high-frequency Laplacian residual statistics with representations from a frozen, foundation-scale vision transformer. The approach employs residual-statistic-gated low-rank adapters (R-FLoRA) and feature-wise residual fusion (Res-FiLM) to enhance sensitivity to local morphing artefacts while preserving the semantic context of the backbone. A novel residual-contrastive alignment loss further regularises the fused token space, improving discrimination under unseen morphing conditions. Comprehensive experiments on four ICAO-compliant datasets, encompassing seven morph generation techniques, demonstrate that the proposed method consistently surpasses nine recent state-of-the-art S-MAD algorithms in detection accuracy and cross-domain (or dataset) generalisation. With a frozen backbone and minimal trainable parameters, the model achieves real-time efficiency and interpretability, making it suitable for real-life scenarios in biometric verification systems.

</details>


### [335] [Robust Diabetic Retinopathy Grading Using Dual-Resolution Attention-Based Deep Learning with Ordinal Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17341)
*Afshan Hashmi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种鲁棒的双分辨率深度学习框架，结合注意力机制特征融合与序数回归（CORAL），提升糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）自动分级在跨数据集场景下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在不同成像条件下采集的数据集间性能下降，亟需提升跨数据集泛化能力以支持大规模DR筛查。

Method: 采用双分支EfficientNet主干网络处理不同空间分辨率图像；引入可学习注意力机制融合多分辨率特征；使用基于累积链接模型（CORAL）的序数回归建模DR等级的有序性；并结合圆形裁剪、对比度增强和直方图匹配进行域适配预处理。

Result: 在APTOS 2019验证集上QWK达0.88，在外部Messidor-2测试集上QWK达0.68，显著优于基线方法，验证了跨数据集鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该双分辨率+注意力融合+序数回归框架有效提升了DR自动分级模型对成像差异的鲁棒性，适用于真实世界多中心筛查部署。

Abstract: Diabetic retinopathy (DR) is a leading cause of vision impairment worldwide, and automated grading systems play a crucial role in large-scale screening programs. However, deep learning models often exhibit degraded performance when deployed across datasets acquired under different imaging conditions. This study presents a robust dual-resolution deep learning framework for DR grading that integrates attention-based feature fusion with ordinal regression to improve cross-dataset generalization. The proposed method employs two parallel EfficientNet backbones operating at different spatial resolutions to capture complementary retinal features. A learnable attention mechanism adaptively fuses multi-resolution representations, while an ordinal regression formulation based on the cumulative link model (CORAL) explicitly accounts for the ordered nature of DR severity levels. To mitigate domain discrepancies between datasets, a preprocessing strategy combining circular cropping, contrast enhancement, and histogram matching is applied. The model was trained on the APTOS 2019 dataset and evaluated on both an internal validation split and an external Messidor-2 test set. Experimental results demonstrate strong grading performance, achieving a quadratic weighted kappa (QWK) of 0.88 on the APTOS validation set and 0.68 on the unseen Messidor-2 dataset, indicating improved robustness for cross-dataset DR grading applications.

</details>


### [336] [When Text Hijacks Vision: Benchmarking and Mitigating Text Overlay-Induced Hallucination in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17375)
*Cui Yakun,Xingqun Qi,TianTian Geng,Yuyao Zhang,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Text Overlay-Induced Hallucination（TOIH）问题，即VLMs在屏幕文字与视觉内容矛盾时倾向于错误依赖文字而忽略真实画面；为此构建首个大规模人工验证基准VisualTextTrap，并提出基于解耦的多专家模型VTHM-MoE以缓解该幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在屏幕文字与视觉场景矛盾时会系统性地产生幻觉（优先采信文字而非图像），这一关键问题被长期忽视。

Method: 构建了大规模、细粒度、人工验证的TOIH评测基准VisualTextTrap（含6057样本、88个属性、5级幻觉强度标注）；并提出VTHM-MoE框架：采用双编码器结构，集成4个维度专用专家模块（时序、动作、物体、空间），通过自适应Token路由策略实现动态专家分配。

Result: VTHM-MoE在VisualTextTrap上显著优于现有SOTA方法，在多种视频问答任务中展现出对TOIH的强鲁棒性，同时在无干扰视频上保持原有性能。

Conclusion: TOIH是VLM视频理解中一个真实且严重的问题；VisualTextTrap为评估和推动抗幻觉能力提供了新标准；VTHM-MoE验证了跨模态解耦与专家协同是缓解TOIH的有效范式。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have substantially enhanced their ability across multimodal video understanding benchmarks spanning temporal, action, object, and spatial understanding. However, we identify a critical yet overlooked issue: when embedded on-screen text contradicts the visual scene, existing VLMs systematically hallucinate, prioritizing overlay textual semantics over the actual visual content. We define this phenomenon as Text Overlay-Induced Hallucination (TOIH). In this work, we propose VisualTextTrap, the first comprehensive benchmark, including large-scale human-validated samples with specifically designed evaluation metrics. In particular, we construct VisualTextTrap from widely-used public datasets using a scalable hybrid pipeline of VLMs assisted text generation and rigorous manual verification. The benchmark features 6,057 samples annotated across 88 fine-grained attributes within four dimensions, with hallucination intensity quantified on a five-level scale (L1--L5) that reflects the semantic contradiction between overlay text and visual reality. Moreover, we propose Visual Text Hallucination Mitigation Mixture-of-Experts (VTHM-MoE), a novel Vision-Text Disentanglement framework that employs a dual-encoder architecture. Concretely, four dimension-specialized expert modules spanning Temporal, Action, Object, and Spatial reasoning are first pre-trained to identify and leverage cross-modal discrepancies between textual semantics and actual video content. We develop an Adaptive Token Routing Strategy to enable dynamic expert allocation, conferring robust resistance to TOIH while preserving performance on uncontaminated videos. Extensive experiments conducted on our VisualTextTrap benchmark verify the effectiveness of VTHM-MoE, outperforming state-of-the-art counterparts with diverse video question answering tasks.

</details>


### [337] [Towards Generalizable Deepfake Image Detection with Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17376)
*Kaliki V Srinanda,M Manvith Prabhu,Hemanth K Mogilipalem,Jayavarapu S Abhinai,Vaibhav Santhosh,Aryan Herur,Deepu Vijayasenan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于微调视觉Transformer（如DINOv2、AIMv2、ViT-L/14）集成的通用深伪图像检测方法，在DF-Wild数据集上达到96.77% AUC和9% EER，显著优于现有SOTA方法Effort，并获IEEE SP Cup 2025冠军。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度伪造检测方法泛化能力差，难以应对快速演进的生成模型。

Method: 构建DINOv2、AIMv2与OpenCLIP ViT-L/14的微调视觉Transformer集成模型，并在DF-Wild数据集（含多样操纵与生成技术）上训练；对比基线为CNN空间特征分类器。

Result: 在DF-Wild测试集上取得96.77% AUC和9% EER，较SOTA方法Effort分别提升7.05% AUC和8% EER。

Conclusion: 基于多视觉Transformer微调的集成方法具有强泛化性，是当前深伪检测的有效新范式，并成功应用于SP Cup 2025竞赛夺冠。

Abstract: In today's day and age, we face a challenge in detecting deepfake images because of the fast evolution of modern generative models and the poor generalization capability of existing methods. In this paper, we use an ensemble of fine-tuned vision transformers like DINOv2, AIMv2 and OpenCLIP's ViT-L/14 to create generalizable method to detect deepfakes. We use the DF-Wild dataset released as part of the IEEE SP Cup 2025, because it uses a challenging and diverse set of manipulations and generation techniques. We started our experiments with CNN classifiers trained on spatial features. Experimental results show that our ensemble outperforms individual models and strong CNN baselines, achieving an AUC of 96.77% and an Equal Error Rate (EER) of just 9% on the DF-Wild test set, beating the state-of-the-art deepfake detection algorithm Effort by 7.05% and 8% in AUC and EER respectively. This was the winning solution for SP Cup, presented at ICASSP 2025.

</details>


### [338] [SpatialImaginer: Towards Adaptive Visual Imagination for Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17385)
*Yian Li,Yang Jiao,Bin Zhu,Tianwen Qian,Shaoxiang Chen,Jingjing Chen,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpatialImaginer框架，通过结合文本链式推理与视觉想象，解决多模态大语言模型在空间智能任务中几何结构保持脆弱的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在空间智能任务中推理轨迹脆弱，源于空间识别机制与纯文本推理行为之间的不匹配；文本表征易丢失关键低层几何细节，难以支持稳定的空间状态跟踪。

Method: 提出SpatialImaginer：采用分治策略，用文本思维链进行高层语义规划，用视觉想象实现几何敏感的状态变换与一致性保持；并设计难度感知的闭环验证数据引擎，训练模型按需调用视觉想象。

Result: 在多个空间智能基准测试中达到SOTA性能，显著提升了复杂多步空间推理任务的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 融合文本推理与视觉想象的统一生成框架，能有效弥补纯文本表征在空间结构建模上的缺陷，为提升MLLM空间智能提供新范式。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence, which refers to the ability to reason about geometric and physical structure from visual observations, remains a core challenge for multimodal large language models. Despite promising performance, recent multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often exhibit fragile reasoning traces in spatial intelligence tasks that involve consistent spatial state recognition. We argue that these failures stem from a mismatch between the spatial recognition mechanism and the text-only reasoning behavior of these MLLMs. Effective spatial reasoning requires low-level geometric structure to be faithfully preserved and updated throughout the reasoning process, whereas textual representations tend to abstract away precisely these critical details. To address this issue, we propose SpatialImaginer, a unified multimodal generation framework that integrates textual reasoning with visual imagination. Our framework adopts a divide-and-conquer strategy, using text chain-of-thought for high-level semantic planning and the visual imagination for geometry-sensitive state transformation and consistency preservation. To support this capability, we further introduce a difficulty-aware data engine with closed-loop verification to train the model to invoke visual imagination selectively when stable spatial state tracking is required. Extensive experiments on diverse spatial intelligence benchmarks show that SpatialImaginer achieves state-of-the-art performance and substantially improves robustness on complex multi-step spatial reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [339] [Deep learning based Non-Rigid Volume-to-Surface Registration for Brain Shift compensation Using Point Cloud](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17389)
*Eashrat Jahan Muniya,Gernot Kronreif,Ander Biguri,Wolfgang Birkfellner,Sepideh Hatamikia*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度学习的非刚性体-面配准框架，利用术中稀疏皮层表面点云估计全脑密集位移场，无需术中体积成像或显式点对应，实现手术流程兼容的脑移位补偿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软组织形变（如脑移位）导致术前影像与术中解剖不一致，严重影响神经导航精度与手术安全；现有补偿方法依赖术中MRI/CT/超声，干扰手术流程；而术中立体显微镜或激光扫描可获取部分皮层点云，但稀疏、噪声大、覆盖有限，传统配准方法难以应对。

Method: 提出端到端深度学习框架，实现从稀疏术中表面点云到完整术前体积的非刚性配准；采用多尺度点云特征提取与分层形变解码器，将部分术中表面信息映射至完整术前点云域，隐式学习对应关系并恢复稠密位移场。

Result: 在挑战性的部分表面条件下，达到终点误差EPE为1.13±0.75 mm、RMSE为1.33±0.81 mm；支持全自动、不中断手术流程的脑移位补偿。

Conclusion: 该方法为术中无侵入、实时、高精度脑移位建模提供了新范式，显著提升图像引导神经外科导航的鲁棒性与临床适用性。

Abstract: Soft-tissue deformation remains a major limitation in image-guided neurosurgery, where intra-operative anatomy can deviate substantially from pre-operative imaging due to brain shift, compromising navigation accuracy and surgical safety. Existing compensation methods often rely on intra-operative MRI, CT, or ultrasound, which are disruptive and difficult to integrate repeatedly into the surgical workflow. In contrast, partial 3D cortical surfaces can be reconstructed as point clouds from stereoscopic microscopes or laser range scanners (LRS), capturing only a limited portion of the exposed cortex. This makes point cloud registration a practical alternative without interrupting surgery; however, such partial and noisy observations make deformation estimation highly challenging. In this study, we propose a deep learning-based framework for non-rigid volume-to-surface registration, enabling dense displacement field estimation from sparse intra-operative surface observations without explicit point correspondences or volumetric intra-operative imaging. The network leverages multi-scale point-based feature extraction and a hierarchical deformation decoder to capture both global and local deformations. The key contribution lies in integrating partial intra-operative surface information into the full pre-operative point cloud domain, enabling implicit correspondence learning and dense deformation recovery under limited visibility. Quantitative results demonstrate accurate recovery of fine-scale deformations, achieving an Endpoint Error (EPE) of 1.13 +/- 0.75 mm and RMSE of 1.33 +/- 0.81 mm under challenging partial-surface conditions. The proposed approach supports automatic, workflow-compatible brain-shift compensation from sparse surface observations.

</details>


### [340] [MESA: A Training-Free Multi-Exemplar Deep Framework for Restoring Ancient Inscription Textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17390)
*Vasileios Toulatzis,Ioannis Fudos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MESA方法，利用多示例和风格感知技术恢复古代铭文图像，通过VGG19特征编码、层选择与权重优化提升修复效果，并提供实用修复策略指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 古代铭文常因破碎、侵蚀等原因导致文字缺失或损坏，影响阅读与分析，现有图像修复方法在铭文恢复任务中存在局限性。

Method: 提出MESA（Multi-Exemplar, Style-Aware）图像级修复方法：使用同碑刻、同材质或相似字形的完好铭文作为示例；以VGG19的Gram矩阵编码纹理、风格与笔画结构；每层选择与受损图像Mean-Squared Displacement最小的示例；根据OCR估计的字符宽度设定层贡献权重；引入训练掩码仅修复受损区域。

Result: 实验表明MESA在铭文图像修复任务中优于现有方法，能更准确重建文字结构与风格；并系统总结了以往网络架构与修复范式（如单图修复、inpainting、GAN）及其局限。

Conclusion: MESA有效结合多示例引导与风格感知机制，提升了古代铭文图像修复的准确性与实用性；同时提供了依据可用示例与元数据选择修复策略的实践指南。

Abstract: Ancient inscriptions frequently suffer missing or corrupted regions from fragmentation, erosion, or other damage, hindering reading, and analysis. We review prior image restoration methods and their applicability to inscription image recovery, then introduce MESA (Multi-Exemplar, Style-Aware) -an image-level restoration method that uses well-preserved exemplar inscriptions (from the same epigraphic monument, material, or similar letterforms) to guide reconstruction of damaged text. MESA encodes VGG19 convolutional features as Gram matrices to capture exemplar texture, style, and stroke structure; for each neural network layer it selects the exemplar minimizing Mean-Squared Displacement (MSD) to the damaged input. Layer-wise contribution weights are derived from Optical Character Recognition-estimated character widths in the exemplar set to bias filters toward scales matching letter geometry, and a training mask preserves intact regions so synthesis is restricted to damaged areas. We also summarize prior network architectures and exemplar and single-image synthesis, inpainting, and Generative Adversarial Network (GAN) approaches, highlighting limitations that MESA addresses. Comparative experiments demonstrate the advantages of MESA. Finally, we provide a practical roadmap for choosing restoration strategies given available exemplars and metadata.

</details>


### [341] [Speculative Decoding for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17397)
*Yuezhou Hu,Jintao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SDVG框架，将推测解码（speculative decoding）引入块级自回归视频扩散模型，通过图像质量路由器替代传统token验证，实现高效视频生成加速；无需训练、不改变架构，可在保持高质量的同时达到最高2.09倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究推测解码能否适配连续时空张量的视频块生成——因缺乏离散token分布，无法直接套用语言模型中的精确拒绝采样机制。

Method: 提出SDVG：1.3B小模型（drafter）以4步去噪生成候选视频块；块经VAE解码后，用ImageReward按最差帧聚合打分；得分高于阈值tau者接受并写入14B目标模型KV缓存，否则由目标模型重生成；首块强制拒绝以稳定场景构成；tau作为统一调节参数控制质量-速度权衡。

Result: 在1003个MovieGenVideoBench提示上，tau=-0.7时达1.59×加速且保留98.1% VisionReward质量（0.0773 vs. 0.0788）；更激进设置下达2.09×加速、95.7%质量保留；始终比纯draft生成提升超17%。

Conclusion: 推测解码可成功迁移至自回归视频生成，关键在于用图像质量路由替代token验证；SDVG是训练无关、即插即用的通用加速方案，定义了清晰的质量-速度帕累托前沿。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion is emerging as a promising paradigm for streaming video synthesis, with step distillation serving as the primary means of accelerating inference. Whether speculative decoding, the dominant acceleration strategy for large language models, can be effectively adapted to autoregressive video generation remains an open question, because video blocks are continuous spatiotemporal tensors with no token-level distribution for exact rejection sampling. We introduce SDVG, which brings speculative decoding to block-based autoregressive video diffusion by replacing token verification with an image-quality router. A 1.3B drafter proposes candidate blocks via four denoising steps; each block is VAE-decoded and scored by ImageReward using worst-frame aggregation--taking the minimum per-frame reward to catch single-frame artifacts that averaging would mask. Blocks scoring above a fixed threshold tau are accepted into the 14B target's KV cache; the rest are regenerated by the target. Two additional design choices prove critical: the first block is always force-rejected to anchor scene composition, and tau serves as a single knob that traces a smooth quality-speed Pareto frontier. On 1003 MovieGenVideoBench prompts (832x480), SDVG retains 98.1% of target-only VisionReward quality (0.0773 vs. 0.0788) at a 1.59x speedup with tau=-0.7, and reaches 2.09x at 95.7% quality retention--while consistently outperforming draft-only generation by over +17%. The framework is training-free, requires no architectural changes, and can be seamlessly integrated into existing autoregressive video generation pipelines.

</details>


### [342] [Where to Focus: Query-Modulated Multimodal Keyframe Selection for Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17422)
*Shaoguang Wang,Weiyu Guo,Ziyang Chen,Xuming Hu,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Q-Gate框架，通过查询调制的门控机制动态路由视觉、全局语义与字幕上下文三种专家流，实现无需训练的高效长视频关键帧选择，显著提升多模态大模型在长视频理解任务上的性能与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关键帧选择方法采用单一或静态融合策略，难以兼顾视觉细节与叙事理解，导致模态噪声严重、泛化能力差。

Method: 提出Q-Gate：一种即插即用、无需训练的框架，将关键帧选择建模为动态模态路由问题；设计三个轻量专家流（视觉定位、全局匹配、上下文对齐），并引入基于LLM上下文推理的查询调制门控机制，动态加权各专家输出。

Result: 在LongVideoBench和Video-MME基准上，Q-Gate在多个MLLM骨干网络上显著超越现有最优方法，有效抑制模态噪声，提升推理鲁棒性与可解释性。

Conclusion: Q-Gate为长视频理解提供了一种通用、高效且可解释的动态模态融合范式，突破了传统‘一刀切’关键帧选择的瓶颈。

Abstract: Long video understanding remains a formidable challenge for Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) due to the prohibitive computational cost of processing dense frame sequences. Prevailing solutions, which select a keyframe subset, typically rely on either a single visual-centric metric (e.g., CLIP similarity) or a static fusion of heuristic scores. This ``one-size-fits-all'' paradigm frequently fails: visual-only metrics are ineffective for plot-driven narrative queries, while indiscriminately incorporating textual scores introduces severe ``modal noise'' for purely visual tasks. To break this bottleneck, we propose Q-Gate, a plug-and-play and training-free framework that treats keyframe selection as a dynamic modality routing problem. We decouple the retrieval process into three lightweight expert streams: Visual Grounding for local details, Global Matching for scene semantics, and Contextual Alignment for subtitle-driven narratives. Crucially, Q-Gate introduces a Query-Modulated Gating Mechanism that leverages the in-context reasoning of an LLM to assess the query's intent and dynamically allocate attention weights across the experts. This mechanism intelligently activates necessary modalities while ``muting'' irrelevant ones, thereby maximizing the signal-to-noise ratio. Extensive experiments on LongVideoBench and Video-MME across multiple MLLM backbones demonstrate that Q-Gate substantially outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. By effectively suppressing modality-specific noise, it provides a robust, highly interpretable solution for scalable video reasoning.

</details>


### [343] [Long-CODE: Isolating Pure Long-Context as an Orthogonal Dimension in Video Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17428)
*Zhijiang Tang,Jiaxin Qi,Bing Zhao,Jianqiang Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种专用于长视频评估的新框架Long-CODE，指出传统短时视频指标无法捕捉叙事丰富性与全局因果一致性等长程特性，并设计基于镜头动态的新型指标与配套基准数据集，显著提升与人类判断的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频评估指标主要针对短视频，难以衡量长视频中的叙事丰富性、全局因果一致性等长程特性；短时感知与长时上下文是正交维度，需解耦评估。

Method: 提出长视频属性扰动测试以揭示现有指标缺陷；设计基于镜头动态的新型长视频评估指标；构建专用数据集Long-CODE，其人工标注聚焦纯长程特性。

Result: 所提指标在多项实验中达到与人类判断最高的相关性；Long-CODE数据集和新指标可无缝补充现有短视频评估标准。

Conclusion: 长视频评估需独立于短视频指标体系；本文提出的Long-CODE框架为视频生成模型建立了更全面、无偏的评估范式。

Abstract: As video generation models achieve unprecedented capabilities, the demand for robust video evaluation metrics becomes increasingly critical. Traditional metrics are intrinsically tailored for short-video evaluation, predominantly assessing frame-level visual quality and localized temporal smoothness. However, as state-of-the-art video generation models scale to generate longer videos, these metrics fail to capture essential long-range characteristics, such as narrative richness and global causal consistency. Recognizing that short-term visual perception and long-context attributes are fundamentally orthogonal dimensions, we argue that long-video metrics should be disentangled from short-video assessments. In this paper, we focus on the rigorous justification and design of a dedicated framework for long-video evaluation. We first introduce a suite of long-video attribute corruption tests, exposing the critical limitations of existing hort-video metrics from their insensitivity to structural inconsistencies, such as shot-level perturbations and narrative shuffling. To bridge this gap, we design a novel long-video metric based on shot dynamics, which is highly sensitive to the long-range testing framework. Furthermore, we introduce Long-CODE (Long-Context as an Orthogonal Dimension for video Evaluation), a specialized dataset designed to benchmark long-video evaluation, with human annotations isolated specifically to genuine long-range characteristics. Extensive experiments show that our proposed metrics achieve state-of-the-art correlation with human judgments. Ultimately, our metric and benchmark seamlessly complement existing short-video standards, establishing a holistic and unbiased evaluation paradigm for video generation models.

</details>


### [344] [DEM Refinement and Validation on the Lunar Surface Using Shape-from-Shading with Chandrayaan-2 OHRC Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17436)
*Aaranay Aadi,Jai Gopal Singla,Nitant Dube*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于阴影恢复形状（SfS）的新框架，利用Chandrayaan-2轨道器高分辨率相机（OHRC）影像提升亚米级月球数字高程模型（DEM）精度，突破立体成像基线限制，实现独立地形数据生成与精细地貌增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升亚米级月球DEM精度，克服传统立体成像受基线长度限制、难以获取精细地形信息的瓶颈，拓展SfS在行星遥感中的应用价值。

Method: 构建SfS框架，对同一区域的独立OHRC影像进行处理；在三个典型月面区域（Cyrillus陨石坑、Vikram着陆区、南极Mons Mouton）开展实验；系统实施三阶段SfS平滑权重参数扫描，并分析俯仰角差异与影像覆盖度对结果的影响。

Result: 显著提升地形细节，尤其改善表面坡度统计特征，揭示此前未解析的微小撞击坑形态；识别出大俯仰角差导致SfS灵敏度下降、影像部分覆盖引发空间增强质量不均等关键限制因素。

Conclusion: SfS可作为独立地形数据源而非仅DEM后处理工具，为高分辨率月球及类地行星地形建模提供新范式，但其性能高度依赖成像几何一致性与影像覆盖完整性。

Abstract: This study presents a Shape from Shading (SfS) framework to enhance sub-metre resolution lunar digital elevation models (DEMs) using imagery from the Orbiter High Resolution Camera (OHRC) aboard Chandrayaan-2. The framework applies SfS to an independent OHRC image of the same region, enabling SfS not just as a refinement tool, but as a source of new topographic data, unconstrained by stereo baseline limitations. The method is applied across three lunar sites, including the Cyrillus crater, the Vikram landing region, and the lunar south pole (Mons Mouton), with a systematic three-stage parameter sweep on the SfS smoothness weight.
