Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior editing performance with precise and consistent views for complex scenes.

</details>


### [381] [Re$^2$MoGen: Open-Vocabulary Motion Generation via LLM Reasoning and Physics-Aware Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17807)
*Jiakun Zheng,Ting Xiao,Shiqin Cao,Xinran Li,Zhe Wang,Chenjia Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Re²MoGen框架，通过增强大语言模型（LLM）的推理能力生成初始运动规划，并结合强化学习进行物理合理性优化，实现开放词汇下的高质量文本驱动动作生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到动作（T2M）模型在训练分布外的文本描述下性能显著下降，亟需提升开放词汇泛化能力与物理合理性。

Method: Re²MoGen包含三阶段：1）用蒙特卡洛树搜索增强LLM推理，生成关键帧（仅指定根节点和若干关键关节位置）；2）利用人体姿态先验和动态时序匹配目标优化全身姿态并补全时空动作；3）基于物理感知奖励的强化学习后训练进一步修正动作物理不合理性。

Result: 在开放词汇动作生成任务上达到SOTA性能，生成的动作兼具语义一致性与物理合理性。

Conclusion: 将LLM的高层语义推理与物理引导的精细化优化相结合，是提升开放词汇T2M生成质量的有效范式。

Abstract: Text-to-motion (T2M) generation aims to control the behavior of a target character via textual descriptions. Leveraging text-motion paired datasets, existing T2M models have achieved impressive performance in generating high-quality motions within the distribution of their training data. However, their performance deteriorates notably when the motion descriptions differ significantly from the training texts. To address this issue, we propose Re$^2$MoGen, a Reasoning and Refinement open-vocabulary Motion Generation framework that leverages enhanced Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning to generate an initial motion planning and then refine its physical plausibility via reinforcement learning (RL) post-training. Specifically, Re$^2$MoGen consists of three stages: We first employ Monte Carlo tree search to enhance the LLM's reasoning ability in generating reasonable keyframes of the motion based on text prompts, specifying only the root and several key joints' positions to ease the reasoning process. Then, we apply a human pose model as a prior to optimize the full-body poses based on the planned keyframes and use the resulting incomplete motion to supervise fine-tuning a pre-trained motion generator via a dynamic temporal matching objective, enabling spatiotemporal completion. Finally, we use post-training with physics-aware reward to refine motion quality to eliminate physical implausibility in LLM-planned motions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework can generate semantically consistent and physically plausible motions and achieve state-of-the-art performance in open-vocabulary motion generation.

</details>


### [382] [AnyLift: Scaling Motion Reconstruction from Internet Videos via 2D Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17818)
*Hongjie Li,Heng Yu,Jiaman Li,Hong-Xing Yu,Ehsan Adeli,C. Karen Liu,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种两阶段框架，利用2D扩散模型从互联网视频中重建3D人体运动和人-物交互（HOI），解决了动态相机下全局一致3D运动重建难、罕见动作和复杂HOI建模差的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以在动态相机下恢复全局一致的3D人体运动，尤其对动捕数据集中稀少的动作类型；同时难以在3D空间中建模连贯的人-物交互。

Method: 采用两阶段框架：第一阶段基于互联网视频提取的2D关键点，为各领域合成多视角2D运动数据；第二阶段训练相机条件下的多视角2D运动扩散模型，在领域特定合成数据上重建世界坐标系下的3D人体运动与3D HOI。

Result: 在体操等挑战性动作及野外人-物交互视频上验证有效，生成的3D人体运动和HOI比先前方法更真实、更一致。

Conclusion: 该方法显著提升了从无约束互联网视频中重建高质量、多样化3D人体运动与HOI的能力，为构建大规模人类行为数据集提供了新途径。

Abstract: Reconstructing 3D human motion and human-object interactions (HOI) from Internet videos is a fundamental step toward building large-scale datasets of human behavior. Existing methods struggle to recover globally consistent 3D motion under dynamic cameras, especially for motion types underrepresented in current motion-capture datasets, and face additional difficulty recovering coherent human-object interactions in 3D. We introduce a two-stage framework leveraging 2D diffusion that reconstructs 3D human motion and HOI from Internet videos. In the first stage, we synthesize multi-view 2D motion data for each domain, leveraging 2D keypoints extracted from Internet videos to incorporate human motions that rarely appear in existing MoCap datasets. In the second stage, a camera-conditioned multi-view 2D motion diffusion model is trained on the domain-specific synthetic data to recover 3D human motion and 3D HOI in the world space. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our method on Internet videos featuring challenging motions such as gymnastics, as well as in-the-wild HOI videos, and show that it outperforms prior work in producing realistic human motion and human-object interaction.

</details>


### [383] [GR4CIL: Gap-compensated Routing for CLIP-based Class Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17822)
*Tianqi Wang,Jingcai Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GR4CIL框架，通过任务判别与知识路由结合，解决CLIP模型在类增量学习中旧知识漂移和跨任务响应校准差的问题，提升任务感知路由可靠性并保持零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CLIP的类增量学习方法面临共享参数适配导致旧知识漂移、任务特定知识组织引发跨任务响应校准不佳两大挑战。

Method: 提出GR4CIL框架：1）分离保留任务特定视觉知识，同时维护稳定的共享文本语义空间；2）引入正交补偿机制缓解模态间隙偏差、增强任务内判别力、扩大真实任务与竞争任务间的分数间隔。

Result: 在多个基准上实验表明，GR4CIL持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: GR4CIL有效缓解了CLIP在类增量学习中的知识干扰与路由不可靠问题，在保持零样本泛化能力的同时提升了增量性能。

Abstract: Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) aims to continuously acquire new categories while preserving previously learned knowledge. Recently, Contrastive Language-Image Pre-trained (CLIP) models have shown strong potential for CIL due to their powerful generalization ability. However, existing methods still face two key challenges: shared-parameter adaptation tends to cause old-knowledge drift, and task-specific knowledge organization often leads to poorly calibrated cross-task responses, making reliable routing difficult. To address these issues, we propose GR4CIL, a framework combining task discrimination and knowledge routing for CLIP-based CIL. GR4CIL preserves task-specific visual knowledge while maintaining an incrementally stable shared textual semantic space, thereby reducing interference across tasks. Moreover, we introduce an orthogonal compensation mechanism to mitigate modality-gap-induced bias, enhance within-task discrimination, and enlarge the score margin between the ground-truth task and competing tasks. As a result, GR4CIL enables more reliable task-aware routing over learned knowledge while retaining the zero-shot generalization capability. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that GR4CIL consistently outperforms strong baselines.

</details>


### [384] [PCM-NeRF: Probabilistic Camera Modeling for Neural Radiance Fields under Pose Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17831)
*Shravan Venkatraman,Rakesh Raj Madavan,Pavan Kumar Sathya Venkatesh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PCM-NeRF 提出一种概率框架，在神经表面重建中引入每相机可学习的位姿不确定性，通过不确定性正则化损失和梯度调制机制提升对不良SfM位姿的鲁棒性，显著改善复杂几何结构的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经表面重建方法假设SfM估计的相机位姿完全准确，但在实际中位姿存在误差，导致重建失真或不完整。

Method: 在SG-NeRF基础上构建PCM-NeRF，将每个相机位姿建模为具有可学习均值与方差的概率分布，方差初始化自SfM对应点质量；引入不确定性正则化损失，并用学习到的不确定性动态调节位姿优化的学习率（不确定相机梯度衰减）。

Result: 在含严重位姿异常值的挑战性场景上，PCM-NeRF在Chamfer Distance和F-Score指标上持续超越当前最优方法，尤其提升复杂几何结构重建精度，且无需前景掩膜。

Conclusion: 引入相机级位姿不确定性建模与梯度调制是一种轻量、高效且通用的改进策略，能显著增强神经辐射场在真实世界不完美位姿下的鲁棒性与重建质量。

Abstract: Neural surface reconstruction methods typically treat camera poses as fixed values, assuming perfect accuracy from Structure-from-Motion (SfM) systems. This assumption breaks down with imperfect pose estimates, leading to distorted or incomplete reconstructions. We present PCM-NeRF, a probabilistic framework that augments neural surface reconstruction with per-camera learnable uncertainty, built on top of SG-NeRF. Rather than treating all cameras equally throughout optimization, we represent each pose as a distribution with a learnable mean and variance, initialized from SfM correspondence quality. An uncertainty regularization loss couples the learned variance to view confidence, and the resulting uncertainty directly modulates the effective pose learning rate: uncertain cameras receive damped gradient updates, preventing poorly initialized views from corrupting the reconstruction. This lightweight mechanism requires no changes to the rendering pipeline and adds negligible overhead. Experiments on challenging scenes with severe pose outliers demonstrate that PCM-NeRF consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both Chamfer Distance and F-Score, particularly for geometrically complex structures, without requiring foreground masks.

</details>


### [385] [From Heads to Neurons: Causal Attribution and Steering in Multi-Task Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17941)
*Qidong Wang,Junjie Hu,Ming Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HONES框架，用于多任务视觉语言模型中任务感知的神经元归因与调控，通过结合任务相关注意力头来评估FFN神经元的因果写入贡献，并进行轻量缩放调控，在多个任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经元分析局限于单任务，难以跨任务比较神经元重要性；且忽略任务依赖的信息通路对FFN神经元写入效应的影响，加剧多任务下的神经元多义性，影响关键神经元识别与干预。

Method: 提出HONES（Head-Oriented Neuron Explanation & Steering）：一种无需梯度的框架，基于任务相关注意力头对FFN神经元进行因果写入贡献排序，并通过轻量缩放调控显著神经元。

Result: 在四个多模态任务和两个主流VLM上实验表明，HONES在识别任务关键神经元方面优于现有方法，并在神经元调控后提升模型性能。

Conclusion: HONES有效缓解多任务VLM中神经元多义性问题，实现更精准、可解释、可干预的任务感知神经元分析。

Abstract: Recent work has increasingly explored neuron-level interpretation in vision-language models (VLMs) to identify neurons critical to final predictions. However, existing neuron analyses generally focus on single tasks, limiting the comparability of neuron importance across tasks. Moreover, ranking strategies tend to score neurons in isolation, overlooking how task-dependent information pathways shape the write-in effects of feed-forward network (FFN) neurons. This oversight can exacerbate neuron polysemanticity in multi-task settings, introducing noise into the identification and intervention of task-critical neurons. In this study, we propose HONES (Head-Oriented Neuron Explanation & Steering), a gradient-free framework for task-aware neuron attribution and steering in multi-task VLMs. HONES ranks FFN neurons by their causal write-in contributions conditioned on task-relevant attention heads, and further modulates salient neurons via lightweight scaling. Experiments on four diverse multimodal tasks and two popular VLMs show that HONES outperforms existing methods in identifying task-critical neurons and improves model performance after steering. Our source code is released at: https://github.com/petergit1/HONES.

</details>


### [386] [AI Approach for MRI-only Full-Spine Vertebral Segmentation and 3D Reconstruction in Paediatric Scoliosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17846)
*Nathasha Naranpanawa,Maree T. Izatt,Robert D. Labrom,Geoffrey N. Askin,J. Paige Little*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于AI的全自动MRI脊柱三维重建框架，通过GAN合成MRI样CT数据并结合真实MRI数据训练U-Net模型，在无辐射前提下实现T1-L5高精度分割与3D重建，显著提升效率（从1小时缩短至1分钟）和精度（Dice达88%），保留AIS畸形特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MRI虽避免电离辐射、适合儿童脊柱成像，但缺乏自动、高分辨率骨性三维重建能力，临床仍依赖CT；现有MRI重建受限于手动流程和全脊柱标注数据稀缺。

Method: 利用GAN将历史低剂量CT图像转换为MRI样图像，并与已有的标注胸椎MRI数据融合，训练U-Net模型，实现胸腰段（T1-L5）MRI全自动分割与三维重建。

Result: 算法实现了连续胸腰段3D重建，分割Dice分数达88%，处理时间由约1小时缩短至1分钟以内，并准确保留青少年特发性脊柱侧弯（AIS）的畸形特征。

Conclusion: 该AI框架首次支持仅用MRI完成辐射-free的高精度三维脊柱畸形评估，有望推动儿科脊柱临床评估、手术规划与导航的变革。

Abstract: MRI is preferred over CT in paediatric imaging because it avoids ionising radiation, but its use in spine deformity assessment is largely limited by the lack of automated, high-resolution 3D bony reconstruction, which continues to rely on CT. MRI-based 3D reconstruction remains impractical due to manual workflows and the scarcity of labelled full-spine datasets. This study introduces an AI framework that enables fully automated thoracolumbar spine (T1-L5) segmentation and 3D reconstruction from MRI alone. Historical low-dose CT scans from adolescent idiopathic scoliosis (AIS) patients were converted into MRI-like images using a GAN and combined with existing labelled thoracic MRI data to train a U-Net-based model. The resulting algorithm accurately generated continuous thoracolumbar 3D reconstructions, improved segmentation accuracy (88% Dice score), and reduced processing time from approximately 1 hour to under one minute, while preserving AIS-specific deformity features. This approach enables radiation-free 3D deformity assessment from MRI, supporting clinical evaluation, surgical planning, and navigation in paediatric spine care.

</details>


### [387] [Mitigating Multimodal Hallucination via Phase-wise Self-reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17982)
*Yu Zhang,Chuyang Sun,Kehai Chen,Xuefeng Bai,Yang Xiang,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PSRD框架，通过相位感知的自奖励解码机制，在推理阶段动态抑制大视觉语言模型（LVLMs）的视觉幻觉，无需外部监督或大规模微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖大量标注数据微调（计算开销大）或静态后处理策略（无法适应幻觉的动态演化），难以有效应对LVLMs中随生成过程动态变化的视觉幻觉问题。

Method: 基于发现视觉幻觉在语义相位起始处呈阶段性峰值的规律，提出相位感知的自奖励解码（PSRD）框架：1）构建轻量级蒸馏奖励模型，替代LVLM自身重复自评估；2）在解码过程中依据相位划分，利用该奖励模型提供实时、靶向干预信号以抑制幻觉。

Result: PSRD将LLaVA-1.5-7B的幻觉率降低50.0%，在五个幻觉评测基准、四个LVLM上均显著优于现有后处理方法；同时有效阻断幻觉传播，并在性能与推理效率间实现可控平衡。

Conclusion: PSRD是一种高效、通用且无需外部监督的在线幻觉校正方法，其相位动态建模与轻量奖励蒸馏设计为LVLMs可信推理提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) still struggle with vision hallucination, where generated responses are inconsistent with the visual input. Existing methods either rely on large-scale annotated data for fine-tuning, which incurs massive computational overhead, or employ static post-hoc strategies that overlook the dynamic nature of hallucination emergence. To address these, we introduce a new self-rewarding framework, enabling dynamic hallucination mitigation at inference time without external supervision. On the empirical side, we reveal that visual hallucination exhibits phase-wise dynamic patterns, peaking at the onset of each semantic phase. Drawing on these insights, we propose \textbf{PSRD} (\textbf{Phase-wise \textbf{S}elf-\textbf{R}eward \textbf{D}ecoding) for online hallucination correction guided by phase-wise self-reward signals. To reduce the cost of repeated self-evaluation during decoding, we distill the hallucination guidance signal from LVLMs into a lightweight reward model. The reward model subsequently provides on-the-fly guidance for targeted intervention during the decoding process, enabling precise hallucination suppression. The proposed PSRD significantly reduces the hallucination rate of LLaVA-1.5-7B by 50.0% and consistently outperforms existing post-hoc methods across five hallucination evaluation benchmarks for four LVLMs. Further analysis confirms that PSRD effectively mitigates hallucination propagation and achieves a highly controllable trade-off between strong performance and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [388] [UniCSG: Unified High-Fidelity Content-Constrained Style-Driven Generation via Staged Semantic and Frequency Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17850)
*Jingwei Yang,Ruoxi Wu,Wei Shen,Meng Li,Yulong Liu,Huimin She,Lunxi Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniCSG is a unified framework for style transfer that addresses content-style entanglement in DiT-based diffusion models through staged latent-space disentanglement and frequency-aware reconstruction, enhanced by pixel-space reward learning.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT-based diffusion models suffer from content-style entanglement, causing reference-content leakage and unstable generation in style transfer.

Method: UniCSG uses staged training: (i) latent-space semantic disentanglement via low-frequency preprocessing and conditioning corruption; (ii) frequency-aware detail reconstruction with multi-scale frequency supervision; plus pixel-space reward learning for perceptual alignment.

Result: Improved content faithfulness, style alignment, and robustness in both text-guided and reference-guided style transfer settings.

Conclusion: UniCSG effectively decouples content and style in diffusion-based style transfer, enabling more reliable and high-fidelity generation.

Abstract: Style transfer must match a target style while preserving content semantics. DiT-based diffusion models often suffer from content-style entanglement, leading to reference-content leakage and unstable generation. We present UniCSG, a unified framework for content-constrained, style-driven generation in both text-guided and reference-guided settings. UniCSG employs staged training: (i) a latent-space semantic disentanglement stage that combines low-frequency preprocessing with conditioning corruption to encourage content-style separation, and (ii) a latent-space frequency-aware detail reconstruction stage that refines details via multi-scale frequency supervision. We further incorporate pixel-space reward learning to align latent objectives with perceptual quality after decoding. Experiments demonstrate improved content faithfulness, style alignment, and robustness in both settings.

</details>


### [389] [ESsEN: Training Compact Discriminative Vision-Language Transformers in a Low-Resource Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18452)
*Clayton Fields,Casey Kennington*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级双塔式视觉-语言模型ESsEN，通过结合传统卷积网络和灵活的跨模态融合模块，在低资源场景下实现了参数高效且性能优异的端到端训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型参数量大，难以部署于边缘设备或独立机器人平台；同时缺乏针对小数据集和轻量化模型的研究。受儿童语言学习过程及数据稀疏性的启发，本文系统性地探索轻量、低资源适配的视觉-语言建模方法。

Method: 采用双塔编码器结构（优于单塔），在视觉分支中引入传统卷积网络以提升参数效率；设计可变形状与尺寸的跨模态融合模块；构建紧凑模型ESsEN并支持端到端训练。

Result: 双塔模型在低资源判别式英文任务上表现更优；卷积+双塔结构显著提升参数效率；跨模态融合模块具有高度灵活性且不影响性能；ESsEN以极小参数量在多个任务上媲美大模型。

Conclusion: 双塔架构、卷积增强与灵活融合模块是构建高效轻量视觉-语言模型的关键；ESsEN为资源受限场景提供了实用可行的新范式，并推动该领域向更广泛研究者开放。

Abstract: Vision-language modeling is rapidly increasing in popularity with an ever expanding list of available models. In most cases, these vision-language models have parameters in the tens of billions, which is necessary for some needs, but in many cases smaller models are necessary (e.g., on edge devices or independent robotic platforms). Unfortunately, there is little research in producing light-weight models or in training them with small datasets. Inspired by the language learning progression and data sparsity in child development, in this paper, we address both of these goals in a systematic fashion. We show that two-tower encoder models are superior to one-tower encoders in low-resource settings for discriminative English tasks. We show also that incorporating traditional convolutional networks into the two-tower transformer architecture can help produce parameter efficient vision-language models. Finally, we show that the cross-modal fusion module of two-tower encoders can vary significantly in shape and size while producing the same results. In addition, we present ESsEN, a compact vision-language model that can be trained end-to-end with relatively few resources that performs as well on several tasks with only a fraction of the parameters compared to other models. The experimental results and the tools we present here make vision-language modeling more accessible to a wider variety of researchers.

</details>


### [390] [PlankFormer: Robust Plankton Instance Segmentation via MAE-Pretrained Vision Transformers and Pseudo Community Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17856)
*Masaharu Miyazaki,Yurie Otake,Koichi Ito,Wataru Makino,Jotaro Urabe,Takafumi Aoki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PlankFormer框架，通过合成伪社区图像（PCI）解决标注数据稀缺问题，并采用基于MAE自监督预训练的Vision Transformer与Mask2Former解码器实现高精度浮游生物实例分割，显著优于Mask R-CNN等传统方法，尤其在高杂质密度场景下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浮游生物监测对水生生态系统评估至关重要，但人工显微分析费时费力；自动化分割面临像素级标注数据稀缺和CNN难以区分浮游生物、杂质及重叠个体两大挑战。

Method: 提出PlankFormer：1）合成伪社区图像（PCI），将单个浮游生物图像合成到多样化背景（含生成模型生成背景）以扩充标注数据；2）构建ViT主干+Mask2Former解码器的分割模型，并利用MAE对无标签单体图像进行自监督预训练，增强对遮挡与杂质干扰下的全局结构建模能力。

Result: 在真实数据集上实验表明，该方法显著优于Mask R-CNN等传统方法，尤其在高杂质密度环境下表现更优；合成训练策略与MAE架构可在减少人工标注需求的同时实现高精度分割。

Conclusion: PlankFormer有效缓解了浮游生物实例分割中标注数据稀缺与复杂背景干扰问题，验证了合成数据与自监督ViT架构在生态图像分析中的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Plankton monitoring is essential for assessing aquatic ecosystems but is limited by the labor-intensive nature of manual microscopic analysis. Automating the segmentation of plankton from crowded images is crucial, however, it faces two major challenges: (i) the scarcity of pixel-level annotated datasets and (ii) the difficulty of distinguishing plankton from debris and overlapping individuals using conventional CNN-based methods. To address these issues, we propose PlankFormer, a novel framework for plankton instance segmentation. First, to overcome the data shortage, we introduce a method to generate labeled Pseudo Community Images (PCI) by synthesizing individual plankton images onto diverse backgrounds, including those created by generative models. Second, we propose a segmentation model utilizing a Vision Transformer (ViT) backbone with a Mask2Former decoder. To robustly capture the global structural features of plankton against occlusion and debris, we employ a Masked Autoencoder (MAE) for self-supervised pre-training on unlabeled individual images. Experimental results on real-world datasets demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms conventional methods, such as Mask R-CNN, particularly in challenging environments with high debris density. We demonstrate that our synthetic training strategy and MAE-based architecture enable high-precision segmentation with requiring less manual annotations for individual plankton images.

</details>


### [391] [OneVL: One-Step Latent Reasoning and Planning with Vision-Language Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18486)
*Jinghui Lu,Jiayi Guan,Zhijian Huang,Jinlong Li,Guang Li,Lingdong Kong,Yingyan Li,Han Wang,Shaoqing Xu,Yuechen Luo,Fang Li,Chenxu Dang,Junli Wang,Tao Xu,Jing Wu,Jianhua Wu,Xiaoshuai Hao,Wen Zhang,Tianyi Jiang,Lingfeng Zhang,Lei Zhou,Yingbo Tang,Jie Wang,Yinfeng Gao,Xizhou Bu,Haochen Tian,Yihang Qiu,Feiyang Jia,Lin Liu,Yigu Ge,Hanbing Li,Yuannan Shen,Jianwei Cui,Hongwei Xie,Bing Wang,Haiyang Sun,Jingwei Zhao,Jiahui Huang,Pei Liu,Zeyu Zhu,Yuncheng Jiang,Zibin Guo,Chuhong Gong,Hanchao Leng,Kun Ma,Naiyang Wang,Guang Chen,Kuiyuan Yang,Hangjun Ye,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OneVL提出一种单步潜在推理与规划框架，通过语言解码器和视觉世界模型解码器联合监督潜在表征，首次使潜在链式思维方法在准确率上超越显式链式思维，同时保持回答级低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在链式思维方法因仅用语言监督压缩符号抽象，忽略驾驶中的因果动力学（如道路几何、智能体运动），导致性能不及显式链式思维。

Method: OneVL构建统一的VLA与世界模型框架，使用双辅助解码器（语言解码器重建文本CoT、视觉世界模型解码器预测未来帧token）监督紧凑潜在token；采用三阶段训练对齐轨迹、语言与视觉目标；推理时丢弃辅助解码器，所有潜在token并行预填充。

Result: 在四个基准上，OneVL成为首个超越显式CoT的潜在CoT方法，在保持回答级延迟的同时达到SOTA精度，并证明语言+世界模型联合监督能产生比逐token显式推理更泛化的表征。

Conclusion: 潜在推理性能瓶颈不在于压缩程度，而在于监督信号是否涵盖因果动力学；引入视觉世界模型解码器可使潜在空间内化真实驾驶动态，从而实现高精度与低延迟的统一。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has become a powerful driver of trajectory prediction in VLA-based autonomous driving, yet its autoregressive nature imposes a latency cost that is prohibitive for real-time deployment. Latent CoT methods attempt to close this gap by compressing reasoning into continuous hidden states, but consistently fall short of their explicit counterparts. We suggest that this is due to purely linguistic latent representations compressing a symbolic abstraction of the world, rather than the causal dynamics that actually govern driving. Thus, we present OneVL (One-step latent reasoning and planning with Vision-Language explanations), a unified VLA and World Model framework that routes reasoning through compact latent tokens supervised by dual auxiliary decoders. Alongside a language decoder that reconstructs text CoT, we introduce a visual world model decoder that predicts future-frame tokens, forcing the latent space to internalize the causal dynamics of road geometry, agent motion, and environmental change. A three-stage training pipeline progressively aligns these latents with trajectory, language, and visual objectives, ensuring stable joint optimization. At inference, the auxiliary decoders are discarded and all latent tokens are prefilled in a single parallel pass, matching the speed of answer-only prediction. Across four benchmarks, OneVL becomes the first latent CoT method to surpass explicit CoT, delivering state-of-the-art accuracy at answer-only latency, and providing direct evidence that tighter compression, when guided in both language and world-model supervision, produces more generalizable representations than verbose token-by-token reasoning. Project Page: https://xiaomi-embodied-intelligence.github.io/OneVL

</details>


### [392] [Sharpening Lightweight Models for Generalized Polyp Segmentation: A Boundary Guided Distillation from Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17865)
*Shivanshu Agnihotri,Snehashis Majhi,Deepak Ranjan Nayak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LiteBounD框架，通过轻量级边界引导蒸馏，将大视觉基础模型（VFMs）的语义与结构先验知识迁移至轻量分割模型，在保持高效性的同时显著提升结肠息肉分割精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轻量分割模型难以捕捉复杂息肉区域所需的丰富语义与结构线索；而大视觉基础模型虽泛化性强，却因领域不匹配、边界敏感性不足和计算开销高，难以直接迁移到息肉分割任务。

Method: 提出LiteBounD：一种轻量级边界引导蒸馏框架，包含（i）解耦语义与边界感知表征的双路径蒸馏机制，（ii）分别监督低频全局语义与高频边界细节的频域感知对齐策略，（iii）融合多尺度编码器特征与蒸馏所得边界信息的边界感知解码器。

Result: 在多个已见（Kvasir-SEG、CVC-ClinicDB）和未见（ColonDB、CVC-300、ETIS）数据集上，LiteBounD显著超越轻量基线模型，性能媲美SOTA方法，同时满足临床实时部署需求。

Conclusion: LiteBounD成功弥合了轻量模型与大视觉基础模型在息肉分割任务中的性能与效率鸿沟，为临床实用的自动化结直肠癌早期筛查提供了新范式。

Abstract: Automated polyp segmentation is critical for early colorectal cancer detection and its prevention, yet remains challenging due to weak boundaries, large appearance variations, and limited annotated data. Lightweight segmentation models such as U-Net, U-Net++, and PraNet offer practical efficiency for clinical deployment but struggle to capture the rich semantic and structural cues required for accurate delineation of complex polyp regions. In contrast, large Vision Foundation Models (VFMs), including SAM, OneFormer, Mask2Former, and DINOv2, exhibit strong generalization but transfer poorly to polyp segmentation due to domain mismatch, insufficient boundary sensitivity, and high computational cost. To bridge this gap, we propose \textit{\textbf{LiteBounD}, a \underline{Li}gh\underline{t}w\underline{e}ight \underline{Boun}dary-guided \underline{D}istillation} framework that transfers complementary semantic and structural priors from multiple VFMs into compact segmentation backbones. LiteBounD introduces (i) a dual-path distillation mechanism that disentangles semantic and boundary-aware representations, (ii) a frequency-aware alignment strategy that supervises low-frequency global semantics and high-frequency boundary details separately, and (iii) a boundary-aware decoder that fuses multi-scale encoder features with distilled semantically rich boundary information for precise segmentation. Extensive experiments on both seen (Kvasir-SEG, CVC-ClinicDB) and unseen (ColonDB, CVC-300, ETIS) datasets demonstrate that LiteBounD consistently outperforms its lightweight baselines by a significant margin and achieves performance competitive with state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining the efficiency required for real-time clinical use. Our code is available at https://github.com/lostinrepo/LiteBounD.

</details>


### [393] [Spatiotemporal Sycophancy: Negation-Based Gaslighting in Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17873)
*Ziyao Tang,Pengkun Jiao,Bin Zhu,Huiyan Qi,Jingjing Chen,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了视频大语言模型（Vid-LLMs）在否定式煤气灯效应下的‘时空谄媚’现象——即模型放弃正确视觉判断、编造虚假时空解释以迎合误导性用户反馈；提出GasVideo-1000基准与评估框架，实证表明该脆弱性普遍存在且难以通过提示工程彻底缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vid-LLMs在视频理解任务中表现优异，但其在对话交互中的鲁棒性尚不明确，尤其缺乏对 adversarial conversational feedback（如煤气灯效应）下行为的研究。

Method: 提出否定式煤气灯效应评估框架，并构建GasVideo-1000基准，系统评测多类开源与闭源Vid-LLMs在时空推理与视觉接地任务中的响应行为；同时尝试prompt-level grounding约束作为缓解手段。

Result: 实验证明，几乎所有被测Vid-LLMs均易受否定式煤气灯效应影响，出现信念反转与幻觉式解释；提示级接地约束仅能部分缓解，无法根治。

Conclusion: 当前Vid-LLMs缺乏在对抗性对话反馈下维持稳健时空信念的能力，亟需新的建模机制与训练范式提升其可信性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Video Large Language Models (Vid-LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance in video understanding tasks, yet their robustness under conversational interaction remains largely underexplored. In this paper, we identify spatiotemporal sycophancy, a failure mode in which Vid-LLMs retract initially correct, visually grounded judgments and conform to misleading user feedback under negation-based gaslighting. Rather than merely changing their answers, the models often fabricate unsupported temporal or spatial explanations to justify incorrect revisions. To systematically investigate this phenomenon, we propose a negation-based gaslighting evaluation framework and introduce GasVideo-1000, a curated benchmark designed to probe spatiotemporal sycophancy with clear visual grounding and temporal reasoning requirements. We evaluate a broad range of state-of-the-art open-source and proprietary Vid-LLMs across diverse video understanding tasks. Extensive experiments reveal that vulnerability to negation-based gaslighting is pervasive and severe, even among models with strong baseline performance. While prompt-level grounding constraints can partially mitigate this behavior, they do not reliably prevent hallucinated justifications or belief reversal. Our results indicate that current Vid-LLMs lack robust mechanisms for maintaining grounded spatiotemporal beliefs under adversarial conversational feedback.

</details>


### [394] [Exploring Boundary-Aware Spatial-Frequency Fusion for Camouflaged Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17879)
*Song Yu,Yang Hu,Haokang Ding,Zhifang Liao,Yucheng Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出BASFNet，一种基于边界感知的频域与空域融合的伪装目标检测框架，通过频域相位谱增强边缘探索和空域核心分割模块联合捕捉伪装目标的边界和对象特征，并利用空间-频率融合交互模块有效整合这些特征，同时采用边界感知训练策略进一步优化边界检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伪装目标检测方法主要依赖空间域边缘提取和局部像素级信息，忽略了全局结构特征的重要性，且未能有效利用频域特征中的相位谱信息。

Method: 提出BASFNet框架，包含相位谱驱动的频域增强边缘探索模块（FEEM）、空间核心分割模块（SCSM）以及空间-频率融合交互模块（SFFIM），并采用边界感知训练策略。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上超越了当前最先进的方法，验证了频域与空域信息融合在伪装目标检测任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 融合频域（尤其是相位谱）与空域特征，并结合边界感知机制，能显著提升伪装目标检测性能。

Abstract: Camouflaged Object Detection is challenging due to the high degree of similarity between camouflaged objects and their surrounding backgrounds. Current COD methods mainly rely on edge extraction in the spatial domain and local pixel-level information, neglecting the importance of global structural features. Additionally, they fail to effectively leverage the importance of phase spectrum information within frequency domain features. To this end, we propose a COD framework BASFNet based on boundary-aware frequency domain and spatial domain fusion.This method uses dual guided integration of frequency domain and spatial domain features. A phase-spectrum-based frequency-enhanced edge exploration module (FEEM) and a spatial core segmentation module (SCSM) are introduced to jointly capture the boundary and object features of camouflaged objects. These features are then effectively integrated through a spatial-frequency fusion interaction module (SFFIM). Furthermore, the boundary detection is further optimized through an boundary-aware training strategy. BASFNet outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods on three benchmark datasets, validating the effectiveness of the fusion of frequency and spatial domain information in COD tasks.

</details>


### [395] [AeroRAG: Structured Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented LLM for Fine-Grained Aerial Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17889)
*Junxiao Xue,Quan Deng,Tingqi Hu,Meicong Si,Xinyi Yin,Yunyun Shi,Xuecheng Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AeroRAG框架，通过将航拍图像转化为结构化场景图（含物体类别、数量、位置与关系），再进行语义检索以构建紧凑提示，提升视觉问答性能，尤其在密集航拍和关系推理任务中效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航拍图像中关键信息常由小物体、显式数量、粗略位置及物体间关系承载，而传统密集视觉token表示难以对齐此类结构化语义，导致视觉问答可靠性不足。

Method: 提出场景图引导的多模态检索增强生成框架AeroRAG：先将输入图像解析为结构化视觉知识（对象类别、数量、空间位置、语义关系），再检索与问题相关的语义块，构造精简提示供纯文本大语言模型使用。

Result: 在AUG航拍数据集和VG-150通用基准上显著优于六个强MLLM基线，尤其在密集航拍场景和关系敏感推理任务中增益最大；在VQAv2上验证了该接口对标准视觉推理任务的兼容性。

Conclusion: 结构化检索是一种面向部署与具身视觉推理系统的实用设计方向。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in multimodal large language models (MLLMs), reliable visual question answering in aerial scenes remains challenging. In such scenes, task-critical evidence is often carried by small objects, explicit quantities, coarse locations, and inter-object relations, whereas conventional dense visual-token representations are not well aligned with these structured semantics. To address this interface mismatch, we propose AeroRAG, a scene-graph-guided multimodal retrieval-augmented generation framework for visual question answering. The framework first converts an input image into structured visual knowledge, including object categories, quantities, spatial locations, and semantic relations, and then retrieves query-relevant semantic chunks to construct compact prompts for a text-based large language model. Rather than relying on direct reasoning over dense visual tokens, our method introduces a more explicit intermediate interface between perception and language reasoning. Experiments on the AUG aerial dataset and the general-domain VG-150 benchmark show consistent improvements over six strong MLLM baselines, with the largest gains observed in dense aerial scenes and relation-sensitive reasoning. We further evaluate the framework on VQAv2 to verify that the proposed interface remains compatible with standard visual reasoning settings. These results suggest that structured retrieval is a practical design direction for deployment-oriented and grounded visual reasoning systems.

</details>


### [396] [ReTrack: Evidence-Driven Dual-Stream Directional Anchor Calibration Network for Composed Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17898)
*Zixu Li,Yupeng Hu,Zhiwei Chen,Qinlei Huang,Guozhi Qiu,Zhiheng Fu,Meng Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReTrack框架，通过校准多模态组合特征的方向偏差，提升视频检索中参考视频与修改文本的联合理解能力，解决模态贡献纠缠、组合特征优化和检索不确定性三大挑战，并在CVR与CIR任务上均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统组合方法偏向参考视频，导致组合特征信息密度失衡，难以准确响应文本修改意图，且面临模态贡献纠缠、组合特征缺乏显式优化、检索结果不确定三大挑战。

Method: 提出证据驱动的双流定向锚点校准网络ReTrack，包含语义贡献解耦、组合几何校准和可靠证据驱动对齐三个模块，通过估计各模态语义贡献来校准组合特征方向偏差，并利用校准后的定向锚点计算双向证据以提升相似度估计可靠性。

Result: 在CVR和CIR任务上均取得SOTA性能，尤其在三个标准数据集上验证了其泛化能力和有效性。

Conclusion: ReTrack是首个针对CVR任务中组合特征方向偏差进行校准的框架，显著提升了多模态查询理解能力，并展现出跨任务（CVR→CIR）的良好泛化性。

Abstract: With the rapid growth of video data, Composed Video Retrieval (CVR) has emerged as a novel paradigm in video retrieval and is receiving increasing attention from researchers. Unlike unimodal video retrieval methods, the CVR task takes a multi-modal query consisting of a reference video and a piece of modification text as input. The modification text conveys the user's intended alterations to the reference video. Based on this input, the model aims to retrieve the most relevant target video. In the CVR task, there exists a substantial discrepancy in information density between video and text modalities. Traditional composition methods tend to bias the composed feature toward the reference video, which leads to suboptimal retrieval performance. This limitation is significant due to the presence of three core challenges: (1) modal contribution entanglement, (2) explicit optimization of composed features, and (3) retrieval uncertainty. To address these challenges, we propose the evidence-dRivRn dual-sTream diRectionAl anChor calibration networK (ReTrack). ReTrack is the first CVR framework that improves multi-modal query understanding by calibrating directional bias in composed features. It consists of three key modules: Semantic Contribution Disentanglement, Composition Geometry Calibration, and Reliable Evidence-driven Alignment. Specifically, ReTrack estimates the semantic contribution of each modality to calibrate the directional bias of the composed feature. It then uses the calibrated directional anchors to compute bidirectional evidence that drives reliable composed-to-target similarity estimation. Moreover, ReTrack exhibits strong generalization to the Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) task, achieving SOTA performance across three benchmark datasets in both CVR and CIR scenarios. Codes are available at https://github.com/Lee-zixu/ReTrack

</details>


### [397] [MEDN: Motion-Emotion Feature Decoupling Network for Micro-Expression Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17899)
*Chenxing Hu,Kun Xie,Qiguang Miao,Ruyi Liu,Quan Wang,Zongkai Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Motion Emotion Feature Decoupling Network (MEDN)，通过双分支结构分别提取显式运动特征和隐式情绪特征，利用AU检测、正交损失、稀疏情绪视觉Transformer（SEVit）和协同融合模块（CoFM）实现运动与情绪特征解耦，显著提升了微表情识别（MER）的准确率和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微表情缺乏情绪与动作单元（AUs）之间严格一致的映射关系，导致相同AUs可能对应相反情绪、视觉高度相似；现有方法多依赖显式运动线索，忽略隐式情绪信息。

Method: 提出MEDN模型：1）双分支框架——运动分支采用AU检测任务加正交损失以约束并解耦运动-情绪特征；2）情绪分支采用稀疏情绪视觉Transformer（SEVit），通过多尺度空间token稀疏化突出局部时序变化；3）协同融合模块（CoFM）自适应融合解耦后的两类特征。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上实验表明，MEDN能有效解耦运动与情绪特征，在微表情识别任务中取得更优性能。

Conclusion: 解耦显式运动与隐式情绪特征是提升微表情识别精度与泛化性的有效新思路，MEDN为此提供了可行且高效的实现框架。

Abstract: Unlike macro-expression, micro-expression does not follow a strictly consistent mapping rule between emotions and Action Units (AUs). As a result, some micro-expressions share identical AUs yet represent completely opposite emotional categories, making them highly visually similar. Existing microexpression recognition (MER) methods mostly rely on explicit facial motion cues (e.g., optical flow, frame differences, AU features) while ignoring implicit emotion information. To tackle this issue, this paper presents a Motion Emotion Feature Decoupling Network (MEDN) for MER. We design a dual-branch framework to separately extract motion and emotion features. In the motion branch, an AU-detection task restricts features to the explicit motion domain, and orthogonal loss is adopted to reduce motion emotion feature coupling. For implicit emotion modeling, we propose a Sparse Emotion Vision Transformer (SEVit) that sparsifies spatial tokens to highlight local temporal variations with multi-scale sparsity rates. A Collaborative Fusion Module (CoFM) is further developed to fuse disentangled motion and emotion features adaptively. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets validate that MEDN effectively decouples motion and emotion features and achieves superior recognition performance, offering a new perspective for enhancing recognition accuracy and generalization.

</details>


### [398] [Beyond Binary Contrast: Modeling Continuous Skeleton Action Spaces with Transitional Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17914)
*Yingjie Feng,Yi Wang,Jiaze Wang,Anfeng Liu,Zhuotao Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TranCLR框架，通过引入动作过渡锚点（ATAC）和多级几何流形校准（MGMC）机制，在自监督对比学习中建模人体动作的连续性，提升骨架动作识别的表征平滑性与判别性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督对比学习方法采用二元对比目标，忽略了人体运动固有的连续性，导致特征聚类碎片化、类别边界僵硬。

Method: 提出基于过渡锚点的对比学习框架TranCLR，包括动作过渡锚点构建（ATAC）以建模过渡状态几何结构，以及多级几何流形校准（MGMC）机制来自适应校准不同连续性层级的动作流形。

Result: 在NTU RGB+D、NTU RGB+D 120和PKU-MMD数据集上取得更高准确率与校准性能，学习到更连续、不确定性感知的骨架表征。

Conclusion: TranCLR有效建模动作空间的连续几何结构，显著提升了骨架动作识别中自监督对比学习的表征质量与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Self-supervised contrastive learning has emerged as a powerful paradigm for skeleton-based action recognition by enforcing consistency in the embedding space. However, existing methods rely on binary contrastive objectives that overlook the intrinsic continuity of human motion, resulting in fragmented feature clusters and rigid class boundaries. To address these limitations, we propose TranCLR, a Transitional anchor-based Contrastive Learning framework that captures the continuous geometry of the action space. Specifically, the proposed Action Transitional Anchor Construction (ATAC) explicitly models the geometric structure of transitional states to enhance the model's perception of motion continuity. Building upon these anchors, a Multi-Level Geometric Manifold Calibration (MGMC) mechanism is introduced to adaptively calibrate the action manifold across multiple levels of continuity, yielding a smoother and more discriminative representation space. Extensive experiments on the NTU RGB+D, NTU RGB+D 120 and PKU-MMD datasets demonstrate that TranCLR achieves superior accuracy and calibration performance, effectively learning continuous and uncertainty-aware skeleton representations. The code is available at https://github.com/Philchieh/TranCLR.

</details>


### [399] [OneDrive: Unified Multi-Paradigm Driving with Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17915)
*Yiwei Zhang,Xuesong Chen,Jin Gao,Hanshi Wang,Fudong Ge,Weiming Hu,Shaoshuai Shi,Zhipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预训练视觉-语言模型（VLM）的统一自动驾驶框架，通过在单个因果Transformer解码器中整合文本生成、目标检测与轨迹规划等异构任务，实现共享注意力主干的联合优化，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶系统因需处理多任务（如自回归文本生成、并行目标检测、轨迹回归）而引入分离或级联解码器，导致架构碎片化和主干复用受限。

Method: 将视觉特征与结构化查询（如检测框、轨迹点）统一组织于单个因果Transformer解码器中，利用预训练VLM的注意力机制自然建模视觉-结构化条件关系，使文本与结构化输出共享同一注意力主干，并在相同解码器内实现轨迹规划。

Result: 在nuScenes开环评估中达到0.28 L2误差和0.18碰撞率，在NAVSIM闭环评估中获得86.8 PDMS；全模型保留多模态生成能力，高效推理模式降低约40%延迟。

Conclusion: 预训练VLM的注意力机制具备强跨任务迁移能力，单一因果解码器可有效统一异构自动驾驶任务，提升性能与效率并兼顾架构简洁性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models(VLMs) excel at autoregressive text generation, yet end-to-end autonomous driving requires multi-task learning with structured outputs and heterogeneous decoding behaviors, such as autoregressive language generation, parallel object detection and trajectory regression. To accommodate these differences, existing systems typically introduce separate or cascaded decoders, resulting in architectural fragmentation and limited backbone reuse. In this work, we present a unified autonomous driving framework built upon a pretrained VLM, where heterogeneous decoding behaviors are reconciled within a single transformer decoder. We demonstrate that pretrained VLM attention exhibits strong transferability beyond pure language modeling. By organizing visual and structured query tokens within a single causal decoder, structured queries can naturally condition on visual context through the original attention mechanism. Textual and structured outputs share a common attention backbone, enabling stable joint optimization across heterogeneous tasks. Trajectory planning is realized within the same causal LLM decoder by introducing structured trajectory queries. This unified formulation enables planning to share the pretrained attention backbone with images and perception tokens. Extensive experiments on end-to-end autonomous driving benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance, including 0.28 L2 and 0.18 collision rate on nuScenes open-loop evaluation and competitive results (86.8 PDMS) on NAVSIM closed-loop evaluation. The full model preserves multi-modal generation capability, while an efficient inference mode achieves approximately 40% lower latency. Code and models are available at https://github.com/Z1zyw/OneDrive

</details>


### [400] [Prompting Foundation Models for Zero-Shot Ship Instance Segmentation in SAR Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17920)
*Islam Mansour,Francescopaolo Sica,Michael Schmitt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需像素级标注的零样本SAR图像船舶实例分割方法，利用YOLOv11检测器提供空间约束，引导SAM2生成精确掩码，显著缩小光学与SAR域间差距，并在SSDD数据集上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR图像分析缺乏像素级标注，限制了深度学习的应用；现有SAM-based方法需微调或适配器，难以直接迁移。

Method: 采用YOLOv11在开源SAR数据集上训练检测器获取船舶边界框，再以这些框作为提示输入SAM2生成实例分割掩码，全程无需掩码监督。

Result: 在SSDD基准上达到平均IoU 0.637（为全监督基线的89%）和89.2%整体检测率，并支持船舶分类、尺寸估计与尾迹分析等下游任务。

Conclusion: 仅靠SAR训练的检测器提供的空间约束即可有效引导SAM2，实现高效、可扩展、免像素标注的SAR图像理解新范式。

Abstract: Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) plays a critical role in maritime surveillance, yet deep learning for SAR analysis is limited by the lack of pixel-level annotations. This paper explores how general-purpose vision foundation models can enable zero-shot ship instance segmentation in SAR imagery, eliminating the need for pixel-level supervision. A YOLOv11-based detector trained on open SAR datasets localizes ships via bounding boxes, which then prompt the Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2) to produce instance masks without any mask annotations. Unlike prior SAM-based SAR approaches that rely on fine tuning or adapters, our method demonstrates that spatial constraints from a SAR-trained detector alone can effectively regularize foundation model predictions. This design partially mitigates the optical-SAR domain gap and enables downstream applications such as vessel classification, size estimation, and wake analysis. Experiments on the SSDD benchmark achieve a mean IoU of 0.637 (89% of a fully supervised baseline) with an overall ship detection rate of 89.2%, confirming a scalable, annotation-efficient pathway toward foundation-model-driven SAR image understanding.

</details>


### [401] [Brain-Inspired Capture: Evidence-Driven Neuromimetic Perceptual Simulation for Visual Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17927)
*Feixue Shao,Guangze Shi,Xueyu Liu,Yongfei Wu,Mingqiang Wei,Jianan Zhang,Jianbo Lu,Guiying Yan,Weihua Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受人类视觉系统启发的神经解码方法BI-Cap，通过模拟HVS的动态与静态处理机制，并结合互信息引导的模糊调控和证据驱动的隐空间表示，显著提升了零样本脑到图像检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视觉解码方法受限于神经信号与视觉模态之间的系统性和随机性差异，且忽视了人类视觉系统（HVS）的内在计算机制。

Method: 提出Brain-Inspired Capture（BI-Cap），构建包含四个生物合理动态/静态变换的神经模拟流水线，并引入互信息（MI）引导的动态模糊调控和证据驱动的隐空间表示以建模神经活动的不确定性。

Result: 在两个公开基准上的零样本脑到图像检索任务中，BI-Cap显著超越现有最优方法，相对提升分别为9.2%和8.0%。

Conclusion: BI-Cap通过神经模拟与不确定性建模，有效弥合神经与视觉模态鸿沟，为BCI与计算神经科学提供了更鲁棒、更具生物合理性的解码范式。

Abstract: Visual decoding of neurophysiological signals is a critical challenge for brain-computer interfaces (BCIs) and computational neuroscience. However, current approaches are often constrained by the systematic and stochastic gaps between neural and visual modalities, largely neglecting the intrinsic computational mechanisms of the Human Visual System (HVS). To address this, we propose Brain-Inspired Capture (BI-Cap), a neuromimetic perceptual simulation paradigm that aligns these modalities by emulating HVS processing. Specifically, we construct a neuromimetic pipeline comprising four biologically plausible dynamic and static transformations, coupled with Mutual Information (MI)-guided dynamic blur regulation to simulate adaptive visual processing. Furthermore, to mitigate the inherent non-stationarity of neural activity, we introduce an evidence-driven latent space representation. This formulation explicitly models uncertainty, thereby ensuring robust neural embeddings. Extensive evaluations on zero-shot brain-to-image retrieval across two public benchmarks demonstrate that BI-Cap substantially outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving relative gains of 9.2\% and 8.0\%, respectively. We have released the source code on GitHub through the link https://github.com/flysnow1024/BI-Cap.

</details>


### [402] [ZSG-IAD: A Multimodal Framework for Zero-Shot Grounded Industrial Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17949)
*Qiuhui Chen,Jiaxiang Song,Shuai Tan,Weimin Zhong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ZSG-IAD 是一种面向工业异常检测的零样本、多模态视觉-语言框架，能生成结构化异常报告和像素级异常掩码，通过语言引导的两跳定位模块和可执行规则强化学习提升解释性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的工业异常检测器多为黑箱模型，难以提供具有物理意义的缺陷证据来支撑决策。

Method: 提出 ZSG-IAD 框架：融合 RGB 图像、传感器图像和 3D 点云；设计语言引导的两跳定位模块（先选证据相关潜变量槽位获得粗略空间支持，再通过通道-空间门控与轻量解码器生成细粒度掩码）；引入可执行规则 GRPO 强化学习优化结构化输出、异常区域一致性与推理结论连贯性。

Result: 在多个工业异常检测基准上展现出优异的零样本性能，并提供比先前方法更透明、更具物理依据的解释。

Conclusion: ZSG-IAD 实现了零样本、多模态、可解释的工业异常检测，在可信 AI 系统构建中具有重要价值；代码与标注将开源以推动后续研究。

Abstract: Deep learning-based industrial anomaly detectors often behave as black boxes, making it hard to justify decisions with physically meaningful defect evidence. We propose ZSG-IAD, a multimodal vision-language framework for zero-shot grounded industrial anomaly detection. Given RGB images, sensor images, and 3D point clouds, ZSG-IAD generates structured anomaly reports and pixel-level anomaly masks. ZSG-IAD introduces a language-guided two-hop grounding module: (1) anomaly-related sentences select evidence-like latent slots distilled from multimodal features, yielding coarse spatial support; (2) selected slots modulate feature maps via channel-spatial gating and a lightweight decoder to produce fine-grained masks. To improve reliability, we further apply Executable-Rule GRPO with verifiable rewards to promote structured outputs, anomaly-region consistency, and reasoning-conclusion coherence. Experiments across multiple industrial anomaly benchmarks show strong zero-shot performance and more transparent, physically grounded explanations than prior methods. We will release code and annotations to support future research on trustworthy industrial anomaly detection systems.

</details>


### [403] [Chatting about Upper-Body Expressive Human Pose and Shape Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17959)
*Yuxiang Zhao,Wei Huang,Yujie Song,Liu Wang,Huan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoEvoer是一种新颖的一阶段协同交叉依赖Transformer框架，专门用于上半身表达式人体姿态和形状估计（EHPS），通过不同身体部位间的显式特征级交互实现相互增强，显著提升了面部、手部和躯干参数估计的准确性及对野外图像的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在面部和手部区域参数估计精度不足，且对野外图像泛化能力有限。

Method: 提出CoEvoer框架，采用一阶段协同交叉依赖Transformer结构，实现面部、手部与躯干之间的显式特征级交互与互惠增强。

Result: 在上半身EHPS基准测试中达到SOTA性能，并在未见过的野外图像上展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: CoEvoer是首个专为上半身EHPS设计的框架，能有效建模面部、手部与躯干间的强耦合与语义依赖关系，推动EHPS在AR/VR中的实用化。

Abstract: Expressive Human Pose and Shape Estimation (EHPS) plays a crucial role in various AR/VR applications and has witnessed significant progress in recent years. However, current state-of-the-art methods still struggle with accurate parameter estimation for facial and hand regions and exhibit limited generalization to wild images. To address these challenges, we present CoEvoer, a novel one-stage synergistic cross-dependency transformer framework tailored for upper-body EHPS. CoEvoer enables explicit feature-level interaction across different body parts, allowing for mutual enhancement through contextual information exchange. Specifically, larger and more easily estimated regions such as the torso provide global semantics and positional priors to guide the estimation of finer, more complex regions like the face and hands. Conversely, the localized details captured in facial and hand regions help refine and calibrate adjacent body parts. To the best of our knowledge, CoEvoer is the first framework designed specifically for upper-body EHPS, with the goal of capturing the strong coupling and semantic dependencies among the face, hands, and torso through joint parameter regression. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoEvoer achieves state-of-the-art performance on upper-body benchmarks and exhibits strong generalization capability even on unseen wild images.

</details>


### [404] [DifFoundMAD: Foundation Models meet Differential Morphing Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17961)
*Lazaro J. Gonzalez-Soler,André Dörsch,Christian Rathgeb,Christoph Busch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DifFoundMAD是一种参数高效的差分活体检测（D-MAD）框架，利用视觉基础模型（FM）的泛化能力来识别伪造图像与真实抓拍图像之间的差异，通过轻量微调和类别平衡优化，在保持FM丰富表征先验的同时仅更新少量参数，显著降低高安全级别下的错误率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统D-MAD系统依赖人脸识别嵌入或手工设计特征差异，泛化性与安全性不足，尤其难以满足边境控制等高安全场景需求。

Method: 提出DifFoundMAD框架，采用视觉基础模型提取特征，遵循标准差分范式；结合轻量微调（仅更新小部分参数）与类别平衡优化策略。

Result: 在多个标准D-MAD基准上跨数据库评测显示，DifFoundMAD在高安全级别下将当前最优错误率从6.16%显著降至2.17%。

Conclusion: 基于视觉基础模型的轻量差分方法可有效提升D-MAD系统的性能与部署实用性，尤其适用于高安全要求的实际场景。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce DifFoundMAD, a parameter-efficient D-MAD framework that exploits the generalisation capabilities of vision foundation models (FM) to capture discrepancies between suspected morphs and live capture images. In contrast to conventional D-MAD systems that rely on face recognition embeddings or handcrafted feature differences, DifFoundMAD follows the standard differential paradigm while replacing the underlying representation space with embeddings extracted from FMs. By combining lightweight finetuning with class-balanced optimisation, the proposed method updates only a small subset of parameters while preserving the rich representational priors of the underlying FMs. Extensive cross-database evaluations on standard D-MAD benchmarks demonstrate that DifFoundMAD achieves consistent improvements over state-of-the-art systems, particularly at the strict security levels required in operational deployments such as border control: The error rates reported in the current state-of-the-art were reduced from 6.16% to 2.17% for high-security levels using DifFoundMAD.

</details>


### [405] [MU-GeNeRF: Multi-view Uncertainty-guided Generalizable Neural Radiance Fields for Distractor-aware Scene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17965)
*Wenjie Mu,Zhan Li,Chuanzhou Su,Xuanyi Shen,Ziniu Liu,Fan Lu,Yujian Mo,Junqiao Zhao,Tiantian Feng,Chen Ye,Guang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MU-GeNeRF框架，通过引入源视图不确定性和目标视图不确定性，联合建模并抑制瞬态干扰物对通用神经辐射场（GeNeRF）重建质量的影响，显著提升其在真实复杂场景下的鲁棒性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实场景中瞬态干扰物破坏跨视角结构一致性，导致监督信号失真、重建质量下降；现有方法依赖单场景优化和不可靠的逐视图误差估计，难以适用于通用NeRF。

Method: 提出MU-GeNeRF：将干扰物感知分解为源视图不确定性（刻画多视角结构差异）和目标视图不确定性（检测目标图像中的瞬态异常），并通过异方差重建损失自适应调节监督强度。

Result: 在多个基准上超越现有通用NeRF方法，性能接近专用场景级无干扰NeRF方法。

Conclusion: 双不确定性建模与异方差损失有效缓解了瞬态干扰对GeNeRF泛化能力的负面影响，为鲁棒通用场景重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Generalizable Neural Radiance Fields (GeNeRFs) enable high-quality scene reconstruction from sparse views and can generalize to unseen scenes. However, in real-world settings, transient distractors break cross-view structural consistency, corrupting supervision and degrading reconstruction quality. Existing distractor-free NeRF methods rely on per-scene optimization and estimate uncertainty from per-view reconstruction errors, which are not reliable for GeNeRFs and often misjudge inconsistent static structures as distractors. To this end, we propose MU-GeNeRF, a Multi-view Uncertainty-guided distractor-aware GeNeRF framework designed to alleviate GeNeRF's robust modeling challenges in the presence of transient distractions. We decompose distractor awareness into two complementary uncertainty components: Source-view Uncertainty, which captures structural discrepancies across source views caused by viewpoint changes or dynamic factors; and Target-view Uncertainty, which detects observation anomalies in the target image induced by transient distractors.These two uncertainties address distinct error sources and are combined through a heteroscedastic reconstruction loss, which guides the model to adaptively modulate supervision, enabling more robust distractor suppression and geometric modeling.Extensive experiments show that our method not only surpasses existing GeNeRFs but also achieves performance comparable to scene-specific distractor-free NeRFs.

</details>


### [406] [E3VS-Bench: A Benchmark for Viewpoint-Dependent Active Perception in 3D Gaussian Splatting Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17969)
*Koya Sakamoto,Taiki Miyanishi,Daichi Azuma,Shuhei Kurita,Shu Morikuni,Naoya Chiba,Motoaki Kawanabe,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了E3VS-Bench，一个面向具身3D视觉搜索的新基准，强调在5自由度（5-DoF）视角控制下主动获取视角依赖性证据以回答问题；该基准基于高保真3D高斯溅射重建场景，揭示当前VLMs在主动感知与视角规划方面显著落后于人类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉搜索与具身AI基准（如EQA）多依赖静态观测或受限的前向运动，无法评估真实3D环境中由无约束5-DoF视角变化引发的细粒度视角依赖现象（如垂直视角导致的可见性变化、容器内内容显现、特定角度才能分辨的物体属性）。

Method: 构建E3VS-Bench：包含99个基于3D Gaussian Splatting重建的高保真3D场景和2014个问答驱动的任务序列；利用其光栅化保真度支持需跨多视角主动观察才能解答的问题设计；评估多种SOTA VLM，并与人类表现对比。

Result: 所有被测VLM在该基准上性能远低于人类，暴露出其在主动感知能力与面向完整5-DoF视角变化的连贯视角规划能力上的根本缺陷。

Conclusion: E3VS-Bench有效揭示了当前具身视觉语言模型在处理复杂、视角敏感的3D搜索任务时的关键短板，为推动具身智能中主动感知与空间推理研究提供了新标准与挑战。

Abstract: Visual search in 3D environments requires embodied agents to actively explore their surroundings and acquire task-relevant evidence. However, existing visual search and embodied AI benchmarks, including EQA, typically rely on static observations or constrained egocentric motion, and thus do not explicitly evaluate fine-grained viewpoint-dependent phenomena that arise under unrestricted 5-DoF viewpoint control in real-world 3D environments, such as visibility changes caused by vertical viewpoint shifts, revealing contents inside containers, and disambiguating object attributes that are only observable from specific angles. To address this limitation, we introduce {E3VS-Bench}, a benchmark for embodied 3D visual search where agents must control their viewpoints in 5-DoF to gather viewpoint-dependent evidence for question answering. E3VS-Bench consists of 99 high-fidelity 3D scenes reconstructed using 3D Gaussian Splatting and 2,014 question-driven episodes. 3D Gaussian Splatting enables photorealistic free-viewpoint rendering that preserves fine-grained visual details (e.g., small text and subtle attributes) often degraded in mesh-based simulators, thereby allowing the construction of questions that cannot be answered from a single view and instead require active inspection across viewpoints in 5-DoF. We evaluate multiple state-of-the-art VLMs and compare their performance with humans. Despite strong 2D reasoning ability, all models exhibit a substantial gap from humans, highlighting limitations in active perception and coherent viewpoint planning specifically under full 5-DoF viewpoint changes.

</details>


### [407] [Identifying Ethical Biases in Action Recognition Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17971)
*Ana Baltaretu,Pascal Benschop,Jan van Gemert*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于合成视频数据的审计框架，用于评估人体动作识别（HAR）模型在不同肤色人群上的公平性，发现部分主流模型存在统计显著的肤色偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HAR模型正被部署于高风险场景，但其在不同人类外观（如肤色）上的公平性尚未被系统评估。

Method: 利用BEDLAM仿真平台生成具有精确控制视觉身份属性（如肤色）的合成视频，保持时间一致性，通过单变量干预实验检测模型对肤色变化的响应。

Result: 多个主流HAR模型在动作完全相同、仅肤色变化时表现出统计显著的预测偏差，揭示了模型对视觉特征的不当关联和跨群体的系统性错误。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了首个面向HAR任务的公平性审计框架，推动构建更透明、可问责的动作识别系统，以应对日益严格的监管要求。

Abstract: Human Action Recognition (HAR) models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes environments, yet their fairness across different human appearances has not been analyzed. We introduce a framework for auditing bias in HAR models using synthetic video data, generated with full control over visual identity attributes such as skin color. Unlike prior work that focuses on static images or pose estimation, our approach preserves temporal consistency, allowing us to isolate and test how changes to a single attribute affect model predictions. Through controlled interventions using the BEDLAM simulation platform, we show whether some popular HAR models exhibit statistically significant biases on the skin color even when the motion remains identical. Our results highlight how models may encode unwanted visual associations, and we provide evidence of systematic errors across groups. This work contributes a framework for auditing HAR models and supports the development of more transparent, accountable systems in light of upcoming regulatory standards.

</details>


### [408] [Trustworthy Endoscopic Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18001)
*Julio Silva-Rodríguez,Ender Konukoglu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于共形风险控制的轻量级误差预测框架（CFM），用于识别超分辨率（SR）重建中不可信区域，提升内窥镜等实时医疗视频SR系统的可靠性与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超分辨率模型在微创手术和诊断视频中具有应用潜力，但其可能引入幻觉结构和放大噪声，影响安全关键场景下的可靠性，亟需可信赖的失败检测机制。

Method: 设计了一个轻量级误差预测网络，作用于中间特征以估计像素级重建误差；结合共形推理构建Conformal Failure Masks（CFM），实现对不可信区域的定位，并提供误差限与误覆盖率的理论保障。

Result: 在图像与视频超分辨率任务上验证了方法有效性，尤其在内窥镜与机器人手术数据中成功检测出不可靠重建区域；具备低延迟、模型无关、实时部署能力。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为实时内窥镜图像超分辨率提供了模型无关且理论可证的安全增强方法，显著提升了SR系统在临床应用中的可信度。

Abstract: Super-resolution (SR) models are attracting growing interest for enhancing minimally invasive surgery and diagnostic videos under hardware constraints. However, valid concerns remain regarding the introduction of hallucinated structures and amplified noise, limiting their reliability in safety-critical settings. We propose a direct and practical framework to make SR systems more trustworthy by identifying where reconstructions are likely to fail. Our approach integrates a lightweight error-prediction network that operates on intermediate representations to estimate pixel-wise reconstruction error. The module is computationally efficient and low-latency, making it suitable for real-time deployment. We convert these predictions into operational failure decisions by constructing Conformal Failure Masks (CFM), which localize regions where the SR output should not be trusted. Built on conformal risk control principles, our method provides theoretical guarantees for controlling both the tolerated error limit and the miscoverage in detected failures. We evaluate our approach on image and video SR, demonstrating its effectiveness in detecting unreliable reconstructions in endoscopic and robotic surgery settings. To our knowledge, this is the first study to provide a model-agnostic, theoretically grounded approach to improving the safety of real-time endoscopic image SR.

</details>


### [409] [Multi-View Hierarchical Graph Neural Network for Sketch-Based 3D Shape Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18019)
*Hang Cheng,Muyan He,Mingyu Fan,Chengfeng Xie,Xi Cheng,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MV-HGNN框架，通过多视图分层图神经网络建模几何关系与多级细节，并结合CLIP文本嵌入实现类别无关对齐，显著提升草图到3D形状检索在类别级和零样本场景下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在3D多视图特征聚合中忽略视图间几何关系与多级细节，且受限于可见类别，在零样本场景下表现差。

Method: 构建视图级图，利用局部图卷积与全局注意力捕获几何依赖与跨视图信息；引入视图选择器进行分层图粗化以扩大感受野并抑制冗余视图干扰；使用CLIP文本嵌入作为语义原型，将草图与3D特征投影至共享语义空间；采用两阶段训练（类别级）与单阶段训练（零样本）策略。

Result: 在两个公开基准上，MV-HGNN在类别级和零样本SBSR任务中均优于当前最优方法。

Conclusion: MV-HGNN有效增强了3D表示能力与跨模态语义对齐能力，解决了传统SBSR方法在几何建模与泛化性方面的关键缺陷。

Abstract: Sketch-based 3D shape retrieval (SBSR) aims to retrieve 3D shapes that are consistent with the category of the input hand-drawn sketch. The core challenge of this task lies in two aspects: existing methods typically employ simplified aggregation strategies for independently encoded 3D multi-view features, which ignore the geometric relationships between views and multi-level details, resulting in weak 3D representation. Simultaneously, traditional SBSR methods are constrained by visible category limitations, leading to poor performance in zero-shot scenarios. To address these challenges, we propose Multi-View Hierarchical Graph Neural Network (MV-HGNN), a novel framework for SBSR. Specifically, we construct a view-level graph and capture adjacent geometric dependencies and cross-view message passing via local graph convolution and global attention. A view selector is further introduced to perform hierarchical graph coarsening, enabling a progressively larger receptive field for graph convolution and mitigating the interference of redundant views, which leads to more discriminate discriminative hierarchical 3D representation. To enable category agnostic alignment and mitigate overfitting to seen classes, we leverage CLIP text embeddings as semantic prototypes and project both sketch and 3D features into a shared semantic space. We use a two-stage training strategy for category-level retrieval and a one-stage strategy for zero-shot retrieval under the same model architecture. Under both category-level and zero-shot settings, extensive experiments on two public benchmarks demonstrate that MV-HGNN outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [410] [CFSR: Geometry-Conditioned Shadow Removal via Physical Disentanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18032)
*Pan Wang,Yihao Hu,Xiujin Liu,Hang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CFSR框架，通过融合3D几何线索与大模型语义先验，将阴影去除建模为物理约束的图像恢复过程，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统阴影去除网络缺乏物理可解释性，难以兼顾局部纹理恢复与全局光照一致性。

Method: 提出多模态先验驱动的CFSR框架：1）构建HVI颜色空间融合RGB与深度先验；2）设计几何与语义双显式引导注意力机制，利用DINO特征和3D法向量调制注意力矩阵；3）引入冻结CLIP编码器注入整体先验；4）提出频域协同重建模块（FCRM），解耦高频边界与低频光照恢复。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的基准数据集上达到当前最优性能（SOTA）。

Conclusion: 将物理约束引入阴影去除任务，结合多模态先验与频域解耦重建，显著提升了恢复结果的真实性与一致性。

Abstract: Traditional shadow removal networks often treat image restoration as an unconstrained mapping, lacking the physical interpretability required to balance localized texture recovery with global illumination consistency. To address this, we propose CFSR, a multi-modal prior-driven framework that reframes shadow removal as a physics-constrained restoration process. By seamlessly integrating 3D geometric cues with large-scale foundation model semantics, CFSR effectively bridges the 2D-3D domain gap. Specifically, we first map observations into a custom HVI color space to suppress shadow-induced noise and robustly fuse RGB data with estimated depth priors. At its core, our Geometric & Semantic Dual Explicit Guided Attention mechanism utilizes DINO features and 3D surface normals to directly modulate the attention affinity matrix, structurally enforcing physical lighting constraints. To recover severely degraded regions, we inject holistic priors via a frozen CLIP encoder. Finally, our Frequency Collaborative Reconstruction Module (FCRM) achieves an optimal synthesis by decoupling the decoding process. Conditioned on geometric priors, FCRM seamlessly harmonizes the reconstruction of sharp high-frequency occlusion boundaries with the restoration of low-frequency global illumination. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CFSR achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple challenging benchmarks.

</details>


### [411] [HABIT: Chrono-Synergia Robust Progressive Learning Framework for Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18037)
*Zixu Li,Yupeng Hu,Zhiwei Chen,Shiqi Zhang,Qinlei Huang,Zhiheng Fu,Yinwei Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出HABIT框架，通过互知识估计与双一致性渐进学习模块，有效应对组合图像检索中因标注噪声导致的Noise Triplet Correspondence（NTC）问题，显著提升模型在噪声下的鲁棒性与检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合图像检索（CIR）面临标注高成本与主观性强导致的Noise Triplet Correspondence（NTC）问题，严重影响实际部署。

Method: 提出HABIT框架：1）互知识估计模块，利用组合特征与目标图像间互信息的转移率评估样本清洁度；2）双一致性渐进学习模块，通过历史模型与当前模型协同模拟人类习惯形成机制，实现噪声下稳健学习。

Result: 在两个标准CIR数据集上，HABIT在多种噪声比例下均显著优于主流方法，展现出更强的鲁棒性与检索性能。

Conclusion: HABIT为解决CIR任务中的NTC问题提供了有效且可扩展的范式，兼顾语义差异精准建模与渐进式噪声适应能力。

Abstract: Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) is a flexible image retrieval paradigm that enables users to accurately locate the target image through a multimodal query composed of a reference image and modification text. Although this task has demonstrated promising applications in personalized search and recommendation systems, it encounters a severe challenge in practical scenarios known as the Noise Triplet Correspondence (NTC) problem. This issue primarily arises from the high cost and subjectivity involved in annotating triplet data. To address this problem, we identify two central challenges: the precise estimation of composed semantic discrepancy and the insufficient progressive adaptation to modification discrepancy. To tackle these challenges, we propose a cHrono-synergiA roBust progressIve learning framework for composed image reTrieval (HABIT), which consists of two core modules. First, the Mutual Knowledge Estimation Module quantifies sample cleanliness by calculating the Transition Rate of mutual information between the composed feature and the target image, thereby effectively identifying clean samples that align with the intended modification semantics. Second, the Dual-consistency Progressive Learning Module introduces a collaborative mechanism between the historical and current models, simulating human habit formation to retain good habits and calibrate bad habits, ultimately enabling robust learning under the presence of NTC. Extensive experiments conducted on two standard CIR datasets demonstrate that HABIT significantly outperforms most methods under various noise ratios, exhibiting superior robustness and retrieval performance. Codes are available at https://github.com/Lee-zixu/HABIT

</details>


### [412] [GS-STVSR: Ultra-Efficient Continuous Spatio-Temporal Video Super-Resolution via 2D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18047)
*Mingyu Shi,Xin Di,Long Peng,Boxiang Cao,Anran Wu,Zhanfeng Feng,Jiaming Guo,Renjing Pei,Xueyang Fu,Yang Cao,Zhengjun Zha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GS-STVSR，一种基于2D高斯点绘的超高效连续时空视频超分辨率框架，通过连续运动建模驱动高斯核的时空演化，避免密集网格查询，在保持SOTA质量的同时显著提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于隐式神经表示（INR）的连续时空视频超分辨率（C-STVSR）方法依赖密集像素级网格查询，导致计算成本随插值帧数线性增长，严重限制推理效率。

Method: 提出GS-STVSR框架：利用2D高斯点绘建模视频；设计光流引导的运动模块预测任意时刻高斯位置与颜色；利用协方差参数的时间稳定性实现轻量中间拟合；引入协方差重采样对齐模块防止协方差漂移；采用自适应偏移窗口处理大尺度运动。

Result: 在Vid4、GoPro和Adobe240数据集上达到SOTA质量；常规时序尺度（X2–X8）下推理时间几乎恒定；极端尺度X32下实现超3倍加速。

Conclusion: GS-STVSR通过摒弃密集查询、引入高斯点绘与连续运动建模，在保证高质量重建的同时大幅提升了C-STVSR的推理效率与实用价值。

Abstract: Continuous Spatio-Temporal Video Super-Resolution (C-STVSR) aims to simultaneously enhance the spatial resolution and frame rate of videos by arbitrary scale factors, offering greater flexibility than fixed-scale methods that are constrained by predefined upsampling ratios. In recent years, methods based on Implicit Neural Representations (INR) have made significant progress in C-STVSR by learning continuous mappings from spatio-temporal coordinates to pixel values. However, these methods fundamentally rely on dense pixel-wise grid queries, causing computational cost to scale linearly with the number of interpolated frames and severely limiting inference efficiency. We propose GS-STVSR, an ultra-efficient C-STVSR framework based on 2D Gaussian Splatting (2D-GS) that drives the spatiotemporal evolution of Gaussian kernels through continuous motion modeling, bypassing dense grid queries entirely. We exploit the strong temporal stability of covariance parameters for lightweight intermediate fitting, design an optical flow-guided motion module to derive Gaussian position and color at arbitrary time steps, introduce a Covariance resampling alignment module to prevent covariance drift, and propose an adaptive offset window for large-scale motion. Extensive experiments on Vid4, GoPro, and Adobe240 show that GS-STVSR achieves state-of-the-art quality across all benchmarks. Moreover, its inference time remains nearly constant at conventional temporal scales (X2--X8) and delivers over X3 speedup at extreme scales X32, demonstrating strong practical applicability.

</details>


### [413] [INTENT: Invariance and Discrimination-aware Noise Mitigation for Robust Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18051)
*Zhiwei Chen,Yupeng Hu,Zhiheng Fu,Zixu Li,Jiale Huang,Qinlei Huang,Yinwei Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出INTENT模型，通过视觉不变性组合和双目标判别学习，解决合成图像检索中的跨模态对应噪声和模态固有噪声问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成图像检索（CIR）方法假设所有样本标注正确，但实际数据集中存在因高昂标注成本导致的三元组标注错误，即噪声三元组对应（NTC）问题；该问题包含跨模态对应噪声和常被忽视的模态固有噪声。

Method: 提出INTENT网络：1）视觉不变性组合模块，利用FFT进行因果干预以生成干预后的组合特征，增强视觉不变性并抑制模态固有噪声；2）双目标判别学习模块，结合正负样本协同优化，构建可扩展、动态调整的决策边界以提升对应判别鲁棒性。

Result: 在两个主流CIR基准数据集上，INTENT显著优于现有方法，展现出更强的性能与抗噪鲁棒性。

Conclusion: INTENT有效建模并缓解CIR中两类噪声，为真实场景下更鲁棒的多模态检索提供了新思路与实用方案。

Abstract: Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) is a challenging image retrieval paradigm that enables to retrieve target images based on multimodal queries consisting of reference images and modification texts. Although substantial progress has been made in recent years, existing methods assume that all samples are correctly matched. However, in real-world scenarios, due to high triplet annotation costs, CIR datasets inevitably contain annotation errors, resulting in incorrectly matched triplets. To address this issue, the problem of Noisy Triplet Correspondence (NTC) has attracted growing attention. We argue that noise in CIR can be categorized into two types: cross-modal correspondence noise and modality-inherent noise. The former arises from mismatches across modalities, whereas the latter originates from intra-modal background interference or visual factors irrelevant to the coarse-grained modification annotations. However, modality-inherent noise is often overlooked, and research on cross-modal correspondence noise remains nascent. To tackle above issues, we propose the Invariance and discrimiNaTion-awarE Noise neTwork (INTENT), comprising two components: Visual Invariant Composition and Bi-Objective Discriminative Learning, specifically designed to handle the two-aspect noise. The former applies causal intervention on the visual side via Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) to generate intervened composed features, enforcing visual invariance and enabling the model to ignore modality-inherent noise during composition. The latter adopts collaborative optimization with both positive and negative samples, and constructs a scalable decision boundary that dynamically adjusts decisions based on the loyalty degree, enabling robust correspondence discrimination. Extensive experiments on two widely used benchmark datasets demonstrate the superiority and robustness of INTENT.

</details>


### [414] [Enhancing Continual Learning of Vision-Language Models via Dynamic Prefix Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18075)
*Hyeonseo Jang,Hyuk Kwon,Kibok Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Dynamic Prefix Weighting (DPW)的新框架，用于提升视觉-语言模型在领域-类别增量学习中的性能。该方法通过动态加权前缀和适配器，根据输入token的重要性自适应调整参数，从而超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于前缀调优或适配器的参数高效方法通常对前缀向量进行统一归一化，忽略了不同输入token需要不同程度调整的事实。

Method: 提出DPW框架，包含两个核心组件：1）门控模块，依据输入token重要性动态调整各前缀权重；2）加权机制，将适配器输出权重设为前缀调优权重的残差，实现按需启用适配器。

Result: 在视觉-语言模型的领域-类别增量学习场景中，DPW取得了当前最优性能。

Conclusion: 动态加权前缀与适配器协同机制能更有效地建模输入token差异性，显著提升VLM在增量学习任务中的泛化能力与适应性。

Abstract: We investigate recently introduced domain-class incremental learning scenarios for vision-language models (VLMs). Recent works address this challenge using parameter-efficient methods, such as prefix-tuning or adapters, which facilitate model adaptation to downstream tasks by incorporating task-specific information into input tokens through additive vectors. However, previous approaches often normalize the weights of these vectors, disregarding the fact that different input tokens require different degrees of adjustment. To overcome this issue, we propose Dynamic Prefix Weighting (DPW), a framework that dynamically assigns weights to prefixes, complemented by adapters. DPW consists of 1) a gating module that adjusts the weights of each prefix based on the importance of the corresponding input token, and 2) a weighting mechanism that derives adapter output weights as a residual of prefix-tuning weights, ensuring that adapters are utilized only when necessary. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in domain-class incremental learning scenarios for VLMs. The code is available at: https://github.com/YonseiML/dpw.

</details>


### [415] [Class-specific diffusion models improve military object detection in a low-data domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18076)
*Ella P. Fokkinga,Jan Erik van Woerden,Thijs A. Eker,Sebastiaan P. Snel,Elfi I. S. Hofmeijer,Klamer Schutte,Friso G. Heslinga*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于扩散模型的合成图像在低数据条件下提升军事车辆检测性能的有效性，通过微调FLUX.1模型生成类别特异性图像，并结合ControlNet增强几何控制，显著提升了小样本下的检测精度（最高+8.0% mAP₅₀）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在军事AI系统训练中，真实标注数据稀缺，传统仿真流程成本高、灵活性差，亟需一种低成本、高可控性的合成数据生成方法来提升低数据场景下的目标检测性能。

Method: 使用LoRA对FLUX.1[dev]进行轻量微调（每类仅8或24张实图），构建15类军事车辆的类别特异性扩散模型；结合自动文本提示生成合成图像；进一步引入ControlNet与Canny边缘图引导，实现视角和姿态可控的结构化合成（FLUX-CN）。所有合成数据均未引入额外真实样本。

Result: FLUX生成图像使检测mAP₅₀最高提升8.0%（8样本/类）；FLUX-CN在极低数据下进一步提升4.1% mAP₅₀，但在更多真实数据时无额外增益；验证了生成式图像可替代传统仿真流程。

Conclusion: 面向特定物体的扩散模型能有效缓解军事目标检测中的低数据瓶颈；结构化引导（如ControlNet）的价值随真实数据减少而凸显；生成式图像数据是构建军事AI系统的可行且高效替代方案。

Abstract: Diffusion-based image synthesis has emerged as a promising source of synthetic training data for AI-based object detection and classification. In this work, we investigate whether images generated with diffusion can improve military vehicle detection under low-data conditions. We fine-tuned the text-to-image diffusion model FLUX.1 [dev] using LoRA with only 8 or 24 real images per class across 15 vehicle categories, resulting in class-specific diffusion models, which were used to generate new samples from automatically generated text prompts. The same real images were used to fine-tune the RF-DETR detector for a 15-class object detection task. Synthetic datasets generated by the diffusion models were then used to further improve detector performance. Importantly, no additional real data was required, as the generative models leveraged the same limited training samples. FLUX-generated images improved detection performance, particularly in the low-data regime (up to +8.0% mAP$_{50}$ with 8 real samples). To address the limited geometric control of text prompt-based diffusion, we additionally generated structurally guided synthetic data using ControlNet with Canny edge-map conditioning, yielding a FLUX-ControlNet (FLUX-CN) dataset with explicit control over viewpoint and pose. Structural guidance further enhanced performance when data is scarce (+4.1% mAP$_{50}$ with 8 real samples), but no additional benefit was observed when more real data is available. This study demonstrates that object-specific diffusion models are effective for improving military object detection in a low-data domain, and that structural guidance is most beneficial when real data is highly limited. These results highlight generative image data as an alternative to traditional simulation pipelines for the training of military AI systems.

</details>


### [416] [Autonomous Unmanned Aircraft Systems for Enhanced Search and Rescue of Drowning Swimmers: Image-Based Localization and Mission Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18088)
*Sascha Emanuel Zell,Toni Schneidereit,Armin Fügenschuh,Michael Breuß*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于无人机系统（UAS）的水上救援方案，利用YOLO系列模型自动检测溺水者，并通过离散事件仿真评估其相较于标准救援操作（SRO）在响应时间上的显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 溺水救援面临时间紧迫、水域广阔且常无人监管、目标定位难及人员运输困难等挑战，亟需技术手段缩短响应时间。

Method: 采用YOLOv3/v5/v8（nano至xlarge）进行溺水者图像检测，构建专用数据集并评估mAP@.5与mAP@.5:.95；同时设计两种离散事件仿真（DES）模型，模拟SRO与UAS救援响应时间，并优化UAS配置（无人机类型、数量与机库位置）。

Result: 在德国卢萨蒂亚湖地区测试中，仅含2个机库（各1架无人机）的小型UAS即可将响应时间缩短为SRO的五分之一；YOLO模型中较新版本与更大尺寸在检测精度上表现更优。

Conclusion: UAS可作为SRO的有效补充，显著提升水上救援效率；YOLO适用于实时溺水者检测任务，结合DES仿真可科学部署UAS资源。

Abstract: Drowning is an omnipresent risk associated with any activity on or in the water, and rescuing a drowning person is particularly challenging because of the time pressure, making a short response time important. Further complicating water rescue are unsupervised and extensive swimming areas, precise localization of the target, and the transport of rescue personnel. Technical innovations can provide a remedy: We propose an Unmanned Aircraft System (UAS), also known as a drone-in-a-box system, consisting of a fleet of Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) allocated to purpose-built hangars near swimming areas. In an emergency, the UAS can be deployed in addition to Standard Rescue Operation (SRO) equipment to locate the distressed person early by performing a fully automated Search and Rescue (S&R) operation and dropping a flotation device. In this paper, we address automatically locating distressed swimmers using the image-based object detection architecture You Only Look Once (YOLO). We present a dataset created for this application and outline the training process. We evaluate the performance of YOLO versions 3, 5, and 8 and architecture sizes (nano, extra-large) using Mean Average Precision (mAP) metrics mAP@.5 and mAP@.5:.95. Furthermore, we present two Discrete-Event Simulation (DES) approaches to simulate response times of SRO and UAS-based water rescue. This enables estimation of time savings relative to SRO when selecting the UAS configuration (type, number, and location of UAVs and hangars). Computational experiments for a test area in the Lusatian Lake District, Germany, show that UAS assistance shortens response time. Even a small UAS with two hangars, each containing one UAV, reduces response time by a factor of five compared to SRO.

</details>


### [417] [Decision-Aware Attention Propagation for Vision Transformer Explainability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.18094)
*Sehyeong Jo,Gangjae Jang,Haesol Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Decision-Aware Attention Propagation (DAP)的归因方法，通过将决策相关先验注入Transformer的注意力传播过程，提升ViT模型的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
